tirukkuṟaḷ urikaḷiyellām kaṟṟa perukkam oruvaṉukku iruntālum parimēlaḻakar uriyi naṉku kaṟṟavaṉ eṉṟu vīṟupaṭappārāṭṭuvatu uṇṭaṉṟō? atupōl eḻuttu, col, poruḷ eṉṟa muppulavaṟivu iruntālum tolkāppiyar tam aintiṇiyilakkaṇa vaṉmiyip poruḷatikārattu virivupaṭa veḷippaṭuttiyuḷḷār. ātaliṉ `aintiram niṟinta' eṉṟa peyar vīṟu peṟṟāhr eṉka. iṉpamum poruḷum aṟaṉum eṉṟāṅku aṉpoṭu puṇarnta aintiṇi maruṅkiṉ kāmakkūṭṭam' eṉṟa tolkāppiyattoṭar oru cāṉṟākum. aintiṇi eṉavē oruvāṟu puṟamum aṭaṅkum, eḻuttum collumē pulantokuttiruntāl tolkāppiyar olkāpperumi eytiyiruppārā? eḻuttum collum illātu. poruḷoṉṟukkē pulantokuttiruntāl apperumi peṟṟirukkamāṭṭārā? uṟaḻntu pārkka.
aintiṟam eṉa valliṉappāṭam iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉpar cilar. attaku pāṭapētam illi; eṉiṉum tiram eṉṟa iṭiyiṉap pāṭattukkē mikutal. āṟṟal eṉṟa poruḷ koḷḷalām.
14-4-1986il ulakat tamiḻ caṅkat toṭakkaviḻāvil veḷiyiṭap peṟṟa aintiṟam eṉṟa nūl aṇmik kālattut tōṉṟiyatu. tūya naṟṟamiḻil nūṟpā yāppiṉatu. palatuṟik kalikkuṟippuṭiyatu. mayakkanaṭi mikkatu. paṉampāraṉār pāyiram kūṟum aintirattukkum ipputu nūlukkum evvakiyāṉum toṭarpilli.
`mayaṅkāmarapiṉ eḻuttumuṟi kāṭṭi' eṉpataṟku muṉṉi nūlkaḷil eḻuttilakkaṇam collilakkaṇattuḷ mayaṅkik kiṭantatu eṉa iḷampūraṇarum, muttamiḻ mayaṅkik kiṭantatu eṉac civañāṉa muṉivarum viḷakkuvar. tol kāppiyattukku muntiya nūl etuvum kiṭikkātapōtu, ivvāṟu poruḷ koḷḷac cāṉṟu eṉṉa? eḻuttumuṟi - nūṉmuṟi eṉṟu porunta poruḷ ceyvar aracañ caṇmukaṉār. `eḻuttumuṟi' eṉṟa eṉ cintaṉik kaṭṭuriyil tolkāppiyar nūl eḻutiya muṟikaḷi ārāyntu eḻutiyuḷḷēṉ. ippāyirattil varum pōkkaṟu paṉuval, nāṉmaṟi, aintiṟam eṉṟa pakutikaḷ mēlum cintaṉikku uriyavi.
pāyiramuṭivuri
ippāyira muṭivuriyāka varalāṟṟu nōkkilum kāppu nōkkilum vaḷarcci nōkkilum tamiḻ, tamiḻiṉam, tamiḻakam paṟṟi eññāṉṟum iṉṟiyamiyāta aṟivuriyi eḻutuvēṉ. iḵtu urikkuppuṟameṉa eṇṇuvatu tavaṟu. urikkāppu, urivaḷarcci, urikkalvi ellām nilipeṟa vēṇṭumeṉiṉ tamiḻ nilipeṟṟāka vēṇṭum; tamiḻ nilipeṟutal eṉpatu tamiḻar nilipēṟākum; tamiḻar nilipēṟu eṉpatu tamiḻakam nili pēṟākum; tamiḻakam iṭattāṟkuṉṟiṉ, tamiḻar eṇṇikki iyalpākak kuṉṟum; tamiḻar eṇ kuṉṟiṉ. kuṭiyaracil tamiḻiṉnili āṟṟalaṟṟuk kuṉṟum. ivvāṟu nilamum makkaḷum moḻiyum kuṉṟiṉ ellākkaliyum tamiḻil kuṉṟum. maṟiyavum aḻiyavum ceyyum. ulakiṉi nilipeṟuttalum iṟivaṉiṉ oru kaṭamiyākac cuṭṭap paṭumāyiṉ. tamiḻiṉi nilattālum āḷeṇṇālum nilipeṟuttal tamiḻiṉattiṉ kaṭami yaṉṟō?
tollūḻik kālattut tamiḻ nākarikam mokeñcotari ārappākāṟum parantu kiṭantatu. paṉampāraṉār, tolkāppiyaṉār kālattut tamiḻulakam teṟkē paṉṉeṭuṅ kāvatam paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟoṭu kumarikkōṭāka nīṇṭiruntiruntatu. peruṅkaṭal kōḷukkuppiṉ kumarikkōṭu tāḻat teṉṉelli mākaṭalāyiṟṟu. kiḻakkē pūmpukār māmallapuram ellām kaṭalvāy amiḻntaṉa. teṉkumariyum paṉmuṟi alivāyp paṭṭuc curuṅkalāyiṟṟu. iyaṟkic cīṟṟattāl tamiḻpperunilam eytiya aḻivukaḷ nilanūliṉar tokuttuc cāṉṟupaṭak kāṭṭuvar. nam kaṇkāṇak kōṭik kariyum kariyalāyiṟṟu. perun tamiḻakam perukkuṟukkampaṭṭataṟkuc ciṉapperuṅkaṭal peruṅkāraṇam. itaṉip poṟāta tuṟavi iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ. “kumarikkōṭum koṭuṅkaṭal koḷḷa” eṉṟu vitu pāṭiṉār; vitu pāṭi nāṭṭuppaṟṟiyūṭṭiṉar.
kaṭalkōḷ pōla moḻikkalappukkōḷum tamiḻp perunilattik kuṟukkiyatu eṉṟa varalāṟṟuṇmi palarkkup paṭavilli; eṭuttuc coṉṉālum ēṟuvatilliyē. cilappatikāramum patiṟṟuppattum iṉṉa piṟavum vaḻaṅkiya cēranāṭum āḻvārkaḷ pācuram icinta vēṅkaṭamaliyum tamiḻiṟ pirintu pāratattiṉ piṟa mānilaṅkaḷ āyiṉa. ayalnāṭu kavaravilli eṉṟa ōr amiti namakkut tōṉṟiṉum. tamiḻukku iḻappu eṉpatu veḷippaṭi. taṅkap poṉkaṭṭi maṭi tavaṟip pārata nilattupputiyuṇṭālum, uṭiyāṉukku iḻapput tāṉē. vēṅkaṭamum kumariyum teyvattaṉmiyaṉa. kēṭila eṉṟu iḷampūraṇar mutalāyiṉōr pukaḻntālum tamiḻarkku eṉṉāyiṉa?
paṉṉeṭuṅkālam tamiḻakamāka irunta cēranāṭṭup pakutiyum vēṅkaṭappakutiyum ayalnilamāṉatu paṭiyeṭup pālaṉṟu. paṇpāṭu māṟṟattālaṉṟu. āṭci vēṟupāṭ ṭālāṉṟu. orē oru kāraṇam ayaloli ayaleḻuttuk kalanta ayalmoḻik kalappākum. tamiḻakattiṉ ellip pakutikaḷi viḻippōṭu tamiḻākak kāttuk koḷḷat tavaṟiyatālum, ayalmoḻikaḷ ellārtam cevivāykaḷil nirampap pukuntamiyālum, ammoḻikaḷit tol kāppiyam mutalāṉa ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ kūṟiyapaṭi tamiḻ vaṭivākkik koḷḷātu viṭṭamiyālum, ayaloli kaḷōṭu ayalvarivaṭivaṅkaḷum eḻuttuvaḻakkil iṭam peṟṟamiyālum tamiḻukku uḷḷa iṭam curuṅkiṟṟu; ayalukku iṭamperukiṟṟu; eṉavē moḻiyolik kalappu tamiḻukku iṭaviḻappu; piṟamoḻikaṭku iṭavirippu. ivvaralāṟṟu niliyi iṉiyēṉum tamiḻiṉattār eṇṇi uḷḷa tamiḻ nilattiyāvatu emmoḻikkalappum evvolik kalappum evveḻuttuk kalappum pukaviṭātu kāttuk koḷḷa vēṇṭāmā? kaṇkāṇa iḻantum aṟivuṇarvu vēṇṭāmā?
kalappiṉṟit tamiḻit tamiḻiyaṟkippaṭi kākkum tuṇivum aṟivum avviyaṟkippaṭi kālattuṟkēṟṟa kalit tuṟikaḷi yellām koṇṭu vantu vaḷarkkum uramum taramum, tamiḻit taṉṉiṟivuṭiyatākkum āḷviṉiyum āciyum ovvoru tamiḻaccikkum tamiḻaṉukkum piṟappuk kaṭamiyākum. ituvari uṇarārum iṉiyuṇarka.
orupāl ivvāṟu palliḻappu nēriṉum intiya moḻikaḷuḷ tamiḻukkut taṉi mutaṉmiyuṇṭu. ulakamoḻi, ulakaḷāviya moḻi eṉṟu ovvoru pārataṉum pāratat tamiḻaṉum cuṭṭikkāṭṭat takka orumoḻi tamiḻmoḻiyē. palaveḷi nāṭukaḷil tamiḻkkuṭikaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  cila nāṭukaḷil tamiḻ āṭci moḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟāka iṭampeṟṟuḷḷatu. aintāvatu ulakattamiḻ mānāṭu naṭattiya ōr intiyamoḻi tamiḻē. intiya moḻikaḷuḷ mutaṟkaṇ ulaka mānāṭu kaṇṭatum tamiḻē, intiyāvil piṟa pala mānilaṅkaḷilum tamiḻar tamiḻp  paṟṟōṭu vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. enta ayalnāṭṭil iruntālum, enta ayalmānilattil iruntālum tamiḻarkku vīṭṭumoḻi tamiḻē. maṉiyakattuḷ tamiḻkkuṭumpattār pēcuvatu tamiḻē. iṉṉanaṭimuṟiyāl kaṉṉittamiḻukkut taṉitta vāḻvuṇṭu. palatoṉ moḻikaḷippōla aḻittoḻintu citiyāmal. niṉṟu vaḷarntu perukum cīriḷamit tiṟam uṇṭu. tamiḻkkulam curuṅkāmal. tamiḻiṉam. curuṅkāmal, tamiḻcuruṅkāmal. kalappu iṉiyum perukāmal tamiḻttāyik kākkum kuṟikkōḷ tamiḻarkku eṉṟum vēṇṭum.  iṉṟiya tamiḻar ikkuṟikkōḷuṭiyavar eṉpatu kaṇṭu ivvuri pārāṭṭukiṉṟatu; etirkālattamiḻariyum yāṇṭiruntālum enniliyil iruntālum tamiḻk kuṟikkōḷōṭu pāratattil taḻittu vāḻuṅkaḷ eṉṟu vaḻikāṭṭi vāḻttukiṉṟāṉ ivvuriyāḷaṉ.
tamiḻar kuṟikkōḷ tavaṟār kalappil
tamiḻē nilipput tarum.

ciṟappuppāyiram māṇikkavuri muṟṟum.


tolkāppiyam – eḻuttatikāram
mutalāvatu nūṉmarapu - māṇikkavuri
iyal muṉṉuri
nūlukku marapāka vaḻaṅkiya kuṟiyīṭukaḷik kūṟuvatu nūṉmarapākum. ivviyalil eḻuttilakkaṇattukku uriya eḻuttu, uyir, kuṟil, neṭil, mey, valliṉam, melliṉam. iṭiyiṉam mutalāṉa pala kuṟiyīṭukaḷ kūṟappaṭum. curukkam, viḷakkam, eṭuttāṭci ivi kuṟiyīṭṭiṉ payaṉ. aṟiviyal kaliyiyal eṉappaṭṭa ellāttuṟikaṭkum kuṟiyīṭukaḷ uḷa.
eḻuttatikārattiṉ mutaliyalāṉa nūṉmarapil eḻuttuk kuṟiyīṭukaḷic colvatu pōlac collatikārattiṉ mutaliyalāṉa kiḷaviyākkattil uyartiṇi, aḵṟiṇi, peyarviṉi iṭi uri eṉṟiṉṉa collilakkaṇak kuṟikaḷiyum poruḷatikārattiṉ mutalāvatu akattiṇi yiyalil peruntiṇi, aintiṇi, kikkiḷi, mutal karu uri eṉṟiṉiya poruḷilakkaṇak kuṟikaḷiyum colluvar āciriyar. pāyiram kūṟuvatupōla itu mayaṅkāmarapiṉ ōr eḻuttumuṟi.
nūl - ilakkaṇanūl; īṇṭu pōkkaṟu paṉuvalāṉa tolkāppiyattik kuṟikkum. mutalāvatu iyalukku eḻuttumarapu eṉṟu peyar irukkalām. āṉāl nūliṉ toṭakkaviyal eṉṟu kāṭṭuvataṟku nūṉmarapu eṉṟa peyar vikkappaṭṭatu. muṉṉuriyi nūṉmukam eṉṟu iṉṟum nām vaḻaṅkukiṉṟōm.
iyaṟkaruttu
tamiḻmoḻi akaramutal ṉakaram vari muppatu eḻuttukkaḷ (olikaḷ) koṇṭatu. kuṟṟiyalikaram. kuṟṟiyalukaram. āytam eṉṟa mūṉṟum eḻuttup pōlvaṉa. ivi cārpeḻuttām. puḷḷipeṟum. kuṟṟeḻuttu aintu oru māttiri icikkum. neṭṭeḻuttu ēḻu. iraṇṭu māttiri icikkum ōreḻuttu mūṉṟu māttiri olikkum taṉmi tamiḻil illi. kūṭutalāṉa māttiri olikkavēṇṭiṉ. vēṇṭiya aḷapukkuriya eḻuttukkaḷik kūṭṭi olikka vēṇṭum. imikkum kālamum noṭikkum kālamum oru māttirik kālavaḷavām.
akaramutal oḷakāram vari paṉṉiraṇṭum uyir eṉṟa kuṟiyum. kakaramutal ṉakaram vari patiṉeṭṭum mey eṉṟa kuṟiyum peṟum. meykkum mūṉṟu cārpeḻuttukkum māttiri ari. makaramey kāl māttiri olikkum iṭaṉuṇṭu.
kāl māttiri peṟum makarameykkup puḷḷi vaṭivu uḷḷāka irukkum. (ma) potuvāka meyyeḻuttukkaḷ taṉiniliyil puḷḷivaṭivukaḷ peṟum. uyireḻuttukkaḷil ekarattukkum okarattukkum puḷḷivaṭivu uṇṭu. (eo) meyyum uyirum cērntu uyir meyyākuṅkāl varivaṭivu māṟṟaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭum. k, c, t eṉavarum meyyeḻuttiṉ mēl irukkum puḷḷiyi maṭṭum eṭuttuviṭṭāl ka, ca, ta, eṉṟu akarattoṭu kūṭiya uyirmeyyākiviṭum. ēṉi uyir meykaḷ kā, ki, kī, ku, kū, ke, kē,  ki, ko, kō, ku eṉṟavāṟu pala vaṭivuttiripukaḷ eytum. uyirmey eṉpatu kuṟiyīṭāka iruntālum mutalil meyyoliyum piṉ uyiroliyum tōṉṟum.
mey valleḻuttu āṟu, melleḻuttu āṟu, iṭiyeḻuttu āṟu eṉṟu muvviṉamām. ivi tammuḷ toṭaruvatu meymmayakkam (kalappu, iyipu,) eṉappaṭum appā, aṇṇā, allā, eṉat taṉmeykkuppiṉ taṉmeyyē varutaluṇṭu. āṭci, āṇṭu āyvu eṉa orumeykkuppiṉ vēṟṟumey varutalumuṇṭu. ivi oroṟṟuṭaṉili eṉappaṭum. patiṉeṭṭu meykaḷil ra, ḻa, nīṅkiya patiṉāṟu meykaḷ tammuṉtām varum. ra, ḻa, iraṇṭiṉ muṉ cārpu vāḻvu eṉap piṟa eḻuttukkaḷē varum.
kuṟṟeḻuttu aintil a, i, u eṉṟa mūṉṟum cuṭṭuk kuṟiyum. neṭṭeḻuttu ēḻil ā, ē, ō eṉṟa mūṉṟum viṉākkuṟiyum peṟum.
uyireḻuttukkaḷum meyyeḻuttukkaḷum mēlē colliya māttiriyaḷavukaḷik kaṭantu, icittamiḻil icittal uṇṭu eṉṟu yāḻmaṟi kūṟum.
karuttu viḷakkam
eḻuttu eṉpatu tamiḻil poruṭcuṭṭuṭiya moḻiyoliyik kuṟikkum; varivaṭiviyum kuṟikkum. nūṉmarapu mutaṟkaṇ olikaḷiyum olikkuriya aḷapukaḷiyum kūṟiya piṉ, cilavaṟṟiṟku varivaṭivum kūṟum. ilakkaṇat teḷivukku vēṇṭappaṭum varivaṭivukaḷi maṭṭum tolkāppiyam nūṉmarapiṟ collum.
mayakkam - oru meyyeḻuttōṭu oru meyyeḻuttut toṭarum nili, cērntiyiyum taṉmi. nām iṉṟu kūṟum kalakkam, kuḻappam eṉṟa poruḷilli. meymmayakkam eṉpatu tamiḻukku uyiraṉiya amippukkūṟu. iṉṉa meykkuppiṉ iṉṉameytāṉ varalām eṉṟa moḻiyiyalpi itaṉāl uṇaralām. moḻi nilipēṟṟukkum moḻik kāppukkum collākkattiṟkum colloḻuṅkukkum iv vilakkaṇam varampāṉatu. itu ceyaṟkiyākap pukuttum puṟavitiyaṉṟu. makkaḷ vāyvaḻakkiliruntu tōṉṟum iyalpu neṟi. moḻikku moḻi immayakkam vēṟupaṭumātaliṉ tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ amippi naṉkuaṟintu vaḻātu pēcavum eḻutavum vēṇṭum. ellā ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum itaṉi yoṭṭi naṭattaliṉ nūṉmarapu meymmayakkam paṟṟi oṉpatu nūṟpākkaḷil virittuk kūṟukiṉṟatu.
oli, eṇṇikki, vaki, aḷapu, vaṭivu, mayakkam eṉṟa eḻuttiṉ aṟukūṟukaḷ nūṉmarapil moḻiyappaṭṭaṉa. mēlum innūṉmarapu taṉiyeḻuttukkut taṉitta niliyil, ataṉ taṉmikaḷi nuvalum. varivaṭivupaṟṟiya ceyti nūṉmarapaḷavil niṉṟuviṭum. puṇarcciyilakkaṇam aṉittum oliniliyil vittu vitikkappaṭuvaṉa eṉpataṉi maṟavātu niṉittuk koḷka. ātaliṉ eḻuttu eṉpatu oliniliyik kuṟikkum eṉavum eḻuttatikāram eṉpatu olinili yaṭippaṭiyil oli, piṟappu, oliyiṭippuṇarcci mutalāṉa ilakkaṇa vitikaḷic cuṭṭum atikāram eṉavum aṟika.
muppatu taṉiyolikkum muppatu taṉivarivaṭivaṅkaḷ tamiḻil uṇṭu; aḵtāvatu ovvōr oliyum taṉakkeṉa oruvarivaṭivu uṭiyatu. ivvuṭimi cemmiyāṉa tamiḻ varalāṟṟukku eṭuttukkāṭṭu. iṅ'ṉam oṟṟumiyiruttaliṉ eḻuttu eṉṟa oru kuṟiyīṭē iraṇṭiṉiyum kuṟikkap payaṉpaṭṭatu; eṉiṉum ikkāla vaḷarcciyil mēlum teḷivu karuvi oliyeḻutti oliyaṉ eṉavum variyeḻutti vaṭivaṉ eṉavum putiya kuṟiyīṭukaḷ amittukkoḷvōm. oliyaṉi `eḻuttoli' eṉpar naṉnūlār.


tamiḻukku eḻuttu muppatu
(1)	eḻutteṉap paṭupa
	akaramutal ṉakara viṟuvāy
	muppaḵ teṉpa
	cārntuvaraṉ marapiṉ mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē
innūṟpā eḻuttu eṉṟa mutaṟkuṟiyīṭum muppatu eṉṟa eṇ varampum kūṟum.
akalavuri
tamiḻmoḻikku aṭippaṭi mutaleḻuttu eṉṟu vaḻivaḻic collappaṭupavi muppatu. ivi akaram toṭaṅki ṉakaraviṟutiyākum. cārpeḻuttu eṉṟu aṭutta nūṟpāvil kūṟappaṭum mūṉṟum ivaṟṟoṭu cērā. mutaleḻuttākā: mutaleḻuttu variciyil eṇṇappaṭā eṉpatu karuttu. mūṉṟalaṅkaṭi - mūṉṟu allāta iṭattu a ā i ī u ū e ē ai o ō oḷa; k ṅ c ¨ñ ṭ ṇ t n p m y r l v ḻ ḷ ṟṉ -ivi ikkāla vaṭivaṉkaḷ.
tiṟaṉuri
eḻuttu eṉpatu īṇṭu oliyeḻuttic cuṭṭum. ellā moḻikkum oli mūlam ātalāṉum, iḵtu eḻuttatikāram ātalāṉum mutalnūṟpā eḻuttu eṉat toṭaṅkukiṉṟatu. makkaḷ vāyiliruntu varum ōciyellām eḻutteṉap paṭuvatilli. poruṇmiyāṟṟaluṭiya oliyē eḻuttu eṉappaṭum. ittaṉic ciṟappiṉic cuṭṭutaṟkē `eḻuttu eṉappaṭupa' eṉṟu naṭiceytār āciriyar. tamiḻmoḻiyil vaḻaṅkum pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa coṟkaḷik koṇṭu kūṭṭiyalacip pārppiṉ, aviyellām muppatu oliyaṉil aṭaṅki niṟkum eṉpatu tamiḻ nūlōr kaṇṭa aṟiviyal muṭipu. ivvarampu tolkāppiyarkkup paṉṉeṭuṅkālattiṟku muṉpē ēṟpaṭṭu vaḻivaḻi vantatu eṉṟa pēruṇmiyi `muppaḵtu eṉpa' eṉṟa toṭarāl aṟiyalām.
eḻuttu eṉpataṉ ilakkaṇam yātu? poruṇmic cuṭṭu maṭṭum uṭiyataṉṟu; ataṉōṭu taṉakkeṉṟa poruṭkuṟippāṟṟalum poruḷ vēṟupaṭukkum muraṇāṟṟalum uṭiyatu. muppatu eḻuttukkaḷum ivvilakkaṇappaṭi oṉṟaṉiyoṉṟu vēṟupaṭuttum āṟṟaluṭiyavi; tam poruḷāṟṟali nili niṟuttupavi. avaṉ, avaḷ, avar eṉṟa mūṉṟu coṟkaḷum etaṉāl poruḷvēṟum pālvēṟum paṭukiṉṟaṉa? īṟṟiluḷḷa ṉakaram ḷakaram rakaram eṉṟa vēṟupaṭṭa oli varavē kāraṇam. vāl, vāḷ, vāḻ ivi īṟṟil varum lakaram ḷakaram ḻakaram ivaṟṟāl poruḷ vēṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē ivi oliyaṉkaḷ aṭu, āṭu, iṭu, īṭu: uṭu, ūṭu, eṭu, ēṭu; oṭu, ōṭu iviyellām poruḷ vēṟupaṭutaṟku vēṟuvēṟu uyireḻuttukkaḷē aṭippaṭiyām. iṅṅaṉam pallāyiram tamiḻccoṟkaḷip poruḷpaṭa vittu vēṟṟumikkāraṇam pārppiṉ, aviyellām muppatu eḻuttukkaḷiṉ cērkkiyākavum muppatu eḻuttukkaḷiṉ vēṟṟu nilit taṉiyāṟṟalākavum muṭiyak kāṇalām. ulakap poruṭkaḷiyellām 110 mūlaṅkaḷāka vētiyiyal aṭakki muṭippati oppunōkkuka.
vētiyiyal poruḷmūlattiṟkum tamiḻ oli mūlattiṟkum oru vēṟṟumiyuṇṭu. putupporuḷ kāṇakkāṇa vētiyiyal mūlakkūṟukaḷiṉ eṇṇikki kūṭivarum. paṇpaṭṭa tamiḻukku muppatu oliyaṉ eṉpatu muṭintavarampu. ayaloliyaṉ vantu kūṭutaṟkut tamiḻil iṭaṉilli, tamiḻukku iyaṟkiyilli eṉpatu immutal nūṟpā kāṭṭum muṭintanili. ataṉālaṉṟō kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukaram āytam mūṉṟum tamiḻolikaḷāka iruntālum mutaleḻuttāka eṇṇappaṭa villi. itaṉi mūṉṟalaṅkaṭi eḻutteṉappaṭuvaṉa muppaḵtu eṉṟa etirmaṟi naṭiyāl valiyuṟuttiṉār āciriyar. cārpeḻuttām tamilolikkē itu varampāyiṉ, ayaloliyum ayaleḻuttum tamiḻ varampukkuḷ nuḻitalō nuḻittalō cālumā? tolkāppiyattukkup piṉ vanta ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷum itālkāppiyattaṭam patintaṉavē. muppatu eṉṟa eṇ varampum eḻuttu varampum tamiḻ naṉceykku eṉṟum māṟātavi. tolkāppiyar varinta immoḻi varampiṉit tiruvaḷḷuvarum uṭaṉpaṭṭaṉar. mutaṟkuṟaḷ `akaramutal' eṉat toṭaṅkuvatum iṟutikkuṟaḷ `kūṭi muyaṅkaṟpeṟiṉ' eṉa ṉakara īṟāka muṭivatum kāṇka.
eṟumpumutal yāṉi īṟāka eṉavum oṉṟu mutal nūṟu īṟāka eṉavum kumari mutal imayam vari eṉavum naṭivaḻakku uṇṭu. atupōl akaramutal ṉakaraviṟuvāy eṉṟār. palarkkum iyalpākat terinta ceyti eṉpatu innaṭik karuttu. evvaki eḻuvāyum kūṟāmal `eṉpa' eṉṟataṉāl muppatu eṉṟa toki vaḻakku kālañcuṭṭa muṭiyāt toṉmittākum.
cārntu varal marapiṉmūṉṟu - taṉ piṟappukkum veḷippāṭṭiṟkum oru mutaleḻuttic cārntu varum iyalpuṭiyaṉa. itaṉāl eḻutteṉappaṭṭa muppatum piritoṉṟiṉi oṭṭinillātu tāmē piṟantu veḷippaṭum taṉittaṉmiyāṉa eṉpaṉa teḷivām. tolkāppiyar `eḻuttu' eṉṟu īṇṭuk kūṟiyataṉip piṉṉiyōr mutaleḻuttu eṉṟu teḷivupaṭuttuvar. ayal nāṭṭavaṉi nōkki uḷnāṭṭavaṉ eṉavum ayalmoḻiyi nōkkit tamiḻmoḻiyit tāymoḻi eṉavum colvati oppunōkkuka. `cārntu varal marapiṉ' eṉṟa aṭiyē im mūṉṟum mutaleḻuttākā eṉpataṟkuriya ētuvikkāṭṭi niṟkum.
oruviṉā: “eḻutteṉap paṭupa akaramutal ṉakaraviṟuvāy muppaḵteṉpa” eṉṟa aḷavil mutal nūṟpā amin tirukkalāmē; mūṉṟum allāta viṭattu eṉṟu vilakkic colliyataṉ payaṉ eṉṉa? itu paṟṟi aṭutta nūṟpā irukkumpōtu, ivvilakkuc collavēṇṭumā? itu vilakkiccollum kuṟippaṉṟu. muppattu mūṉṟu eṉṟu eṇṇāviṭiṉum okka uṭaṉ niṉikkat takkavi eṉpatu kuṟippu. muppatu paḻaṅkaḷ ikkūṭiyil uḷḷaṉa. mūṉṟu piñcukaḷ tavira eṉṟa naṭipōṉṟatu mutal nūṟpā naṭi. “mūṉṟutaliyiṭṭa muppatiṟṟeḻuttu” eṉṟu tolkāppiyam puṇariyal iyittumoḻitali niṉika. immūṉṟukkum taṉippuṇarccikaḷ uṇmiyiṉ okkak kūṟavēṇṭiya kaṭappāṭu ilakkaṇikku uṇṭu. ivvāṟu oru nūṟpāviṟ kūṟāviṭṭāl. immūṉṟum cārpeṉat tōṉṟātu taṉimutaṉmi peṟṟu viṭumaṉṟō? itaṉit tavirkkavē `mūṉṟalaṅkaṭi' eṉa iṟutiyiṟ kūṟiṉār. `muppaḵtu eṉpa cārntu varal marapiṉ mūṉṟalaṅkaṭiyē' eṉpataṉāl muppatu eḻuttuppōla immūṉṟum tolmarapuṭiyavi eṉṟa varalāṟu peṟappaṭum.
tamiḻmoḻikku eḻuttu muppatu eṉṟa oru ceytiyit taruvatu tolkāppiyattiṉ nōkkamaṉṟu ivvāṟu ivvarampik kaṭippiṭittu oḻukuka eṉpatu nūṟpāviṉ payaṉ.
yāmeyyāk kaṇṭavaṟṟuḷ illi eṉittoṉṟum
vāymiyiṉ nalla piṟa
eṉṟu tām teḷintuṇarntati veḷippaṭuttik koḷvatā tiruvaḷḷuvariṉ eṇṇam? illi. illi. ittaku ciṟappuṭiya vāymiyoḻukkattip poyyātu kaṭip piṭittu vāḻka eṉṟu vaḻikāṭṭuvatu tirukkuṟaḷiṉ payaṉ. inneṟippiṭiyit tolkāppiyattukkum tirukkuṟaṭkum pōṟṟal vēṇṭum.

cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟu
(2)	avitām
	kuṟṟiya likaram kuṟṟiya lukaram
	āyta meṉṟa
	muppāṟ puḷḷiyu meḻutō raṉṉa	
itu muṉnūṟpāviṟ cuṭṭiya cārpu mūṉṟiṉik kūṟum.
akalavuri
mēlinūṟpāvil cārntu varal marapuṭiya mūṉṟu eṉṟārallavā? avi kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, āytam eṉṟu kuṟiyaṉa. ivi mūṉṟum puḷḷi peṟuvaṉa. mēlinūṟpāviṟ colliya eḻutti yottaṉa.
vaḻakku kēṇmiyā, nāṭu, e.ku (eḵku)
tiṟaṉuri
kuṟiyīṭu nērkuṟi, viḷakkakkuṟi eṉa iruvaki. mukkāli, nāṟkāli, kāṟṟāṭi eṉṟa coṟkaḷ viḷakkakkuṟikaḷ; ivaṟṟiṉ ōviyaṅkaḷ nērkuṟikaḷ, taṉakkuttāṉē peyar nērkuṟi: taṉṉiyalpic cuṭṭum peyar viḷakkakkuṟi ivvakiyil eḻuttu (mutaleḻuttu) muppatum nērkuṟikaḷ. a ā k ṅ eṉpaṉa piṟappup peyar. kuṟṟiyalikaram mūṉṟum kāraṇappeyarkaḷ allatu viḷakkap peyarkaḷ eṉpatu veḷippaṭi. ivaṟṟukku nērpeyarkaḷ ila, cārntu varuvaṉavātaliṉ itukarutiyē, `eḻuttōraṉṉa' eṉṟu cārpu kūṟiṉār.
kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉṟa peyarkaḷ ikara vukara eḻuttukkaḷiṉ cāyaliṉa eṉpatu teḷivu. ikaramum ukaramum mutaleḻuttām. kuṟṟeḻuttām avi oru māttiri yuṭiyavi; kuṟṟikaram kuṟṟukaram eṉavum collappaṭum. ivaṟṟi vēṟupaṭuttavē arimāttiriyuṭiya cār peḻuttukkaḷ kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉappeyar eytiṉa. tēynta kācu, kiḻinta āṭi, iṭinta cuvar pōla eṉka.
kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukaram pōla āytamum kuṟumi yuṭiyatēṉum nēraṭiyāka oru mutaleḻuttiṉ cāyalaṉṟu. cila vuyirum cila meyyum kalanta cārpuppiṟappiṉatu. tēyvut taṉmiyatu. āytam eṉpataṉi “āytal uḷḷataṉ nuṇukkam” eṉṟa uriyiyalāṉum, “nalipuvaṇṇam āytam payilum”eṉṟa ceyyuḷiyalāṉum aṟiyalām. āyutaṅkaḷ kūrmikkākat tīṭṭit tēykkap paṭutali vaḻakkāṉum uṇarka.
immūṉṟum ōriṉam ātalāl māttiriyaḷavu kūṟumpōtu `avviyal niliyum ēṉi mūṉṟē' eṉa nūṉmarapilum piṟappilum kūṟuṅkāli tērntu veḷippaṭutta ēṉi mūṉṟum eṉap piṟappiyalilum `ēṉimūṉṟu' eṉṟa tokikkuṟi āḷuvar. muppatiṉoṭu otta ēṉi mūṉṟu eṉpatu itaṉ karuttu. immūṉṟum cārntuvarum oli niliyil maṭṭum ōriṉam alla; puḷḷipeṟum varivaṭivilum ōriṉappaṭum eṉṟu uṭaṉterivikkavē `muppāṟpuḷḷi' eṉṟār. eṉavē ovvoṉṟum puḷḷipeṟum eṉpatu teḷivu.
“oṟṟeḻuttiyaṟṟē kuṟṟiya likaram” eṉpatu ceyyuḷiyal itaṉāl kuṟṟiyalikaram oṟṟākiya puḷḷi peṟum eṉpatu pōtarum. “meyyīṟellām puḷḷiyoṭu niliyil “kuṟṟiya lukaramum aṟṟeṉa moḻipa” eṉṟa puṇariyal nūṟpākkaḷāl kuṟṟiyalukaram puḷḷipeṟum eṉpatu veḷippaṭi. `āytappuḷḷi' eṉṟē moḻi marapilum puḷḷi mayaṅkiyalilum kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyalilum kūṟuvatāl puḷḷipeṟum eṉpatu teḷivu. puḷḷi eytum taṉmiyālum ivi ōriṉam eṉpataṉi veḷippaṭuttavē `muppāṟpuḷḷi' eṉṟār āciriyar. ivi puḷḷipeṟuṅkāl puḷḷi niṟkum niliyil cila vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu. kuṟṟiyalikaramum kuṟṟiya lukaramum uriya ikara ukara vaṭiviṉmēl orupuḷḷi peṟum. miyā, nāṭu eṉa varum; mutaleḻuttu vaṭivaṉē ivaṟṟiṉ vaṭivaṉ; puḷḷi mēlē peṟuvatu cārpu kāṭṭuvatām.
āytam cilavuyirum cila meyyum kalanta cārpātaliṉ. enta uyirvaṭivaṉ mēlō enta meyvaṭivaṉ mēlō puḷḷi peṟutal poruntātu. avvāṟu peṟṟāl meykkuriya puḷḷiyākavō cilapōtu ekara okaraṅkaṭkuriya puḷḷi yākavō mayakkat tarum. taṉiyuyirum ākātu taṉimeyyum ākātu eṉṟa iraṇṭuṅ keṭṭāṉ niliyil naṭuvē oru puḷḷi iṭippaṭṭu niṟkum. eḵku, aḵṟiṇi, veḵku, eṉavarum. kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiya lukaram tattam mutal vaṭivaṉic cārntaṉa. āytattukku aṉṉa cārpiṉmiyāl oru naṭuppuḷḷiyē ataṉ vaṭivaṉ āyiṟṟu. 0 eṉṟa iṉmik kuṟipōla āytam (.) eṉṟa puḷḷiyē koṇṭatu eṉavum. oru puḷḷitāṉ uṭiyatu eṉavum atu naṭuvil niṟpatu eṉavum.
kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar āytap puḷḷi
uyiroṭu puṇarnta vallāṟaṉ micittē
eṉṟa moḻimarapu nūṟpāvāl teḷiyavēṇṭum. āytappuḷḷi eṉpataṉāl puḷḷiyuruvē ataṉuru eṉpatum puḷḷi miciya eṉṉāmal `micittu' eṉṟa orumip payaṉiliyāl appuḷḷi oṉṟutāṉ eṉpatum muṉṉar micittē eṉpataṉāl niṟkumiṭam iṭi allatu naṭuvu eṉpatum teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkum.
ivviḷakkattāl innūṟpāviṉ nēruri piṉvarumāṟu: kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, āytam eṉṟa mūṉṟuvakiyāṉa puḷḷiyolikaḷum mēṟkūṟiya eḻuttōṭu oppaṉa.
kuṟṟeḻuttu
(3)	avaṟṟuḷ
	a i u
	e o veṉṉu mappā lintum
	ōraḷa picikkuṅ kuṟṟeḻun teṉpa
iḵtu oru māttiri peṟum eḻuttukkuṟi kūṟum.
akalavuri
mutal nūṟpāvil eḻuttu muppatu eṉappaṭṭatallavā? avaṟṟuḷ a i u e o eṉṟa antappakuti aintum taṉittaṉi oru māttiriyaḷavu olikkum. ataṉāl kuṟṟeḻuttu eṉṟa kuṟipeṟum eṉṟu colluvar nūlōr.
tiṟaṉuri
`avaṟṟuḷ' eṉpatu `akaramutal ṉakara viṟuvāymuppaḵtu' eṉpataṉic cuṭṭum. mēli nūṟpāvik kuṟikkātu,aṅku tokic col illāmiyiṉ, `ōraḷavu icikkum kuṟṟeḻuttu' eṉpatil kuṟil eṉṟu colvataṟkuk kāraṇam amintuḷḷatu. itu peyareccak kāraṇanaṭi. poy collum ciṉṉavaṉ, paṭitta mēti eṉpaṉa vaḻakku.
aintum oru māttiri icikkum eṉṟālum ovvoru kuṟṟeḻuttum oru māttiri peṟum eṉpatu naṭiyiṉ karuttu. kuṭumpa aṭṭikkārarkkellām poṅkalukku oru kilō cīṉi kūṭutalāka vaḻaṅkappaṭum eṉṟa naṭiyi nām iṉṟum kūṟavilliyā?
aḷapum aḷavum vēṟuvēṟu poruḷaṉa. aḷapu - māttiri, oliyiṉ kālakkaṇakku. aḷapeṭiyeṉa varumēyaṉṟi aḷaveṭiyeṉa vārātu. aḷavu - taram, kūṟu itu eṇṇaḷavu niṟiyaḷavu eṉa varum. kuṟṟeḻuttu oru māttiriyaḷavu. neṭṭeḻuttu iru māttiriyaḷavu eṉakkūṟalām. koḷḷaḷavu, aḷavōṭu peṟuka eṉpaṉa iṉṟiya vaḻakku.
icittal - poruṇmippaṭa oḻuṅkupaṭa olittal. itu moḻiyoliyik kuṟikkum.
neṭṭeḻuttu
(4)	ā ī ū
	ē ai 
	ō oḷa veṉṉu  mappā lēḻum
	īraḷa picikkumneṭṭeḻut teṉpa.
iḵtu iraṇṭu māttiri peṟum eḻuttukkuṟi kūṟum.
akalavuri
ā ī ū ē ai ō oḷa eṉṟa appakuti eḻum taṉittaṉi iraṇṭu māttiri olikkum. aṅṅaṉam irumāttiri kāraṇamāka neṭṭeḻuttu eṉṟa kuṟipeṟum eṉṟu colluvar nūlōr.
tiṟaṉuri
mēlic cēvaṟpaṭṭi kīḻiccēvaṟpaṭṭi eṉṟāṟpōl kuṟṟeḻuttu neṭṭeḻuttu eṉpaṉa oṉṟiyoṉṟu nōkkiṉa; eṉṟālum kuṟṟeḻuttu oru māttiri. mēlum nīṇṭu neṭṭeḻuttāka māṟavilli. a a iraṇṭu cērntu ā eṉṟa neṭilāvatilli i i  iraṇṭu cērntu ī eṉṟa neṭilāvatilli. a eṉpatum taṉippiṟappu; ā eṉpatum taṉippiṟappu. i eṉpatum taṉippiṟappu; ī eṉpatum taṉippiṟappu. ai oḷa mutalāṉa piṟavum aṉṉa. itaṉāṟṟāṉ kuṟṟeḻuttu nīṇṭu neṭṭeḻuttu ākum eṉṉāmal `īraḷapicikkum neṭṭeḻuttu' eṉṟār. mutal nūṟpāvil variyaṟuttatupōl eḻutteṉappaṭum ovvoṉṟum poruṇmiyil taṉittaṉmiyum mutaṉmiyum uṭiyatu. ivvilakkaṇam eṉṟum niṉivu koḷḷattakkatu.
aintum kuṟṟeḻutteṉṟālum aḷapoṟṟumiyil ōriṉamāmaṉṟip poruḷ vēṟṟumiyilum piṟappilum taṉittaṉmip peṟṟiyaṉa. immoḻiyuṇmiyi neṭṭeḻuttukkum meyyeḻuttukkum valliṉa melliṉa iṭayiṉattukkum koḷka. tāy tanti kaṇavaṉ maṉivi makkaḷ uṭaṉpiṟantār eṉa uṟavoṟṟumiyiruntālum vāyum vayiṟum vēṟu eṉṟa vaḻakkuṇṭaṉṟō.
oruviṉā: kuṟṟeḻuttu aintāka neṭṭeḻuttu ēḻu varalāmā? uyir paṉṉiraṇṭāka meypatiṉeṭṭu varalāmā?
naṉṟāka varalām. kuṟiyiliruntu neṭil piṟappatilli; uyirukkum meykkum otta toṭarpilli ellām poruṇmiyāṟṟalāl taṉittaṉip piṟappiṉavi aim poṟikaḷuḷ meyoṉṟu, vāy oṉṟu, mūkku iraṇṭu, kaṇ, iraṇṭu, ceviyiraṇṭu irukkalāmā eṉpatu pōṉṟa viṉā itu. itu piṟappiṉ iyaṟki. neṭṭeḻuttu īṇṭu ōr oliyaṉittāṉ kuṟikkum; vaṭivaṉik kuṟikkavilli. ataṉāl ū oḷa eṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil ovvoṉṟukkum iraṇṭu vaṭivaṉkaḷ vantuḷḷaṉavē eṉṟu mayaṅka vēṇṭā.
aḷapeṭi
(5)	mūvaḷa picittal ōreḻut tiṉṟē
	nīṭṭam vēṇṭi ṉavvaḷa puṭiya
	kūṭṭi yeḻūuta leṉmaṉār pulavar
iḵtu eḻuttu varampiṉi uṟutippaṭuttuvatōṭu kūṟutalolikku vaḻi kūṟum.
akalavuri
tamiḻmoḻikkaṇ oru māttiriyuṭiya ōr oliyaṉ uṇṭu; iru māttiriyuṭiya ōr oliyaṉ uṇṭu. mūṉṟu māttiriyuṭiya ōr oliyaṉ illi. iraṇṭu māttirikku mēlāka vēṇṭiṉāl anta aḷapukkuriya olikaḷik kūṭṭi olittuk koḷka eṉṟu vaḻi kūṟuvar ilakkaṇappulavar.
uṭiya - uṭiya olikaḷi; uṭiyaṉavaṟṟi; aṉcāriyi peṟāta kuṟippu viṉiyālaṇiyum peyar ceyaṟpaṭuporuḷ, eḻuutal - oliyeḻuppuka, olikka, viyaṅkōḷviṉi.
vaḻakku
aṇṇāa, tōḻīi, āṭūu, vēel, kōoṉeṉavum pukāaar nampīii eṉavum varum. `naṟṟāḷ toḻāar (2) `ceṟāa ay vāḻiya' (1200) eṉpaṉa tirukkuṟaḷ.
tiṟaṉuri
(a) innūṟpā tamiḻiṉ aṭippaṭi eḻuttuvarampi niṉivūṭṭi uṟutippaṭuttum peruñciṟappiṉatu; oppiyal moḻiyāyvukku uṟutuṇiyāvatu. eḻutteṉappaṭuvatu veṟṟoliyaṉṟu; veṟum vāyōciyaṉṟu; poruṇmi potintataṉṟē. ōraḷapicikkum kuṟilukkum īraḷapicikkum neṭilukkum poruṇmiyuṇṭu eṉpatum kuṟililiruntu neṭṭeḻuttu nīṇṭu piṟakkavilli eṉpatum ovvōr eḻuttum taṉakkeṉṟa taṉmiyum vēṟupaṭuttum āṟṟalum vāyntatu eṉpatum muṉṉart teriyak kaṇṭōm. ivviḷakkam piṉvarum patiṉeṭṭu meyyeḻuttukkum okkum.
kāṇka:	
ali		aḷi		aḻi;
	āli		āḷi		āḻi;
	kali		kaḷi		kaḻi;
	vali		vaḷi		vaḻi;
	naṭa		naṭi		naṭu;
	kaṭa		kaṭi		kaṭu;
	paṭṭu		pāṭṭu	piṭṭu		pūṭṭu		poṭṭu;
	nāṉ		nām		nāṇ		nāy		nār		nāl 	 nāḷ.
(ā) iṅṅaṉam pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa coṟkaḷ oliyaṉ māṟṟaṅkaḷāl vēṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. perukukiṉṟaṉa. vaḷantarukiṉṟaṉa. ōr oliyaṉ ōriṭattu māṟiṉālum pala oliyaṉkaḷ mutaṉili iṭinili iṟutinilikaḷil māṟiṉālum poruḷ māṟṟatti aṟikiṉṟōm. immuṟi ulaka voliyaṉmuṟi. cila moḻikaḷil mūṉṟu māttiri yuṭiya oliyaṉum uṇṭu. tamiḻil attaku amippu illi. īṇṭu oliyaṉip paṟṟiyap pēcum pōtellām kaṇ pārkkum vaṭivaṉkaḷi uṭaṉ eṇṇakkūṭātu cevikēṭkum oliyaḷaviyē karutal vēṇṭum.
(i) aṭu - kuṟṟeḻuttut toṭaṅkiyatu;
āṭu - neṭṭeḻuttut toṭaṅkiyatu. ā eṉṟa neṭilukkumēl poruḷ tarum mūvuḷavuṭiya uyirneṭil tamiḻil illi. māttiri kūṭutalāka olippati innūṟpā taṭukkavilli aṅṅaṉam olikkalām eṉpataṉiyum vitikkiṉṟatu. āṉāl akkūṭutalāṉa olikaṭkup poru ḷilli eṉavum poruḷilliyeṉavē eḻuttākā eṉavum teḷivu ceykiṉṟatu. āaṭu āaaṭu eṉṟu mūṉṟu māttiriyākavum nāṉku māttiriyākavum kūṭutalāka nīṭṭiṉālum āṭu eṉṟa poruḷ vēṟupaṭutalumilli kūṭutalumilli eṉpatu moḻiyiyal.
(ī)  `ōreḻuttu iṉṟē' eṉpataṉ karuttu:
mūṉṟaḷavu olittal ōr oliyaṉ illi eṉpatāl iraṇṭu oliyaṉkaḷil uṇṭu eṉṟu karuttuk koḷḷal tavaṟu tamiḻil evvakiyālum appaṭippaṭṭa oliyaṉ illi eṉpatuvē muṭinta muṭipu. iru oliyaṉkaḷil mūvaḷavu icikkum eṉṟu koṇṭāl oliyaṉukku iṉṟiyamiyāta ilakkaṇamāṉa poruṇmi kūṭutal vēṇṭumaṉṟō? āaṭu eṉṟa nīṭciyāl poruṇmi vēṟupaṭāmiyiṉ muppatukku mēṟpaṭṭa eḻuttē illi eṉpatu teḷivu. eṉavē aḷapeṭi oliyaṉ aṉṟu.
(u) `nīṭṭam vēṇṭiṉ avvaḷapuṭiya kūṭṭi eḻūutal. eṉpataṉ karuttu: tēvippaṭṭāl eṉpataṉāl itu moḻikku aṭippaṭiyaṉṟu eṉpatum kūṭṭi olittuk koḷka eṉpataṉāl ceyaṟki eṉpatum peṟalām toḻāar, pōoy, pulāal, tūuymi vāaymi eṉṟaviṭattu oru māttiri kūṭṭiṉāṟpōtum. viṭūum, peṟūum, varūum, tarūum eṉṟaviṭattu irumāttiri kūṭṭa vēṇṭum. ātaliṉ `avvaḷapuṭiya' eṉṟu paṉmippaṭa moḻintār. `eḻuutal' eṉanūṟpāvilē akakkāṭṭu amintirukkum nayam kāṇka.
(ū) itu aḷapeṭi eṉṟa kuṟipeṟum ceyyuṭkum potu nīṭṭam vēṇṭiṉ eṉṟu potumpaṭac coṉṉami yāl mūṉṟu mutalākap pala māttiriyaḷavu olikkum. oruvarik kūppiṭum viḷivaḻakkil nīṇṭu payiṉṟu varum eṉpataṉi viḷimarapiyaliṟ kāṇka.
(ē) aḷapeṭiyic colluvatilum eḻutuvatilum iṉṟu naṭimuṟippiḻi malintu varukiṉṟatu. nūṟpāviṉpaṭi kūṭutal oliyik kūṭṭinīṭṭic colla vēṇṭumēyaṉṟip pirittut taṉittuc collavum kūṭātu. eḻutavum kūṭātu.
kaṟṟataṉā lāya payaṉeṉkol vālaṟivaṉ
naṟṟāḷ toḻāar eṉiṉ.
keṭuppatūum keṭṭārkkuc cārvāy maṟṟāṅkē
eṭuppatūum ellām maḻi
ḻā, tū ivaṟṟi mēlum oru māttiriyaḷavu appaṭiyē nīṭṭic colluka; ar, um, eṉap pirittuc collaṟka; pirittu eḻutaṟka; aṭutta varikkuk koṇṭu cellaṟka. itu kaṟpikka vēṇṭiya oli neṟi.
māttiri
(6)	kaṇṇimi noṭiyeṉa avvē māttiri
	nuṇṇiti ṉuṇarttōr kaṇṭa vāṟē
itu māttiri eṉṟa kuṟiyiṭum ataṟku viḷakkamum kūṟum.
akalavuri
māttiri eṉpatu kuṟiyīṭu. itu oliyaḷavukku varum. māttiri yeṉṟāl eṉṉa? ovvoru muṟiyum iyalpākak kaṇtiṟantu imippataṟku ettuṇik kālam irukkum. attuṇiyaḷavuṭiya oli oru māttirikku oppām. atu pōlavē peruvirali naṭuviralōṭu cērttuk kivirali iyalpākac cuṇṭum pōtu entavaḷavu kālam vēṇṭum. antavaḷavu oru māttirikkuc camam. māttiriyik kaṇṇālum ceviyālum nuṇmiyāka uṇarnta muttamiḻp pulavarkaḷ aḷantu kaṇṭamuṟikaḷ ivi. avvē appaṭippaṭṭa īraḷavām. kaṇṭavāṟu avvē eṉa muṭikka māttiri iraṇṭām vēṟṟumit toki.

tiṟaṉuri
aḷapu eṉpataṉ maṟṟoru tamiḻp peyar māttiri emmāttiram eṉpatu vaḻakku. mā eṉṟa col ciṟiya aḷavukku oru peyarākum eṉpataṉi mākāṇi orumā irumā eṉṟa vaḻakkiṟ kāṇka. `vaḻaṅkiyal mā' eṉat tolkāppiyamum. `māniṟivillatum paṉṉāṭkākum' (184) eṉappuṟanāþṉūṟum moḻiyum.
kaṇṇimi iyaṟki. toḻiṟkālam; noṭi ceyaṟki; ōcikkālam. iraṇṭumē kāla vaḷavik kuṟikkum. imippoḻutu noṭippoḻutu eṉpatu vaḻakku. eḻuttiṉ māttiri ōciyum kālamum iṇintatātaliṉ, tolkāppiyam irucāṉṟu collum. iraṇṭum orē nērattuc ceytaṟ kuriyavi. īṇṭukkūṟum ilakkaṇam oru māttirikku uriyatu. imittali imi eṉavum noṭittali noṭi eṉavum mutaṉilit toḻiṟpeyarākak kūṟiṉār. pukittalip puki eṉṟum veṭittali veṭi eṉavum kūṟumāppōla eḷitākap purintu koḷḷalām eṉpatu `avvē' eṉṟa cuṭṭiṉ kuṟippu. kaṇṇimiyum noṭiyum yārkkum eḷimiyallavā? `maṉattukkaṇ mācilaṉātal aṉittaṟaṉ eṉpatu tirukkuṟaḷ naṭikkuṟippu.
māttiriyi nuṇṇitiṉ uṇarntōr - kaṇṇāl imiyaḷaviyum ceviyāl noṭiyaḷaviyum kaṇittōr, ikkaruttiṉiyē.
kaṇṇiṉum ceviyiṉum tiṇṇiti ṉuṇarum
uṇarvuṭi māntark kallatu teriyiṉ
naṉṉayap poruḷkōḷ eṇṇaruṅ kurittē
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar meyppāṭṭiyaliṟ pulappaṭuttuvar.
aḷapu, māttiri eṉṟa kiḷavikaḷ oru poruḷaṉa vāyiṉum toṭarāṭciyil vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu. aḷapeṭi eṉpōm. māttiriyeṭi eṉṉōm; māttiriyaḷavu eṉpōm aḷapaḷavu eṉṉōm aḷapiṟantu uyirttal eṉpōm. māttiriyiṟantu uyirttal eṉṉōm. ivvēṟṟumikkuk kāraṇam aḷapu eṉpatu māttiriyilum viritta taṉmiyuṭiyatu.


uyirkkuṟi
(7) 	oḷakāra viṟuvāyp
	paṉṉī reḻuttum uyireṉa moḻipa.
itu muppatu eḻuttukkaḷum uyireḻuttuk kuṟi kūṟum.
akalavuri
mutal nūṟpāvil eḻutteṉap paṭuvaṉa akaramutal eṉṟu colliyiruppataṉāl, innūṟpāvil oḷakāra iṟuti eṉṟār. eṉavē akara mutalāka oḷakāram vari paṉṉiraṇṭu oliyaṉum uyir eṉṟa kuṟiyīṭu koḷḷum eṉṟu colluvar nūlōr.
vaḻakku
a ā i ī u ū e ē ai o ō oḷa eṉap paṉṉiraṇṭām.
tiṟaṉuri
mēlē aintum kuṟṟeḻuttām, ēḻum  neṭṭeḻuttām eṉṟu uṭkuṟiyīṭu cuṭṭiṉavar innūṟpāvil avviru pakutikkum uyir eṉṟa potukkuṟi kāṭṭuvar.
uyir eṉṟa peyarkkāraṇam yātu? olippāṉ. olippaṭuvāṉ, olippiṭam, olippumuṟi eṉṟa nāṉkuñ cērvatāl oliyaṉ piṟakkum. innāṉkum oliyaṉ piṟappukku vēṇṭum. “uṟṟavaṉtīrppāṉmaruntu uḻic celvāṉ eṉṟu appāl nāṟkūṟṟē maruntu” eṉṟa kuṟaḷi oppunōkkuka uḷḷiruntu varuṅkāṟṟu varumvaḻiyil nā aṇṇam pal itaḻ mūkku mutalāṉa paṭuvuṟuppukkaḷit toṭutalum uṟutalu miṉṟit taṉṉaḷavil olikkumpōtu uyirpiṟakkum. meyyuṟupput tīṇṭaliṉṟi uyirppaḷavil piṟattaliṉ uyir eṉpatu kāraṇappeyar. uyir - oruvakik kāṟṟu `kāṟṟaṭitta piyaṭā' eṉpa.
“paṉṉī ruyirum tannili tiriyā
  miṭaṟṟup piṟanta vaḷiyiṉ icikkum”
eṉṟa piṟappiyal nūṟpā uyirkaḷ meytīṇṭit tiriyātu miṭaṟṟiliruntu varum kāṟṟiyakattāṟ piṟakkum oliyaṉkaḷ eṉpataṉi veḷippaṭuttum.
meykkuṟi
(8)	ṉakāra viṟuvāyp
	patiṉeṇ ṇeḻuttum meyyeṉa moḻipa.
itu muppatu eḻuttukkaḷuḷ meyyeḻuttuk kuṟi kūṟum.
akalavuri
kakaramutal ṉakaram vari yuḷḷa patiṉeṭṭu oliyaṉkaḷum mey eṉṟa kuṟiyīṭu peṟum eṉṟu moḻivar nūlōr.
vaḻakku
k ṅ c ñ ṭ ṇ t n p m y r l v ḻ ḷ ṟ ṉ eṉap patiṉeṭṭām.
tiṟaṉuri
muppatu eḻuttukkaḷ iviyeṉat toṉṟutoṭṭu ellārum aṟiyappaṭṭa ulaka vaḻakkātaliṉ, `akaramutal ṉakara viṟuvāy' eṉavum `oḷakāraviṟuvāy' eṉavum `ṉakāraviṟuvāy' eṉavum vāḷā kūṟiṉār. oṉṟu mutal nūṟuvari. oṉṟumutal pattuvari eṉṟu kūṟum vaḻakkiṉi niṉika.
muntunūl etaṉiyum, muntunūlōr evariyum tolkāppiyam peyar cuṭṭavilli. eṉpa moḻipa, eṉmaṉār pulavar eṉmaṉār pulamiyōrē, kaṭiniliyiṉṟē āciriyaṟka eṉṟa naṭipaṭat tolkāppiyattup paṉþṉūṟu iṭaṅkaḷil varum. ivvilakkaṇaṅkaḷ toṉṟutoṭṭuvarum aṭippaṭṭa iruvaki vaḻakku marapukaḷ eṉṟu kāṭṭuvatē innaṭiyiṉ karuttu. ataṉālaṉṟō eḻuvāy potuvākavuḷḷatu; mēlum pala nūṟpākkaḷ moḻipa, eṉpa eṉṟaḷavil eḻuvāyiṉṟiyum uḷa.
mutalnūṟpā kūṟum muppatu eḻuttukkaḷ uyir eṉavum mey eṉavum iru pakutippaṭum eṉṟu aṟivatōṭu, mūṉṟāvatu pakuti oṉṟu illi eṉavum teḷikiṉṟōm.
mey eṉpataṉ peyarkkāraṇam yātu? uḷḷiruntu varuṅkāṟṟu nā aṇṇam pal itaḻ mūkku mutalāṉa uṟuppukkaḷiṉ tacippakutiyōṭu tacippakuti toṭa oli piṟattaliṉ mey eṉappaṭum; ataṉāṟṟāṉ oṟṟu eṉṟa kuṟiyum meykku uṇṭu.
kuṟṟeḻuttu, neṭṭeḻuttu, uyireḻuttu, mey yeḻuttu, valleḻuttu, melleḻuttu, iṭiyeḻuttu eṉpavi iṉa muḻuvatiyum kuṟikkum tokutik kuṟiyīṭu kaḷākavum varum; a eṉṟa kuṟṟeḻuttu, ā eṉṟa neṭṭeḻuttu, ya eṉṟa iṭiyeḻuttu eṉṟāṟpōla avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiccuṭṭum pakutik kuṟiyīṭukaḷākavum āḷappaṭum.
uyiriṉ nili
(9)	meyyō ṭiyiyiṉum uyiriyal tiriyā
itu meyyoṭu cērntu olikkumpōtu uyiriṉ nili kūṟum.
akalavuri
mutaleḻuttu uyir eṉa mey eṉa iruvakippaṭum eṉṟu mēlē kūṟiṉār. avi tammuṭ cērntu varum; meymmēl uyir ēṟivarum. taṭippaṭṭup piṟakkum meyyoliyaṉoṭu cērntiruntālum uyir taṉṉiyalpiṉ paṭi uṟupput toṭātu kāṟṟaḷavil olikkum. ittaṉmi yiliruntum māṟupāṭilli. itaṉāl nām aṟivatu eṉṉa? uyirmey eṉṟa kūṭṭuṟavil uyirō meyyō taṉ piṟappiṉ muṟiyiyum taṉpeyark kāraṇattiyum iḻappatilli.
uyiriyal - uyirāṉatu meyyoṭupaṭātu piṟakkum vaḷinili.
tiṟaṉuri
collāṅkuṅkāl uyirum meyyumākiya muppatu eḻuttukkaḷiṉ nili eṉṉa? ivi taṉittaṉiyē niṟpatilli. uyirmeyyāka iṇintē varum.
uyirmoḻikku mutalil maṭṭum taṉittu niṟkum avvaḷavē.
kāṇka: aṉpu, āṇ, ici.
mutalallāta iṭi iṟutiyellām meyyoṭu kūṭittāṉ niṟka vēṇṭum.
kāṇka: aṟam, aṭavi, aṟuvaṭi
moḻimutalāṉa coṟkaḷiluṅkūṭa meykkuppiṉṉē tāṉ uyirvarum.
kāṇka: kacaṭu, kācu, kuṟippu, kūṭṭuṟavu
coṟkaḷil ivvāṟu meykaḷōṭu kūṭi varuvatē uyirkaḷiṉ veḷippāṭu.  ātaliṉ `meyyōṭu iyiyiṉum' eṉṟu uyirmēl vittu nūṟpittār āciriyar.
meykkō ellāviṭattum uyirōṭu cērntutāṉ veḷippaṭum eṉṟa niliyilli. moḻimutalil maṭṭum veḷippāṭṭiṟkumeykaḷ uyirkaḷōṭu kūṭi uyir meykaḷāka niṟkum. kāṇka: paṭu, pāṭu, piṭi, pīṭu, itaṉi, “uyir meyyallaṉa moḻi mutalākā” eṉṟa nūṟpā teḷikkum. ēṉip piṉṉiṭaṅkaḷil ellām mey uyirōṭu kalantum varum. taṉittum varum. kāṇka: tolkāppiyaṉ vaḷḷuvar, pacciyappar ātaliṉ mey uyirōṭu iyintu varutal peritilli. meyyōṭu uyir iyintu varutalē peritu eṉṟa vēṟupāṭu innūṟpāvāl viḷaṅkum.
pulāluṇṇā oruvar pulāluṇ oruvarik kātalittuk kūṭiṉālum, uṇṇāta tanniliyiliruntu māṟār eṉṟa uvamiyi innūṟpāviṟku ēṟṟit teḷika.
meykku māttiri
(10)	meyyi ṉaḷapē ariyeṉa moḻipa
itu meykku māttiriyaḷavu kūṟum.
akalavuri
muppatu eḻuttuḷ oruvakiyāṉa uyirukku mēlē māttiri kūṟappaṭṭatu. iṉṉoru vakiyāṉa meykku māttiri ariyākum eṉṟu moḻivar nūlōr. ovvoru meyyum arimāttiri peṟum eṉpatu karuttu.
tiṟaṉuri
uyir vakiyil māttiri kāraṇamākak kuṟṟeḻuttu neṭṭeḻutteṉa iru pakutiyuṇṭu. valliṉam melliṉam iṭiyiṉam eṉṟu mey pirivu paṭṭālum māttiri kāraṇa māka vēṟṟumiyiṉmi teḷiyalām. maṉitaṉ āṟaṟivuṭiyaṉ eṉṟāl, ovvoruvaṉum āṟaṟivuṭiyāṉ eṉṟu naṭip poruḷpaṭumaṉṟō. atupōl meymmāttiri ari eṉpatu ovvoru meyyum arimāttiri eṉap poruḷpaṭum.
tūymi, iṉpam, tamiḻ eṉa uyirmeyyāka iruntālum, oṟṟāka iruntālum enniliyilum meykka ari māttiri uṇṭu.
cārpukku māttiri
(11) 	avviyal niliyum ēṉi mūṉṟē
itu cārpeḻuttukku māttiri kūṟum.
akalavuri
muppatu eḻuttukku uyir vakiyākavum meyvaki yākavum māttiri kūṟiṉār. kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟṟiyalukaram āytam eṉṟa muppatu eḻuttuḷ aṭaṅkāta ēṉi mūṉṟum meyyeḻuttup pōla arimāttiri peṟṟu niṟkum.
vaḻakku:  kēṇmiyā. pāṭu. eḵku.
tiṟaṉuri
meyyum ēṉi mūṉṟum ariyaḷavu peṟum eṉṟu orē nūṟpāvākak kūṟiyirukkalāmē eṉiṉ, mey uyirōṭu otta mutaleḻuttātaliṉ taṉiyē muṉpākak kūṟiṉār. mey eḻuttām eṉavum ēṉi mūṉṟum `eḻuttōraṉṉa' eṉavum colliya vēṟṟumiyi yuṇarka.
āṉ īṉ eṉpaṉapōla ēṉ eṉpatuvum iṭam paṟṟiya aṭiccol. itu eñciyatik kuṟikkum. ēṉi, ēṉiya, ēṉatu, ēṉavi, ēṉōr eṉṟellām varutal kāṇka.
makarakkuṟukkamum vaṭivum
(12)	ariyaḷapu kuṟukal makara muṭittē,
	iciyiṭaṉ arukun teriyuṅ kāli
	uṭpeṟu puḷḷi yuruvā kummē.
itu meyyeḻuttuḷ oṉṟaṟku māttirik kuṟivum ataṟkuriya varivaṭivum kūṟum.

akalavuri
meyyeḻuttukku māttiri ariyeṉṟu muṉpu kaṟṟōm. anta arimāttiri pātiyaḷavākak kuṟukutal - atāvatu kāl māttiriyākak kuṟital makarameykku maṭṭum uṇṭu. ārāyntu pārttāl ikkuṟukkam oli nerukkaṭiyil aritāka varum. ari māttiriyāka irukkum makarameykkum kāl māttiriyāka olikkum makarak kuṟukkattiṟkum varivaṭivil orē oru vēṟupāṭuṇṭu. iyalpāṉa makarameykku uriyavaṭiviṉmēl oru puḷḷi irukkum. kuṟukiya makara meykkō uriyavaṭiviṉ uḷḷē appuḷḷi irukkavēṇṭum. eṉavē puḷḷi mēliruntāl kuṟukāta iyalpu makaram; puḷḷi uḷḷēyiruntāl kuṟukiya makaram.
vaḻakku:	iyalpāṉa makaram, ari māttiri, mēṟpuḷḷi -m makarakkuṟukkam, kālmāttiri, uṭpuḷḷi - ma
	pōṉma - orumoḻiyil makarakkuṟukkam; ceyyuḷ vaḻakkil maṭṭum varum.
	varuma vaṇṭi - irumoḻippuṇarcciyil makarakkuṟukkam; ulaka vaḻakkilum ceyyuḷ vaḻakkilum varum.
tiṟaṉuri:	makaram kuṟukumiṭaṉ iraṇṭu:
	“ceyyu ḷiṟutip pōli moḻivayiṉ
	ṉakāra makāram īroṟ ṟākum”
	“ṉakāri muṉṉar makāraṅ kuṟukum”
eṉa moḻi marapil irunūṟpākkaḷ uḷa.
itu pōṉma eṉṟa oru collaḷavil atuvum ceyyuḷiṟṟāṉ kāṇappaṭum.
“vakāra miciyum makāraṅ kuṟukum”
eṉpatupuḷḷi mayaṅkiyalil varum ēṉiyiṭam. itu vaḻakkiṟkum ceyyuṭkum potu. irumoḻippuṇarcciyil nilimoḻi makaram varumoḻi vakarattiṉmuṉ māttiri kuṟukum.
(kāṇka: taruma vaḷḷal, paṟakkuma vaṇṭu, collum vāy)
`iciyiṭaṉ arukum teriyuṅkāli' eṉṟu nūṉmarapiṟ colliyatit terivikkum mukattāṉ. moḻimarapilum puḷḷi mayaṅkiyalilum uriya iṭaṅkaḷic cuṭṭiṉār tolkāppiyar. ataṉālaṉṟō `mayaṅkā marapiṉ eḻuttumuṟi kāṭṭi' eṉṟu pāyiram tolkāppiya nūṉeṟiyip pārāṭṭukiṉṟatu. inneṟi tāṉē teḷivittal eṉappaṭum.
ariyaḷavu kuṟukal eṉpatu iruvakiyil oru poruḷē koḷḷa iṭaṉuṇṭu. meykkukkūṟiya ari māttiriyaṉiṉṟu kuṟukutal eṉṟum uri ceyyalām. ataṉōṭu ariyaḷavākak kuṟukutal eṉṟum uri ceyyalām. oru kallil irumāṅkāy vīḻttum naṭi itu. tamiḻilakkaṇakkattil oṉṟaṉ kuṟukkamāvatu cempākam kuṟitalām.
`teriyuṅkāli' eṉpataṉi `uṭpeṟu puḷḷi yuruvākummē' eṉpataṉōṭum iyittu muṭikkalām. ārāyumiṭattu puḷḷi niṟkum iṭavēṟṟumi yāṉum makarakkuṟukkam teḷivākum eṉpatu karuttu.
tamiḻ vaṭivaṉkaḷuḷ puḷḷikku vēṟupāṭu terikkum ciṟappāṟṟal uṇṭu. āytattic cuṭṭumpōtu naṭuvilum, makarak kuṟukkattiṟku uḷḷēyum, meym mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku mēlēyum irukkum.
makarak kuṟukkattiṉ vaṭivaṉ piṟkālattu vaḻakku viḻuntatu.
meykkup potuvaṭivaṉ
(13)	meyyi ṉiyaṟki puḷḷiyoṭum niliyal
itu meyyeḻuttukkup potu vaṭivu kūṟum.
akalavuri
meykaḷ patiṉeṭṭaṉṟō ovvoru meykkum taṉi vaṭivu uṇṭu. itu urivaṭivaṉ eṉappaṭum. ataṉmēl mey eṉṟa iṉaṅkāṭṭap puḷḷi eṉṟa oru potu vaṭivaṉum irukkum. iru vaṭivum cērntatutāṉ meyyiṉ uru.
iyaṟki - iyalpu, taṉmi. niliyal - niṟka.
vaḻakku: k ṅ c ña ṭ ṇ t n p m y r l v ḻ ḷ ṟ ṉ

tiṟaṉuri
meypuḷḷiyaṭiyatu eṉṟa naṭimāṭu kompuṭiyatu eṉṟa naṭiyi okkum. kompuṭiyateṉavē talimēl eṉpatu peṟappaṭumāṟu pōla, puḷḷiyuṭiyateṉavē uri vaṭivattiṉ mēl eṉpatu peṟappaṭum veṟum puḷḷi meyyākātu. puḷḷiyoṭum niliyil eṉṟa ummiyāl, puḷḷiyum meykku iṉṟiyamiyā vaṭivam eṉpatu uṟutiyām. puḷḷi nīṅkiṉ meyyiṉ taṉmiyum nīṅkiviṭum.
oruviṉā: meykaṭku urivaṭivaṉkaḷ irukkumpōtu mēlum oru puḷḷip potuvaṭivaṉ ēṉ? ivvaṭikaṉ iṉṟēl kaṅacaña mutalāyiṉa akaram ēṟiya uyirmeyyāki viṭum. iyaṟkiyāṉa taṉimeykkum akaram ūrnta uyir meykkum vēṟṟumi terivippatu taṉimey puḷḷi peṟuki yākum. eṉavē puḷḷip pēṟṟiṉ payaṉ otta vaṭiviṉil; vēṟṟumit teḷivu ceyvatu. muṉcoṉṉa uṭpeṟu puḷḷiyum piṉṉē collappaṭum ekara okarappuḷḷiyum ippayaṉē koṇṭavi.
māttiric curukkattukkup puḷḷiyiṭal eṉṟa kōṭpāṭu peritum poruntātu. avvāṟāyiṉ, neṭṭeḻutti nōkkak kuṟṟeḻuttellām puḷḷipeṟutal vēṇṭum. aḷapeṭiyi nōkka neṭṭeḻuttup puḷḷi peṟutal vēṇṭum. ekaramum okaramum puḷḷi peṟuki māttirik kuṟukkattālaṉṟu. avi ōraḷapu icikkum iyaṟkik kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ īraḷapicikkum ēkāra ōkārattōṭu vaṭivaṉ vēṟṟumi terikkavē ekaramum ōkaramum puḷḷi peṟalāyiṉa. āytam etaṉ kuṟukkam? arimāttiriyuṭiya mey etaṉ kuṟukkam? atu niliyāṉa puḷḷipeṟutaliṉ vaṭivaṉ vēṟṟumi teḷippatu puḷḷippayaṉ eṉpatu kaṇkūṭu. puḷḷiṭiya ōr eḻuttu māttiri kuṟivākavum irukkalām; ataṉit terikkap puḷḷi kuttappeṟṟatu eṉṟāl niṟiyāṉa vitiyaṉṟu. oliyaṉkaḷiṉ urivaṭivaṉkaḷ ottaṉavāka irukkum niliyil, vēṟṟumippaṭutta puḷḷikkoṭi vēṇṭappaṭum. itu tamiḻ vaṭiva neṟi.
meyppuḷḷi iṉṟum uṇṭu. eṉṟum vēṇṭum. itu mēṟpuḷḷiyākum.
	 puḷḷipeṟal
(14)	e	ekara okarat tiyaṟkiyu maṟṟē
itu kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷuḷ ekara okara vaṭivaṉ kūṟum
akalavuri
kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷuḷ ekara voliyaṉum okara voliyaṉum mēṟcolliyappu urivaṭivattiṉmēl puḷḷi peṟṟē niṟkum iyalpiṉa.
vaḻakku: e ō
tiṟaṉuri
`meyyiṉiyaṟki' eṉṟatupōla `ekara vokarattiyaṟki' eṉpataṉāl meypōla iviyum puḷḷiyoṭu niṟṟal toṉmarapu eṉpatu teḷivām. moḻivaralāṟṟil oliyaṉkaḷ mika muṉ eḻuntavi. vaṭivaṉkaḷ mikap piṉtōṉṟiyavi. ataṉmēlum oppunōkkiṉ tamiḻmoḻi pallūḻit toṉmi, cemmi, iyaṟki, nilipēṟu uṭiyatām. ātaliṉ piṟamoḻikaḷic cārttit tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ oliyaṉ vaṭivaṉkaḷik kaṇippatu aṟivuṭimi yākātu.
aṇmikkāla āyvukaḷ tamiḻiṉ mututoṉmiyi uṇarntu ulakattup palapiṟa moḻikaḷit tūkkip pārkkum naṉṉaṭiyip pulappaṭutti varukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl paṇṭutoṭṭē ulakap paṉmoḻikaṭkut tamiḻtāyākavō ceviliyākavō vaḻaṅkiya ūṭṭam veḷippaṭukiṉṟatu. innaṟceyti virippiṟ perukiyōṭumātaliṉ, āṅkāṅku vēṇṭumaḷavu vaḻikkalpōṟ kāṭṭappaṭum.
oruviṉā: a i u eṉṟa kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ puḷḷipeṟa villiyē : e o iraṇṭu maṭṭum puḷḷi peṟuvatu ēṉ? a i u eṉṟa kuṟṟeḻuttukkum ā ī ū eṉṟa neṭṭeḻuttukkum tolkāppiyar kālattum vaṭivaṉkaḷ teḷivākat tammaḷavil vēṟṟumippaṭṭiruntaṉa.
iccikkalil aritāṉa oṉṟiṉi āḻamāka nām niṉiya vēṇṭum. neṭṭeḻuttukku e o vaṭivaṉkaḷ iruntapōtē kuṟṟeḻuttukkum puḷḷiyoṭu kūṭiya e o vaṭivaṉkaḷ iruntaṉa. kuḻappam vantu mayaṅki vēṟṟumip paṭuttuvāṉ ippuḷḷikaḷ piṉpu vikkappaṭṭaṉavalla eṉpataṉi naṉku purintukoḷḷa vēṇṭum. muppatu mutaleḻuttukkaḷiṉ varivaṭiva vēṟṟumiyik kūrntu pārppiṉ māṟṟam ciṟiya kōṭākavō ciṟiya vaḷivākavōtāṉ irukkum. eṉiṉum ivvēṟṟumik kūṟukaḷ uṭaṉkālattuk kaṇṭavi. makarak kuṟukkappuḷḷiyum meyppuḷḷiyum ekara vokarap puḷḷiyum kuḻappam vantu vēṟṟumi kāṭṭa vēṇṭip piṉcērntavi yalla eṉpatu mutaṉmiyāṉa karuttu.
kōṭu, vaḷivu pōlap puḷḷiyum tamiḻ vaṭivaṉkaḷil ōr kūṟu avvāṟāyiṉ puḷḷiyi maṭṭum vitantu nūṟpākkaḷ ceyvataṉ kāraṇam eṉṉa? nalla viṉā. muppatu oliyaṉkaḷum paṇṭum ovvōreḻuttālāṉavi. aḵtāvatu ōreḻutti eṭutteḻutavēṇṭum eṉpatilli. eḻutteṉappaṭupa muppaḵtu eṉpa eṉṟa naṭiyil, varivaṭivām eḻuttu muppatuvē eṉṟa kuṟippup peṟappaṭum. pattu nūṟu āyiram kuṟitta tamiḻeṇkaḷ (ya, ḷa, ta)ōreḻuttāka iruppati uṇarka. immuppatu eḻuttukkaḷuḷ oru cāraṉa puḷḷiyum peṟuvaṉavāka iruntaṉa. eḻuttāṇiyi eṭutteḻutāmal puḷḷi vikka muṭiyātaṉṟō? iṉṟiya niliyum atutāṉē. mēlum eḻutum vēkattil puḷḷi viṭappaṭutalum uṇṭaṉṟō? iṉṟum inniliyuṇṭu. eṭutteḻutum amippi niṉintu viṭṭeḻutak kūṭāta uṟuppu puḷḷiyeṉpatic cuṭṭik kāṭṭavum puḷḷi kuṟitta nūṟpākkaḷi yāttār tol kāppiyar eṉa aṟika. meyyiṉ iyaṟki ekara vokarat tiyaṟki eṉṟa collāṭciyālum ivvuṇmi pōtarum.
ekara okarattiyaṟki eṉpataṉāl ippuḷḷikaḷ uyirk kuṟilukkē. (kāṇka: eṭu, oṭu) meyyiṉ iyaṟki eṉpatu uyirmeyyik kuṟikkātatupōla itaṉiyum koḷvōm. ēri puḷḷi peṟiṉ eri eṉṟa neruppik kuṟikkum; ōti puḷḷi peṟiṉ oti eṉṟa marattik kuṟikkum eṉṟa ceyyuḷ mēṟkōḷḷilum uyirkkuṟilē puḷḷipeṟali oppiṭuka.
ekara okaram puḷḷiyiṉṟi eḻutappaṭuvatum ēkāram kīḻppaṭukkikkōṭu peṟuvatu; ōkāram kīḻkkātu peṟuvatum aṇmiyi vaḻakku.
uyirmey vaṭivaṉkaḷ
(15) 	puḷḷi yillā ellā meyyum
	uruvuru vāki akaramō ṭuyirttalum
	ēṉi yuyirō ṭuruvutirin tuyirttalum
	āyī riyala vuyirtta lāṟē.
itu patiṉeṭṭu meyyum paṉṉiraṇṭu uyiroṭum cērum uyirmeykaṭku vaṭivaṉ muṟikaḷik kūṟum.
akalavuri
puḷḷiyoṭu niṟkum meyvaṭivaṉkaḷ (kṅcñ) muṉṉē kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. appuḷḷikaḷ illāta meyvaṭivaṉkaḷ (kaṅacaña) avvaṭivē vaṭivāka akaram ēṟiya uyirmeyyāka olikkappaṭum. iḵtu orumuṟi. puḷḷiyillāta vaṭivaṉkaḷ ā mutalāṉa patiṉoru uyirkaḷōṭu cērumpōtu tam urivaṭivu palavāṟu tirintu olikkum. iḵtu orumuṟi. patiṉeṇ meyyum paṉṉiruyiroṭum cērntu uyirmeyyākuṅkāl puḷḷi nīṅkiya meyvaṭivattiṉ aṭippaṭiyil ivviru muṟiyil uyirmeykaḷiṉ tiri vaṭivaṉkaḷ tōṉṟum.
urupu - vaṭivu. uyirttal - olittal. āṟu - neṟi.
vaḻakku: irumuṟikaḷāvaṉa:
1. akaravuyirmeykkup puḷḷiyillāta meyvaṭivaṉkaḷ:
	ka ṅa ca  ña ṭa ṇa ta na pa ma ya ra la va ḻa ḷa ṟa ṉa
2. ākāra mutalāṉa ēṉi uyirmeykaṭkup puḷḷi yillāta meyvaṭivaṉkaḷ palvēṟu tiripupaṭutal:
	kā ki kī ku kū ke kē ki ko kō ku
ivi ikkāla vaṭivaṉkaḷ
uyirmeyyil varum tirikuṟikaḷ
ā	-	pakkattuk kāl vāṅkutal: h - kā - ṅā cā iṉṉa piṟa (15)
	-	kīḻvilaṅku iṭamāka vaḷital : i    	 - ṇā ṟā ṉā (3)
i	-	kokki mēlē peṟutal : 	 - ki ṅi ci iṉṉa piṟa (18)
ī	-	ciṟitu nīṇṭa mēl vaḷivuccuḻi peṟuvaṉa 		-  kī ṅī cī iṉṉa piṟa 	(18)

	-	kīḻiḻuppum iṭamākac cuḻiyum :       	-  ṅū, cū, ṭū, pū, mū, ṟā, rū, vū, ḻū, 		ḷū (10)

	-	iṭappakkak kompum kīḻ vilaṅku iṭamāka vaḷivum: b	 - b‚, bƒ, b„ (3)

tiṟaṉuri
mēṟkāṭṭiya uyirmeyk kuṟikaḷ cila nūṟṟāṇṭu kaḷākap peruvaḻakkil uḷḷavi. acciyalmuṟi naṉku kāl koṇṭa piṉ ellāmoḻikkum pōlat tamiḻmoḻikkum eḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷ cemmiyeytiṉa kiyeḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷiṟkūṭa vēṟupāṭukaḷ kaṟintu varukiṉṟaṉa. vaṭṭāra vaḻakkup pōlavum kuḻūukkuṟi pōlavum vaṭiva vaḻakkilum cila vēṟupāṭukaḷ tavirkka muṭiyātavi. eṉiṉum accuruvaṅkaḷ pallāṇṭukaḷākac cevvuruvam āyiṉa. aṇmiyil tamiḻaka aracāṇiyiṉpaṭi uyirmeyyil ākārattilum aikārattilum okarattilum ōkārattilum patiṉmūṉṟu tiruttaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. ivvāṇi yeḻuttukkaḷ paḷḷiyilum aracu viṉiyaḷavilum parappappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ittiruttaṅkaḷil peruṅkūṟukaḷ muṉpē iruntu piṉpu teḷivum eḷimiyum karuti māṟiyavi eṉṟu varalāṟu aṟital nallatu. aracāṇiyiṭṭa periyār eḻuttukkaḷāvaṉa: ṇā ṟā ṉā; ṇi li ḷi ṉi; ṇo ṟo ṉo; ṉō ṟō ṉō.
eḻuttuvaṭivaṅkaḷ kālakkōṭpaṭutalum karuvikkōṭ paṭutalum kikkōṭpaṭutalum uṇmiyāṉum, oliyaṉ poruḷoṭu nēr toṭarpu iṉmiyāṉum iṉṉapiṟa kāraṇattāṉum tolkāppiyam uyirvaṭivaṉkaḷiyum mey vaṭivaṉkaḷiyum viḷampavilli. vēṟṟumit terivukku vēṇṭiyavaḷavē naṉi cila nūṟpākkaḷil atuvum puḷḷi paṟṟiya eḻutturu viṉik kūṟiyuḷḷatu. iṭikkālattu eḻunta ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum tolkāppiyattip piṉpaṟṟic cila uriyuruvaṅkaḷiyē toṭṭuccelkiṉṟaṉa. accuppativuc cemmi vanta iññāṉṟu vaṭivaṅkaḷ orunili vārppurup peṟṟaṉavātaliṉ pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭil aṟuvaki yilakkaṇam kaṇṭa taṇṭapāṇi cuvāmikaḷ uyirkkum meykkum uyirmeykkum ellām taṉittaṉi vaṭivaṅkaḷi virivupaṭap puṉintu nūṟpāviṟ kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. itu niṟka.
`puḷḷiyillā ella meyyum' eṉṟa innūṟpāvil uyirmey vaṭivaṉkaḷi iruvakiyuḷ aṭakkik kāṭṭuvar tolkāppiyar. uyirmey akarattiṟku maṭṭum oruvaki; ēṉiya patiṉoṉṟukkumākap piṟitoruvaki. mey puḷḷi nīṅki viṭṭāṟ pōtum, ka ṅa mutalāṉa uyirmeyyākiviṭum. iṅku puḷḷi nīkkam eṉṟa aḷavil eḷiya vilakku muṟiyākum. ēṉiyuyirōṭu urupu tirintu uyirttal eṉṟu curuṅka moḻintamiyāl, ā mutaliyaṉa cērumpōtu varum tiripukaḷ orupāṟ paṭātaṉa eṉpatu peṟappaṭum. uyireḻuttukkuriya paṉṉiraṇṭu taṉivaṭivaṉkaḷum uyirmeykaḷākumpōtu ciṟitum cērakkāṇōm. meykkuriya vaṭivaṅkaḷō eṉiṉ naṭuvurut tiripiṉṟiyē uyir meyyākum. nilip pōkkik kāṇalām. itaṉālaṉṟō `ellāmeyyum' eṉat tiripil meyvaṭiviṉ mēl vittu uyirmeykaṭku vaṭivam cuṭṭukiṉṟār tolkāppiyar. ka kā ki kī ku kū eṉṟāṅku iṉṟuvari meyvaṭivamē naṭunāyakamāka niṉṟu nilavum orumi varavi uṇarka.
uyirmey nili
(16)	meyyiṉ vaḻiya tuyirtōṉṟa niliyē.
ituuyirmeyyil uyiriṭam kūṟum.
akalavuri
mēlinūṟpāvil uyirummeyyum kalantu uyir meyyākuṅkāl varum vaṭivat tiripukaḷ maṭṭum iruvakiyākac cuṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. uyirmey eṉṟa kuṟiyīṭṭil uyir mutalil uḷḷatu. “paṉṉīreḻuttum uyireṉa moḻipa” eṉṟataṉ piṉpē “patiṉeṇṇeḻuttum meyyeṉa moḻipa” eṉac collappaṭṭatu. ātaliṉ uyirmeyk kalappil uyirē muṉṉolikkum eṉṟu karutappaṭuvatu iyalpu itaṉit terivikkavē avvāṟilli: meyyolimuṉ tōṉṟum. ataṉ piṉṉarē uyiroli tōṉṟum eṉṟu tamiḻoli muṟi kūṟuvar āciriyar. aiyaṅkaḷital innūṟpāviṉ payaṉ.
vaḻakku: ka kā ki kī ku kū iṉṉapiṟa.
tiṟaṉuri
uyirmey - uyiroṭu kūṭiya mey, tayirvaṭi eṉpatu pōla. vēṟṟumittoki nili cilaviṭaṅkaḷil uyirum meyyum eṉa ummit tokiyākavum varum. iravupakal eṉpatu pōla. uyirmeyk kalappil etumuṉ etupiṉ eṉṟu teḷivippataṟku oru kāraṇam uṇṭu. mēlinūṟpāvil `ēṉiyuyirōṭu uruvu tirintu niṟkum' eṉṟār. aṅṅaṉam niṟkum tiripu vaṭivaṅkaḷ cila muṉ ciṟkum. (kāṇka: ke kē ki); cila piṉṉēvarum (kāṇka: kā ki kī ku kū ) iṉṉuñ¨cila muṉṉum piṉṉum niṟkum kāṇka: ko kō ku) iṅṅaṉam uyirmeyyil tirikuṟi muṉṉum piṉṉum varutaliṉ, tolkāppiyak kālattum innili iruntirukku mātaliṉ, kuṟi niṟkum variciyaṉṟu olitōṉṟum nili eṉṟu teḷivu ceyvar tolkāppiyar.
oru viṉā: uyirmey vaṭivil meyyoli muṉṉum uyiroli piṉṉum iruttaliṉ niṉṟa muṟippaṭi `meyyuyir' eṉṟē kuṟiyīṭu ceytirukkalāmē? vaṭivukkāka avvāṟum irukkalām; eṉiṉum uyirmey eṉpatu mutaleḻuttu variciyātaliṉ, aṅṅaṉam moḻintār. `oṟṟu muṉṉāy varum uyirmeyyē' eṉṟu pavaṇantiyār itaṉiyē piṉpaṟṟuvar. āṇ peṇ, iravu pakal, tāy tanti eṉṟa vaḻakkumuṟikaḷiyum niṉika.


meym mūviṉam
(17)	 valleḻut teṉpa kacaṭa tapaṟa.
(18) 	melleḻut teṉpa 'ñaṅaṇa namaṉa.
(19)	iṭiyeḻut teṉpa yarala vaḻaḷa
ivi meyyeḻuttiṉ mūviṉaṅkaḷiyum kūṟum.
akalavuri
kuṟil neṭileṉṟu paṉṉiraṇṭu uyireḻuttum iruvakippaṭṭataṉṟē. atupōl valliṉam melliṉam iṭiyiṉam eṉappatiṉeṭṭu meyyeḻuttum āṟu āṟu āṟāka mūvakippaṭum eṉṟu colluvār nūlōr.
valleḻuttu meykaḷ - k c ṭ t p ṟ
melleḻuttu meykaḷ - ñ ṅ ¨ ṇ n m ṉ
iṭiyeḻuttu meyykaḷ - y r l v ḻ ḷ
tiṟaṉuri
vallāṟu, vaṉṟoṭar, valloṟṟu, valittal, valippu, vallici, vaṉmi eṉavum; meṉṟoṭar, melloṟṟu, melittal, melippu, mellici, meṉmi eṉavum; iṭit toṭar, iṭiyoṟṟu, iṭimi eṉavum immūviṉamum āṭcipeṟum.
muṉṉiraṇṭum valittal, melittal eṉa viṉiyāka varum. iṭiccollukku viṉiyāntaṉmi yilli. iṅku mūviṉa meykaḷiyum ka ṅa ya eṉṟāṅku akaram paṭak kūṟiṉālum ivi oṟṟukkaḷēyām. “meyyiṉiyakkam akaramoṭu civaṇum” eṉṟa tolkāppiya vitippaṭi, akaravoliyaṉākak koḷḷāmal akara vōciyākak koḷḷavēṇṭum. `puḷḷiyillā ellā meyyum' eṉṟu muṉṉē akaravuyir meyyākak kūṟappaṭṭaṉavē. aviyalla ivi eṉṟu teḷika.
vāyvaḻik kāṟṟu mikka aṭippālum uṟupputtacit taṭiyālum piṟappatu valliṉam. vaṉmi pōlavē uṟupput taṭippaṭṭu mūkkiṉvaḻik kāṟṟōṭṭattāl piṟappatu melliṉam. kāṟṟum uṟuppum mikka taṭiyiṉṟi vaṉmi yuṟāmalum meṉmip paṭāmalum piṟappatu iṭiyiṉam. itaṟku ōraḷavu uyirttaṉmiyum uṇṭu. iṭiyeṉpatu orupakkam cārāta nekiḻ viciyik kuṟikkum. `akalātu aṇukātu tīkkāyvārpōla eṉpati oruvāṟu oppu nōkkuka. ceyyuḷiyalil.
vallici vaṇṇam valleḻuttu mikumē
mellici vaṇṇam melleḻuttu mikumē
iyipu vaṇṇam iṭiyeḻuttu mikumē
eṉṟa vaṇṇa nūṟpākkaḷil iyipu eṉṟa col iṭi eṉpataṟku nikarāka āḷappaṭum. ivvāṭciyi nōkkiṉ vallolip piṟappup kūṟum. mellolip piṟappuk kūṟum iyinta nili iṭiyeḻuttām eṉṟu uṇaralām. peyariṉum viṉiyiṉum mey taṭumāṟi varum colli iṭiccol eṉṟu ilakkaṇam kūṟumaṉṟō? oppiṭṭuk kāṇka. mēlum iṭiyeḻuttukkaḷ ceyyuḷil āciṭi yetukit toṭiyāka vantu iyitaliyum eṇṇuvōm.
valleḻuttum melleḻuttum vaḷivēṟṟumi yiruppiṉum uṟupputtaṭiyāl iṉamāka oṟṟumippaṭṭaṉa eṉiṉum poruṇmi niliyil ovvoṉṟum mutaleḻut tām eṉṟa oliyaṉiyali maṟavātu koḷḷavēṇṭum. ōraḷavu īraḷavu eṉṟa māttiri vēṟṟumiyāl uyirirupāṟpaṭṭatu. meykaḷukkuḷ māttiri vēṟṟumiyilli. vaḷiyiṉ ticiyāṉum uṟupput tīṇṭum muṟiyāṉum mey mūviṉamāyiṟṟu.
poruṇmi matippil muppatu oliyaṉkaḷum taṉip peṟṟiyaṉa eṉpatu muṭinta teḷivu. oṉṟiṉuḷ oṉṟu aṭaṅkātātaliṉ ovvoṉṟiyum taṉiyākac cuṭṭuvatutāṉ matippiyal. eṉiṉum vaḻakkeḷimiyum kaṟpōr niṉi vāṟṟalum tokinalamum āṭcit teḷivum puṇarccip potumiyum nōkki uyirāka meyyākak kuṟilāka neṭilāka vaṉmiyāka meṉmiyāka iṭimiyāka ellām iṉaṅkaḷāyiṉa.
maṉatti ṉeṇṇi mācaṟat terintukoṇṭu
iṉattiṟ cērtti yuṇarttal vēṇṭum
nuṉittaku pulavar kūṟiya nūlē
eṉṟa tolkāppiya iṟuti nūṟpā iṉaneṟi kūṟal niṉiyattakum.
meym mayakkaṅkaḷ
(20)	ammū vāṟum vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅkiṉ
	meymmayaṅ kuṭaṉili teriyuṅ kāli
	ṭa ṟa la ḷa veṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉark
	ka ca pa veṉṉum mūveḻut turiya.
itumutal eṭṭu nūṟpākkaḷ meymmayakkam kuṟitta varampukaḷ kūṟuvaṉa. iḵtu ōroṟṟuṭaṉili eṉappaṭum.
akalavuri
muṉṉar - piṉpu, aṭuttu, uriya - uriyaṉa.
mayakkam - kalattal. oṉṟōṭoṉṟum aṭuttu niṟṟal eṉpatu poruḷ.
meymmayakkam - iṉṉameyyeḻuttukkuppiṉ iṉṉa meyyeḻuttu varutal : meyyum meyyum iṇital.
patiṉeṭṭu meyyum tamiḻ oliyaṉkaḷāyiṉum aṭuttaṭuttu niṟkum uṟavil variyaṟiyuṇṭu aṭuttu niṟkattakum meyyi naṭpumey eṉavum niṟkak takā meyyip pakimey eṉavum koḷvōm. itaṉi mūṉṟu vakippaṭuttalām.
1. 	taṉmeykkuppiṉ taṉatē 	-	itu taṉatē 				varum uppu (k c t p - 4)		mayakkam 
2.	orumeykkuppiṉ piṟitē varum	-	itu piritē mayakkam			vāḻka (rḻ-2)	
3.	taṉmeykkuppiṉ taṉatum kaṉṉi	-	itu taṉatoṭum			varum allatu piṟitum varum kaṉṟu 		mayakkam piṟitoṭum			(ṭ ṟ ṅñṇ n m ṉ y l v ḷ - 12)		mayakkam	
meyyōṭu meykalakkum immayakkattil mutalmey puḷḷiyuṭiya taṉimeyyākavē irukkum; aṭuttu varumey uyirmeyyāka irukkum eṉiṉum uyirmeyyil mutaṟkaṇ olippatu meyyēyaṉṟō ātaliṉ meymmayakkamākak koḷḷappaṭum.
uri: mēṟkūṟiya patiṉeṭṭu meykaḷum makkaḷiṉ pēccuvaḻakkil oru meyyōṭu iṉṉorumey cērum iṇiniliyi viḷaṅkak kāṇpōmāṉāl  ṭ ṟ l ḷ eṉṟa puḷḷiyeḻuttu ovvoṉṟukkup piṉṉum ka ca pa eṉṟa mūṉṟu meykaḷum varuvataṟku uriyavi. itu piṟitoṭum mayakkam.
vaḻakku:	ka	ca	pa
ṭ	veṭkam	kāṭci	naṭpu
ṟ	toḻiṟkaḷam	toḻiṟcāli	toḻiṟpayiṟci
l	elki	palcuṉi	velpaki
ḷ	koḷkalam	koḷcāṭi	koḷpali
tiṟaṉuri
mayakkam colliṭi vittuk kāṇappaṭumāyiṉum, taṉiyeḻuttu niliyilēyē iṉṉataṟkuppiṉ eṉṟu cuṭṭa muṭiyumātaliṉ, eḻuttuk kuṟitta nūṉmarapiyalil mayakkaṅ kūṟiṉār. oru moḻiyil eḻuttiṇital eṉṟa mayakkattip palavakippaṭuttalāmeṉiṉum payaṉum varampum uṭiya mayakkamē kūṟappaṭum. uyirmey ōreḻuttaṉṟu; ceyki naṭimuṟiyil uyirākavō meyyākavō taṉippaṭuttappaṭum ātaliṉ mayakkap pēccilli. meyyum uyirum iṇivatu oruvaki eṉalāmāyiṉum, “uṭaṉmēl uyirvan toṉṟuva tiyalvē” eṉṟu naṉṉūl veḷippaṭuttiya paṭi, ituvum mayakkam eṉappaṭātu.
uyirmeyyum uyirmeyyum mayakkamāmō eṉiṉ uyirumeyyum eṉṟa iraṇṭē eḻuttiṉ aṭi vakiyākak koḷḷappaṭumpōtu uyirmeym mayakkattiṟku iṭaṉilli.
uyiroṭu uyirum uyiroṭu meyyum mayaṅkutal uṇṭēyeṉiṉum, iviyellām etaṉiyaṭutta etuvum varalāmātaliṉ, varampiṉmikkum payaṉiṉmikkum ilakkaṇappēṟilli. mūṭiyariciyi eṇṇip payaṉeṉṉa? kuṭattil nīrttuḷi mayakkattik kūṟip payaṉeṉṉa? ilakkaṇattiṉ payaṉ teḷivum kaṭippiṭippumām. varampuṭiyatē ilakkaṇa vaḷivuḷ iṭampeṟum. eḻuttu mayakkattil meyyoṭu mey oṉṟiniṟkum mayakkamē tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ amippik kāṭṭuvatu. varampuṭiyatu piṟaḻātu kaṭippiṭikka vēṇṭiyatu. puṇarcciyilakkaṇat tiṟkum taḷamāvatu. ātaliṉ virivupaṭa eṭṭu nūṟpākkaḷ meymmayakkattiṟkē yāttār tolkāppiyar. iyaṟṟamiḻ kūṟum ivvilakkaṇatti icit tamiḻil varum iṇi kiḷi naṭpu paki narampukaḷōṭu oppiṭuka.
vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅku - makkaḷ pēccu vaḻakkil uḷḷatu eṉṟapaṭi. eṉavē ceyyuḷ vaḻakkukkum ituvē aṭippaṭiyām meymmayaṅku uṭaṉili eṉpataṉ viḷakkam: vāḻka eṉṟa collil ḻakaram puḷḷiyāka irukkum. `ka' uyir meyyāka irukkum. itu ōroṟṟuṭaṉili eṉappaṭum. varumey uyirōṭu kūṭiniṟkum eṉpatic cuṭṭavē uṭaṉili eṉṟār. ituvē poruḷ eṉpataṉi ṭa ṟa la ḷa eṉṉum puḷḷi eṉavum ka ca pa veṉṉum mūveḻuttu eṉavum kūṟuvatāl teḷivām. mēlum  ka ca pa eṉa akaravuyir mēlaṉavākak kūṟiṉālum kā cā yā ki ci pi eṉṟāṅku ellāvuyir meykkum koḷḷavēṇṭum. ivviḷakkam piṉvarum nūṟpākkaṭkum ēṟkum. iḵtu iṉantaḻuvutal eṉṉum neṟi.
oru viṉā: meymmayakkam orumoḻiyil varumā? moḻit toṭaril varumā?
payiṟci
kāṭci
velka		orumoḻiyil vantaṉa
koḷka	
kaṭaṟkari	moḻittoṭaril vantaṉa
nāṭkāṭṭi
veṭṭum puli
pūṅkā		
orumoḻiyāyiṉum paṉmoḻiyāyiṉum īṇṭu olittoṭarcciyē ilakkaṇamākum. `iṉṉavolikkuppiṉ iṉṉavoli' eṉṟa aṇukkaniliyē meymmayakkamām. piṉvarum puṇariyalkaḷ kiḷavit toṭarāyiṉum avaṟṟukkum immayakkamē ilakkaṇam eṉṟaṟika nūṉmarapu kūṟum eḻuttilakkaṇam puṇarcciyil māṟuvatilli.
ituvumatu
(21) 	avaṟṟuḷ,
	laḷaḵkāṉ muṉṉar yavavun tōṉṟum
itu muṉcoṉṉavaṟṟuḷ irupuḷḷikaṭku mēlum mayakkam kūṟum.
akalavuri
mēṟcoṉṉa nāṉku puḷḷikaḷuḷ l ḷ eṉṟa irupuḷḷikaṭkuppiṉ yakara vakara eḻuttukaḷum varum. eṉavē ka ca pa ya va eṉṟa aintum mayaṅkum eṉpatu karuttu.
vaḻakku:
	   ya		     va
l - 	nalyāṉi	nalvaravu
ḷ - 	āḷyār	āḷvār
ituvumatu
(22) 	ṅañaṇa namaṉa veṉumpuḷḷi muṉṉart
	tatta micikaḷ ottaṉa niliyē
itu melliṉattukku iṉamāṉa valliṉattōṭu mayakkam kūṟum.
akalavuri
eḻuttu variciyil ṅ ñ ṇ n m ṉ eṉṟa melloṟṟukkaḷukku mēlē irukkum ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟa eṉṟa valleḻuttukkaḷ iṉamuṟippaṭi piṉṉē vantu niralē mayaṅkum.
ṅka: ¨ñca; ṇṭa; nta; mpa; ṉṟa eṉṟavāṟu iṇintuvarum. micikaḷ - mēlē niṟkum eḻuttukkaḷ.
vaḻakku:	 ṅka	- 	vaṅkāḷam
¨	ñca	-	tañci
	ṇṭa	-	ilaṇṭaṉ
	nta	-	intiyā
	mpa	-	pūmpukār
	ṉṟa	-	 kuṉṟakkuṭi
tiṟaṉuri
muppatu eḻuttukkaḷiṉ varici iṉṟu akarac cuvaṭiyiṟ kāṇpatu pōlavē tolkāppiyak kālattukku muṉpum iruntu vantatu. akaramutal ṉakara viṟuvāy, oḷakāraviṟuvāy, kuṟṟeḻuttu, neṭṭeḻuttu, valleḻuttu, melleḻuttu, iṭiyeḻuttu mutaliya nūṟpākkaḷum, piṟappiyal uyir mayaṅkiyal puḷḷimayaṅkiyalil varum eḻuttu varicikaḷum, `tattam micikaḷ' eṉṟa innūṟpāvum `appatiṉoṉṟē puḷḷiyiṟuti’ eṉṟa moḻimarapu nūṟpāvum `ñakāra mutalā ṉakāra  vīṟṟup puḷḷiyiṟuti iyipu' eṉṟa ceyyuḷiyal nūṟpāvum tamiḻeḻuttu māṟāta varalāṟṟorumikkuc cāṉṟākiṉṟaṉa.
ituvumatu
(23) 	avaṟṟuḷ,
	ṇaṉaḵkāṉ muṉṉark
	kacañapa mayavav vēḻum uriya
itu muṉ coṉṉavaṟṟuḷ iru puḷḷikaṭku mēlum mayakkam kūṟum
akalavuri
mēṟcoṉṉa āṟu puḷḷikaḷuḷ ṇṉ eṉṟa iru puḷḷikaṭkup piṉ ka ca ña pa ma ya va eṉṟa ēḻu eḻuttukkaḷum varum. eṉavē ivaṟṟoṭu ṇakarattukku ṭakaramum ṉakarattukku ṟakaramum muṟkūṟiyapaṭi cērum.
vaḻakku:	ka	ca	ña	pa
ṇ	uṇkalam	veṇcāntu	  oṇñāṇ	veṇpūti

	ma	ya	va

ituvumatu
(24)	ñanamava veṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar
	yaḵkāṉ niṟṟal meypeṟ ṟaṉṟē
itu nāṉku puḷḷikaṭku oru meymmuṉ mayakkam kūṟum.
akalavuri:
ñ n m v eṉṟa nāṉku puḷḷikaṭkup piṉṉē
yakaram varuvatu poruḷuṭiyatē.
mey - poruḷ peṟṟaṉṟu - peṟṟatu
vaḻakku
uṟiñ yātu, porun yātu, tirum yātu, tev yātu
uriñ - uriñcutal, porun - poruntutal, tirum - tirumputal, tev - paki.
tiṟaṉuri
immeymmayakkam puṇarmoḻil varukiṉṟatu. muṉviḷakkiyapaṭi meymmayakkaṅkaḷ oru moḻiyilum varalām. atu kaṇakkaṉṟu. iṉṉa eḻuttukkupippiṉ iṉṉa eḻuttu varum eṉṟa attuṇiyē īṇṭu ilakkaṇam; eḻuttut toṭarccikkē ilakkaṇam eṉpati maṟavaṟka.
tolkāppiyar kālattilē immayakkam aritāka iruntamiyiṉ, vaḻakkil aritāka uṇṭu eṉṟu terivippataṟ kāka `meypeṟṟaṉṟu' eṉa naṭiceytār. yakaram eṉṟu potuppaṭak kūṟiṉālum' āvō ṭallatu yakaram mutalātu' eṉpataṉāl `yā' eṉṟa ōruyirmeyyē varum eṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
pala meym mayakkaṅkaḷ oru collilum puṇarmoḻiyilum varum. cila mayakkaṅkaḷ puṇarmoḻiyil maṭṭum varum. innūṟpā kūṟum mayakkam iraṇṭām vakiyic cārntatu.
uṟiñ, porun, tirum eṉṟa mūṉṟum uṟiñcu, poruntu, tirumpu eṉak kuṟṟiyalukaramākap piṟkālattut tirintaṉa.
immeym mayakkam piṟkālattu vaḻakkāṟilli.
`aiyaṉ ven viṭāta koṟṟat tāvam vantaṭinta taṉṟē' eṉṟa vālivatippaṭalac ceyyuḷil nakara vakara mayakkam putiya varavākum.
ituvumatu
(25)	maḵkāṉ puḷḷimuṉ vavvun tōṉṟum.
itu muṉcoṉṉavaṟṟuḷ orupuḷḷikku mēlum mayakkam kūṟum.
akalavuri:
mēṟkūṟiya nāṉku puḷḷikaḷuḷ m eṉṟa oru puḷḷikkup piṉṉē vakaramum varum. eṉavē ya va, eṉa iraṇṭu varum eṉpatu karuttu.
vaḻakku:  valam   varuka.
tiṟaṉuri
`yavavun tōṉṟum' `ēḻumuriya' `vavvun tōṉṟum' eṉṟa ummikaḷ ivaṟṟukkumēl illi eṉṟa muṟṟumi kāṭṭuvaṉa laḷaḵkāṉ, ṇaṉaḵkāṉ, yaḵkāṉ, maḵkāṉ eṉṟa āytavolikaḷ icinayam tarupavi.
ituvumatu
(26)	yaraḻa veṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar
	mutalā keḻuttu ṅakaramoṭu tōṉṟum
itu mūṉṟu puḷḷi muṉ patteḻuttukkaḷiṉ mayakkam kūṟum.
akalavuri
moḻimarapu eṉṟa aṭuttaviyalil ka ca ta pa ña na ma va ya eṉṟa oṉpatu meyyeḻuttukkaḷ moḻikku mutalāka varum eṉṟu colluvar. y r ḻ eṉṉum puḷḷikkup piṉṉē avvoṉpatu eḻuttukkaḷum ¨ṅakaramum vantu mayaṅkum.


vaḻakku
	      ka	       ca	    ta	      pa	   ña
y	ceyki	tāycēy	ceyti	āypavi	āyñar
r	tērki	vārcili	ūrti	mārpu	ūrñamali
ḻ	āḻkaṭal	vīḻcuṭar	vāḻti	vīḻpaṭi	vīḻñāyiṟu
	     na	      ma	      va	      ya	      ṅa
y	āynar	vāymi	teyvam	ceyyāṟu	vēyṅaṉam
r	tērnar	nērmi	nērvaḻi	pōryāṉi	vērṅaṉam
ḻ	vāḻnar	kīḻmi	vāḻvu	pāḻyātu	vīḻṅaṉam
tiṟaṉuri
moḻimarapil varum etiratu nōkki `mutalākeḻuttu’ eṉṟa kuṟiyīṭu īṇṭu āḷappaṭṭatu. mutaleḻuttukkum mutalākeḻuttukkum vēṟṟumi yuṇarka. ṅakarattip pirittuk kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, atu nēriya mutalākeḻuttilli eṉpatu veḷippaṭi. mutalākeḻuttu eṉa moḻimēl vittuc cuṭṭappaṭṭatupōl tōṉṟiṉum atu curukkaṅ karutiyatēyām. olit toṭarcciyeṉṉum meymmayakkamē īṇṭup poruḷām.
mutalākeḻuttu ṅakarattōṭu cērntu variciyāka iraṇṭum varum eṉpatu karuttaṉṟu. yaraḻakkaḷiṉ piṉpu ṅakaramum taṉi ōr eḻuttāka vēyṅaṉam, vērṅaṉam vīḻṅaṉam eṉa mayaṅkum eṉṟu koḷka. ṅaṉam - taṉmip poruḷum iṭap poruḷum tarum cārpuccol, tāṉē niṟkum mutaṉmiyilli yātaliṉ, moḻi mutaleḻuttōṭu eṇṇappaṭavilli pōlum.
meymmayakkattil muṉṉatu puḷḷiyāka irukkum. piṉṉatu uyirmeyyākavum irukkum eṉṟa muṟippaṭi ṅakaratti `ṅaṉam' eṉa uyirmeyyākavē koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
`āṅṅaṉam virippiṉ' āṅṅaṉam aṟipa' `āṅṅaṉam oḻukiṉ' eṉat tolkāppiyattil iccol cuṭṭaṭuttu āḷappaṭum.
ṅakaratti tūyamoḻi mutaleḻuttākak koḷḷāmikku oru kāraṇam āṅṅaṉam eṉpatu āṅkaṉattiṉ melliṉat tiripākak karutiyirukkalām. āṅkaṉam eṉṟa vaḻakkum uṇṭu. āṅku, āṅkaṇ eṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil kakaram varutalik kāṇalām. maliyāḷa moḻiyil niṅṅaḷ, māṅṅā, māṅṅā, tēṅṅā eṉa vallolikaḷ mūkkolikaḷāka māṟiya perumpōkkiṉi oppiṭuka. tirintaniliyil varum eḻuttiṇi moḻi mutalākat tolkāppiyam matikkavilli. `avvi yoṭṭi ṅavvum mutalākummē' eṉṟa naṉṉūlār cuṭṭiya muṭavanaṭi ēṟkattakum.
eṅṅaṉ iruntatev ṟevvaṇṇam collukēṉ
aṅṅaṉ iruntateṉ ṟuntīpaṟa
eṉṟa uyyavanta tēvanāyaṉār tiruvuntiyāril, ṅaṉam eṉpatu ṅaṉ eṉṟa vaṭivil niṟpatik kāṇalām. ituvē aṭivaṭivāyiṉ neñcam eṉpatiṟpōl am eṉpatu cāriyiyākum.
ituvumatu
(27)	meyn nili cuṭṭiṉ ellā veḻuttum
	tammuṟ ṟāmvarūum raḻavalaṅ kaṭiyē
itu patiṉāṟu mey cuṭkut taṉvaravumayakkamum irumeykaṭkup piṟavaravu mayakkamum kūṟum.
akalavuri
itukāṟum meymmayakka niliyil oru puḷḷimuṉ piṟameykaḷ varum piṟavaravu meymmayakkamē kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. innūṟpāvil k ṅ c ñ¨ ṭ ṇ t n p m y l v ḷ ṟ ṉ eṉṟa patiṉāṟu eḻuttukkaḷum taṉmuṉ tāṉ varum eṉavum r ḻ eṉṟa iru eḻuttukkaḷum taṉmuṉ piṟitē varum eṉavum varampu ceyyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
uri
meyyōṭu meymmayaṅku niliyip potup paṭiyākac cuṭṭiṉāl, ra ḻa eṉṟa iraṇṭu nīṅka ēṉip patiṉāṟu puḷḷikaḷum taṉmuṉ tāṉ varum. naṭiyāṟṟalāl, ra ḻa iraṇṭaṉmuṉ piṟavē varum eṉpatu peṟappaṭum. meynnili - meymmayakka nili.

tiṟaṉuri
itu meym mayakkam paṟṟiya iṟuti nūṟpā. iṉṉameykkup piṉ iṉṉamey varutal eṉpatu tamiḻiṉ iyaṟkiyātaliṉ ivvamippiṉi naṉku viḷaṅkik koḷḷavēṇṭum. mikat toṉmiyāṉa tamiḻ iṉṟuvari nilipeṟṟiruppataṟkum varuṅkālamellām nilipeṟṟa vaḻakkup peṟutaṟkum ittakiya amippiṉik kaṭip piṭippatu makkaḷ kaṭami. tamiḻmoḻikku nilipēṟum kālavorumiyum kaṉṉimiyum tarum meymmayakkaviyal kuṟittu ivviṭattu vēṇṭumaṭṭum terintu koḷvōm.
patiṉeṭṭumeykaḷi mayakka varavil taṉatē mayakkam, piṟitē mayakkam, taṉṉoṭum piṟitoṭum mayakkam eṉa mūṉṟākap pakukkalām.

orumoḻiyiṉ ilakkaṇak kūṟukaḷ tāymoḻi yāḷarkkum kuḻantikaṭkum moḻic cūḻniliyuṭiyārkkum kēḷviyuṭiyārkkum mūccōṭṭam pōlavum kuruti yōṭṭam pōlavum tāmē patiyum kēṭṭuvaruvatu moḻi: moḻiyaṟivu peṟac cevi vaḻiyē eḷiya iyaṟkiyākum. cevivaḻi uṇarappaṭutaliṉ icikkuk kēḷvi eṉṟa peyaruṇṭu.
ittaṉmiyiyum itupōṉṟa moḻit taṉmi kaḷiyum tāymoḻi makkaḷ tam moḻiyip pēcat toṭaṅkiyanāḷ mutalē uṇarntiruppar eṉavum putiya karuttukkaḷi marapumuṟiyiṟ paṭittukkoḷvataṟkum ilakkiya vuṭimikaḷip pēṇikkoḷvataṟkum piṟatākku talāl moḻikkuk kēṭu vārātu kāttukkoḷvataṟkum vitimīṟalkaḷik kaḷintukoḷvataṟkum ilakkaṇa varaṉ vēṇṭum eṉavum ilakkaṇamillāmoḻi aracillā nāṭu okkum. eṉavum kēraḷa pāṇiṉīya āciriyar araca varumaṉ cāṟṟiya poṉmoḻi tolkāppiyattukkuc cevvaṉ poruntum. pēcakkaṟṟuk koṭuppatu. ilakkaṇamaṉṟu: kākka ākka vaḷarkka vaḻivaḻiviḷaṅkat taṭaṅkāṭṭuvatē ilakkaṇam eṉṟu teḷika. ilakkaṇam puṟamiruntu tiṇikkappaṭum ceyaṟkiyaṉṟu. makkaḷiṉ vāymoḻik kūṟukaḷikkaṇṭu kariyuṭiyātu kaṭṭi uyartti vaḷarppatē ilakkaṇa nōkkam.
tamiḻukku ivvaraṇkaḷuḷ oṉṟu meymmayakkam eṉappaṭum. ataṉi oṉpatu nūṟpākkaḷil viritturippar tolkāppiyar. piṉvarum puṇarccikaṭkum immayakka naṭpu pēraṭippaṭiyākum. itaṉip piṟaḻātu kaṭippiṭippatē moḻikkāppu. parantu ōṅkiya paṉmoḻit tākkam cāṭuṟum ikkālattu, tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ iyaṟkiyik karuttūṉṟik kākkavēṇṭum. kariyuṭintāl maturāntaka ēripōl āṟutariyākit taṭamāki mēṭākik kāṭākātā? kalappup perukivarutaliṉ moḻi viḻippu vēṇṭum kālam itu ātaliṉ ulakattup palamakkaṭ peyarkaḷiyum paṉṉāṭṭup peyarkaḷiyum palaporuṭ peyarkaḷiyum, ayaṉmoḻic coṟkaḷiyum iṉṉōraṉṉa ellāvaṟṟiyum, uṇavi vāy varampukkuḷ aṭakkik koḷvatu pōlat tamiḻ meymmayakka varampukkuḷ aṭittuc cevviyal kāṇka. tamiḻccoṟkaḷik kūṭap piḻimayakkamāka eḻutumpōkku eḻuttāḷariṭi vaḷarntu varukiṉṟatu. kavalippaṭuvār cilarē. cīttalic cāttaṉārkaḷ perukavēṇṭum. paṭupiḻi kaḷiyum tamiḻ vaḷarcciyeṉa vātiṭuvārkku iraṅkiyoḻika.
āṅkilamoḻi intiyap peyarkaḷit taṉakkēṟpa māṟṟik koṇṭatiyum nām ēṟṟukkoṇṭatiyum naṉku cintiyuṅkaḷ. perumpiḻiyāka eppaṭiyellām tamiḻip pāṭupaṭuttukiṉṟaṉar eṉpataṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷ taravarumpavilli evaṟṟi evvāṟu eḻutavēṇṭum eṉṟa nērkāṭṭukkaḷ cilavaṟṟi varivaṉ.
meccikkō, teṉmārkku, ālantu, kāntilantu, cerumaṉi, pirañcu, āttirēli, vāciṅkaṉ, āppirikkā, pākittāṉ, icurēl, mācukkō, iraṅkūṉ, tilli, nākapuri, pāṭṭiṉā, kācumīram eṉṟāṅku ūrppeyarkaḷiyum:
kurōmikō, cerunaṉkō, kōrppaccēv, ciṭṭiṉi, teṉṉicaṉ, cāṉcaṉ, parukku, alaccāntar, peṉṉāṭcā, arittāṭṭi, akamatu. aputul, akkupar, patumāvati, vīṭaṇaṉ, cukkirīvaṉ, aruccuṉaṉ, vīṭumaṉ, cantirakuttaṉ, cūrapaṉmaṉ, mariyāḷ, ēcu, yōvāṉ. cūciyappar, tōmiyar eṉṟāṅku makkaṭ peyarkaḷiyum;
pippiravari, ēppiral, ākattu, ceṭṭampar, aṭṭōpar, patti, mutti, catti, māṉiyam, puṇṇiyam, kaṇṇiyam, cittiram, cattiram, pattiram, cāttiram, tōttiram, marumam, varuṇaṉi, varumaṉ, tarukkam, akkiṉi, mukkiyam, pākkiyam, cāṅkiyam, karuppiṇi, karuppattaṭi, varukkam, aruccaṉi, coruṇam, āṉmā, āttumā, tarumam, taṉmam, cattam. niruvāṇam, vākkiyam, āciramam, vāttiyam eṉṟāṅku pala coṟkaḷiyum tamiḻiyaṟkippaṭi eḻutuvatu arumiyō? ippala coṟkaḷil tamiḻccoṟkaḷum uṇṭu. ayaṟ coṟkaḷik kiyāḷa nēriṭiṉ appōtum tamiḻ meymmayakkattip pōṟṟa vēṇṭumaḷavukkē mēlik kāṭṭukkaḷ. itumoḻik kāppu neṟi eṉappaṭum. veḷḷi, poṉ, kalikkōṭṭu muṉivaṉ, puki niṟak kaṇṇaṉ, vēḷvi pakiñaṉ, vaccirat teyiṟṟaṉ. māperumpakkaṉ eṉṟu kampar ceytatupōla ollumaḷavu tamiḻākki viṭuvatuvē naṉmarapākum.
moḻimutaleḻuttilum moḻiyīṟṟeḻuttilum ilakkiyaṅkaṇṭu ciṟupāṅku tolkāppiyattilum vēṟupaṭum pavaṇanti mutalāṉōr iṭinili meymmayakkattil vēṟupaṭavilli. itaṉāl tamiḻukku ivvilakkaṇattiṉ uyirppāṅku uṟutiyām.

ceyya tamiḻiṉ tiṟam.
cuṭṭukkuṟi
(28) 	ai uam mūṉṟuñ¨ cuṭṭu.
itu cuṭṭu eṉṟa kuṟi kūṟum.
akalavuri
ōraḷapu icikkum kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ aintām eṉṟu muṉpu kūṟappaṭṭatu. avaṟṟuḷ akaram ikaram ukaram eṉṟa mūṉṟum uyirk kuṟṟeḻuttu niliyil niṉṟē cuṭṭuk kuṟippiṉiyuṇarttutaliṉ cuṭṭu eṉṟa kuṟiyum peṟum. akaram cēymic cuṭṭu, ikaram aṇmiccuṭṭu, ukaram iṭiccuṭṭu.
vaḻakku atteru, itteru, utteru, avvīṭu, ivvīṭu, uvvīṭu, `ammūṉṟum eṉpatuvum akakkāṭṭu. iccuṭṭu peyarkkumuṉ varum.
tiṟaṉuri
a i u mūṉṟum cuṭṭu eṉṟa kuṟi peṟum. ivi taṉiyeḻuttākavē irukkum. ivaṟṟukku uyir mayaṅkiyalil taṉippuṇarcci viti kūṟuvar āciriyar. cuṭṭē iṟutiyākavum amitaliṉ, `cuṭṭiṉiṟuti' eṉpar. atu itu utu eṉpaṉa vaṟṟic `cuṭṭu mutalukaram'  eṉavum avi ivi uvi eṉpaṉavaṟṟic `cuṭṭu mutalākiyavi yeṉ ṉiṟuti' eṉavum av iv uv eṉpaṉavaṟṟic `cuṭṭumutal vakaram' eṉavum iccuṭṭiṉum vēṟupaṭa āḷal kāṇka. atu mutaliyavaṟṟuk kellām urupiyaliṟ cāriyi kūṟum āciriyar cuṭṭukku aṅṅaṉam kūṟāmiyum karutuka.
naṭumi kāṭṭum ukarac cuṭṭu. iṉṟu perumpāṉmi vaḻakkil illi; eṉiṉum iṭiniliyāṉa naṭuviṭam uṇmiyiṉ ataṟku ōrcol vēṇṭumaṉṟō? poru ḷiruppac col vīḻtal, vīḻaviṭutal aṟiyāmiyākum. iṉṟō ippakutiyik kuṟippataṟku meyppāṭu kāṭṭit taṭumāṟukiṉṟōm. ātaliṉ ukaraccuṭṭuk kiḷavikaḷi yellām peruvaḻakkāṟṟuppaṭuttal nallatu. kalviyālum eḻuttālum uyirppikka vēṇṭiyaṉa ivi.
“oru kūṭṭattār iṅkum maṟṟoru kūṭṭattār uṅkum iṉṉoru kūṭṭattār aṅkum niṉṟu kaṭavuḷi vaḻipaṭal vēṇṭum” eṉpatu tiru.vi.ka.naṭi.
a avaṉum iivaṉum uuvaṉum kūṭiyakkāl
eevaṉi vellā rikal
innaṭi vāypāṭu āṅkilattiṉ irupattu nālāvatu eḻuttum irupattintāvatu eḻuttum pōlap payaṉ paṭuvati oppunōkkuka.
viṉākkuṟi
(29)	āē ōam mūṉṟum viṉā.
itu viṉā eṉṟa kuṟi kūṟum.
akalavuri
īraḷavu icikkum neṭṭeḻuttu ēḻeṉa muṉpu kūṟappaṭṭatu. avaṟṟuḷ ā ē ō eṉṟa anta uyir mūṉṟum neṭṭeḻuttu niliyil niṉṟē viṉāpporuḷit tarutaliṉ viṉā eṉṟa kuṟiyum peṟum.
viṉā a eṉa aḷapeṭip pāṭam oṉṟu koḷḷal yāppup poruttamākum eṉṟa vēṅkaṭarāculu kuṟippu okkum.
vaḻakku: avaṉā coṉṉāṉ? nīyē coṉṉāy? yāṉō coṉṉēṉ? ivi peyarvaḻi vanta viṉākkaḷ.
avaṉ varuvāṉā?
nī celvāyē?
yāṉ colvēṉā?
ivi viṉivaḻi vanta viṉākkaḷ.
immūṉṟum peyarkkum viṉikkumpiṉ varuvaṉa.
muccuṭṭil iṭavēṟṟumiyiruppatupōla immūṉṟu viṉāvilum cila vēṟupaṭu poruṇmi tolkāppiyar kālattu iruntiruttal vēṇṭum.
tiṟaṉuri
cuṭṭupporuḷum viṉāpporuḷum tarum ivi nūṉmarapil iṭampeṟalāmā? eṉpatu viṉā. nūṟmarapu muppattu mūṉṟu eḻuttuḷ varuvaṉavaṟṟiṟku ōriṉam nōkkik kuṟiyīṭukaḷ kūṟum iyalātalāl cuṭṭum viṉāvum ivviyaliṟ cērkkappaṭṭaṉa. ivi cuṭṭukkuṟippum viṉākkuṟippum uṭiyavātaliṉ moḻiyāntaṉmiyil aiyamilli eṉiṉum īṇṭu nōkkam kuṟiyīṭē. ataṉāṟṟāṉ cuṭṭu eṉavum viṉā eṉavum kuṟiyīṭukaḷiyē payaṉiliyāka amittār āciriyar. kuṟiyīṭu kūṟuvatu piṉṉar eṭuttāḷum curukkattiṟkaṉṟē. aintiṉik kuṟil eṉavum ēḻiṉi neṭil eṉavum paṉṉiraṇṭiṉi uyir eṉavum patiṉeṭṭiṉi mey eṉavum ivvāṟē vaṉmi meṉmi iṭimi eṉavum orupaṭittāṉa eḻuttukkaḷukku oru kuṟiyīṭu paṭuttiyatik kāṇkiṉṟōm. ammarapuppaṭi a i u mūṉṟu uyirkkuṟilum ōriyalpuṭiya  cuṭṭuk kuṟiyām. ā ē ō mūṉṟum ōriyalpuṭiya viṉāk kuṟiyām. itaṉāl nām aṟiyak kiṭappatu eṉṉa? oruvaḻip paṭṭa eḻuttut tokutikku kuṟiyīṭu oṉṟu vaṭippatē nūṉmarapiṉ karuttu eṉpatām. ātaliṉ muḻuccoṉṉiliyil varum atu, av, avi, avaṉ, yā, yātu, yāvaṉ, evaṉ mutaliyavaṟṟukku nūṉmarapil iṭamilli.
col nōkkil cuṭṭum viṉāvum iṭiccoṟkaḷ eṉka. ēkāramum ōkāramum piṟaporuṭ kuṟippukkum varutaliṉ iṭiyiyalil nūṟpākkaḷ yāttār. ā viṉā eṉṟa oru poruṭkuṟippēyuṭimiyiṉ innūṟpāvē pōtiya tāyiṟṟu. cuṭṭukkum iḵtokkum. itu muṉcolliyatu pōtum eṉṟa neṟi.
kuṟilkaḷ cuṭṭātalum neṭilkaḷ viṉāvātalum iyaṟki nayamuṭiyaṉa.
e eṉṟa kuṟilum oruvaki viṉāvāy varutal uṇṭu. em peyar, emmuṟi, em mey eṉa varumiṭaṅkaḷ uḷa. eṉiṉum perumpālum ekaram akarac cuṭṭukku muṉṉōṭiyāy varum cuṭṭu naṭiyik kāṇkiṟōm.
“epporu ḷāyiṉum alla tilleṉiṉ
  apporu ḷallāp piṟituporuḷ kūṟal”

“evvayiṟ peyarum veḷippaṭat tōṉṟi
  avviyal niliyal cevvi teṉpa”
“evvaḻi variṉum viṉiyē viṉikkuṟippu
  avviru mutaliṟ ṟōṉṟu meṉpa”
mēlum ivviṭaṅkaḷil ekarac cērkki ellām eṉṟa poruṇmik kuṟiyākat tōṉṟuvati uyttuṇarka.
epporuḷ yāyhrvāyk kēṭpiṉum apporuḷ
meypporuḷ kāṇpa taṟivu.
eṉṟa tirukkuṟaḷum ivvoṭṭu naṭiyākum.
iṉattiṟ cērtti uṇarttal vēṇṭum eṉṟa tolkāppiya eṇṇappaṭi, ekarattiyum viṉāvākak koḷḷalām. 
kāṇka: eppaṭi, emmoḻi, ekkuṟaḷ.
iyalum iciyum
(30)	aḷapiṟan tuyirttalum oṟṟici nīṭalum
	uḷaveṉa moḻipa iciyoṭu civaṇiya
	narampiṉ maṟiya veṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu icit tamiḻiṉ aṭippaṭiyum aḷapu mikutiyum kūṟum. 
akalavuri
nūṉmarapiṉ toṭakkattil uyireḻuttukkaḷukkum meyyeḻuttuk kaḷukkum māttiri kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. ivi pēccu vaḻakkukkum iyalvaḻippaṭṭa ceyyuḷ vaḻakkukkum uriyavi. icittamiḻukkum ivveḻuttukkaḷē aṭippaṭi; eṉiṉum ici paṇṇātaliṉ āḷatti ceyyuṅkāl iyalukkuc colliya eḻuttiṉ māttirikaḷ mikku olikkum eṉpatu karuttu.
uri: uyirukkuk kūṟiya māttirikaḷ kūṭutalāka olittalum meyyoṟṟukkaḷ ōci nīṭṭittu olittalum ākiya iraṇṭum uṇṭu eṉṟu collappaṭupavi vāyppāṭṭoṭu poruntiya yāḻici nūliṭattaṉa eṉpar iyaṟpulavar.
aḷapu - māttiri; iyinīṭal - alaku kūṭṭal
moḻipa - moḻiyappaṭupavi, viṉiyālaṇiyum peyar.
ici - miṭaṟṟici. maṟiya - kaliyiṭattaṉa, aḵṟiṇip
palaviṉpāṟ kuṟippu viṉimuṟṟu. moḻipa - eḻuvāy;
maṟiya - payaṉili.
innūṟpā naṭiyi
eṇṇeṇpa ēṉi yeḻutteṉpa ivviraṇṭum
kaṇṇeṉpa vāḻu muyirkku
eṉṟa tirukkuṟaḷ naṭiyōṭu oppiṭṭut teḷika.
tiṟaṉuri
moḻiyiṉṟi iyalilli; atupōl moḻiyum iyalumiṉṟi iciyilli. iyalukkum icikkum taḷamāvatu moḻi eṉiṉum iyalukku uriya eḻuttoli tāṉō icikkalikkum eṉpatu viṉā. ivviṉāvukku viḷakkam taruvatu innūṟpā. iciyil varum collum poruḷuṭiya taṉṟē; poruṇmiyaḷavil iyaloliyaṉkaḷiṉ taṉmi uṇṭu. “ceyyuḷiṭattu eḻuttukkaḷ peṟum māttiri yaḷavurikkum ilakkaṇam iyaṟṟamiḻukkum icittamiḻukkum potuvām” eṉpar yāḻ nūl eṉappaṭum tamiḻ vāḻnūl kaṇṭa aṭikaḷ vipulāṉantar.
iyaloliyaṉi uṭkoṇṭatu ici. paṇkaḷukkum tiṟattukkum porunta ārōci amarōci paṇṇuṅ kālum āḷatti ceyyuṅkālum mutaleḻuttu eṉappaṭum olikaḷ palavāṟu nīṇṭicikkum, uyiroliyē icivīccukku iḻumi yūṭṭumātaliṉ, `aḷapiṟantuyarttal' eṉa avvoliyi mutaṟkaṇ moḻintār. oṟṟu māttiri nīḷuvatilli; ōciyē nīḷumātaliṉ, icinīṭal eṉṟār. uriyāciriyarkaḷuḷ icimaṟi teḷinta aṭiyārkku nallār iciyil varum uyirkaḷiyum meykaḷiyum kuṟittup piṉvarumāṟu uriviḷakkam eḻutukiṉṟār:


“kuṉṟāk kuṟilintuṅ kōṭā neṭilintum
  niṉṟārnta mannakaran tavvoṭu - naṉṟāka
  nīḷattā lēḻum nitāṉattāl niṉṟiyaṅka
  āḷatti yāmeṉ ṟaṟi”
eṉpatu āḷattikku iṉṉum paṭuvatōrilakkaṇam uṇarttu kiṉṟatu. kuṉṟāk kuṟilintum kōṭā neṭilintum eṉṟatu kuṟṟeḻuttālum neṭṭeḻuttālum āḷatti ceyyappaṭum eṉṟavāṟu. kuṟṟeḻuttintāvaṉa a i u e o; neṭṭeḻuttintāvaṉa  ā ī ū ē ō eṉa ivi, niṉṟārnta veṉṟatu - meyyeḻuttākiya patiṉeṭṭeḻuttuḷḷum mavvum navvum tavvumeṉa mūṉṟeḻuttumallā maṟṟi yeḻuttukkaḷ āḷattikku varappeṟā eṉṟavāṟu “eṉavum mutaliṟ pāṭumiṭattu makarattiṉ oṟṟālē nātatti uccarikkum marapu uṇṭu eṉavum kūṟuvar.”
itaṉāl nām uṇarakkiṭappatu eṉṉa? tamiḻiṉ uyirolikaḷē niṟaṅkaḷiṉ aḷattiyāy icippaṇṇiṟku uriya curaṅkaḷāka iruntaṉa eṉpatum carikamapatani eṉṟa peyarkaḷiṉ mutaleḻuttukkaḷ piṉṉar curaṅkaḷākak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatum ici varalāṟṟu muṟiyil teḷivām.
ā ī ū ē ai ō oḷa eṉṟum
ivvēḻeḻuttum ēḻicic kuralē
eṉṟa cēntaṉ tivārakamum cāṉṟātal kāṇka. itaṟku akāracātakam eṉpatu vaṭamoḻip peyar.
`iciyoṭu civaṇiya narampiṉ maṟi' eṉpataṉ viḷakkam: tamiḻiciyil yāḻkkaruviyē mutaṉmipeṟum. cilappatikāram peruṅkati cintāmaṇi mutaliya kāppiyaṅkaḷil kāppiya talimāntarkaḷiṉ yāḻiciyaṟivu pōṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ellā ici nuṇukkattiṟkum koḷḷiṭamāvatu yāḻē ātaliṉ `narampiṉ maṟi' eṉṟār tolkāppiyar. poruḷatikārattiṟ karupporuḷ kūṟumiṭattum `yāḻiṉ pakuti' eṉṟa karuviyē ici cārpāka niṟṟal kāṇalām.
narampiṉ maṟi eṉpatē iciyik kuṟikkap pōtu māyirukka, ` iciyoṭu civaṇiya' eṉṟataṉ karuttu yātu? īṇṭu ici eṉpatumiṭaṟṟici eṉṉum vāyppāṭṭiṉik kuṟikkum. ivvāyicikkuttāṉē eḻuttaṭippaṭiyuṇṭu. aḷapu kaṭantu olittalum meyyōci nīḷalum uṇṭu. kuḻal yāḻ taṇṇumi muḻavu mutaliyaṉavellām kuyiluvak karuvikaḷ eṉappaṭum. ivi miṭaṟṟicikkup piṉvaḻi niṟpaṉa eṉpataṉic cilappatikāram teḷivākkum. ātaliṉ `iciyoṭu civaṇiya narampiṉ maṟi' eṉpatu miṭaṟṟici yoṭu otticikkum yāḻkkaliyik kuṟikkum. tolkāppiyam iyaṟṟamiḻ ilakkaṇa nūlāyiṉum ēṟṟa peṟṟi icittamiḻk kuṟippum koṇṭatu eṉpataṉi, `vallōṉ puṇarā vāram pōṉṟē' eṉṟa marapiyal nūṟpāvāṟ kāṇkiṟōm.
paṇṭik kālattut tamiḻici vallunar tāmē pāṭṭum pāṭi uṭaṉ yāḻum mīṭṭiṉar.
`kōvalaṉ kiyāḻ nīṭṭa vavaṉum
kāviriyi nōkkiṉavum kaṭaṟkāṉal varippāṇiyum
mātavitaṉ maṉamakiḻa vācittal toṭaṅkumaṉ'
eṉṟa cilappatikāravaṭikaḷum,
`iruṅkaṭaṟ pavaḷac cevvāy tiṟantivaṉ pāṭiṉāḷō
  narampoṭu vīṇi nāviṉ naviṉṟatō'
eṉṟa cīvaka cintāmaṇiyaṭikaḷum, 
“maṉperum pāṇa ṉārum māmaṟi pāṭa vallār
  muṉpiruṟ tiyāḻiṟ kūṭal mutalvarip pāṭukiṉṟār.”
eṉṟa tiruttoṇṭar purāṇavaṭikaḷum miṭaṟum yāḻum iṇinta tamiḻici marapi eṇpikkum. `iciyoṭu civaṇiya narampiṉ maṟiya' eṉṟa tolkāppiyattukku ikkāppiyaṅkaḷ uriyutavi ceykiṉṟaṉa.
`iciyoṭu civaṇiya narampiṉ maṟiya' eṉpatu pāyirattil varum. `centamiḻ iyaṟki civaṇiya nilattoṭu muntunūl kaṇṭu' eṉpataṟku oru cāṉṟu. eṉavē `muntunūl' eṉpatu iyalnūlōṭu ēṉikkalikaḷiyum kuṟikkum.
tamiḻil eḻuttu vaṭivukkum eṇ vaṭivukkum ici vaṭivukkum neruṅkiya toṭarpu toṉṟu toṭṭu uṇṭu.
kakaraṅ koṭṭē ekaram aciyē
ēkaram tūkkē aḷavē āytam
eṉpatu aṭiyārkku nallār mēṟkōḷ. “ivimāttirip peyarkaḷ “koṭṭu ari māttiri, ataṟku vaṭivu ka: aci oru māttiri, ataṟku vaṭivu e: tūkku iraṇṭu māttiri, ataṟku vaṭivuu. aḷavu mūṉṟu māttiri ataṟku vaṭivu  ḵ eṉakkoḷka” eṉpatu nallāriṉ viḷakkam. `eṭṭiṉoṭu iraṇṭum aṟiyēṉiyē' eṉṟa tiruvācamum, `a u aṟiyā aṟivil iṭimakaṉē' eṉṟa yāppu mēṟkōḷum. eṭṭēkāl laṭcaṇamē' eṉṟa taṉippāṭalum eḻuttoṭu eṇṇukku vaṭivut toṭarpu cuṭṭal kāṇka. tamiḻukkut taṉimatippu niṟuvum eḻuttu vaṭivu pōlat taṉimatippuc cuṭṭum eṇ vaṭiviyum patipput tuṟiyilum piṟaviṭaṅkaḷilum nalvaḻakkiṟ pēṇik kāttal naṟṟamiḻar kaṭaṉ.
`uḷḷatu citippōr uḷareṉap paṭāar' eṉpatu cāṉṟōr cuṭṭu, ātaliṉ ettuṟiyilum nīṇṭa varalāṟum  taṉittaṉmiyum kāṭṭum tamiḻuṭimikaḷik kāttalum putiya kālattiṟku ēṟpa vaḻakkiṟ payaṉ koḷḷalum tamiḻvaḻit tōṉṟalkaḷiṉ poṟuppu.


nūṉpamarapu - iyal muṭivuri
ciṟappup pāyiram
(a) `ippāyirattiṉi “vāḻttiyal vakiyē nāṟpākkum urittē” eṉṟa ceyyuḷiyaliṉpaṭi, nūlvāḻttākavum nūlāciriyaṉ vāḻttākavum koḷḷa iṭaṉuṇṭu.
`vaṭavēṅkaṭam teṉkumari' eṉṟa pā nilimaṇṭila āciriyappā eṉakkarutalām. maṇṭilayāppu nēraṭiyāka varum eṉavum āciriyam eṉṉum centūkkākum eṉavum pērāciriyar urivaritaliṉ, ivvāṟu karuta iṭaṉuṇṭu; eṉiṉum coṟcīraṭiyāl ippāyiram toṭaṅkuvatu muraṇākavuḷḷatu. nilimaṇṭilam ellā vaṭiyum nāṟcīr koḷvataṉṟō? vēṟuvaki āciriyappāvuṅkūṭa mutalaṭi nāṟcīr peṟuvataṉṟō? ātaliṉ pāyiratti aṭivari yaṟiyillā nūṟpāvākak koḷḷutalum tolkāppiyam nūṟpāvaṇṇattātalāṉ, pāyiramum avvaṇṇamē eṉṟu koḷḷutalum arilaṟṟatu.
(ā) nāṉmaṟi evi? tolkāppiyattukku muntiyanūlkaḷum uṭaṉnūlkaḷum uṭaṉpintiya nūlkaḷum namakkuk kiṭikkāta niliyil, oru taliyāka nāṉmaṟi paṟṟit tuṇitaṟkilli eṉa yāṉum uṭaṉpaṭukiṉṟēṉ; eṉiṉum tolkāppiyam muḻutum kiṭittalāṉum, ammarapiṉ vanta aviyap paṉuvalkaḷ aḷavākak kiṭittalāṉum, takkār cilar nērpaṭa āyvu ceytiruttalāṉum perumpāṅku cila karuttukkaḷik kaṇikkalām. vētaviyācar vakutta irukku ecur cāmam atarvaṇam innāṉmaṟiyākā eṉpatu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kālamutal varum karuttākum. avar kūṟum tittiriyam mutaliyaṉa avar kālattum illāta nūlkaḷātaliṉ yātum collutaṟkilli. mēlum peyaraḷavāṉē avi tamiḻ maṟiyalla eṉpatu teḷivu.
maṟi eṉṟa nūṟcol tolkāppiyattuc cilaviṭaṅkaḷil varukiṉṟatu. icimaṟiyi `narampiṉmaṟi' eṉpar. maṟiīṇṭu kali eṉṟa poruḷik kuṟikkum. antaṇar maṟittu, maṟiyōr āṟu. maṟimoḻi mantiram eṉṟivi maṟippeyar peṟṟaṉa eṉṟu eṇṇalām. vētam niṟinta tamiḻnāṭu eṉpatu pāratiyam `aṟaṅkari nāviṉ nāṉmaṟi' eṉpataṉāl aṟam poruḷ iṉpam vīṭu paṟṟiya nāṉmaṟi eṉṟu karutalām. `aṟa mutalākiya muḻumutaṟ poruṭkum' eṉpatu tolkāppiyam. irukku ecur cāmam atarvaṇam eṉṟa āriya maṟiyic collakkūṭātu eṉṟa karuttil eḻutappaṭuvatu ivvuriyaṉṟu; aṅṅaṉam oru taliyāka moḻitaṟku valuccāṉṟilli. tolkāppiyat toṉmiyi nōkkuṅkāl pintiya viyācar tokuppu cuṭṭattakkataṉṟu. atarvaṇam vaciya mantiraṅkaḷ uṭimiyiṉ nāṉkāka eṇṇattakka maṟikkuriya takutiyuṭiyatumaṉṟu. `nāalvēta neṟi tiriyiṉum' (puṟam.2) eṉṟa perumatippukku uriyatumaṉṟu. tiriyī eṉṟa collil atarvaṇattukku iṭaṉilli.
tolkāppiyatti mutaṟkaṇ tamiḻkkaṇṇōṭu kāṇpatu tāṉ cenneṟi. aṅṅaṉam pārkkum niliyil iyalpāka vēṟṟuc cāyal variṉ meypporuḷ kāṇpatu cārpu neṟi. eṉavē nāṉmaṟi eṉpataṟku aṟamporuḷ iṉpam vīṭu eṉṟa nāṟporuḷik kūṟum oru maṟi eṉṟu ippōtu uriceytirukkiṉṟēṉ. `toyyāvulakattu nukarcciyum' (puṟam.214) `aruḷuṭiyārkku avvukappēṟum' (kuṟaḷ.247) tamiḻkkoḷkiyātal teḷika. puṟanāṉūṟu mutalāṉa aviyap paṉuvalkaḷil varum nālvētam, nāṉmaṟi eṉṟa coṟkaḷukku, maṟikūṟum poruḷ nāṉku eṉṟē koḷḷal takum. maṟi nūlkaḷ nāṉku eṉṟu koḷvatu cālātu. aimperumkuḻu eṇpērāyam eṉa varum toṭarkaḷi oppunōkkuka. kuḻu aintu āyam eṭṭeṉap poruḷpaṭumā?
(i) aintiram eṉpataṉ poruḷ eṉṉa? vaṭamoḻiyil intiraṉaṟ ceyyappaṭṭa aintiram eṉṟa viyākaraṇanūl eṉpatu palar karuttu. attaku orunūl iruntiruppiṉum kālattāl tolkāppiyattukku muntiyatō eṉpatu pēriyam. paṉṉūṟāṇṭukku muṉṉē maṟintatu eṉpatu oru varalāṟu. aintira viyākaraṇam collilakkaṇam kūṟum. moḻiyilakkaṇam eṉpatu palar collum muṭivu. eḻuttukkum collukkum ataṉmēl vāḻkkip poru ḷukkum ilakkaṇam kaṇṭa tolkāppiyar orutuṟippaṭṭa aintiram kaṟṟar eṉpatu eṉṉa perumi yākum? narampiṉ maṟiya, antaṇar maṟittu, yāppaṟi pulavar eṉṟu cuṭṭiyatupōla, aintirak kuṟippēṉum tolkāppiyattuḷ uṇṭā?
pallikkaḻakaṅkaṇṭa aṇṇāmali vaḷḷal eṉat taṉ peyar tōṟṟi, oḷicciṟatal nīḷali kaṇṭa irāmaṉ eṉat taṉ peyar tōṟṟi, amerikkāvik kaṇṭa kolampacu eṉat taṉ peyar tōṟṟi, cārpiyam teritta aiṉciṭiṉ eṉat taṉ peyar tōṟṟi eṉṟu collumāṟupōla, `aintiram niṟinta tolkāppiyaṉ eṉat taṉpeyar tōṟṟi' eṉṟa naṭi ōṭukiṉṟatu. eṉavē ivvīṟu piṟaroruvarkkillāta ciṟappāka irukkavēṇṭum. tolkāppiyarkku aintirakkalvi aṉṉa ciṟappukku uriyatō? aṉṟu.
(ī) akapporuḷāṉa aintiṇiyilakkaṇam tiṟappaṭa vakuttavar tolkāppiyar. aviyac cāṉṟōrkkum tiruvaḷḷuvarkkum iḷaṅkōvukku iṉṉa perumpulavarkkum tiṇi vaḻikāṭṭiyavar tolkāppiyar. poruḷatikāramē tamiḻukkum tolkāppiyattiṟkum ciṟappu. ātaliṉ, aintiṇi allatu aintiṇi yoḻukkam kūṟavalla tiṟam niṟintavar eṉpatē ippōtu eṉakkut tōṉṟum uriyākum.
īṇṭu oruviṉā. ivvurikku aintiṟam eṉa valliṉamāka vāvatu col irukkavēṇṭumallavā? viṉā nēriya viṉāvē. ittoṭarukkup pāṭapētamum illi. eṉiṉum ṟakaramum rakaramum poruḷ vēṟiṉṟi māṟi niṟkum cila tamiḻc coṟkaḷ uḷa. muri, muṟi, puram. puṟam, pirivu, piṟivu, perumpiṟitu, purattal, puṟantaral, nirappu,  niṟi, citar, citaṟu; karuppu. kaṟuppu eṉa varutali oppunōkku. uruttiraṉpōlat tiraṇ eṉṟa collukkē āṟṟaṟporuḷ koḷḷalām. tiraṇi, tiraṭci eṉpaṉavum kāṇka. `ayintiram niṟintavaṉ āṇi yēvalāl' eṉṟa kampariṉ toṭaril `aintiram niṟinta' eṉṟa pāyirappakuti paṭṭāṅkup patintuḷatu. ayintiram niṟintavaṉ aṉumaṉ āvaṉ. ivaṉ aintavinta pērāṟṟalaṉ; māṇippaṭivam koṇṭavaṉ. `māṇiyām paṭivamaṉṟu' eṉa irāmaṉālum `neṟiniṉṟu poṟikaḷ aintum veṉṟavaṉ' `āṟṟal ītē aimpulattiṉ ceṟiyum ītē' eṉac cītiyālum pulaṉaṭakkap pukaḻpeṟṟavaṉ.ātalāl `ayintiram niṟintavaṉ' eṉṟa kampar pāṭalukku aimpulavaṭakkattāl niṟinta āṟṟaluṭiya aṉumaṉ eṉṟu poruḷ koḷvatu mikiyāmō?
(u ) tolkāppiyam kuṟitta palar tam palvēṟu koḷkikaḷi aḷavi muṟiyiṟ cāṉṟupaṭa ārāyntu eṉ muṭiviṉip pulappaṭuttaṟku ivvuri iṭaṉaṉṟu eṉpataṉi uṇarkiṉṟēṉ. ivaṟṟuṭ palavaṟṟip paṟṟiya eṉ karuttuk kaḷit tamiḻkkātal, cintaṉikkaḷaṅkaḷ, tolkāppiyap putumi, tolkāppiyattiṟaṉ, oppiyalnōkku ākiya eṉ uri naṭinūlkaḷil mēṟkōḷ potuḷak kāṇalām; ātaliṉ eṉ tolkāppiyak  koḷkikaḷi ivvuriyip payilvārkku vēṇṭiyavaḷavu karuttāṟṟuppaṭuttu muṟiyil naṭukaṟkaḷ pōlac cila colli vippēṉ.
tolkāppiyaṉ eṉpatu nūlāciriyariṉ iyaṟpeyar. pōkkaṟupaṉuval eṉpatu nūṟpeyarākalām eṉiṉum vaḻivaḻi vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa tolkāppiyam eṉṟa nūṟpeyar eṉakku uṭaṉpāṭē. tamiḻiṉ mutu tolvaralāṟu nōkkiṉ, tamiḻukku mutaṉmutal tōṉṟiya nūlaṉṟu tolkāppiyam. iṉṟu nallūḻāl namakkuk kiṭittuḷḷa paṉuvaṟ celvattuḷ kālattāl toṉmivāynta mutalnūl tolkāppiyam ākum.
tolkāppiyattukku muntiya nūlkaḷum uṭaṉ tōṉṟiya nūlkaḷum uṭaṉaṭuttut tōṉṟiya nūlkaḷum aimpūtac cīṟṟattālum, iṉanalap potunōkku iṉmi yālum, ayaṉmoḻi nākarikavēṭki aṟivi mayakkiyami yālum aḻintoḻintaṉa. oru perunūlakam niṟikkum palkalic cuvaṭikaḷ paṭivāyppiṉṟi aiyakō eṅkō eppaṭiyō maṭintaṉa. celvākkuṭiya perumakkaḷ  vallaṭimiyi nalvāḻveṉa makiḻntu kūlimaṉam koṇṭu tāḻntu iḻintamiyāṉum, kalvi kali camayam caṭaṅkellām vēṟṟumoḻikaḷāy uruveṭuttamiyāṉum cātimēl kīḻ kīḻiṉkīḻ eṉṟa piṟappupvaḻip pūcalkaḷāṇum ettuṇiyō vaṭivil, kēṭukaḷ āḻaṅkoṇṭaṉa.
tamiḻukkup paḻiya peruvāyppukkaḷiyellām tūrttup putiya vāyppukkaḷi evvakiyilum neruṅka viṭāmiyālum, tamiḻ vāḻka eṉṟa ceyaloṭṭā āravārap pakaṭṭu muḻakkattālum, uṇmit tamiḻc cāṉṟōrip piṉpaṟṟā maṭamiyāṉum evvaḷavō paṉþṉūṟāyiramāṇṭut tamiḻc celvaṅkaḷ eñciyaṉavum aḻintu varukiṉṟaṉa. ettuṇi māperiya tamiḻiṉattukku ituvā varalāṟu? etirkāla varalāṟu eṉṉō? nampikki koḷvōm. inniliyil tolkāppiyam oṉṟē ñālap paḻiya tamiḻiṉattiṉ tolluyirum puttuyirumāka maṉṉi viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu inta oṉṟiyāvatu cikkeṉappaṟṟi vaḷarntu uyti peṟuvōmāka.
