(ū) tolkāppiyattiṉ kālam ki.mu. aintām nūṟṟāṇṭukku muntiyatu eṉpārtam kālakkoḷki eṉakku uṭaṉpāṭu. iṉṟu nam pēṟākavuḷḷa aviyanūlkaḷ tirukkuṟaḷ, cilappatikāram pōṉṟavi tolkāppiyatti nōkkak kālappintiyaṉa eṉṟālum intiya moḻikaḷiṉ varalāṟṟōṭum ulaka moḻikaḷiṉ varalāṟṟōṭum oppiṭuṅkāl nam nūṟcelvaṅkaḷ toṉmi cāṉṟaṉavē. tolkāppiyak kālakkalvi muṟikaḷ ki.pi. aintām nūṟṟāṇṭuvari uyirōṭṭam peṟṟiruntaṉavātaliṉ, tolkāppiyavuri kāṇpataṟku ittoṉṉūlkaḷ peruviḻukkāṭu naṟṟuṇiyāvaṉavē. muṟkāla marapuṇarcciyum marapuppaṟṟum cila nūṟṟāṇṭuvari toṭarntu vantamiyiṉ iṭikkālattum tolkāppiyak kalvimarapu tollāṇi nallāciriyarkaḷpāl taḻittintatu. ataṉālaṉṟō ki.pi.oṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkuppiṉ tolkāppiyam ilakkaṇavulakilum uriyulakilum taṇiyāp puttuyir peṟṟatu. ilakkaṇa viḷakkam ilakkaṇakkottu variyil tolkāppiya vūṟṟukkaṇkaḷik kāṇkiṉṟōm. tolkāppiyam ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiliruntu mīṇṭum tamiḻakattiṉ tāy nūlākavum ulakappaṇmoḻiyaraṅkil oppuyar vaṟṟa potu moḻiyiyal nūlākavum aṟivoḷi vīcukiṉṟatu.
(e) tolkāppiyar kālattu vaṭamoḻivaravum uṇṭu; ayal nākarika varavum uṇṭu. ivi toṭarvarukiyākum. oppumi cuṭṭum pāṅkil ivi mikac ciṟuvaraviṉa. aṉṟu āciriyac cūḻniliyum āriyat tākkamum avvaḷavāka illi. ātaliṉ āriyak karuttut tōṉṟa nūṟpākkaṭku uriyeḻutal poruttamaṉṟu. tamiḻ neñcaṅkoṇṭu tamiḻ vaḻiyākavē tolkāppiyattukku uriyum viḷakkamum kāṇal eḻuttu neṟiyākum.
tolkāppiyattu iṭiccerukalāṉa cila nūṟpākkaḷ uḷa eṉṟa karuttu eṉakku uṭaṉpāṭaṉṟu. ciṟiyavaḷavu pāṭapētaṅkaḷ uṇṭu.
tolkāppiyam yātta tolkāppiyar iruvar āvar eṉṟa maruṭkoḷki. āyvukkaḷattukkē varattakkataṉṟu. itu il poruḷ eṉka.
tamiḻukkum tamiḻiṉattukkum vaḷamiyāṉa uyarnta putiya etirkāla vāḻvu tolkāppiyattip pōṟṟum aracālum tolkāppiyap paṟṟuṭiya makkaḷālum tolkāppiyavaṟi vuṭiya iḷiñarkaḷālum tolkāppiya uṇarvuṭiya eḻuttāḷarkaḷālum uṇṭākum eṉpatu eṉ nampikki. ituvē ivvuri eḻutuvataṉ nōkkam.
nūṟpā 3
kuṟṟiya likaram kuṟṟiya lukaram
āytam eṉṟa
muppāṟ puḷḷiyum eḻuttō raṉṉa
eṉṟa nūṟpāviṟku iruvēṟu uriyuṇṭu.


orucārār uri
`kuṟṟiyalikaramum kuṟṟiyalukaramum āytam eṉṟa muppāṟ puḷḷiyum eḻuttōraṉṉa' eṉpatām. āytam muppāṟpuḷḷi eṉa maṟupeyar eṉpatum aḵtu aṭuppukkūṭṭuppōla mūṉṟu puḷḷi vaṭivu peṟum eṉpatum ivvuriyiṉ mukkiya karuttu. `āytam eṉṟa muppāṟpuḷḷiyum' eṉpatu oru toṭarāka amiyum. ivvurikkuk kuṟṟiyalikaramum kuṟṟiyalukaramum eṉṟa īriṭaṅkaḷil ummit tokiyākak karutavēṇṭum. iḷampūraṇar, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, veḷḷivāraṇar ittakuvuriyiṉar.
maṟucārār uri
kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukaram āytam eṉṟa mūṉṟuvakip puḷḷikaḷum `eḻuttōraṉṉa' eṉpatām. īṇṭumuppāṟ puḷḷi eṉpatu āytatti maṭṭum kuṟiyātu mūṉṟiyum kuṟikkum. `eṉṟa’ eṉpatu mūṉṟiṉiyum cērkkum. āytam muppāṟpuḷḷi yaṉṟu; kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukaramum pōla atuvum oṉṟu pērāciriyar. civañāṉa muṉivar cuppiramaṇiya cāttiriyār. vēṅkaṭarācalu, mīṉāṭci cuntaraṉār, turiyaraṅkaṉār ittakuvuriyiṉar.
karuta vēṇṭiya viṉā eṉṉa? muppāṟ puḷḷi āytattik kuṟikkumā? mūṉṟiyum kuṟikkumā? mutaluri cila iṭarppāṭuṭiyatu. kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukaram āytam mūṉṟum eṉṟa naṭiyiruntāṟpōtum. āytam eṉṟa oṉṟukkumaṭṭum āytam eṉṟa muppāṟpuḷḷiyum eṉṟu virittuccolvatu mikiyākum. `eṉṟa' eṉpatu āytam oṉṟukkē cēruvatāyiṉ ikaramum ukaramum eṉa ummi nūṟpāvil `virintāṟṟāṉ poruḷ teḷivākum. `mutal karuvuripporuḷ eṉṟa mūṉṟē' eṉṟa akattiṇiyiyal nūṟpavilum, `koṭinili kantaḻi vaḷḷi eṉṟa vaṭunīṅku ciṟappiṉ mutalaṉa mūṉṟum' eṉṟa puṟattiṇiyiyal nūṟpā vilum varum `eṉṟa' eṉṉum peyareccam mūṉṟiṉiyum akappaṭuttali oppunōkkuka.
āytam puḷḷipeṟum eṉṟa karuttil vēṟupāṭilli. ataṉic cuṭṭa vēṇṭiṉ piṟa iṭaṅkaḷiṟ colliyatupōla `āytappuḷḷi; eṉṟāṟ pōtumē. mutaṟcārār karuttuppaṭi āytam mūṉṟu puḷḷiyuṭiyateṉak koṇṭālum `muppuḷḷi' eṉṟu varuvatutāṉ poruttamēyaṉṟi muppāṟ puḷḷi eṉṟu pāliṭi varutal poruttamaṉṟu. pāl eṉpatu pakutiyik kuṟikkum. `appāl aintum ōraḷa picikkum' eṉavum. `appāl ēḻum īraḷapicikkum' eṉavum aṭuttu varum nūṟpāvukkaḷil pāl - pakuti eṉap poruḷpaṭutali uṇaralām. eṉavē `muppāṟpuḷḷi eṉpatu mūṉṟeḻuttukkum uriya puḷḷikaḷiyē kuṟikkum. immūṉṟum vaṭivālum cārpeḻuttām eṉpataṟku ituvumōr cāṉṟu. āytamē muppāṟpuḷḷi peṟumeṉṟāl mūṉṟu puḷḷikkuḷ tammuṉ vēṟupāṭu irukka vēṇṭumaṉṟō? aṭuppuk kūṭṭuppōl eṉṟaliṉ vēṟupāṭiṉmi veḷippaṭi. muppuḷḷi eṉṉātu muppāṟpuḷḷi eṉṟu kūṟiyiruttalāṉum, `eṉṟa' eṉpatu ummi tokka oruniliyil mūṉṟiyumē kuṟikkumātalāṉum iraṇṭāvatu uriyē poruntum.
nūṟpā - 8
uyirmey eṉṟa kuṟiyīṭukaḷi uvami yākupeyarākak koṇṭu meypporuḷ kūṟuvar palar. uyir taṉittiyaṅkum taṉmiyatu. taṉittiyaṅkamāṭṭā meyyi iyakkuvatu eṉavum meykkut taṉittiyaṅkum āṟṟaliṉṟu. uyir cērntutāṉ iyakkam peṟum eṉavum paṇṭit tamiḻariṉ meymiyiyali ilakkaṇamum kāṭṭuvatu eṉavum viḷakkañ ceypa. tamiḻar kaṇṭa meymiyiyaliṉpaṭi, uyir cārntataṉ taṉmittu, iṟiyiyō kaṭṭiṉiyō paṟṟi niṟkumiyal piṉatu. iṟikku uṭampāvatu, meyyuṅkūṭa uyirkku viḷakkāvatu eṉṟa uṇmikaḷi aṟikiṉṟōm. uyir ētāvatu oṉṟiṉip paṟṟātu nillātu eṉṟa meyymmiyip
paṟṟuka paṟṟāṟṟāṉ paṟṟiṉi appaṟṟip
paṟṟuka paṟṟu viṭaṟku
eṉṟa tuṟavukkuṟaḷ pulappaṭuttum. `aviyē tāṉē yāyiru viṉiyiṉ' eṉṟa civañāṉa pōta maṟiyālum, `eḷḷum eṇṇeyum pōlniṉṟa entiyē' eṉṟa tiruvācaka muṟiyālum, `uṭampār aḻiyiṉ uyirār aḻivar' eṉṟa tirumantiraniṟi moḻiyāṉum; uyiriṉ cārpiyal viḷaṅkum. ātaliṉ immeyp poruḷuṇarvi ilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭukaḷāṉa uyirmēlum meymmēlum ēṟṟik kāṇpatu karuttēṟṟam eṉṟa kuṟṟattiṉpāṟpaṭum ēṉi ilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭukaṭkum ivvāṟu meymiyiyal kāṭṭuvatu ovvātu. itaṟkumēl iṅku ikkaruttārāycci urippuṟamāyviṭum.
uyirāyiṉum meyyāyiṉum muppatum mutaleḻuttu eṉappaṭupavi; piṟappu vēṟṟumiyum vēṟṟup poruṇmiyum taṉitta oliyaṉ niliyum koṇṭavi; tam poruṇmikku meykaḷ uyirkaḷic cārntu niṟkum cārpeḻuttukkaḷ alla; uyiroliyaṉkaḷi ottavi meyyoliyaṉkaḷ. mutal nūṟpāviyal `akaramutal ṉakara viṟuvāy' eṉṟu muṭikkum toṭarniliyāl camattaṉmi teḷivām. ātaliṉ vāyuḷ uṟupput taṭiyiṉṟi varum oliyaṉ uyir eṉavum uṟupput tīṇṭip piṟakkum oliyaṉ mey eṉavum oliyiyal muṟiyiṟ kāraṇaṅ kūṟuvatē moḻineṟi.
nūṟpā -9 
piṟarkaruttu: innūṟpā uyirmeykku aḷavu kūṟuvatu; meyyōṭu kūṭiṉālum kūṭiya uyir taṉ māttiriyil tiriyātu; ka eṉṟa uyirmeykkuṟil oṉṟari māttiri yākātu; kā eṉṟa uyirmeyneṭil iraṇṭari māttiri yākātu eṉavum; arināḻi yuppunīrpōla uyirmeyyil mey taṉ māttiriyi iḻakkum eṉavum karuttuk kūṟuvar. uyiriyal - uyirkkuriya māttiri eṉapporuḷ ceyvar. muṉṉum piṉṉum varum atikāram māttirippaṭṭatātaliṉ ivvāṟu poruḷkoḷḷat tūṇṭiyatu.
uyirmeyyuḷ meykku māttiri illi eṉpatuvē āciriyar karuttāyiṉ, meyyiyal tiriyum eṉṟu nūṟpittiruppar. `uyiriyal tiriyā' eṉṟa naṭiyāl tiriyumō aṉṟō eṉṟa aiyappāṭu uyiri oṭṭiyatākum. meyttiripu paṟṟiya kuṟippukkē iṭiṉilli. aiyappāṭu mey paṟṟiya tāyiṉ, uyirōṭiyiyiṉ meyyaḷavu iḻakkum eṉa nūṟpā piṟantirukkum.  īṇṭu atikāramum; muṟṟum aḷaviṉ mēlataṉṟu. uyireṉa moḻipa, meyyeṉa moḻipa eṉpataṉāl kāraṇappeyart toṭarcci peṟappaṭum. paṭavē, taṭippaṭum meyyoliyōṭu cērntālum ivviyipiṉāl uyir taṉ piṟappu vaḻiyiṉiṉṟu tirivatilli; atāvatu taṭippaṭā niliyiṉiṉṟum māṟuvatilli eṉpatē teḷivu.
nūṟpā 10
oruviṉā meykku māttiri ari eṉpatil karuttuvēṟupāṭu illi itu taṉimeykkā? uyirmeykkum uṇṭā? uyirmey iṇippil mey taṉ ari māttiriyi iḻantuviṭukiṉṟatu eṉpatum `ka' oru māttiri yām  `kā' irumāttiriyām eṉpatum ellāhrtaṅ karuttu. ikkoḷki poruntumā? meykku māttiri yuṇṭeṉa veḷippaṭiyāka nūṟpittirukkum tolkāppiyam uyirmeyyākuṅkāl meykku māttiriyiḻappuc cuṭṭak kāṇōmē. ātaliṉ oṉṟari iraṇṭari uyirmeykaṭku uṇṭeṉac coṉṉāl varum piḻiyeṉṉa?

moḻippaṭut ticippiṉum terintuvē ṟicippiṉum
  eḻuttiyal tiriyā veṉmaṉār pulavar”
eṉṟu moḻimarapu valiyuṟuttuvatālum,
“māttiri yaḷavum eṭuttiyal vakiyum
  mēṟkiḷarn taṉ veṉmaṉār pulavar”
eṉṟu ceyyuḷiyal eṭuttu moḻivatālum uyarmeyk kaṇṇum māttiri meykkuk koḷḷalām eṉpatu karuttu
“uyiril leḻuttum eṇṇap paṭāa
  uyirttiṟa miyakkam iṉmi yāṉa”
eṉṟa ceyyuḷiyal nūṟpāvil uyirmey vilakkappaṭavilli eṉpataṉiyum niṉika. uyirmeykku māttiriyilli yeṉiṉ oliyaṉām peṟṟiyirātu. `nīr taṉittu aḷantuḻiyum nāḻiyāy arināḻi uppiṟ kalantuḻiyum kūṭi oṉṟari nāḻiyāy mikātavāṟu pōlvatōr peṟṟi yeṉṟu koḷvatallatu kāraṇam kūṟalākāmi yuṇarka; eṉavum āciriyaṉ āṇi yeṉpārum uḷar eṉavum nacciṉārk kiṉiyar colliyamivar eṉ karuttu mēlum cintaṉik kuriyatu.
nūṟpā 12
piṟarkaruttu: “uṭpeṟu puḷḷi yuruvā kummē” eṉṟa nūṟpā pakarattōṭu makarattiṟku varivaṭivu vēṟṟumi kūṟuvatu eṉa iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉākkiṉiyarum urippar. ivvuri muṟṟup poruttamiṉṟu; eṉiṉum iruperum uri yāḷarkaḷum ivvāṟu urittiruppataṟku avar taṅkālattu varivaṭivac cūḻnili oru kāraṇamāka irukkalām.
iyalpu makarattukkum makarak kuṟukkattukkum varivaṭivu vēṟṟumi kūṟuvatu eṉavum makarakkuṟukkam puṟattuppeṟum oru puḷḷiyōṭu uḷḷēyum oru puḷḷipeṟum eṉavum itukāṟum palar uri ceytu varukiṉṟaṉar.
kuṟukā makarattoṭu kuṟukiya makarattiṉ vaṭivu vēṟṟumi moḻiyum eṉṟa karuttu uṭaṉpaṭattakkatē. āṉāl iru puḷḷi peṟum eṉṟa karuttu poruntiya uriyaṉṟu. vēṟupāṭu cuṭṭuvatu innūṟpāviṉ nōkkam. ataṟku iṉṉoru puḷḷi peṟum eṉṉṟu oru viti coṉṉāṟ pōtumē. anta iraṇṭāvatu puḷḷi uḷḷē irukkum eṉṟu mēlum oru viti vēṇṭumā?
iraṇṭu puḷḷi varum eṉpatu tolkāppiya nōkkāyiṉ uṭpeṟu puḷḷiyum uruvākum eṉṟa ummi vēṇṭum. ummiyiṉmiyiṉ nām karutavēṇṭiyatu eṉṉa? makarak kuṟukkam mey eṉpataṟkuriya puḷḷi peṟum eṉavum kuṟukkam eṉpataṉit terikka avvorupuḷḷi tāṉum uḷḷē irukkum eṉavum koḷḷavēṇṭum. (kāṇka. pōṉma) itu oru kaṇita muṟi. ātaliṉ iru puḷḷipeṟum eṉṟa urikaḷ cālā.
nūṟpā: 15 eḻuttuvaṭivaṅkaḷum karuttukkaḷum
tamiḻ eḻuttu vaṭivu kuṟittut tolkāppiyam cilavaṟṟip pāṅkupeṟa vitikkum. ippakutikkaṇ varivaṭivupaṟṟik kālattukkēṟṟa cintaṉikaḷic cintikka vippatu iv vuriyiṉ kaṭamiyākum. yāṇṭum tamiḻkkāppum tamiḻ vaḷarcciyumē ivvuriyiṉ kuṟikkōḷ.
tamiḻoliyaṉ paṟṟiyum vaṭivaṉpaṟṟiyum aṇmik kālattup palatuṟiyiṉar palvēṟu karuttukkaḷi eḻuttuc cīrtiruttam eṉṟa peyarāl vēkamāka veḷippaṭutti varukiṉṟaṉar. itu kuṟittuk kaṭṭurikaḷum nūlkaḷum pala vantuḷa. ulakattu aṟiviyalāl iṉṟu ellām virivum māṟṟamum peṟuvatupōla moḻikaḷum peṟṟāka vēṇṭum. eṇṇaṅkaḷiṉ vēkavōṭṭattukku ēṟpa moḻikaḷ takutipeṟa vēṇṭum. ātaliṉ moḻic cīrtiruttam eṉpatu kālattāy peṟṟa cintaṉik kuḻaviyākum.
palar karuttukkaḷ
uyiroliyaṉkaḷuḷ aikāram oḷakāram; iraṇṭum vēṇṭiyatilli; meyyoliyaṉkaḷuḷ ṅakaram ñakaram nakaram ṟakaram ḻakaram vēṇṭiyatilli; uyirmeyyilum aikāra oḷakāraṅkaḷ vēṇṭiyatilli; uyirmey eṉṟa iṉamē vēṇṭiyatilli eṉpārum uḷar.
vaṭamoḻiyolikaḷuḷ eṭṭāmeyyum muppatā meyyum muppattōrāmeyyum muppattiraṇṭāmeyyum muppattumūṉṟāmeyyum muppattintāmeyyum āṅkila moḻiyuḷ āṟām oliyum pala olippuṭiyolikaḷum vēṇṭum eṉpārum uḷar.
varivaṭivam paṟṟi veḷiyiṭum karutturikaḷ palapala. eḻuttu muppatu eṉṟālum āytattiyum uyir meyyeḻuttukkaḷiyum eṇṇuṅkāl olikaḷ 247 ām. ivaṟṟil varum 131 kuṟiyīṭukaḷ vēṇṭiyatilli. 28 allatu 30 kuṟiyīṭukaḷ pōtum. ai oḷa eṉpaṉavaṟṟi ay, av eṉa eḻutalām. aintu kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷum kāl vāṅkiṉāl neṭṭeḻuttākiviṭum. taṉineṭṭeḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷ vēṇṭiyatilli. putiya uyirkkuṟikaḷi ākkik koṇṭāl a eṉṟa oru vaṭivē uyiriṉattukku amiyum. āytam vēṇṭiyatilli.
ke kē ki eṉṟiṉiya uyirmeykaḷil uyirkkuṟikaḷ muṉṉē varukiṉṟaṉa; ko kō ku eṉṟiṉiya uyirmeykaḷil uyirkkuṟikaḷ muṉṉum piṉṉum varukiṉṟaṉa. ivi poruttamila eṉavum uyiroḷi piṉ nikaḻvatāl uyirkkuṟilkaḷ ellām meyvaṭivukkup piṉpu varuvatutāṉ muṟi eṉavum uyirmey vaṭivukaḷ tēviyilli; āṅkilam pōl meyvaṭivaṉiyum aṭuttaṭuttu eḻutiṉāṟpōtum eṉavum kūṟuvārum uḷar.
ivaṟṟiṉum palapaṭi kaṭantu intiyavorumip pāṭṭiṟku eḻuttellām oruvaṭivāka atuvum tēvanākari yāka irukka vēṇṭum eṉpārum ulakamuḻunōkkil urōmaṉ eḻuttukkaḷit taḻuvik koḷḷalām eṉpārumākat tamiḻeḻuttuc cīrtiruttam kuṟittuk karutturi vaḻaṅkuvar mikappalar.
uyirmeyyeḻuttukkaḷip perukkik kāṭṭi eḻutteṇṇikki cumi eṉṟu kūṟuvatu tavaṟu; palvēṟu vaṭivaṅkaḷil orumippāṭu uṇṭu; āṇiyiṉṟiyum iyakkamiṉṟiyum kaṭṭāyamiṉṟiyum neṭuṅkālamāka iyalpāṉa māṟṟaṅkaḷ peṟṟu nilippatam eytiyavi tamiḻ vaṭivaṅkaḷ. kiyeḻuttil tēvikkēṟṟa vaṭṭāra vaḻakkukaḷum uṇṭu. evvaki vaḷarccikkum muppatu oliyaṉkaḷum iṉṟuḷḷa varivaṭivukaḷum pōtum. uḷḷati muṟivittuk kaṟpikka vēṇṭum. uḷḷatē nallatu, vallatu eṉpārum uḷar.
tamiḻettuc cīrtiruttam kūṟuvōr kālattukkēṟpat tamiḻ peruvaḷarcciyuṟa vēṇṭum. kuḻantikaṭku eḻuttuc cuvi kuṟintu tamiḻ eḷimiyāka vēṇṭum. kalvi perukik kallāmi oḻiya vēṇṭum. taṭṭaccu variyaccu mutalāṉa putupputup poṟiyilukkēṟpa eḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷ curuṅkavēṇṭum. ulaka muḻutum tamiḻ parava vēṇṭum eṉṟa naṉṉōkkam koṇṭavarkaḷē. yārtām innalluḷḷam tamiḻukku illātavarkaḷ? eṉavē nalluḷḷam īṇṭu aiyap poruḷilli. eḻuttuk karuttukkōḷkaḷi yellām taṉipporuḷākat tarukkippataṟku itu muḻu viṭamaṉṟu uḷḷatu kāppataṟkum varuṅkālap peruvaḷarccikkum eṉṟu muḷatamiḻukkum paṇput tamiḻiṉattukkum ulakat tamiḻp parappukkum nallaṉa eṉṟu yāṉ urāyntu kaṇṭa cila muṭipukaḷi eḻuti vippēṉ. itu maṟṟoṉṟu virittalaṉṟu; uṟṟoṉṟu kūṟutalām.
irunūṟāṇṭukaṭku mēlākat tamiḻmoḻikku aṭittaḷattil viḷinta vērkkēṭukaḷ palappala. ayalaracu māṟṟakkaḷāl tamiḻmoḻi vaḻakkilum iḷaṅkalviyilum aḻivum iḻivum uṟṟatu. āṅkila āṭciyil makkaḷuṇarvu āṅkila moḻimēl ōṅki vaḷarntatu. virittup payaṉeṉ? tamiḻmakkaṭkut tamiḻiḻeḻutteṉṉa, tamiḻē cumiyākat tōṉṟiviṭṭatu. illaṟap peṇṇukkuk karu cumiyeṉṟāl yātu ceyyamuṭiyum? pallāyiram āṇṭākat tamiḻ māntar eṇṇiyaṟiyāta eḻuttuc cumi eṉṟa pōliyuṇarvu innūṟṟāṇṭil eḻuntataṟkuk kāraṇam tamiḻmoḻi kuṟṟuyirāṉatu maṭṭumaṉṟu; āṅkila mutalāṉa piṟamoḻikaḷiṉ aḻuttamāṉa mayalāṉa ātikkamum ām. oru tamiḻkkuḻanti tamiḻ kaṟkum nērattiyum āṅkilam kaṟkum nērattiyum tamiḻuṇarviyum āṅkilavuṇarviyum kaṇittuppārttāl, tamiḻil evvaḷavu eḻuttut tiruttamum eṉṉa tiruttamum uḻuttuppōm eṉpatu veḷḷiṭimali. ikkoṭum pōkku ennilit tamiḻariṭattum taṭittuviṭṭatu. eṉavē eḻuttuc cumiyeṉpatu poyyāṉa oru tōṟṟam. ikkaruttu tamiḻmoḻikku maṭṭumaṉṟu; intiya nāṭṭiṉ palamoḻikkum ēṟkum. āṅkilattiṉ 26 eḻuttukku nālvaki vaṭivam illiyā?
tamiḻmoḻi varalāṟṟil eḻutturuvaṅkaḷ māṟivantuḷa eṉpataṉi nāṉum aṟivēṉ. orumoḻiyil coṟkaḷ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ naṭikaḷ māṟṟamuṟuvatu pōla vaṭivaṅkaḷum māṟṟam peṟuvatu iyalpē. oru nalluṭampu nāḷtōṟum kaṇṇukkut tōṉṟāmal vaḷarum iyalpu vaḷarcciyē māṟṟam eṉappaṭum. atupōl moḻiyiṉ pala kūṟukaḷum vaḷarcciyeṉṉum māṟutalkaḷ peṟuvatu poruḷiṉ iyaṟki. avvāṟutāṉ eḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷ ciṟitākavum nuṇṇitākavum, vēṟṟumiyuṇarvillāmalum māṟi vantuḷa. ataṉāl eḻutappaṭṭa ilakkiyaṅkaḷum piṟa paṉuvalkaḷum alimāṟuvatupōl māṟṟam tōṉṟāmalē uḷmāṟṟam peṟṟaṉa.
itukāṟum tamiḻvaṭivaṉkaḷ māṟi varavilliyā eṉṟu viṉavum eḻuttut tiruttikaḷ māṟṟam eṉpatu tōṉṟāmal iyalpiṉum iyalpāka māṟi vantirukkum pāṅkiṉiyum. ataṉāl ellā ilakkiyaṅkaḷum ūṟupaṭā vāḻviyum karutip pārkkavilli. kaṭṭāya māṟṟam ivar tam vitiyākum. vaṭivaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟiyoḻittup paḻiyavum putiyavumāṉa 30 kuṟikaḷuḷ tamiḻmoḻiyi aṭakkiviṭalām eṉṟāl, aṭakkiviṭalām. āṉāl itukāṟum vanta ilakkiyaṅkaḷum nallaṭakkam eytāvā? ipputiya tiruttat tāl eḻutappaṭṭa nūlē tamiḻ eṉṟa mayakkap pōkkukaḷ ēṟpaṭāvā? muṟiyāṉa tamiḻkkalviyum tamiḻk kalvikkuriya cūḻniliyum iṉṟupōlavē iḻivāka iruntāl ipputuveḻuttuk kaḷiṉ vāḻvutāṉ eṅkē? mēlum tamiḻ varalāṟē cilaveḻuttukku muntiyanūl, cila veḻuttukku pintiya nūl eṉṟu iruperum piḷavupaṭṭu, muṟkālattu neruppālum nīrālum palatokuti aḻintamipōla, eḻuttālum iṉṟuḷḷaṉavum  aḻintoḻiyāvā? itaṉi niṉikkum pōtu neñcam kulikiṉṟatu. uḷḷa nūlkaḷi yellām āṇiyeḻuttil koṇṭuvantu viṭuvōm eṉṟa nayappukku yār uṟuti? ituvari coṉṉatupōtum pōtum. ēṭṭuc cuvaṭikaḷum kalveṭṭukkaḷum patippumukam peṟā niliyi iṉṟum kāṇkiṉṟōmē?
paḻiya eḻuttumarapil uḷḷa tamiḻ nūlkaḷ ellām iṉi varuṅkāla makkaḷ vaḷarccikku vēṇṭiyatilli; avi paḻaṅkoḷkikaḷ eṉṟu orēyaṭiyākat tūkkiyeṟintu perumi koḷḷum āṇavamē eḻuttut tiruttattāl viḷiyum viṉi eṉṟu añcukiṉṟēṉ. anta eṇṇam uṭiya valiya oru cārarum illāmal illi iṉṟuḷḷa eḻuttu vaṭivilēyē immaṉappōkki kāṇumpōtu, vēṟu evvāṟu eṇṇam tōṉṟum? `uḷḷatu citippōr uḷareṉap paṭāar’ eṉṟa caṅkamoḻi nam vaḻikāṭṭiyākuka. nāṭu viṭutali peṟṟup pallāṇṭu ceṉṟapiṉ, iṉṟu maḻaliyar paḷḷiyellām tamiḻ vitikuḻikaḷāka illāmal putikuḻikaḷāka irukkum avalattip pārkkumpōtu, tamiḻil tavaṟi oru coṟcoṉṉālum tamiḻk kuḻanti kaḷiṭam taṇṭakkācu vāṅkum paḷḷikaḷ tamiḻmānilattil koṭikaṭṭi vaḷarumpōtu, eḻuttuc cīrtiruttam pēcuvār mutaṟkaṇpēca vēṇṭiyatu tamiḻaṉṟō. tamiḻ vippaṉṟō, tamiḻ kalvimoḻiyātalaṉṟō? ivvuṇmi maṉam patiyumāka.
kālattiṟ kāṇappeṟum poṟikaṭku ēṟpat tamiḻvaṭivaṅkaḷi amittukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. atutāṉ vaḷarcci. iṉṟēl tamiḻ piṉṉaṭiyum eṉṟa kōṭpāṭu aṟivuṭimiyaṉṟu. toḻilnuṭpaṅkaḷ mikavirivil paḻimiyāki varukiṉṟaṉa. poṟikaḷ virintu māṟṟam peṟukiṉṟaṉa. pattāṇṭaḷavil poṟi māṟṟaṅkaḷ talikīḻāka vantuviṭak kāṇkiṉṟōm. miṉṉaṇuviyalum kaṇippāṉum ceyyum māṟutalkaḷ purāṇattiyum viñcukiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē makkaḷiṉattukku uriya potuvuṭimiyum taṉṉikarillāk karuviyumāṉa moḻiyik kālattuviḻum karuvikaḷiṭam oppaṭittaṟkilli. ceypoṟikaḷ māṟuntōṟum eḻutturuvaṅkaḷik māṟṟikkoṇṭu pōvatu. aṟivākumā? aṟiviyalākumā?
moḻi nōkkic ceypoṟikaḷ varavēṇṭum. kāṇappaṭa vēṇṭumēyaṉṟi, ceypoṟikku moḻiyaṭimip paṭakkūṭātu. ippōkku ceypoṟivīḻccipōla, ceyyeḻuttum vīḻcciyāki, palkālappiḷavu ēṟpaṭṭu aṟivut toṭarcciyi aṟuttuviṭum. toḻilnuṭpam iṉṉum vaḷaruṅkāl iṉṟiya tamiḻ eḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷ appaṭiyē eḷimi ceyyum putuppoṟikaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikka muṭiyum eṉpatu eṉ nampikki. evvāṟāyiṉum moḻi  eṉṟa nilik karuviyik kālantōṟum caṭutiyil māṟum niliyillāc ceypoṟikaṭku vaṇakkakkūṭātu. vaṇakkiṉ, uṉṉālē nāṉ keṭṭēṉ eṉṉālē nī keṭṭāy eṉṟa katiyākkiviṭum. muḻutum nikaḻkālak kaṇkoṇṭu moḻit tiruttam colvatu oruvakik kuruṭu eṉa viṭuka.
muṟkālattu moḻivāyil kēḷvic celvamāka iruntatu. cila nūṟṟāṇṭāka eḻuttuvāyil pēriṭam peṟṟu varukiṉṟatu. accuppoṟi palavakiyil kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa piṉ eḻutappaṭikkat terivatē kalviyeṉṟa nili patintu viṭṭatu. moḻikku oliyaṉ uḷḷuṟuppākavum vaṭivaṉ puṟavuṟuppākavum mutaṉmiyāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa ātaliṉ eḻutturuvaṅkaḷip putupputup poṟikaṭkēṟpat tirittal eṉpatu ituvari tōṉṟiya nūlkaḷip paḻam poruṭ kāṭciyākkuvatōṭu, ōraḷavu kaṟṟāriyum kallātārākki viṭum. tiruntāta cila nāṭōṭi moḻikaṭku eḻuttukkaḷi iṟṟi moḻiyiyalār vaṭittuk koṭuppatu pōla ñālat tāyāṉa tamiḻiyum karutiviṭṭaṉarē eḻuttuttiruttikaḷ. ēḻiyeḷiyavarkaṭkum iḷaṅ kuḻantikkum iraykuvārpōla eḻuttut tiṇippikaḷ ceyyum muṉippu aḻi kolliyāki viṭum. cevveḻuttil irupattintu variyuḷḷavi tiṇippeḻuttil muppattiraṇṭu varikaḷākiṉṟaṉa eṉṟa peruṅkuṟiyālum ōraḷavu kaṟṟataṟkuṟit tamiḻarkaḷum ivveḻuttāl kallārāki viṭuvar eṉṟa nallaccattālum āṇiyeḻuttit tamiḻitaḻkaḷ naṭimuṟiyiṟ piṉpaṟṟa villi.
āṅkilatti muṉmātiriyāka vittu eḻuttut tiruttikaḷ tamiḻvaṭivaṅkaḷip putukka niṉikkiṉṟaṉar. uyirmey eṉṟa iṉamē vēṇṭā eṉṟa taṭippukku ivvātikkam oru piṉṉaṇi. eḻuttuttiruttattāl āṅkilam pōl tamiḻ vaḷarntu viṭum eṉpatu iṉṉōr eṇṇam pōlum, eṇṇam paḻutilli; ceymuṟiyē paḻutu.
ōṅkivaḷar āṅkilam moḻiyamippil oliyāṉum vaṭivāṉum olippāṉum muṉṉoṭṭup piṉṉoṭṭālum viṉiyoṭṭāṉum kālavuṟuppukkaḷāṉum orumi paṉmiyāṉum palvēṟu kuṟiyuṭiyatu; oḻuṅkaṟṟa moḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟu eṉpatu; moḻiyiyalār oppiya karuttu. `oliyaṉukkotta vaṭivaṉ eṇṇikkiyilli eṉpatum veḷippaṭṭa ceyti, pireñcu moḻiyaḷavu illāviṭiṉum āṅkilattil colliṉ eḻuttu vēṟu, ataṉi olikkum nili vēṟu; aḵtāvatu eḻutiyati olikkamuṭiyātu; olippataṟkuttaṉip payiṟci vēṇṭum. āṅkilavakarātikaḷ ippayiṟcikku mutaṉmi koṭukkiṉṟaṉa.
intiya moḻikaḷilum ciṟappākat tamiḻmoḻiyilum eḻutaṟkum olittaṟkum vēṟupāṭilli kiṭantāṅku eḻuttik kūṭṭuvatē oliyākiviṭum. kiyakala variccuvaṭi yaṭṭiyit terintukoḷḷa oru kiḻami pōm. terintu koṇṭāl tolkāppiyattiyē eḻuttukkūṭṭip paṭikkalāmē. itaṟkumēl ōr eḷimiyuṇṭā? airōppiya iṉattic cārnta vaṭamoḻiyum piṟa intiya āriya moḻikaḷum tamiḻ marapiṉavē.
itaṉi iṅkuk kūṟuvataṉ karutteṉṉa? kuṟipāṭu niṟintatu eṉac cuṭṭap peṟum ulaka āṅkilam kuṟipāṭukaḷi nīkki ulaka moḻiyāyiṟṟā? āṅkilattil cila tiruttattukku āṅkoṉṟu īṅkoṉṟumākac ceyta muyaṟciyum celavum eṉṉāyiṉa? āṅkilattār tam uḷḷa vaṭivaṅkaṭku accup poṟi vaki kaṇṭaṉarēyaṉṟi ivvakikkāka vaṭivaṅkaḷic cetukkavilli. vaḻivaḻivanta cevvaṭivaṅkaḷik kālattu vīḻum poṟikaṭkeṉa māṟṟavilli. uṭalukku uṭiyā, uṭikku uṭalā? kaṇṇukku āṭiyā? āṭikkuk kaṇṇā? kālukku naṭiyaṉā, naṭiyaṉukku kālā? etaṟku etu, ataṟku eppaṭi eṉṟa vāypāṭu teḷivāka vēṇṭum.
oppiṭṭu oppiṭṭup pārttāl vaḷarntōṅkiya moḻikaḷ kūṭap pala kuṟipāṭukaḷ uṭiyaṉavākavē tōṉṟum. e o eṉṟa kuṟṟuyir iraṇṭum illāta moḻikaḷum palavuḷa. pala oliyaṉkaḷukkuc cilavaṭivaṉkaḷi vittuk koṇṭu carikkaṭṭi vaḷarum moḻikaḷum palavuḷa. āṅkilattil paṉṉūṟāyiram col paṭittālum ovvoṉṟiyum eppaṭi olippatu, eppaṭi eḻutuvatu eṉṟu taṉittaṉiyāṉa ciṟappuppayiṟci kaṭicivari vēṇṭum. epporuḷukkup periya eḻuttuppōṭuvatu eṉṟa niṉivu vēṇṭum. āṅkilattil 104 eḻuttukkaḷ toṭakkattilēyē variya vēṇṭum. urōma eṇkaḷiṉ amippu (āūāōṭuaiē - 1944) piṉmuṉ nōkkiyatallavā? intuṇik kuṟi pāṭuḷḷum āṟṟal vāynta oḻuṅkiyal moḻitōṟum uṇṭu eṉpati uṇarvōmāka. ammoḻi vaḷarvataṟku anta uḷḷoḻuṅku marapu iṭaṅkoṭuttuk koṇṭiruk kiṉṟatu eṉpataṉiyum uṇarvōmāka.
moḻikaḷiṭi oliyaṉ eṇṇikkiyum variyaṉ eṇṇikkiyum vēṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. coṟcērkkiyum toṭaramippum vēṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. evvaḷavu vēṟum māṟum eṉṟu tōṉṟiṉālum kuḻantikaḷum kallārum cevivaḻip payiṉṟu tārāḷamākap pēcukiṉṟaṉar. uḷḷē oru marapu vāypāṭu kiṭakkiṉṟatu eṉpatu teḷivu. ātaliṉ nam aḷavukōlum aḷakkumuṟiyum iṉṉum collappōṉāl marapāḻum aṟiyāmiyumē kuṟiyuṭiyaṉa. vaḷarnta cemmoḻi kaḷi avaṟṟiṉ marapaḻuttam kaṇṭu attaṭaṅkaḷil putiya pāttipiṭittu vaḷarkka vēṇṭum. māṟṟamum tiripum iyalpukku viṭṭuviṭa vēṇṭum.

āṅkilamoḻi ulaka moḻiyākap parantōṅki yirukkum vāyppu neṟikaḷit tamiḻpparappukku nām kaṭippiṭikkak kaṭamippaṭṭirukkiṉṟōm. evvaki moḻiyum amippiṉ ciṟappālum poṟikkaruviyiṉ tuṇiyālum ulaka moḻiyāki viṭavilli. āṭcimoḻi yākavum kalvimoḻiyākavum araṅku moḻiyākavum ceytimoḻiyākavum nūlmoḻiyākavum ellāt tuṟiyilum āṅkila vāyilkaḷ virintaṉa. āṅkilavāṭci perukiya piṟatici nāṭukaḷilum āṅkilamē mēṉmoḻi yāyiṟṟu. ciṟiya nāṭṭukkuriyatāka irunta āṅkilam ñālamoḻiyāṉataṟkup perunāṭāṉa aṭimippāratam peruṅ kāraṇamākum. ciṟanta tol palamoḻikaḷik koṇṭa intiya makkaḷ tattam vaḷañcāṉṟa tāymoḻiyi ellā vaḻiyālum nalkuravuppaṭutti, āṅkilattiyē nāṭi vaḷarttatum āṅkilam ulaka moḻiyāṉataṟkup peruvāyp pāyiṟṟu. itu irunūṟāṇṭukkati. ikkatiyi virittup payaṉilli.
āṅkilēyariṭamiruntu nām nāṭṭurimi mīṭṭōm; eṉiṉum nam moḻikaḷ nammiṭaruntu urimi peṟa muṭiya villi. viṭutali peṟṟu muppatteṭṭāṇṭukaḷ ākiyum nam patiṉāṉku moḻikaḷil oṉṟukūṭa āṅkilattukkup pakkamāka vaḷaravilli. nam nāṭṭuppaṟṟu aiyakō ittakittu. aṅṅaṉam āṅkilatti vaḷarttuviṭṭa intiyarkaḷuḷ orupakutiyāṉa tamiḻarkaḷ āṅkilam vaḷarnta ceyal neṟikaḷip pārāmal, tamiḻmoḻiyi āṅkila amippukkuc cetukki otukkip pitukki koṇṭuvara muṉikiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻukkēṟṟa arumpoṟikaḷ kāṇa aṟiviyal muṉippuk koḷḷāmal, elikkūṇṭiṟkuḷ puliyi aṭakkamuyalvatupōla, etaṟkō kaṇṭa accuppatippup poṟikaḷuḷ tamiḻiṉ iyaṟkiyi oṭukkat tuṭikkiṉṟaṉar. ataṟkumēl itutāṉ vaḷarcci eṉṟu aṭāta vāḻttuk kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. teḷintu colliṉ ippōk kellām kiviṭa vēṇṭiyavi eṉpatu eṉ karuttu.
cintuveḷi nākarikattukku muṟpaṭṭē eḻuttuvaṭivu peṟṟut tiṇmi eytiya nam tamiḻukkuc conta eḻuttē yilli; ituvum oru moḻiyā eṉṟu piṟkālattu vaciyuṇṭākumpaṭi ippōkkuk koṇṭupōyviṭumō eṉṟu kavalkiṉṟēṉ. tamiḻ taṉimoḻi. emmoḻiyiliruntum piṟavāta taṉimoḻi, pala moḻikaḷit tōṟṟiya tāymoḻi, cevili moḻi eṉṟa uṇmip perumi aṇmik kālattuttāṉ putiporuḷ pōla veḷippaṭṭu varum innaṉṉiliyil matippiḻakkum oru tavaṟu nām ceyyalāmā? taṉmāṉam ceyya iṭaṅkoṭukkumā?
āṭat teriyātavaḷ muṟṟam kōṇal eṉpatu pōlavum, pāṭatteriyātavaḷ aviyōr eṇṇikki kūṭutal eṉpatu pōlavum, ōṭatteriyātavaṉ uḷḷūr eṉpatu pōlavum cilariṉ tiruttamuyaṟcikaḷ namakkut tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. vallavaṉukkup pullum āyutam eṉpataṉi āṅkila moḻiyuṇartti viṭṭatu. ātaliṉ tamiḻ eḻuttu varivaṭivu paṟṟip piṉvarum teḷivukaḷiyum uṟutikaḷiyum ceyaṟpāṭukaḷiyum nām koḷvōmāka.
(1) uḷḷa eḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷi nilipēṟāka vittu ataṟkēṟṟa accuppoṟikaḷi nuṇṇiya aṟiviyal muṟiyil kāṇa vēṇṭum. virintu kālakkōṭpaṭṭu māṟum poṟikaṭku niliyāṉa tamiḻmoḻiyi aṭimippaṭuttak kūṭātu. muṉpōla iyalpāṉa vaḻakkāṟṟil varum māṟutalkaḷ vantu pōkaṭṭum valinta iyakkamāṉa eḻuttuppuraṭṭu vēṇṭā. aṅṅaṉam ceyvataṟkut tamiḻ putiya moḻiyō tiruntā moḻiyōyaṉṟu. eḻuttuvaṭivaṅkaḷ mutalāvatākak kiyeḻuttukkum eḷitāka irukkavēṇṭum eṉṟa potu niliyip pōṟṟa vēṇṭum. poṟivaḻakkāṟṟiṉum kivaḻakkāṟē moḻikku uyirppuṭiyatu.
(2) tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ oru cumiyalla. itu uṇmi, veṟum mayakkaṉṟu. vaḻivaḻi vanta tamiḻkkalvi muṟippaṭi kuḻantikaṭkuc collik koṭuttāl ellām eḷimiyē. taṭamvāynta varivaṭivaṅkaḷit tiruttattirunta avi kuḻantikaṭkup paḻiyavaṟṟōṭu cērntu kūṭutalāki viṭukiṉṟaṉa. itu muraṇāṉa viḷivu. vīṭṭil tamiḻ, pālar paḷḷiyil tamiḻ, palkalikkaḻakam vari tamiḻ, āṭciyil tamiḻ, kōyilil tamiḻ, eṉṟa iciyil tamiḻ, kaṭiyil tamiḻ, uriya aṉittilum tamiḻ eṉṟa uriminili tamiḻukkut tamiḻakattu irukka vēṇṭum; itu iruntuviṭumāṉāl tamiḻ pōla iṉiya eḷiya moḻi tamiḻarukku vēṟoṉṟilli.
eḻuttut tiruttam kuṟitta ōru kālacceytiyi ittuṇi mukamākac colliyataṟkuk kāraṇam. tamiḻum tamiḻkkalviyum tamiḻkkāppum vaḻivaḻit toṭarcciyum iruntāṟṟāṉē tolkāppiyam caṅkavilakkiyam tirukkuṟaḷ cilappatikāram pāratiyam mutalāka ellām vāḻum; tamiḻiṉattukku naṉmatippum etirkālat taṉittaṉmiyum uṇṭākum. ātaliṉ kālattiṟkēṟṟa ivvuriyil tamiḻ eḻuttu vaṭivam paṟṟit tamiḻ makkaṭkut teḷivu ceyvatu eṉ ceṅkaṭamiyāyiṟṟa.
ceytakka valla ceyakkeṭum ceytakka
ceyyāmi yāṉum keṭum
eṉṟār poyyil pulavar. eḻuttut tiruttam pōṉṟavi tamiḻukkuc ceyyat takātavi; eṅkum ellām mutaliṭam eṉpavi tamiḻukkuc ceyyat takkavi.
palvēṟu olinilikkūṟukaḷiyum cila aṟiviyal nuṭpaṅkaḷiyum mayakkamaṟakkāṭṭutaṟku avvappōtu nuṇkuṟiyīṭukaḷ vēṇṭappaṭum. ivi uyarkalvikkum uyarāyvukkum vēṇṭiya ciṟappukkuṟikaḷ; varumiṭaṅkaḷum payaṉkaḷum vēṟāṉavi. ivi nām mēlē kūṟiya eḻuttu vaḻakkāṟalla; aṟivu vaḻakkāṟu.
nūṟpā 20
`meymmayaṅ kuṭaṉili eṉpataṟku oru mey piṟameyyōṭu mayaṅkutal meymmayakkam; oru mey taṉ meyyōṭu tāṉ mayaṅkutali uṭaṉili mayakkam eṉa iruvakippaṭum eṉṟu poruḷurippa. avvāṟu iruvaki yeṉak kūṟuvatu karuttāyiṉ, “āyiriyala vuyirtta lāṟē” “meyyēyuyireṉa ṟāyī riyala” “āyīriyala puṇarnilic cuṭṭē” eṉṟa naṭi amintirukkum. teriyuṅkāli eṉpataṟku meymmayaṅkuṭaṉili ceyappaṭuporuḷākum.
ammū vāṟum vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅkiṉ
meymmayaṅ kuṭaṉili teriyuṅ kāli
eṉṟa innūṟpāviṉ mutalīraṭikaḷip piṉvarum meymmayakka nūṟpākkaṭ kellām tōṟṟuvāyākak koḷka.
tolkāppiyam
ciṟappuppāyiramum nūṉmarapum māṇikkavuriyum muṟṟum.


iraṇṭāvatu
moḻimarapu - māṇikkavuri
iyalmuṉṉuri
nūṉmarapil eḻuttu niliyil taṉiyeḻuttukkaḷiṉ kuṟiyīṭukaḷ kūṟappeṟṟaṉa. ikkuṟikaḷ curukkamāka āḷutaṟkaminta āṭcic coṟkaḷākum. ivveḻuttukkaḷ poruḷtarum collākic collil mutalilō iṟutiyilō niṟkum nilipaṟṟi moḻimarapu kūṟum. coṉṉiliyil eḻuttukkaḷ paṭum ilakkaṇaṅkūṟutaliṉ moḻimarapu eṉappaṭṭatu. iḵtu eḻuttatikāram ātaliṉ moḻimarapu eṉpataṟkuc collil varum eḻuttukkaḷiṉ marapunili eṉapporuḷ koḷka.
oli, eḻuttu, col eṉpaṉa pañcu, nūliḻi, āṭi pōṉṟavi. arici cōṟākum pōtutāṉ uṇpayaṉ tarum. eḻuttoli eṉṟa oliyaṉukkup poruṇmiyuṇṭu. avvaḷavē. atuvē oru poruḷ kāṭṭātu. oliyaṉ ciṟupāṉmi taṉiyākavum perumpāṉmi eḻuttukkaḷ toṭarntum poruḷi nēraṭiyākak kuṟikkumpōtu col allatu moḻi eṉappaṭum. eḻuttukkum collukkum uriya toṭarpu kurutiyōṭṭamum nīrōṭṭamum okkum. col, moḻi, kiḷavi eṉṟa tamiḻccoṟkaḷ makkaḷ vaḻakkiṟ pēcappaṭum uyirppu moḻiyik kuṟikkum.
eḻuttu col eṉṟa kuṟiyīṭukaḷ mutaṟkaṇ olinilikku uriyavi. ā eṉṟa eḻutati olikka, āṭu eṉṟa collic colluka eṉpatuvē oli mutaṉmarapu. ā eṉṟa eḻutati eḻutuka. āṭu eṉṟa colli eḻutuka eṉpatu piṉ vanta varivaṭiva marapu. immarapum poruntum; eṉiṉum ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ olinilikkē uriyaṉa eṉpati maṟavātu niṉivu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
iyaṟ pakuppukkaḷ tammuḷ toṭarpuṭiyavi. ōriyalukkuk kūṭutalāṉa viḷakkaṅkaḷi aṭutta viyalilō piṉṉiyalkaḷilō āciriyaṉ colluvāṉ. itu malippil neṟi eṉappaṭum. ōriyalil aṉittiyum colliviṭutal eṉpatu uṇṇum uṇavaṉittiyum mutalilēyē iliyiṟ pōṭṭuviṭuvati yokkum.
nūṉmarapiṟ kūṟiya cārpeḻuttukkaḷ, aḷapeṭi, meymmayakkam, varivaṭivu paṟṟiya mēlviḷakkaṅkaḷ moḻimarapiṉ toṭakkattu varuvaṉa.
moḻimarapiṟkē yuriya iṉṟiyamiyāta īrilakkaṇaṅkaḷ moḻi mutaleḻuttukkaḷum moḻiyiṟuti yeḻuttukkaḷum ām. moḻivaralāṟṟiṭi ivaṟṟil cila varavum cila pōkkum ēṟpaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa; ēṟpaṭuvatum iyalpē. eṉiṉum immāṟṟaṅkaḷ talitaṭṭalō taritaṭṭalō vārāmal viḻippākap pōṟṟik koḷḷattakuvaṉa. kari palliṭattu uṭintāl pukuvatum pōvatum etu eṉṟu colla muṭiyumā? ellā moḻikaḷuḷḷum paḻantamiḻmoḻi ulaka vaḻakku aḻintoḻintu citiyāmal cīriḷamit tiṟam kuṉṟāmal iṉṟum putumoḻiyāka vāḻvataṟku uriya kāraṇaṅkaḷuḷ iraṇṭu moḻi mutaleḻuttuk kaṭṭuppāṭum moḻiyiṟutiyeḻuttuk kaṭṭuppāṭum ākum. piṉ iyalkaḷil varampupaṭuttum puṇarcciyellām inta irutaḷaṅkaḷiṉ mēl eḻuvaṉa. ittaḷam taḷariṉ tamiḻ taḷarum. ātaliṉ mutalīṟu eṉṟa amippu tamiḻmoḻikku nalloḻukkamākum; cevviḻuppamumākum; eṉavē immoḻiyuyiriṉic cevvaṉam ōmputal ilakkaṇattiṉ kaṭami.
mutalum iṟutiyum muṭṭātu kākkum
atukāṇ tamiḻiṉ araṇ.
iyaṟkaruttu
kuṟṟiyalikaram `miyā’ eṉa oru moḻiyilum, vīṭiyātu nāṭiyātu eṉap puṇarmoḻiyilum varum. orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram āṟuvalleḻuttiṉ mēl ēṟi, nāṭu, ulaku, vāttu, pañcu, pāmpu, oṉṟu eṉṟavāṟu aṟuvakiyāka varum. cukkukkōṭu eṉap puṇarmoḻiyilum varum eḵku, aḵtu, kaḵcu eṉṟavāṟu orumoḻi āytam kuṟṟeḻuttukkup piṉṉum uyirvalleḻuttukku muṉṉum varum. aḵṟiṇi, paḵṟuḷi eṉṟavāṟu puṇarmoḻiyilum varum. cilaviṭaṅkaḷil āytam moḻikkuṟippāka nīṇṭu eḻutappaṭuvatilli. nūṉmarapiṟ kūṟiya cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟum evvāṟu cārntu tōṉṟum eṉṟa cārpu niliyi moḻimarapil mutaleḻu nūṟpākkaḷ viḷakkuvaṉa.
ici kuṉṟumpōtu aḷapeṭi eṉṟa muṟi taḻuvap paṭumaṉṟē. aṅṅaṉam aḷapu eḻunta oli nīṭṭattik kāṭṭuvataṟku neṭṭeḻuttukkup piṉ ataṟku otta kuṟṟeḻuttu (pōoy) pakkamāka eḻutappaṭum. ai, oḷa eṉṟa iraṇṭu neṭilukkum nēraṭiyāṉa kuṟṟeḻuttu illāmi yāl aikārattukku ikaramum oḷakārattiṟku ukaramum otta kuṟṟeḻuttāka aḷapeṭiyiṟ koḷḷavēṇṭum.
tamiḻccoṟkaḷ ōreḻuttorumoḻi (ā, mā) eṉavum īreḻuttōru moḻi (āṉ,māṉ) eṉavum iraṇṭukku mēṟpaṭṭa eḻuttuṭiya toṭarmoḻi (āṇṭu, āṇṭāḷ, āṇṭavar) eṉavum mūṉṟu vakippaṭum. ippākupāṭu tamiḻccoṟkaḷiṉ tōṟṟattiyum amippiyum ākkattiyum aṟivikkavallatu.
mey mutaleḻuttāyiṉum piṟappil taṉittaṉmi yuṭiya tāyiṉum colluṅkāl ka ṅa ca ña eṉṟu akarattōṭu collappaṭum.
nūṉmarapil iṉṉa meyyeḻuttukkuppiṉ iṉṉa meyyeḻuttu varal vēṇṭum eṉṟa varampiṉik kaṟṟōm. enta meyyeḻuttum taṉṉik kūṟumpōtu etaṟkup piṉṉum varalām eṉṟa kaṭṭuppāṭaṟṟa potu niliyuṇṭu. itu naṭimuṟikkotta ilakkaṇam.
meymmayakkattil pīrkku, vārppu, pāḻṅkiṇaṟu eṉa īroṟṟum varum. ivi ya ra ḻa eṉṟa mūṉṟiṉ piṉṉar tōṉṟum.
tamiḻc collamippiṉpiṭi rakaravoṟṟum ḻakaravoṟṟum taṉikkuṟṟeḻuttukkup piṉ varamāṭṭā. varkkam, varṇaṉi, karppiṇi eṉṟu colvatum eḻutuvatum tavaṟu. varukkam, varuṇaṉi, karuppiṇi eṉpatē takum.
ceyyuḷil `pōṉm eṉṟu īroṟṟu niṟkum. avviṭattu makaravoṟṟu kālmāttiriyāyk kuṉṟi yolikkum.
eḻuttukkaḷic colliṭippaṭuttic coṉṉālum, colliṭi vitantu vēṟākac coṉṉālum, kūṭṭu vaṭivu peṟṟālum tam taṉmiyiṉiṉṟu māṟupaṭā. taṉiyeḻuttāka iruntapōtu eṉṉa taṉmiyuṇṭō attaṉmi vantāṉ iruntāṉ ceṉṟāṉ eṉṟu eḻuttukkūṭṭic colvataṟkāka māṟiviṭātu. vaṭivu māṟupaṭṭālum poruḷ vēṟupaṭātu. itu eḻuttiyal eṉappaṭum. itu moḻiyiṉ nili viti. ai eṉṟa oliyaṉukkut taṉivarivaṭivu uṇṭu. ai ay eṉṟa kūṭṭu vaṭivukaḷum uṇṭu. ai ay eṉpaṉa cilaviṭaṅkaḷil māṟi varum. oḷa oliyaṉukku uriya vaṭivu uṇṭu. au eṉṟa kūṭṭu vaṭivum uṇṭu. ivi vaṭivu mayakkaṅkaḷ. ivaṟṟāl poruḷ māṟupaṭuvatilli. eḻuttiyal tirivatilli.
moḻimarapiṉ mutaṉmiyāṉa ilakkaṇakkūṟukaḷ iraṇṭē. tamiḻil moḻikku mutalākum eḻuttukkaḷ; moḻikku iṟutiyil varum eḻuttukkaḷ.
moḻi mutaleḻuttukkaḷ
itu paṟṟip pattu nūṟpākkaḷ uḷa. 12 uyirkaḷum moḻi mutalāki varum. meykaḷ moḻimutalākum eṉṟālum uyirōṭu kūṭiya uyir meykaḷtām aṅṅaṉam varamuṭiyum. itu tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ maṉṉiya amippu. ayaṉmoḻic coṟkaḷi orukāl taḻuviṉālum, ivvamippukku uṭpaṭṭē tiyāṉam, niyāyam, kirupi eṉṟu varavēṇṭum.
mutalāku meykaḷukkum avipeṟum uyirkaḷukkum varampuṇṭu. patiṉeṭṭu meykaḷuḷ ka ca ta pa eṉa valliṉattil nāṉkum ña na ma eṉa melliṉattil mūṉṟum ya va eṉa iṭiyiṉattil iraṇṭum āka oṉpatu meykaḷ moḻi mutalākum. ivaṟṟuḷ,
12 uyiroṭum varuvaṉa 		k t n p m 
9 a ai oḷa nīṅka 			c
8 u ū o ō nīṅka 			v
3 ā e o cērntu 			ñ
1 ā cērntu	 			y
meymmayakkattiṟkuc colliyatupōla mutalākā eṉappaṭṭa ellā veḻuttukkaḷumē taṉṉiccuṭṭum pōtu (ṇo, ḷo, ṭo eṉṟavāṟu) moḻimutalāka varum eṉpatu naṭimuṟikku otta potuviti. ituvē moḻiyiṟutiyākavum koḷḷappaṭum.
nunti, nuntāy eṉa moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram oṉṟu uṇṭu. itaṟkum nakara meyyē mutal.
moḻiyiṟutiyeḻuttukkaḷ
itu paṟṟip patiṉāṉku nūṟpā uṇṭu.
oḷakāram nīṅkiya patiṉōruyirum iṟutiyāka varum. oḷakāramum ku veḷa eṉa ōreḻuttorumoḻikaḷil īṟākum. ekara vuyir īṟākātu. no eṉṟa ōriṭattu okaram īṟām. ñē ñō eṉa ñakaram īrāka vārātu. ucu, mucu eṉa īriṭattuc cukaram īṟām. tapu eṉṟa oru collil pukaram īrāki iruniliyil aḻuttam peṟum. patiṉeṭṭu meykaḷuḷ ña ṇa na ma ṉa eṉa melliṉattuḷ aintu ya ra la va ḻa ḷa eṉa iṭiyiṉam āṟu ākap patiṉoru puḷḷiyum moḻikku īṟāka varum. verin, porun eṉa irucoṟkaḷil nakaramey īṟām. uriñ eṉṟu ñakaram oru collil īṟām āṉāl iruporuḷ aḻuttam peṟuvatilli. aviv uv tev eṉa vakaram nāṉku coṟkaḷil kaṭiciyāka varum.
aḵṟiṇip peyarkaḷil ṉakarameykku īṟāka makaramey (puṟaṉ - puṟam) vantuniṟkum; eṉiṉum oṉpatu coṟkaḷ vilakkākum.
orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalikaram
(31)	kuṟṟiya likaram niṟṟal vēṇṭuma
	yāveṉ ciṉimici yuriyacik kiḷavik
	kāvayiṉ varūu makara mūrntē.
iḵtu orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalikarattiṉ cārpunili kūṟum.
akalavuri
cārntu varalmarapiṉ eṉṟu nūṉmarapiṉ mutalnūṟ pāviṟ kūṟiṉāraṉṟē. kuṟukutaṟku eccollil etaṉic cārntu varum eṉa uriya iṭaccūḻnili kāṭṭukiṉṟār.
miyā eṉṟa uriyaciyiṭic col oṉṟu uṇṭu. itaṉ kaṇ yā eṉṟa eḻuttukku mēlē avviṭattu makarameyyic cārntu kuṟṟiyalikaram naṭuvē niṟkum.
uri - pēccu vaḻakku. niṟṟal vēṇṭum - iṭippaṭṭu naṭuvē niṟka vēṇṭum.
vaḻakku: miyā - itu pēccu vaḻakkiṭi muṉṉiliyil varum ēvaṟ col. kēṇmiyā, coṉmiyā, kēḷappā, collappā eṉpatu poruḷ.
tiṟaṉuri
nūṟpāvil eṭuttukkāṭṭē tantu ilakkaṇam viḷakkum nūṟpā itu. itu kāṭṭuneṟi eṉappaṭum. ākārattōṭu kūṭiya yakaravuyirmeykkum makaravoṟṟukkum iṭippaṭṭu nerukkuṇṇumpōtu ikaram arimāttiriyākak kuṟukum eṉpatu cūḻviti. yā eṉṟa ākāravuyirmey muḻutum piṉpaṟṟukkōṭu; oṟṟu muṉpaṟṟukkōṭu eṉka. ivvitiyi yoṭṭi viyāḻaṉ eṉṟa collil ikaram ikkālattuk kuṟukuvati uṇaralām. viyāti, viyācam, tiyāṉam, piyāṇṭu vāttiyam eṉṟa piṟamoḻi varukiyilum iccūḻniliyāl ikaram kuṟukuvatik kāṇka. eṉavē iccūḻviti ulakamoḻip potuviti pōlum.
coṟku uṟuppāka varum olikaḷic ciṉi eṉpatu oru kuṟiyīṭu. kuṟiyataṉ iṟutic ciṉi eṉa uyir mayaṅkiyalilum cuṭṭuc cuṉi nīṭiya ‘īṟuciṉiyoḻiya eṉa’ īriṭattuk kuṟṟiyalukarappuṇariyalilum ikkuṟiyāṭci varum.
`kuṟṟiyalikaram niṟṟal vēṇṭum’ eṉṟa oru talinaṭi ippaṭic cārpu iruntāṟṟāṉ ikaram kuṟukum eṉṟa vaṉpuṟiyākum. miyā miyā eṉṟu varum pūṉiyiṉ poruḷil olikkuṟippi nīkkutaṟku. uriyacik kiḷavi eṉap paṭṭatu. payaṉiliyi muṉṉum eccattip piṉṉum kūṟiya naṭiyōṭṭam itu. ilakkiyampōl tolkāppiyattu ivvāṟu naṭiyōṭṭaṅkaḷ palavuḷa.
miyā eṉṟa uriyacic col piṉṉāḷ vaḻakku vīḻntatu.
puṇarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalikaram
(32)	puṇariyal niliyiṭik kuṟukalu murittē
	uṇarak kūṟiṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟum.
itu kuṟṟiyalikaram irumoḻippuṇarcciyiṭik kuṟukum eṉakkūṟum.
akalavuri
kuṟṟiyalikaram miyā eṉpatu pōl oru moḻiyiṟ kuṟukutalōṭu irucoṟpuṇarcciyil kuṟukutalum uṇṭu. moḻimarapu oru collaḷaviṟkuk kūṟum iyalātaliṉ īṇṭu virinturippatu poruntātu. ippuṇarmoḻik kuṟukkam piṉpu kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyalil veḷippaṭum.
vaḻakku: nāṭu + yātu - nāṭiyātu; mācu + yātu - māciyātu
tiṟaṉuri
cārpeḻuttukkāṉa kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, āytameṉṟa mūṉṟum oru collilum varum; puṇarmoḻi yilum varum. moḻimarapu oru collil varum eḻuttukkaṭku ilakkaṇam kūṟum iyalpiṟṟu ātaliṉ puṇarmoḻiyuḷ cārpeḻutta varuvaṉa paṟṟi avi uṇṭu eṉa īṇṭuc cuṭṭiṉārēyaṉṟi iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum viritturikka villi tolkāppiyar. `uṇarak kūṟiṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟum eṉap puṇarmoḻik kuṟṟiya likarattukkum, `kaṭappāṭaṟinta puṇariyalāṉa’ eṉap puṇarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukarattukkum, `īriyal maruṅkiṉum’ eṉap puṇarmoḻi āytattukkum uriya vēṟiyalkaḷiṟ kāṇka eṉṟār āciriyar. ivvāṟu ilakkaṇat teḷivupaṭa nūṉmuṟi vakuttiruppatiyē `mayaṅkāmarapiṉ eḻuttumuṟi kāṭṭi’ eṉap pāyiram pārāṭṭukiṉṟatu.
“yakaram varuvaḻi yikaraṅ kuṟukum
  ukarak kiḷavi tuvarat tōṉṟātu” 
eṉavarum
kuṟṟiyalukara nūṟpāvittāṉ `uṇarakkūṟiṉ piṉṉart tōṉṟum’ eṉpatu cuṭṭum; eṉavē oru moḻik kuṟṟiyalukāc coṟkaḷ nilimoḻiyāka niṟka varumoḻi yakaram yakaram puṇarumpōtu kuṟukiya ikaram tōṉṟum eṉa aṟikiṉṟōm. yakaram varumoḻi eṉṟu kūṟiṉālum, `āvōṭavallatu yakaram mutalātu’ eṉṟapaṭi, yā eṉṟa ākāravuyir meyyē koḷḷappaṭum.
orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalikarattiṟkum puṇarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalikarattiṟkum cūḻniliyil ōr oṟṟumiyuṇṭu. miyā nāṭiyātu eṉa īriṭattum yā eṉṟa olikkumuṉ ikaram kuṟukukiṉṟatu.
muṉṉar eṉpatu etirkālattik kuṟikkum. muṟpōkku eṉpatu pōla, `puṇariyal niliyiṭik kuṟukalum urittē eṉṟa ummikkuk kuṟukāmalum varum eṉpatu poruḷaṉṟu. oru moḻiyilēyaṉṟik kuṟṟiyalukaram puṇarmoḻiyilum kuṟurum eṉṟa eccavummiyākum. `iṭaṉumāruṇṭē eṉṟu varum nūṟpā naṭikkum ituvē poruḷ.
īṇṭu orunal viḷakkam
nāṭiyatu eṉṟa puṇarmoḻi nāṭu + yātu eṉavum nāṭi + yātu eṉavum pirippataṟku iṭaṉākiṉṟatu. vēṟupāṭu kāṇpatu eppaṭi? nāṭu eṉpatu nilimoḻiyāyiṉ avviṭattu ikaram kuṟṟiyalikaramāy arimāttiriyāy olikkum. eḻuttuvaṭivil nāṭiyātu eṉa ikarattiṉ mēl puḷḷipeṟum. nāṭi eṉpatu nili moḻiyāyiṉ ikaram kuṟṟukaramāy oru māttiri icikkum. nāṭi yātu eṉa eḻuttu vaṭivil puḷḷipeṟātu. emmoḻiyilum ittakiya mayakkaṅkaṭku iṭaṉuṇṭeṉiṉum pēccuc cūḻnili nōkkip poruḷtāṉē viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum. māṭiyātu vaṇṭi yātu, kāciyātu, kokkiyātu eṉpaṉavum attaṉmiyaṉa. tūciyātu eṉṟa puṇarcciyil tūcum tūciyum oru poruḷaṉavātaliṉ ilakkaṇattāl poruḷ vēṟupaṭavilli eṉa aṟika.
ivvilakkaṇam piṟkālattu vaḻakku vīḻntatu.
orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram
(33) 	neṭṭeḻut timparum toṭarmoḻi yīṟṟum
	kuṟṟiya lukaram vallā ṟūrntē.
iḵtu orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukarattiṉ cārpunili kūṟum.
akalavuri
`cārntuvaral marapiṉ eṉṟa nūṟpāviṟ kūṟiya varicippaṭi kuṟṟiyalukaram kuṟukutaṟku evaṟṟic cārntuvarum eṉakkāṭṭukiṉṟār. kuṟṟiyalukaram taṉi neṭṭeḻuttukkup 	piṉ āṟu valleḻuttukkaḷ mēl ēṟivarum. toṭar moḻikku iṟutiyil atupōl āṟu valleḻuttukkaḷic cārntu varum.
impar - piṉpu. toṭarmoḻi - eḻuttukkaḷ toṭarnta oru col vallāṟu - ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟa eṉṟa valliṉam.
vaḻukku: pōku, mācu, āṭu,  ōtu, kipu, āṟu - neṭil
	pōkku: vīccu, āṭṭu, cāttu, kāppu, māṟṟu - vaṉṟoṭar
	aṅku, añcu, aṇṭu, antu, ampu, aṉṟu - meṉṟōṭar
	eytu, mārpu, palku, māḻku, māḷku - iṭittoṭar
	eḵku, kaḵcu, paḵtu - āytattoṭar
	paḻaku, tarācu, taviṭu, pātīṭu, kaḻuku, vallūṟu, colleṭu, nāḷēṭu, niṟipu. - uyirttoṭar.
tiṟaṉuri
kuṟṟiyalukaram, neṭil, uyirttoṭar, vaṉṟoṭar, meṉṟoṭar, iṭittoṭar, āytat toṭar eṉa āṟu vakippaṭum eṉak kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyaṟkaṇ vakiceyvar āciriyar ivvakikkēṟpap puṇarcci vēṟupaṭutali av viyaliṟ kāṇka. moḻimarapu eṉṟa ivviyalil kuṟṟiyalukaram cārpeḻuttākum moḻic cūḻniliyē kūṟuvar.
tolkāppiyattik kaṟpōr `toṭarmoḻi’ eṉṟa kuṟiyīṭṭiṉi mayaṅkātu viḷaṅkikkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. iraṇṭeḻuttukku mēl toṭarntu orucol eṉpatu poruḷ. makaṉ, pāṭṭu, piṇaṅku, vaḷḷuvar, tolkāppiyar iviyellām toṭarmoḻikaḷ ā, vā, pō, tī ivi ōreḻuttorumoḻi eṉappaṭum. aṭi, atu. nām kācu ivi īreḻuttorumoḻi eṉappaṭum. piṟkālattum ikkālattum toṭarmoḻi eṉpatu coṟkaḷ toṭarnta naṭiyik kuṟikkum. citamparaṉār cekkiḻuttār; citamparaṉār ciṟiccāliyil nāṭṭu viṭutalikkākac cekkiḻuttār; pāratiyār ivar perumiyip pāṭiṉār. iviyellām toṭarmoḻi eṉa ikkālattup peyarpeṟukiṉṟaṉa. ivvēṟṟumiyik karuttuṭkoṇṭu tolkāppiyattiṟaṉik kaṟkavēṇṭum. nām iṉṟu toṭarmoḻi eṉpataṉit tolkāppiyar puṇarmoḻi eṉpar. itaṉmēl viḷakkatti `ōreḻuttoru’ moḻi eṉap piṉvarum nūṟpāvuriyiṟ kāṇka.
neṭṭeḻuttimpar eṉpatu nāṭu. pēca, kātu mutaliyavaṟṟik kuṟikkum. ivi  īreḻuttoru moḻikaḷ. toṭarmoḻiyīṟu eṉpatu paṭam. paṇṭu, mārpu, eḵku, pārppu, kūppāṭu mutaliyavaṟṟik kuṟikkum. ivi iraṇṭukkum mēṟpaṭṭa eḻuttuṭic coṟkaḷ. ataṉālaṉṟō orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉappaṭṭaṉa. `neṭṭeḻuttimpar eṉṟataṉāl atu. paṭu, pacu eṉat taṉik kuṟṟeḻuttāl iṟuvaṉa kuṟṟiyalukaram ākā eṉpatum `vallāṟu ūrntu’ eṉpataṉāl tummu, maṇṇu, tāvu, puḷḷu, celavu, varavu eṉpaṉa mellīṟum iṭiyīṟum koḷvatāl kuṟṟiyalukaram ākā eṉpatum teḷiyappaṭum. ātaliṉ innūṟpā vilakkiyuṇarttum naṭiyuṭiyatu eṉakkoḷka.
innūṟpāvil payaṉili illi. niṟṟalvēṇṭum eṉṟa muntikku muntiya nūṟpāt toṭarip payaṉiliyāka māṭṭēṟṟal iyipaṉṟu. tōṉṟā eḻuvāy pōla aritākat tōṉṟāp payaṉiliyum koḷḷalām. naṉṉūlārum pataviyalil `āyīṟiyum īyīṟikaramum’ eṉṟu muṭiyātu muṭittiruttali oppunōkkuka. `ellām maḻi’ eṉṟa tirukkuṟaḷ naṭiyum itaṉpāṟpaṭum. itaṉi avāynili eṉpar ilakkaṇiyar.
iṉṉoru vakiyākavum immuṭipiṉic cintikkalām. maliyāḷattil viṉi muṟṟukkaḷ pālīṟukaḷ peṟuvatilli. paṇṭu tamiḻil vaḻakkilirunta viṉimuṟṟu vaṭivaṅkaḷ piṉpu tamiḻil vaḻakkaṟṟu iṉṟu maliyāḷattil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu kēraḷapāṇiṉīyam eṭuttakkāṭṭiyapaṭi, vallāṟu ūrntu eṉpataṉi pālīṟupeṟā muṟṟākavum karutikkoḷḷa iṭaṉuṇṭu. itu cintaṉikkuriyatu.
iṉṟu evvakic coṟkaḷilum iṟutiyukaram ōraḷavēṉum kuṟukiyolippatik kēṭkiṉṟōm; eṉiṉum puṇarccikkaṇ muṉpōlavē vēṟupāṭukaḷ uḷa.
puṇarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram
(34)	iṭippaṭiṟ kuṟuku miṭaṉumā ruṇṭē
	kaṭappā ṭaṟinta puṇariya lāṉa.
itu kuṟṟiyalukaram irumoḻippuṇarcciyiṭik kuṟukum eṉṟu kūṟum.
akalavuri
orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukarac coṟkaṭku iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum mēlē kūṟiṉār. accoṟkaḷ nilimoḻiyāka varumoḻiyāka puṇarumpōtu ukaram iṭippaṭumallavā? avvāṟu varum ukaram kuṟukum iṭaṅkaḷum uṇṭu; avaṟṟi muṟiyākac colla vēṇṭiya kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyaliṉ kaṇṇē kāṇalām.
iṭippaṭiṉ 	-	naṭuvē variṉ.
kaṭappāṭu	-	collattakum iṭam. 	āṉ - iṭattu.			vaḻakku 
nāṭu ciṟakkum, aḻaku vaḷarum	-	alvaḻittoṭar
nāṭṭuk kaṭami, aḻakuk kali	-	vēṟṟumittoki
tiṟaṉuri
itu moḻimarapātaliṉ oru moḻiyaḷavukkē ilakkaṇam kūṟappaṭum. puṇarmoḻikku uriyaviyalkaḷiṟ kūṟuvatē kaṭappāṭu. ātaliṉ, `kaṭappāṭu aṟinta puṇariyal eṉṟār. aḵtu oṉpatām iyalāṉa kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal ākum.
`allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum
ellā viṟutiyum ukaram niṟiyum
eṉa āṅku varum nūṟpāvē īṇṭuk kuṟikkap peṟuvatu eṉak koḷka.
nāṭu ciṟantatu eṉpatil kuṟṟiyalukaram īṟāka, iruntālum puṇarmoḻiyil naṭuvēyiruttaliṉ `iṭippaṭiṉ eṉappaṭṭatu. 
`iṭaṉumāruṇṭē' eṉpatu arukivaruvatik kuṟikka villi. kuṟṟiyalukaram oru moḻiyil iruppatu pōlap puṇarmoḻivilum uṇṭu eṉpatē poruḷ. iḵtu eccavummi.
orumoḻiyāytam
(35)	kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar āytap puḷḷi
	uyiroṭu puṇarntaval lāṟaṉ micittē.
iḵtu orumoḻiyāy tattiṉ cārpu nilikūṟum.
akalavuri
`cārntu varal marapiṉmūṉṟu' eṉṟa varicippaṭi āytam kūṟukiṉṟār. āytappuḷḷi kuṟṟeḻuttiṟkuppiṉṉē uyirmey valleḻuttukkaḷukku muṉṉē varum.
micintu - iṭappakkamāka aṭuttatu.
vaḻakku: (e.ku) (eḵku) ka.cu (kaḵcu) a.tu (aḵtu) pa. ṟi (paḵṟi)
tiṟaṉuri
āytattiṟku muṉṉum piṉṉum varum irupāṟ cūḻnili kūṟuvatu innūṟpā. itaṉ amippuppaṭi oru moḻiyāytam e.ku (eḵku) ve.kā (veḵkā) eṉa mūṉṟeḻut taḷavil aṭaṅkum. aḵtāvatu iraṇṭiṟanticikkum toṭar moḻi vakiyākum. aṟuvakik kuṟṟiyalukarattuḷ āytat toṭarmoḻiyum oṉṟu eṉpati niṉika.
muṉṉē kuṟṟeḻuttu eṉpataṉāl (e.ku) (eḵku) eṉpatu pōlat taṉikkuṟilākavō ka.cu. (kaḵcu) eṉpatu pōla uyirmeyk kuṟilākavō irukkalām; eṉiṉum ataṟkumuṉ iṉṉoru eḻuttut toṭarvatilli eṉpatu teḷivu.
`uyiroṭu puṇarnta varalāṟu' eṉpataṉāl āytattiṟkup piṉpu enta oṟṟeḻuttum vārātu eṉpatum valleḻuttu variṉum avi uyiroṭu cērnta uyir meyyākavē varum eṉpatum peṟappaṭum. uyiroṭu eṉap potuppaṭak kūṟiyamiyāl ve.kā. (veḵkā) a.ti (aḵti) pa.ṟi. (paḵṟi) eṉa entavuyirum toṭaralām eṉpatum peṟappaṭum.
puḷḷivaṭivu peṟum eḻuttukkaḷ tamiḻiṟ palavuṇṭu. aviyellām oru mutaleḻuttirukkak kūṭutalāka uṭaṉ varupavi. kuṟṟiyalikaramum kuṟṟiyalukaramum mutaleḻuttākiya ikara vukara vaṭivaṅkaḷiṉ mēlē puḷḷiyum peṟum. āytat tiṟkup puḷḷiyallatu mutaleḻuttu vaṭivam iṉmiyiṉ, `āytappuḷḷi' eṉṟē peyaruṇṭāyiṟṟu. iḵtu ōrāṭkuḻu eṉṟu collum iṉṟiya vāḻkki ottatu.
iṉṉoru viḷakkam: puḷḷikkuṟikaḷ niṟkum iṭaṅkaḷil vēṟupāṭuṇṭu. āytappuḷḷi eṉṟa eḻuvāykku `micittu' eṉṟa oṉṟaṉpālviṉi muṟṟu iruttaliṉ, puḷḷi oṉṟē eṉpatum, kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarum āṟaṉ micittum eṉpataṉāl naṭuvē iṭappakkattu aṭuttu niṟkum eṉpatum pōtarum. kāṇka: e.ku. ka.cu a.tu. `tattamiciḷ ottaṉa' eṉa nūṉ marapilum, `vakāra miciyum' eṉappuḷḷi mayaṅkiyalilum `attum āṉmici' eṉa avviyalilum iṭappakkattu aṭuttu eṉṟa poruḷpaṭa varutaliyuṇaralām.
orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalikaram oru collaḷavil varutaliṉ miyā eṉṟa accolliyē nūṟpāvil vittu viḷakkiṉār āciriyar. orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukaramum orumoḻi yāytamum palacoṟkaḷ uṭimiyāṉ, ilakkaṇa vakiyāl varu¨cūḻnilikaḷ kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
āytattic cilavāṇṭukaḷāka itaḻkaḷilum nūlkaḷilum viḷamparaṅkaḷilum eḻuttuvaḻakkil tavaṟākak kiyāḷum oru tīya pōkkiṉic cuṭṭik kāṭṭi iṭit turittuk kaḷiya virumpukiṉṟēṉ. iḷitāka muḷmaram kaḷiyāviṭṭāl ayaṟkaḷikaḷ mēvit tamiḻnāṟṟukkaḷ iṭampeṟā.
kuṟṟeḻuttukkup piṉṉum uyirmeyvalleḻuttukku muṉṉum iraṇṭiṉ cārpāka āytavoli tōṉṟum. iru ciṟakeḻuppa eḻum uṭalatu pōl eṉṟu uriyāciriyarkaḷ uvami kūṟuvar. āytam nuṇukiya nalivōciyātaliṉ kuḻantiyik tūkkuvatupōla irukikaḷālum eḻuppa vēṇṭum. ivvāṟē eḵku, paḵtu, veḵkāmi, aḵkāmi, aḵṟiṇi, paḵṟoṭi, paḵṟuḷi mutalāṉa tamiḻccoṟkaḷ amintuḷa.
iṉṟō āṟāvatu āṅkilavolikku nikarāka āytatti moḻimutalākak kiyāḷum pōkku orupakaṭṭākap peruki varukiṉṟatu. pulliya ippōkkāl āṟāvatu oliyuṭiya pala āṅkilac coṟkaḷ appaṭiyē tamiḻeṉap pukuttappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. viciṟi, kaṭi, nitiyakam, tirippaṭam, nilivippu, cīṭṭukkaṭi, cutti, paṭipputavi, paṭivam, kōppu, kuḷirppoṟi, tūyāli, toḻiṟcāli, aḻaki, tanti. tīppeṭṭit toḻil, nitimuṭakkam eṉṟa poruḷuṭiya āṅkilac coṟkaḷ ellām āytatti mutalākak koṇṭu oruvāṟu tamiḻeḻuttāl eḻutappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. ivvoru olikkaḷiyāl aṇmikkālattut tamiḻukku viḷiyum ūṟukaḷ pala. ivvāṟu ceṉṟāl āṅkilavakarātiyil āṟāvatu oliyuṭiya eṇṇiṟanta coṟkaḷum āytamutalākat tamiḻakarātiyil ēṟumallavā? ēmāntāl vēṟu olikaḷum ēṟalāmeṉṟu patam pārkkumallavā? āṟāvatu oli iṭiyilum iṟutiyilum varum āṅkilac coṟkaḷ kūṭa varavarak kalakkiṉṟaṉa. nalla tamiḻc coṟkaḷuṅkūṭa āytam iruppatāl āṅkila āṟāvatu oliyākap paṭikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl tamiḻākkam ceyvatu taṭippaṭuvatōṭu iṉṟoru aṭimuraṇum ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. eḻuttukku oli, collākum pōtu ōr oli, col lolippu muṟiyellām āṅkilattukku uṇṭu. tamiḻukku aṉṉaniliyilli. eḻuttoliyik kūṭṭip paṭittāl collic colliviṭalām. coṟkaḷic colluvataṟ keṉṟu taṉiyolippup payiṟci vēṇṭiya tilli. itu nam moḻiyiṉ iyalpu. ayalolikaḷiyum ayaṟcoṟkaḷiyum tamiḻeḻuttil eḻutiviṭuvatāl, tamiḻākiviṭātu; tamiḻākkamum ākātu.
iṉṟō āṅkilattiṉ āṟāvatu oliyi mutalilō iṭiyilō iṟutiyilō pōṭuvatāl tamiḻmuṟippaṭi eḻuttuk kūṭṭip paṭikkamuṭiyātu. ippaṭi iccollic colla vēṇṭumeṉṟu taṉiyilakkaṇam teriyavēṇṭum. āṅkilatticcuṭṭit tamiḻc collik kaṟpittāka vēṇṭum. paṇpaṭṭa centamiḻukku viḷintu varum kīḻaṟi itu. eḵku, aḵṟiṇi eṉa iṭiyil irukka vēṇṭiya aṭuppukkūṭṭu vaṭivu moḻikku mutalil vantirukkum tīyūḻkkālam itu. ilakkaṇa varampumikka cemmoḻikku aṟiviyal vaḷarum ikkālattu, orucārār iḻittu varum aṭṭūḻiyam itu. ātaliṉ, `allatu ceytal ōmpumiṉ' eṉṟapaṭi iṉiyēṉum nām kaṭippiṭikka vēṇṭiya moḻiyoḻukkaṅkaḷ yāvi?
1. 	āṅkilattiṉ āṟāvatu olikku nikarāka nam āytavoliyi evviṭattum payaṉpaṭuttakkūṭātu.
2. 	attaku āṅkilac coṟkaḷ palavaṟṟiṟku muṉṉarē takka tamiḻccoṟkaḷ uḷa. avaṟṟi āḷavēṇṭum. uriya coṟkaḷ ilaveṉiṉ tamiḻākkam ceytu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
3. 	tolkāppiyam mutal ellā ilakkaṇikaḷum āytam kuṟṟeḻuttukkup piṉṉum uyirmey valleḻuttukku muṉṉum varum eṉṟu kūṟiya varampiṉik kaṭippiṭikka vēṇṭum.
4. 	tamiḻil eḻuttukkūṭṭic coṟkaḷip paṭittuviṭum iyalmuṟiyi eṉṟum pōṟṟa vēṇṭum.
moḻikkāppu eṉ urinōkkaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟātaliṉ āytappōkkiṉic cuṭṭi neṟippaṭuttuvatu eṉ kaṭamiyāyiṟṟu. muṉ eḻunta urikaḷum itu takum itu takātu eṉṟu moḻiyaṟam cuṭṭi neṟippaṭuttiyiruppati nām aṟiyātavarkaḷallar.
āyta niliyi yaṟika ilakkaṇañ
cāytal tamiḻukkuc cāyvu
puṇarmoḻiyāytam
(36) 	īṟiyal maruṅkiṉum icimi tōṉṟum.
iḵtu āytam irumoḻip puṇarcciyilum varuvatu kūṟum.
akalavuri
mēṟcolliya oru moḻikkaṇṇēyaṉṟi nilimoḻiyīṟu varumoḻiyōṭu puṇarum puṇarcciyilum āytaccārpoli tōṉṟum.
icimi - olittaṉmi.
vaḻakku: 
a. ṟiṇi - aḵṟiṇi	(al + tiṇi)
pa. ṟuḷi - paḵṟuḷi	(pal + tuḷi)
pa. ṟoṭi - paḵṟoṭi	(pal + toṭi)
ka. ṟītu -kaḵṟītu	(kal + tītu)
mu. ṭītu -muḵṭītu	(muḷ +tītu)
a. kaṭiya - aḵkaṭiya	(av + kaṭiya)
tiṟaṉuri
mūṉṟu cārpeḻuttukkaḷumē oru moḻiyilum puṇarmoḻiyilum varumeṉa itukāṟum kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. moḻimarapu orumoḻi paṟṟiyatākaliṉ immūṉṟum uriya paṟṟukkōṭukaḷi īṇṭē kūṟimuṭittār. puṇar moḻik kuriyavaṟṟi uriya puṇariyalkaḷiṟ kaṇṭukoḷka eṉṟum niṉivūṭṭiṉār. `kaṭappāṭaṟinta puṇariyalāṉa' eṉṟu kuṟṟiyalukarattukkuk kūṟiyati iṅkum ēṟṟikkoḷḷa vēṇṭum.
“takaram varuvaḻi āytam niliyalum
  pukariṉ ṟeṉmaṉār pulami yōrē”
“āytam  niliyalum varinili yiṉṟē
  takaram varūuṅ kāli yāṉa”
“vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi yāyta mākum”
eṉṟu puḷḷi mayaṅkiyalil varum mūṉṟu nūṟpākkaḷē puṇarmoḻiyāytam paṟṟiyavi.
ikaravukaraṅkaḷ mutaleḻuttākavum iruttaliṉ kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukarameṉa ivviraṇṭum kuṟumiyaṭi eytiṉa. āytattiṟku aṅṅaṉam oru mutaleḻuttiṉmiyiṉ kuṟṟāytam allatu kuṟṟiyalāytam eṉṟa peyarukku iṭaṉilli, kuṟukkam eṉṟa karuttumilli. arimāttirip pēṟu ataṉiyalpu.
puṇarmoḻikkaṇṇum āytam orumoḻiyiṟ pōlavē kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ piṉṉum uyirmey valleḻuttiṉ muṉṉum varum. kāṇka: aḵṟiṇi, kaḵṟītu, muḵṭītu. īṇṭuvarum valleḻuttu takaram tirinta ṟakaramākavō ṭakaramākavō irukkum `īṟiyal maruṅkiṉ' eṉpataṉāl īṟu tirintu āytamākum eṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
paltamiḻkkaḻakam paḵṟamiḻkkaḻakam eṉavum paltoḻiṟkallūri paḵṟoḻiṟkallūri eṉavum paṇṭi ilakkaṇappaṭi varavēṇṭum. piṟakkālattu vaḻakku vīḻnta puṇarcciyilakkaṇaṅkaḷuḷ ituvum oṉṟātaliṉ, pal tamiḻkkaḻakam, paltoḻiṟkallūri eṉa iṉṟu eḻutuvatu poruntum; iḵtu aṭippaṭikku māṟupāṭaṉṟu.
moḻikkuṟippu
(37)	uruviṉum iciyiṉum arukit tōṉṟum
	moḻikkuṟip pellām eḻutti ṉiyalā
	āyta maḵkāk kāli yāṉa.
itu varivaṭivillāta āytanili kūṟum.
akalavuri
niṟam paṟṟiyum ōci paṟṟiyum aruki varuvaṉa moḻikkuṟippu eṉappaṭum. ivi nēr poruṇmiyuṭiya coṟkaḷ alla. ātaliṉ eḻuttukkaḷāl eḻutikkāṭṭa muṭiyā. ivaṟṟil varum āytavōcikaḷ naliyāmal nīṇṭolippaṉa.
āytam naliyā iṭattu eḻuttuppeṟā eṉṟa etirmaṟiyāl āytam nalintaviṭattum poruṇmiyuṭiya coṟkaḷ eḵku, kaḵcu eṉa vaṭivaveḻuttup peṟum eṉpatu karuttu.
uri : āytam arimāttiriyāka naliyātu nīṇṭa iṭaṅkaḷil niṟattilum ōciyilum ciṟappāka varum moḻikkuṟippellām, coṟkaḷ eḻuttil eḻutappaṭuvatu pōla, eḻutamuṭiyā.
vaḻakku:
kaḵṟu eṉṉum kallatarattam - niṟam paṟṟiyatu.
cuḵṟu eṉṉum taṇṭōṭṭup peṇṇi - ōci paṟṟiyatu.
tiṟaṉuri
kuṟṟiyalikarattukku irunūṟpā, kuṟṟiyalukarattukku irunūṟpā kūṟiya āciriyar āytattukku mūṉṟu nūṟpā ceyvar. mutaliru nūṟpākkaḷ āytam arimāttiri koṇṭa coṟkaḷil varumpōtu ilakkaṇam kūṟuvaṉa. ivi moḻikaḷēyaṉṟi moḻikkuṟippilli. nalivuṭiyāy tam ivaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭi.
niṟakkuṟippum ōcikkuṟippup pulappaṭutta āytavōci varum iṭaṅkaḷum uṇṭu. ivi moḻikkuṟippu eṉappaṭum. ivviṭaṅkaḷil ivvōci māttirikku uṭpaṭātu colvāṉ aḻuttattukkēṟpa nīḷum. aḷapeṭipōla eḻuttiṉ aṭippaṭiyil nīḷāmal, ōci taṉṉaḷavil nīṇṭolikkum. aḷapiṟantuyirttalum oṟṟici nīṭalum icittamiḻil eḻuttup peṟāmiyi oppiṭuka.
varukiṉṟa nūṟpāaḷapeṭi paṟṟiyatu. aḷapeṭi nīṭcikkaṇ aḵtāvatu, aḵkākkāli, moḻivayiṉ otta kuṟṟeḻuttu vaṭiviṉāl eḻutappaṭum eṉṟa ilakkaṇam kūṟuvatu. ataṉōṭu innūṟpāvi iṇittuk kāṇa vēṇṭum. kāṇiṉ, uruviṉum iciyiṉum varum āytavōca moḻikkuṟippātaliṉ eḻuttukkuṟikku vārā eṉpatu pōtarum. aḷapeṭi mutaleḻuttukkē yātaliṉ ivvāyta nīṭci aḷapeṭiyeṉalākātu. “kaṇṇ ṭaṇṇaṇeṉak kaṇṭuṅkēṭṭum” eṉṟa malipaṭukaṭām oṟṟaḷa peṭipōla āytam iraṇṭu peṟutaṟkumilli. āytam tāṉē cārpāka irukkumpōtu tāṉ iṉṉoṉṟukkuc cārpāka utavum eṉpatu poruttamaṉṟu. irunalivu nīṭciyumākātu. ātaliṉ āytavōcippaṭa varum niṟam oli paṟṟiya coṟkuṟippu nīṇṭōcippaṭuṅkāl eḻuttuvaṭivukku uṭpaṭṭu vārā eṉpatum, eḻuttuvaṭivukku uṭpaṭuvaṉa mutaliru nūṟpākkaḷil cuṭṭiya arimāttiri koṇṭa poruṭ coṟkaḷē eṉpatum immūṉṟām nūṟpāviṉ teḷiporuḷām.
ottatu kūṟal eṉṟum neṟiyāl evvakimoḻik kuṟippum nēriya eḻuttu vaṭivam peṟā eṉakkoḷka.
uṇṭuṇṭuṇ ṭeṉṉum uṇarviṉāṟ cāṟṟumē
ṭoṇṭoṇṭoṇ ṭeṉṉum paṟi
eṉpatu nālaṭiyār,
iciniṟi
(38)	kuṉṟici moḻivayiṉ niṉṟici niṟikkum
	neṭṭeḻut timpar ottakuṟ ṟeḻuttē.
iḵtu iciniṟippum ataṟkuk kuṟiyum kūṟum.
akalavuri
cila coṟkaḷil ici kuṟiyumpōtu neṭṭeḻut tukkup piṉṉē ataṟkiṉamāṉa kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ vantuniṉṟu iciyi niṟivuceyyum.
vaḻakku: vāaymi, kurīi, āṭūu, cēey, aḷii, pōoy, kuovi, tuṉpuṟūum, eṭuppatūum, ceṟāaay.
tiṟaṉuri
eḻuttilakkaṇakkūṟukaḷuḷ innūṟpāviṉ karuttu ciṟappuṭiyatu; naṉku ārāyntu teḷitaṟkum uriyatu.
ici kuṟivatu ceyyuḷil eṉṟō ici niṟippatu aḷapeṭi eṉṟō ekkuṟippum nūṟpāvil illi. eṉavē innūṟpā vaḻakkiṟkum ceyyuṭkum potuveṉak koḷḷa vēṇṭum. vaḻakkil āṭūu makaṭūu marūu eṉa varutalum eṉpatūum eṉa urinaṭiyil varutalum kāṇka. cēymi viḷiyil ellām iciniṟivu varum. ceyyuḷiluṅkūṭa iṉṉiciyaḷapeṭi eṉpatu yāppu nōkkiyataṉṟu eṉavum vaḻakkiṟpōla iṉṉōci niṟivukku varuvatu eṉavum koḷḷavēṇṭum. ivvāṟu collōci yiruttaliṉ `kuṉṟici moḻivayiṉ' eṉap potuppaṭak kūṟiṉār āciriyar. kuṉṟutal eṉpatu yāppukkākavum kuṉṟalām, cevikku iṉṉōciyum kuṉṟalāmaṉṟō.
ici kuṉṟuvatu col. ataṉi niṟikkuṅkāl, ac collil mutalilō iṭiyilō iṟutiyilō irukkum neṭṭeḻuttē uriya iṭamākum. an neṭṭeḻuttukkup piṉ uriya kuṟṟeḻuttu vantu ici niṟivu ceyyum.
vāaymi - mutalil - `ā' neṭṭeḻuttukkuppiṉ `a' 						kuṟṟeḻuttu vantatu
pulāal - iṭiyil avvāṟu vantatu
paṭāa - iṟutiyil avvāṟu vantatu
varūum, tarūum, paṭūum eṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil, varum, tarum, paṭum eṉṟu kuṟṟukaram neṭṭeḻuttāki aḷapeṭiyatāl kāṇka.
ici niṟikkum ikkuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ a, i, u, e, o  eṉṟa uyir vaṭivilēyē neṭṭeḻuttiṉpiṉ eḻutappaṭum. tamiḻil coṟkaḷukku iṭiyilō iṟutiyilō uyir vaṭivu varutalilli. aḷapeṭiyil varumpōtu aṟikuṟi eṉṟu vēṟupaṭuttik kāṭṭuvataṟkē uyirvaṭivu niṟkum. kuṟṟeḻuttu eṉpatu uyirkkuṟṟeḻuttu vaṭivaṉ eṉṟu koḷka. niṉṟu eṉpataṉāl innūṟpā varivaṭivu kuṟittatām.
ituvumatu
(39)	ai oḷa veṉṟum āyīreḻuttiṟku
	ikara ukaram iciniṟi vākum.
iḵtu iraṇṭu neṭṭeḻuttukku otta kuṟṟeḻuttuk kūṟum.
akalavuri
neṭṭeḻuttu ēḻu kuṟṟeḻuttu aintu eṉṟa neṭuṅkaṇakkil ai, oḷa eṉṟa iraṇṭu neṭṭeḻuttukkum otta kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ illiyallavā? ici niṟikkuṅkāl, aṟikuṟiyāvaṉa yāvi? eṉpatu viṉā. ataṉit teḷivikkat tōṉṟiyatu innūṟpā.
ai eṉṟa neṭilukku ikaramum, oḷa eṉṟa neṭilukku ukaramum otta kuṟṟeḻuttākik kuṉṟici moḻiyiṭattu ici niṟikkum.
vaḻakku: vaḷit taliiya tīyum; aivaroṭu ciṉii.
tuuvi, muuval.
tiṟaṉuri
aiyaṅkaḷivatu innūṟpāviṉ payaṉ. ai eṉṟu olikkum pōtu ikaramum oḷa eṉṟu olikkum pōtu ukaramum iṟutiyil iḻumeṉ ōciyāka viḻuvatic cevi karuviyāka uṇaralām. ātaliṉ ivviru kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷum iyalpāka ottaṉavē.
muṉnūṟpāvilum innūṟpāvilum kūṟiyapaṭi coṟkaḷē iciniṟivukkuriyaṉa; neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ alla. eṉiṉum anta niṟivi īraḷapuṭiya neṭilkaḷ mēlum nīṇṭu niṟikkum eṉavum anta ōcicceytiyik kāṭṭum aṟikuṟiyāka uyirkkuṟil vaṭivaṅkaḷ niṟkum eṉavum teḷika.
ivviru nūṟpāvilum aḷapeṭik karuttu veḷippaṭiyāka villi. ici niṟivu eṉpatum ōr ilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭākum. aḷapeṭi iciniṟivukkuḷ aṭaṅkiyatu. mēlum ici niṟivu eṉpatu oli nīṭciyiṉ payaṉik kuṟikkum.
neṭṭeḻuttu ēḻaṉuḷ ai oḷa eṉṟa iraṇṭum cilavaṟṟil nerukkam uṭiyavi. `āyīreḻuttiṟku' eṉṟa naṭi itaṉik kāṭṭum.
orumoḻi
(40)	neṭṭeḻut tēḻē ōreḻut torumoḻi
	kuṟṟeḻut tintum moḻiniṟi pilavē.
iḵtu uyireḻuttukaṭku moḻittaṉmi kūṟum.
akalavuri
nūṉmarapil ā ī ū ē ai ō oḷa eṉpaṉa neṭṭeḻuttu eṉṟār. neṭṭeḻuttām avvoliyaṉkaḷē niṉṟāṅku poruṇmi kuṟikkum ōreḻuttoru moḻikaḷāka varum.
kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ nili eṉṉa? a i u e o eṉṟa aintu oliyaṉkaḷum niṉṟāṅku poruṇmi kuṟikkum coṟkaḷāka muḻumi peṟuvatilli.
īṇṭu neṭṭeḻuttu kuṟṟeḻuttu  eṉpaṉa uyirkaḷiyē kuṟikkum. uyirmeykaḷik kuṟiyā. niṟipu - niṟital, toḻiṟpeyar.
vaḻakku:ā	-	pacu mutaliya poruḷ
	ī		- 	koṭu, paṟakkum ī mutaliya poruḷ
	ū 		- 	taci
	ē 		-	ampu
	ai 		- 	talivaṉ, viyappu
	ō 		- 	matakunīrtāṅkum palaki, oḷi
	oḷa 	- 	kavvutal, kōṇal.
tiṟaṉuri
kuṟil neṭileṉa uyir iruvakippaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ neṭilkaḷ kiṭantāṅkē collākavum varum ciṟappuṭiyaṉa.
icciṟappuk kiṭappiṉip pulappaṭuvatē innūṟpāviṉ nōkkam. neṭilkaḷ ivvāṟu varumeṉiṉ, uyiriṉ iṉṉoruvakiyāṉa kuṟilkaḷiṉ nili eṉṉa? eṉṟa viṉā eḻavē ceyyum. avviṉāviṟku viṭi colvatu pōla amintatē `kuṟṟeḻuttintum  moḻi niṟipilavē' eṉṟa aṭi.
aintum eṉpatu muṟṟummi. a i u e o eṉṟa enta uyirk kuṟṟeḻuttum poruṭporuḷ  kāṭṭā. a i u eṉpaṉa cuṭṭupporuḷiyum e tēṟṟattiyum o ciṟappiṉiyum kuṟikkumeṉiṉum avi taṉimoḻiyān taṉmiyila. itaṉi niṉintē āciriyar moḻiyākā eṉṉāmal `moḻi niṟi pila' eṉṟār. mēli nūṟpāviṟ kūṟiyapaṭi kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ ici niṟippaṉa. moḻi niṟippaṉavalla. moḻi muḻumiyalla eṉpatu innūṟpāviṉ teḷivu.
mūṉṟu moḻinili
(41) ōreḻut torumoḻi īreḻut torumoḻi
iraṇṭiṟan ticikkun toṭarmoḻi uḷappaṭa
mūṉṟē moḻinili tōṉṟiya neṟiyē
itu tamiḻc coṟkūṭṭattil mūṉṟu vakiyik kūṟum.
akalavuri
oru moḻi - oru col. toṭarmoḻi - iraṇṭukku mēṟpaṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷ kūṭiya orucol. moḻinili - col vaki.
tamiḻc coṟkaḷ pallāyirakkaṇakkāka iruppiṉum, aṉittiyum mūṉṟuvakiyāka aṭakkik koḷḷalām. ippakuppu puṇarcci nōkkiyatu. piṉvarum puṇarcci yilakkaṇaṅkaḷ inta mūṉṟiṉ aṭippaṭiyil tōṉṟu kiṉṟaṉa. ātaliṉ ataṟku muṉṉōṭiyāka accoṟ pākupāṭukaḷiyum kuṟiyīṭukaḷiyum innūṟpā potintu vittuḷḷatu.
uri: ōreḻuttorumoḻi (ā,kā.); īreḻut torumoḻi (kaṇ, kāṭu) iraṇṭukku mēṟpaṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷ kūṭivarum toṭar orumoḻi (tamiḻ, aṉpu, teyvam, ñāyiṟu) eṉṟu coṟkaḷ puṇarcciyiṭi niṟkum nilivaki mūṉṟēyām.
vaḻakku: ā. mā; ī, kī, ū, pū: ē, cē; ai, ki; ō pō;
ku, veḷa, tu, no - ōreḻuttorumoḻikaḷ.
pal, pāl, pāti, pala cila, kaṭu, kāṭu - īreḻuttoru moḻikaḷ.
kuṟaḷ,kūttu, vayiṟu, kuraṅku: kaṭavuḷ, kapilaṉ, avinayaṉ, - iraṇṭiṟat ticikkum toṭarmoḻi, aḵtāvatu nīṇṭa oru col.
tiṟaṉuri
immuccoṟ pākupāṭṭil tamiḻppuṇarccikaḷ vēṟupāṭuṟuvatik kaṇṭaṟintavar paṇṭi ilakkaṇa nūlōr eṉpatu `mūṉṟē moḻinili tōṉṟiya neṟiyē' eṉṟa naṭippiṭiyāl aṟiyalām. inneṟiyappaṭṭavarē tolkāppiyar.
kā, mā, yā, pū, cē, nī eṉṟāṅku pala ōreḻuttoru moḻikaḷum; āl, vēl, pīr, tāy, mīṉ, vici, ñemi, irā, nilā, paṉi, puḷi, eṭu, ceru eṉṟāṅku pala īreḻuttoru moḻikaḷum; ellām, āviri, ātaṉ, pūtaṉ, aḻaṉ, puḻaṉ, patakku, veyil, makkaḷ, vēṭki, illam eṉṟāṅku pala toṭar orumoḻikaḷum tolkāppiyattil pākupaṭap puṇarkkappaṭuvatik kaṟkiṉṟōm.
kuṟṟiyalukaram aṟuvakippaṭum. avaṟṟik kuṟikkum pōtu,
“īreḻuttorumoḻi uyirttoṭar iṭittoṭar
  āytam toṭarmoḻi vaṉṟoṭar meṉṟoṭar
  āyiru mūṉṟē ukaraṅ kuṟukiṭaṉ”
eṉṟu kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyalil ippākupāṭṭiṉi niṉivūṭṭuvar tolkāppiyar. īreḻuttorumoḻi eṉpatu kācu, kūṭu, kūṟu eṉṟu neṭiṟkuṟṟiyalukaramām. neṭiṟṟoṭar eṉṟu itaṉip peyarppaṭuttavilli. ēṉiya aintiṉiyumē toṭar eṉṟa kuṟiyāṟ cuṭṭuvar. neṭiṟkuṟṟiyalukarattip palaviṭaṅkaḷil neṭṭeḻuttimpar eṉavum kuṟippiṭuvarēyaṉṟit toṭar eṉṟu maṟantum kūṟiyatilli. piṟkāla ilakkaṇikaḷ neṭiṟṟoṭark kuṟṟilukaram eṉṟu  ēṉi aintiṉiyoppa itaṉiyum kuṟikkalāyiṉar. tolkāppiyattu āḷappaṭum ilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭukaḷ palappala. avi mayakkamaṟṟavi. nalluri ceyvataṟku ikkuṟiyīṭukaḷiṉ paṇṭip poruḷik kūrntu aṟiyavēṇṭum.
innūṟpā ciṟappu mikkatātaliṉ iṉṉoru muṟiyum eḻutic cintippōm.
ōreḻut torumoḻi īreḻut torumoḻi
iraṇṭiṟan ticikkum toṭarmoḻi uḷappaṭa
mūṉṟē moḻinili tōṉṟiya neṟiyē.
ōreḻuttuccol, īreḻuttuccol, iraṇṭukku mēṟpaṭṭa col eṉa eḻutteṇṇikkiyil col mūṉṟu vakippaṭum eṉpatu karuttu. ātaliṉ iraṇṭiṟantu icikkum toṭarmoḻi eṉṟiruntālum toṭar orumoḻi aḵtāvatu palaveḻuttut toṭarum orucol eṉṟutāṉ poruḷpaṭum.
orumi paṉmi allatu oṉṟu pala eṉṟa tamiḻeṇ vaḻakku payaṉiliyāka varum viṉimuṟṟukku uriyatu. peyarccoṟkaṭku ellām oruvar iruvar mūvar nālvar eḻuvar paṉṉiruvar aṟupattumūvar eṉa ellā eṇkaḷum; orumoḻi, irukaṇ, aintamiḻ, eḻupiṟappu, eṇkuṇam, patiṟṟuppattu, nūṟṟitaḻ, āyiravūḻi eṉa ellā eṇṇaṭikaḷum varum.
`irupeyar palpeyar aḷaviṉ peyar' eṉa eccaviyalil tolkāppiyarum, `orunāṭ cellalam irunāṭcellalām, palanāṭ payiṉṟu palaroṭu celliṉum' eṉpa (101) puṟaþnāṉūṟum `oṉṟeṉa iraṇṭeṉa mūṉṟeṉa nāṉkeṉa ainteṉa āṟaṉe eḻeṉa eṭṭeṉat toṇṭeṉa' eṉa (3)p paripāṭalum kūṟupa. ātaliṉ `iraṇṭiṟanticikkum toṭarmoḻi' eṉṟa innūṟpā naṭi tamiḻvaḻakkēyākum.
īṇṭu eḻuttu eṉpatu oliyik kuṟikkumā? varivaṭivik kuṟikkumā? nalla viṉā.
 ā	-	ōreḻuttorumoḻi	varivaṭivu : 1	oliyaṉ	ā =1
kā	-	mēṟpaṭi	mēṟpaṭi 		mēṟpaṭi 	k + ā =2
āṇ	 -	 īreḻuttorumoḻi 	varivaṭivu: 2 	mēṟpaṭi	ā + ṇ = 2
kāṇ - mēṟpaṭi	mēṟpaṭi 		mēṟpaṭi       ka+ā+ṇ=3
kāṭu - mēṟpaṭi	mēṟpaṭi		mēṟpaṭi	k + ā + ṭ
						+ u = 4
kāppu - toṭarmoḻi 	varivaṭivu: 3	mēṟpaṭi	k + ā + 						                 	 p + p + u =5
kapilaṉ -mēṟpaṭi	varivaṭivu:	4	mēṟpaṭi     k+a+p+ i 						                    +l+a+ṉ=7
eḻuttuniliyil uyirum meyyum taṉiyāka iruntālum taṉi vaṭivukaḷ peṟṟiruntālum coṉṉiliyil uyirmeyyākavē oṟṟuminayampaṭak kalantuviṭutaliṉ, uyirmeyyi ōreḻuttākak koḷvatē peruvaḻakku. pala, cila, nil, kal eṉpavaṟṟi iraṇṭeḻuttākavum tamiḻ, aṉpu, kuṟaḷ eṉpavaṟṟi mūṉṟeḻuttukkākavum kallātārum eṇṇuvar. `arumaṟi maruṅkiṉ aintiṉum eṭṭiṉum' eṉṟa cilappatikārattil im mantiraṅkaḷ uyirmeykaḷi ōreḻuttākavē koḷkiṉṟaṉa. eḻutteṇṇum kaṭṭaḷi yaṭiyil uyirmey ōreḻuttākavē kaṇippup peṟum. āṇ, kāṇ eṉpaṉa nēraciyākavum avi, cuvi eṉpaṉa niriyāciyum ceyyuṭkaṇ alakupeṟum.
tamiḻmoḻikkaṇ olikkum varivaṭivukkum tolpaḻaṅ kālattē iṇinta cemmiyiyum eḻuttuvaḻakkum nalliṭaṅkoṇṭa āṭciyiyum, ataṉāl muppatu oliyum muppatu eḻuttu eṉṟē peyarpeṟum oṟṟumik kāṭciyiyum ivvuri paṉmāṇum varalāṟṟu muṟiyiṟ cāṟṟi varukiṉṟatu. ātaliṉ ōreḻuttuc col īreḻuttuccol iraṇṭukku mēṟpaṭṭa col eṉṟu eḻuttuniliyil mūṉṟuvaki ceytatu varaṉmuṟip poruttamākum.
iṉṉoru muṟiyākavum teḷiyalām. kā, ke, kē, ko, kō, ku eṉpaṉa ōruyir meyyāyiṉum vaṭivil iraṇṭu mūṉṟākat tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. eṉiṉum īreḻuttu mūveḻuttu eṉṟu yārum; colvatilli. :ōruyirmey eṉṟa oliniliyi nōkki ōreḻuttu eṉṟē colvōm. kākki nāleḻutteṉṟō veḷavāl āṟeḻutteṉṟō collōm. eṉavē ōreḻuttoru moḻi mutalāṉa kuṟiyīṭukaḷ olittaṉmiyiṉiyum uṭkoṇṭaṉavām.
ippākupāṭu iruvaki vaḻakkum eḷimiyum teḷivum paṟṟi koḷḷappaṭṭatu. `ōreḻuttiyal patam' eṉavum, `pakāppatam ēḻum pakupatam oṉpatum eḻuttīṟākat toṭarum' eṉavum pavaṇantiyār muṉṉōr muṟiyip piṉpaṟṟi yiruttal pōṟṟattakum.
taṉimeyyiyakkam
(42) 	meyyi ṉiyakkam akaramoṭu civaṇum.
itu taṉimeyyic collu muṟiyum eḻutumuṟiyum kāṭṭum.
akalavuri
nūṉmarapil “meyyiṉiyaṟki puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal” eṉṟa puḷḷiyoṭuniṟkum iyaṟki meykaḷip paṭṭāṅku eḷitāka olikka muṭiyātallavā? ataṉāl ammeyyoli yaṉkaḷ akavoli cērttu olikkappaṭum aṅṅaṉam oliyoṭu iyaṅkiṉum puḷḷiyuṭiya meyyākavē karutap paṭum. akara uyirmeyyākā. itu col vaḻakkiṟkum eḻuttu vaḻakkiṟkum ēṟṟa naṭimuṟi.
vaḻakku: 
valleḻut teṉpa	ka caṭa ta pa ṟa	tol 17
melleḻut teṉpa	ṅa' ña ṇa na ma ṉa	mēṟpaṭi 18
iṭiyeḻut teṉpa	ya ra la va ḻa ḷa	mēṟpaṭi 19
immuṟiyi nūl muḻutuṅ kāṇalām.
ikku, iccu, ittu, ippu, iṇṇu eṉṟavāṟum kkaṉṉā, ccaṉṉā, ṭṭaṉṉā, eṉṟavāṟum. kaṉa, ṉaṅa, caṉa, ñaṉa eṉṟa vāṟum pēccuvaḻakkil meykaḷ iyakkam peṟutalum uṇṭu eṉṟālum ivi taṉimey eṉa makkaḷ purintu koḷvar.
tiṟaṉuri
piṟappil ovvoru meyyum taṉakkeṉṟa poruṇmi yuṭiya mutaleḻuttu; uyirukku nikarāṉa mutaleḻuttu; eṉiṉum eḷimiyākac colvataṟkum kēṭpataṟkum aṟivataṟkum akaram tuṇiyoliyākum : avvaḷavē 
aṉā āvaṉṉā iṉa īyaṉṉā eṉa uyirum kāvaṉṉā kīyaṉṉā kōvaṉṉā kuvaṉṉā eṉa uyirmeyyuṅkūṭat tuṇiyoli peṟukiṉṟaṉa.
muṟkūṟiyāṅku ovvoru meykkum urivaṭivum ataṉmēl oṟṟu eṉpataṟkuriya puḷḷivaṭivum uṇṭu. urivaṭivukaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirum ēṟi uyirmeyyākum pōtum ka kā ki kī ku kū ke kē ki ko kō ku eṉṟu naṭuvuruvāka niṟkum. oṟṟu eṉpataṟkuriya patiṉeṭṭu meykkum potuvāṉa puḷḷivaṭivu maṭṭum nīṅki uyirēṟa iṭaṅ koṭukkum. ammuṟippaṭi, meyyiṉ iyakkattukaku akaram varumpōtu mēṟpuḷḷi nīṅkum eṉṟu koḷka. `puḷḷi yillā ellā meyyum uruvuru vāki akaramō ṭuyirttalum' eṉṟu nūṉmarapiṟ coṉṉa vitiyi niṉika.
eḻuttu meyyākuka. uyirākuka, naṭimuṟi yiyakkattiṟkuc cila cāriyikaḷ peṟum.  innūṟpāvil `akaramoṭu civaṇum' eṉac karaccāriyi vantiruttal kāṇalām. ṉaḵkāṉ, ṟaḵkāṉ aikāram, oḷakāram, takaram, vakaram, ḷakāram, lakāram, rakāram eṉavum varum.
akaraccāriyiyuṭiya kakarattiṟkum akara vuyirmeyyāṉa kakarattiṟkum vēṟupāṭu naṭimuṟiyil uṇarappaṭum.
`itaṉ naṭimuṟiyitu’ eṉṟu kāṭṭuvatu innūṟpāviṉ payaṉ.
meymmayakkam
(43)	 tammiyal kiḷappiṉ ellā veḻuttum
	meynnili mayakka māṉa milli.
itu meymmayakkap potunili kūṟum.
akalavuri
nūṉmarapil iṉṉa meykkuppiṉ iṉṉamey varum eṉṟu virivākak kūṟappaṭṭatu. atu coṉṉiliyil aminta eḻuttut toṭarcciyākum. ataṟku mēl naṭimuṟi viḷakkamāka eḻuntatu innūṟpā.
ellā meyyeḻuttukkaḷum meyyeḻuttu eṉṟa peyar nilipeṟumpōtu, etaṟkuppiṉ etu vantālum kuṟṟamilli. taṉṉiccuṭṭi varumiṭattu mēṟkūṟiya variyaṟiyilli.
ellāveḻuttum - ellā meyyeḻuttukkaḷum,
māṉam - kuṟṟam, varampu.
vaḻakku: 	avaṉ makaram eḻutiṉāṉ
	avaḷ lukaram eḻutiṉāṉ	     pēccuvaḻakku
	avar nikaram eḻutiṉār	
“ṇaṉaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ ṭaṇaveṉat tōṉṟum”
“oṟṟavum varuṭavum lakāra ḷakāram”
“varuṭa rakāram ḻakāram”
“tām varūum raḻavalaṅ kaṭiyē”
eṉpaṉa tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷ.
tiṟaṉuri
vēṇṭumiṭaṅkaḷil kāraṇam collic ciṟappu vitikūṟuvatu tolkāppiya neṟi. `tammiyal kiḷappiṉ' eṉpatu kāraṇa naṭi. mey eṉpatu eḻuttāyiṉum taṉṉik kuṟikkum niliyil peyarākic collākum. mayaṅkutaṟku uriyavākā eṉṟu muṉvarampiṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷum intak karuttaḷavil poruntiya mayakkamākak koḷḷattakum. ivvarampil varampu ilakkaṇanūl kaḷiṟṟāṉ varum muḻutum naṭimuṟippāṭu nōkkic collappaṭum ilakkaṇaṅkaḷuḷ ituvoṉṟu. “meyyiṉ iyakkam akaramoṭu civaṇum” eṉṟa mēlinūṟpāvum itaṉic cārntatu itu naṭimuṟippāṭu eṉṟa neṟippeyar peṟum.
oru coṟpoḻivuk kūṭṭattiṟku maṉivi makkaḷ mutalāṉa uṟaviṉarkaḷum vantirukkalām. appōtum coṟpoḻiñaṉ eṉ aṉpāṉa uṭaṉpiṟappukkaḷē eṉṟu taṉpoḻivit toṭaṅkukiṉṟāṉ ituvē naṭimuṟip pāṭu eṉappaṭum.
meymmayakkam paṟṟi mīṇṭum oru teḷivi niṉittuk koḷḷavēṇṭum. laṇṭaṉ, leṉiṉ, lūtar, ṭarpaṉ, ṭākkā, ṭelli, raṅkūṉ, raciyā eṉpaṉavellām poruḷ kuṟikkum coṟkaḷātaliṉ, tammiyal kiḷappaṉavākā. ivaṟṟit tamiḻ marapuppaṭi uyir cērttō vaṭivu tirittō tamiḻmiyākkik koḷḷavēṇṭum.
īroṟṟuṭaṉili
(44)	ya ra ḻa veṉṉum mūṉṟumuṉ oṟṟak
	ka ca ta pa ṅa ña na ma īroṟ ṟākum.
iḵtu īroṟṟuṭaṉili kūṟum; ituvum meymmayakkattic cārntatu.
akalavuri
nūṉmarapil meymmayakkam virivākak kūṟappaṭṭatu. aṅkuk kūṟiyavi ōroṟṟuṭaṉili meymmayakkamākum. appōtu mutalmey puḷḷiyaṭiyatākum. aṭuttuvarumey uyirmeyyāka irukkum (aṉpu) eṉa viḷakkiṉēṉ. innūṟpā kūṟuvatu eṉṉa? iraṇṭupuḷḷi allatu iraṇṭoṟṟu irukkum.
aṭuttuvarumey muṉpupōl uyirmeyyāka irukkum (vāḻttu)
uri: y r ḻ eṉṉum mūṉṟum muṉ oṟṟāka irukka k c tp ṅa ña n m eṉpavi mīṇṭum oṟṟāka vara īroṟṟup peṟum.
yk, yc, yt, yp, yṅ, yñ, yn, ym:
rk, rc, rt, rp, rṅ, rñ, rn, rm:	      eṉa iraṇṭiraṇṭu
ḻk, ḻc, ḻt, ḻp, ḻṅ, ḻñ, ḻn, ḻm;      oṉṟāka varum.
oṟṟu - meyppuḷḷi, puḷḷi eṉpatu meyppuḷḷi. cārpuppuḷḷi, ekara okarappuḷḷi, makarak kuṟukkappuḷḷi eṉap palavaṟṟiyum kuṟikkum. oṟṟu eṉpatu meyppuḷḷi oṉṟaṉiyē cuṭṭum.
vaḻakku	:	k    c 	t   p	 ṅ    ña 	n    m 
y	:	vāykkāl	pāyccu	cēytatu	vāyppu
		vēyṅkuḻal	tēyñcu	māyntu	moympu
r	:	īrkku	cārcci	cārttu	vārppu
	ārṅkōṭu	kūrñcili	kūrntu	kūrmpaṭi
ḻ	:	vāḻkki	vīḻcci	vāḻttu	kāḻppu
	pāḻṅkiṇaṟu	pāḻñcuṉi	vāḻntu	vīḻmpaṭi
tiṟaṉuri
“yaraḻa veṉṉum puṉṉi muṉṉar
  mutalā keḻuttu ṅakaramoṭu tōṉṟum”
eṉa nūṉṉmarapiṟ kūṟiyatu (āyka, ārka, āḻka) ōroṟṟu meym mayakkamākum. innūṟpā vēykkuṟi, vērkkuṟi, vīḻkkuṟi, eṉa īroṟṟup paṟṟiyatu eṉṟu teḷivupaṭuttu kiṉṟatu. `īroṟṟākum' (irupuḷḷipeṟum) eṉpataṉāl vaṭivu nōkkiyum innūṟpā amintatu eṉakkoḷḷalām.
y r ḻ eṉṟa mutaloṟṟukkup piṉ varuvaṉa k c t p ṅ ñ n m. iraṇṭāvatu oṟṟukkuppiṉ varuvaṉa evi eṉpataṉi nūṉmarapiṟ kaṇṭu koḷka.
meymmayakkam eṉpatu iṉṉa meyyoliyaṉukkup piṉ iṉṉa meyyoliyaṉ varum eṉa eḻuttaḷavil kiṭakki nili terippatu eṉavum itu oru collukkum, puṇar moḻikkum potuveṉavum oṟṟukku aṭuttuvaruvatu uyirmeyyāka irukkum eṉavum muṟkūṟiya karuttukkaḷi īṇṭum niṉivukoḷka.
`mūṉṟēmoḻinili tōṉṟiya neṟiyē' eṉṟa coṉṉiliyamippuk kūṟukiṉṟārātalāṉum, īroṟṟu toṭarmoḻiyuḷ varuvatātalāṉum innūṟpā moḻi marapāyiṟṟu. “īroṟṟut toṭarmoḻi iṭittoṭarākā” eṉṉum kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal nūṟpā itaṟkuc cāṉṟu. iṉṉoru kōṭpāṭuṅkoḷḷa iṭamuṇṭu. nūṉmarapu iyalpāṉa aṭippaṭiyilakkaṇaṅkūṟum; iyalpukku kūṭutalāṉavaṟṟiyum iyalpukku vēṇṭiyamēl viḷakkaṅ kaḷiyum moḻimarapu kūṟum. ivvāṟu koḷḷiṉ iyalvippum nūṟpāvippum iṉṉum teḷivutarum.
r ḻ amippu
(45)	avaṟṟuḷ
	rakāra ḻakāram kuṟṟoṟ ṟākā.
itu rakaravoṟṟum ḻakaravoṟṟum mutaṟkaṇ varumnili kāṭṭum.
akalavuri
meymmayakkam eḻuttukkiṭakki nili paṟṟiyatēṉum coṉṉiliyil vittuttāṉē viḷakkañ¨ ceyya vēṇṭum.
ya ra ḻa eṉṟa mūṉṟaṉuḷ rakaramum ḻakaramum mutaṟkaṇ taṉikkuṟilukkup piṉ oṟṟāka vantunillā. aḵtāvatu ar par eṉavōaḻ, paḻ eṉavō vārā. eppaṭi varum? ār, pār,  āḻ, pāḻ eṉa neṭilukkup piṉṉum pakar, tamiḻ eṉak kuṟiliṇikkup piṉṉum oṟṟāka varum. ivvāṟē pukār, piṅkūḻ eṉap palaveḻuttut toṭarmoḻiyil ellām varum. taṉikkuṟiṟkīḻ varumpōtu uri tiru kari eṉavum aḻi viḻu maḻi eṉavum uyirmeyyākavē varum. taṉikkuṟiṟkīḻ oṟṟē vārā eṉa itaṉāl vilakkappaṭṭatu avvāṟu vilakkuvatu eḷitākaliṉ, yakaravoṟṟē mey, poy, cey, koy eṉa kuṟṟoṟṟākavum varum eṉpatu veḷippaṭi.
kuṟṟoṟṟu - mutaṟkaṇ taṉi uyirk kuṟṟeḻuttu allatu taṉi uyirmeykkuṟṟeḻuttu.
vaḻakku: nīr, nakar; pāḻ; pukaḻ eṉavarum.
aruccaṉi, aruccuṉaṉ, narumati, taruppam, curuṇaṉ eṉṟa ayaṟcoṟkaḷi āḷa vēṇṭiṉ ivvamippuppaṭi eḻutal tamiḻām. karuppiṇi, varuṇaṉi  varukkam, karuppūram, marumam, karumam, karuttaṉ eṉṟa tamiḻc coṟkaḷi ivvāṟē piḻiyiṉṟi eḻutuka.
tiṟaṉuri
aikāra oḷakāram pōl iṭiyiṉaṅkaḷuḷ rakaramum ḻakaramum oru taṉmiyaṉa.
“meynnili cuṭṭiṉ ellā veḻuttum
  tammuṟṟām varūum raḻavalaṅ kaṭiyē”
eṉṟa nūṉmarapil rakara ḻakaram taṉmuṉ tāṉ vārā eṉṟu vilakkiyati niṉika.
itukāṟum iṉṉameykkuppiṉ iṉṉameyvarum eṉṟu meymmayakkam kūṟivanta āciriyar iṉṉa vuyirukkup piṉ iṉṉa meyvārā eṉa innūṟpāvil uyiroṭu meymmayaṅkāmi kūṟum oru moḻiyamippik kaṟkiṉṟōm.
īṇṭu oru viḷakkam
`ār ār pa veṉa varūu mūṉṟum'
`ir īr miṉ eṉa varūu mūṉṟum
eṉa viṉiyiyaṟkaṇ rakaram kuṟṟoṟṟāka vantuḷatē eṉṟu viṉavalām. ām; ar ir eṉa vantuḷa. āṉāl ivi collamippil coṟkiṭiyē varum uṟuppukkaḷ teḷiyiṟkākat taṉittukkāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. poṟimutalāṉa poruḷkaḷiṉ pākaṅkaḷik kaṟpippataṟkāka avvamayam vēṇṭumeṉṟē taṉiyuṟuppukkaḷip pirittuk koḷkiṉṟō mallavā? ceṭiyiṉ narampukaḷi, malariṉ itaḻkaḷi, āṭiyiṉ iḻikaḷi, paṭiyiṉ tirukukaḷi. ūrtikaḷiṉ cērkkikaḷi utirikaḷākap pākuppaṭuttik kāṇpati oppiṭuka.
iṅṅaṉam vārā, varakkūṭātu eṉṟu collumiṭaṅ kaḷilum tavaṟāṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷit tantutāṉ viḷakkuvatum vilakkuvatum uṇṭu. kalvineṟi eṉṟu itaṉik koḷḷa vēṇṭum. cevvaṉ vaṭivu eṉṟa ikkāla viṉāmuṟiyum inneṟittu.
toṭarmoḻi viḷakkam
(46)	kuṟumiyum neṭumiyum aḷaviṟ kōṭaliṉ
	toṭarmoḻi yellām neṭṭeḻut tiyala.
itu toṭarmoḻikku oru viḷakkam ceyyum.
akalavuri
ōreḻuttorumoḻi, īreḻuttorumoḻi, iraṇṭiṟat ticikkum toṭarmoḻi eṉṟu tamiḻc colliṉi muvvakiyuḷ aṭakkik koṇṭār tolkāppiyar. avaṟṟuḷ toṭar moḻikku oru putuviḷakkam taruvatu innūṟpā.
kuṟumi eṉpatu neṭumi eṉpatum eḻutteṇṇikki aḷaviṟ koḷḷutalāl ellāt toṭar oru moḻikaḷum neṭilōṭiya iyaṉṟaṉavākak karutappaṭum.
neṭṭeḻuttu - iraṇṭiṟkumēl nīṭiya eḻuttukkaḷ, neṭṭōṭṭam kaḻineṭil pōl.
vaḻakku: 	pukaḻ, aḻakaṉ, aṇṇāmali, paṉampāraṉ
tiṟaṉuri
ilakkaṇa nūlil ellāviyalkaḷum vaṉkariyuṭiyaṉa valla; oṉṟiṉoṉṟu ceytikaḷ tantuppiṉṉiyalkaḷukku varampu kāṭṭupavi. itu paṭikkaṭṭuneṟi eṉappaṭum. mīṇṭum niṉivu kūrvōm.
ōreḻuttorumoḻi	-	ā, mā
īreḻuttorumoḻi	-	āṉ, māṉ, cila, palā, āṭu, ūtā
toṭarmoḻi		-	nalam, nāṉku, aṇaṅku, maturi, 								iḷaṅkō, tiruvaḷḷuvar
muvvaki moḻikaḷilum neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ uḷḷaṉa eṉṟālum avaṟṟi innūṟpā poruḷākak koḷḷa villi. varivaṭivāṉa eḻutteṇṇikkiyi aḷavāka vittup pārkkumpōtu toṭarmoḻitāṉ iraṇṭukku mēṟpaṭṭa palaveḻuttuṭiyavi. oru vaṭivu koṇṭatu ōreḻut torumoḻi: iru vaṭivu koṇṭatu īreḻut torumoḻi: kuṟiyīṭukaḷē taṟcāṉṟu. īṇṭu eḻuttu eṉpatu varivaṭivik kuṟikkum eṉa uriya nūṟpāvil muṉteḷivu ceyyappaṭṭatu.
innūṟpā māttiri paṟṟiyataṉṟu eṉpataṟku aḷaviṟ kōṭal eṉṟa col amintiruppatu oru cāṉṟu. īṇṭu aḷavu eṉpatu eḻutteṇṇikkiyik kuṟikkum. `eḻuttaḷaveñciṉum cīrnilitāṉē' eṉṟa ceyyuḷiyal ivvurikku araṇceyvatiyuṇarka.
piṉvarum iyalkaḷ puṇarmoḻicceyki kūṟuvaṉa. puṇarmoḻi eṉpatu moḻikaḷiṉ iyipātaliṉ, moḻi marapil puṇarmoḻikkut takka pāṅkukaḷi aṭikōluvar āciriyar. mūvvakic coṟpākupāṭu innōkkiṉatu.
“kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarum ōreḻuttu moḻikkum
  aṟiyat tōṉṟum akarak kiḷavi”

“kuṟṟeḻut timparum ōreḻuttu moḻikkum
  niṟṟal vēṇṭum ukarak kiḷavi”
“īreḻut torumoḻi uyirttoṭar iṭittoṭar”
“makarattoṭarmoḻi mayaṅkutal varinta
  ṉakarat toṭarmoḻi oṉpaḵ teṉpa”
“palavaṟ ṟiṟuti nīṭumoḻi yuḷavē
  ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya toṭarmoḻi yāṉa”
“toṭaral liṟuti tammuṟ ṟāmvariṉ
  lakaram ṟakaravoṟ ṟākalu murittē”
eṉap parantu varum iṭaṅkaḷil immūvakik kuṟikaḷ āḷappaṭuvatit teḷiyalām. `kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar' eṉpatu īreḻuttorumoḻiyik kuṟikkum.
pōṉm
(47)	ceyyu ḷiṟutip pōli moḻivayiṉ
	ṉakāra makāram īroṟ ṟākum
	ṉakāri muṉṉar makāraṅ kuṟukum.
iḵtu īroṟṟum kuṟukkamum peṟum oru collik kāṭṭum; ituvum meymmayakkamākum.
akalavuri
“ya ra ḻa veṉṉum mūṉṟumuṉ oṟṟak
  ka ca ta pa ṅa ña na ma īroṟ ṟākum”
eṉṟu parantupaṭṭa niliyil vaḻakkiṟkum ceyyuṭkum potuvāka īṟoṟṟumayakkam kūṟiṉār. innūṟpāvil pōlum eṉṟa oru collic cuṭṭikkāṭṭi, atu pōṉm eṉa īṟoṟṟāka varum. ceyyuṭkaṇṇē varum. varuṅkāl makaram kuṟukum eṉṟu mūṉṟu ilakkaṇam kūṟukiṉṟār.
uri: ceyyuṭkaṇ iṟutiyaṭiyil iṟuticcīril `pōlum' eṉṟa viṉimuṟṟuc col pōṉm eṉakkuṟuki niṟkumpōtu `ṉm' eṉa īroṟṟākum. avviṭattu ṉakarattiṉ piṉvarum iṟuti makaram kālmāttiriyākak kuṟukkam peṟum.
vaḻakku:
“citiyuṅ kalattip payiṉāṟ ṟiruttum
  ticiyaṟi mīkāṉum pōṉm”	 (paripā.10)
“neñcam piḷantiṭṭu nērār naṭuvaṭṭaṉ
   vañciṉam vāyttāṉum pōṉm”	 (kali.101.)
itu pōṉma eṉṟa vaṭivupeṟum. makarattiṟku uḷḷē (ma) puḷḷiyirukkum.
tiṟaṉuri
innūṟpāviti cevvarampuṭiyatu, iṭanteḷivāṉatu. ivvīroṟṟu vaḻakkil varātu, vēṟucollil vārātu, ceyyuṭkaṇṇum vēṟiṭaṅkaḷil vārātu eṉṟa etirnili peṟappaṭum.
ceyyuḷiṟuti eṉa iṭaṅkūṟuvatāl pōlum eṉpatu ceyyum eṉṟa muṟṟārum; peyareccam iṟutiyil nillātaṉṟē.
itukāṟum kaṟṟa meymmayakkaṅkaḷil ōroṟṟukkup piṉ (vāḻka) uyir meyyum, īroṟṟukkuppiṉ (vāḻttu) uyirmeyyum irukkum. pōṉm eṉṟa inta īroṟṟil piṉ etuvumilli; īroṟṟaḷavākavē niṉṟu makaram nalikiṉṟatu. itaṉal makarattiṟkuk kāl māttirik kuṟukkam tōṉṟum.
“ariyaḷavu kuṟukal makara muṭittē
  iciyiṭa ṉarukun teriyuṅ kāli
  uṭpeṟu puḷḷi yuruvā kummē”
eṉṟa nūṉmarapu nūṟpāvi niṉivu koḷka.
oru viḷakkam: pōṉm eṉṟa muṟṟu ceyyuḷiṭiyil vantāl īroṟṟum ākātu; kuṟukkamum peṟātu.
“peruṅkal laṭārum pōṉmeṉa virumpi”	(puṟam. 19)
maḻiyuṇ māmati pōṉmeṉat tōṉṟumē 	(cīvakacin.127)
iṅṅaṉam iṭiyil varum `pōṉm' eṉṟa muṟṟuc coṟkaḷ.
ṇa ṉaḵkāṉ muṉṉark
kacañapa mayavav vēḻum uriya
eṉṟa ilakkaṇappaṭi ōroṟṟu mayakkamākum.
arantiṉvāy pōṉmpōṉm pōṉm
piṉṉu malarkkaṇ puṉal 	(paripāṭal. 10)
eṉṟa paripāṭaṟkaṇ varuvaṉa īroṟṟākum eṉiṉum iṟutiyāki iciyiṭaṉ arukāmiyiṉ makaram kuṟukkam peṟātueṉa uyttuṇarka.
īroṟṟu eṉpataṟkākak kuṟukkam tōṉṟuvatilli; īroṟṟu niṟkum ici nerukkattip poṟuttē kuṟukkam varum.
ceyyuḷiṟuti eṉap potuppaṭak kūṟiṉālum ēṟpuḻik kōṭalāl' `pōṉm' eṉṟa īroṟṟuk kuṟukkam veṇpāviṉ iṟutic cīrkkē koḷḷattakum, pōlum eṉṟu niṟka iyalātākaliṉ,
pōṉm eṉpatu iraṇṭiṟanticikkum toṭarmoḻi,
mūṉṟeḻuttu uṭimiyiṉ.
icceyyuḷ vaḻakku piṟkālattu vīḻntatu.
eḻuttiyal nili
(48) 	moḻippaṭut ticippiṉum terintuvē ṟicippiṉum
	eḻuttiyal tiriyā veṉmār pulavar.
iḵtu enniliyilum eḻuttiyal māṟāmi valiyuṟuttum.
akalavuri
muppatu oliyaṉkaḷ allatu eḻuttukkaḷ tamiḻiṉ mūlaṅkaḷ eṉṟu nūṉmarapiṉmutalnūṟpāviṟ kaṟṟōm. poruṇmit tiṟaṉkoṇṭa oliyē oliyaṉ eṉappaṭum.
ivvoliyaṉiṉ palvēṟu iyakkaṅkaḷum cērkki kaḷum eḻuttatikāraviyalkaḷiṟ collappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. māttiri, vaṭivu, mayakkam, cārpu, iciniṟi, muppākupāṭu, īṟoṟṟu nili ivaṟṟi itukāṟum aṟintōm. iṉimoḻi mutaleḻuttukkaḷ. moḻiyiṟutiyeḻuttukkaḷ, puṇarmoḻi yiyalpukaḷ, tiripukaḷ ivaṟṟi virivāka aṟivōm. in niliyil oru perunteḷiviyum maṉṉiya aṭippaṭiyiyum cuṭṭikkāṭṭap piṟantatu innūṟpā.
eḻuttu allatu oliyaṉ poruṇmi yāṟṟaluṭiyatu eṉṟālum collākic collukku uṟuppākittāṉ poruḷi veḷippaṭiyākak kuṟikkamuṭiyum.
akara mutala veḻuttellām āti
pakavaṉ mutaṟṟē ulaku.
eṉṟu varumpōtu eḻuttukkaḷ kūṭic collān taṉmi peṟukiṉṟaṉa. moḻiyāki olikkiṉṟaṉa. iṅṅaṉam uṟuppākik kūṭuṅkāl oliyaṉkaḷ tam poruṇmi yāṟṟali aḵtāvatu eḻuttiyali iḻakkumā? tiripupaṭumā? uyirmeyvaṭivil uyir vaṭivukaḷ tiripu peṟukiṉṟaṉa; uyirmey eṉṟu peyar peṟṟiruntālum meykkuppiṉ uyirkaḷ tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa; arimeyyuṭiya makaravaḷavu kālamākak kuṟikiṉṟatu; uyirkaḷ aḷapu kaṭakkiṉṟaṉa; oṟṟukkaḷ ici nīḷukiṉṟaṉa. kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ niṉṟa iciniṟikkiṉṟaṉa; moḻinili mūṉṟukku varivaṭivu aṭippaṭiyākiṉṟatu; mey akaramoṭu iyaṅka vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu; tammik cuṭṭumpōtu meymmayakka varampu oḻikiṉṟatu; rakāra ḻakāram kuṟṟoṟṟātal illi; pōṉm eṉpatu īṟoṟṟāy nalikiṉṟatu.
piṉvarum puṇariyalkaḷilum palvaki eḻuttu māṟṟaṅkaḷip pārkkiṉṟōm.
“meypiṟi tātal mikutal kuṉṟaleṉa
  ivveṉa moḻipa tiripu māṟē”
eṉṟapaṭi ōreḻuttu, iṉṉoru eḻuttākat tirikiṉṟatu patiṟṟuppattu, puḷiyaṅkuṭi, naṉṉilam, maṭkalam, aḵṟiṇi, puṟattiṇi, nāṭkūli, aṟakkaṭṭaḷi, puṟanakar, kaṭaṟkari, maṇaṟpāṟi, pūmpukār, māṉakkēṭu, pūntōṭṭam, pūñceṭi, aṟupatu eṉṟāṅku ōreḻuttuk kūṭavum kuṟiyavum iṉṉoru eḻuttākat tiriyavum kāṇkiṉṟōm.
nūṉmarapiṟ colliya muppatu eḻuttukkaḷ iṉṉaṇam māṟupaṭuvati niṉiyuṅkāl oliyaṉkaḷ tiripuṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟa uṇarvu namakku eḻutuvatu iyaṟki. nilamatirntāl neṭumāṭam eṉ ceyyum? ivvuṇarviṉi viḷaṅkik koṇṭa tolkāppiya muṉṉōr oliyaṉ enniliyilum taṉ poruṇmittiṟam tiriyātu eṉṟa uṇmi kūṟum nūṟpā itu.
uri: collukku uṟuppāka olikkumpōtum puṇarmoḻiyil vevvēṟu māṟṟaṅkoṇṭu olikkum pōtu, kūṭṭuvaṭivu peṟumpōtum, oliyaṉkaḷ tamakkuriya poruṇmit taṉmiyiṉiṉṟum tirivatilli eṉṟu teḷivuceyvar ilakkaṇap pulavōr.
aḵtāvatu collukku uṟuppāvataṟku muṉṉum puṇarmoḻi māṟṟam peṟuvataṟku muṉṉum ōr eḻuttup peṟṟirunta mutaṟporuṇmiyāṟṟal etuvō atu avaṟṟukkup piṉṉum tiripu paṭātu avvāṟē irukkum.
eḻuttu iyal tiriyā	-	eḻuttukkaḷ tam poruṇmit 								taṉmiyiṉiṉṟu māṟupaṭā.
terintu vēṟicittal	-	nilimoḻi varumoḻiyāka 									varum puṇarccit tiripu; kūṭṭu 									varivaṭivupeṟutal.
tiṟaṉuri
eḻuttu allatu oliyaṉiṉ ilakkaṇatti mīṇṭum niṉivukoḷvōm. oliyaṉāvatu taṉakkeṉṟa poruṇmiyum iṉṉoṉṟiṉi vēṟupaṭuttik kāṭṭum muraṇmiyum uṭiyatu. aṭu, ām; paṭu, pāṭu; āl, āṉ, āḻ; kāl, māl, vāl kāṇka.
ṉakara makara mayakkaṅkaḷ :	nilaṉ-nilam
					aṟaṉ - aṟam
					vaḷaṉ - vaḷam
					iṭaṉ-iṭam
iṉiya coṟkaḷil ṉakarattukkuriya iṭattil makaram mayaṅki varakkāṇalām eṉṟālum ṉakaram iruntāl eṉṉa poruḷuṇṭō apporuḷtāṉ makaram mayaṅkiya piṉṉum uṇṭu. eṉavē mutal eḻuttiṉ iyal tiriyavilli. atu pōlavē pōṉm eṉṟa collil makaram kāl māttiri yāṉālum taṉ poruṇmiyil māṟavilli. ivvāṟē aṭuttuvarum aintu nūṟpākkaṭkum karuttukkoḷka. iviyellām `moḻippaṭutticippiṉum eḻuttiyal tiriyā' eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ.
kuḷam + ciṟitu	=	kuḷañ¨ciṟitu	- makaram ñakaramātal
kuḷam + karam 	=	kuḷakkari	- makaram kakaramātal
āṟu + paṭi 	=	aṟupaṭi	- neṭil kuṟilātal
nī + ai	= 	niṉṉi	- neṭil kuṟilāki ṉakāram peṟal
pala + pala	= 	paṟpala	- lakaravuyirmey ṟakarameyyātal
paṉi + kāy 	= paṉaṅkāy-ai keṭal, amvaral,
iṉṉaṇam varum puṇarmoḻikaḷil ōr oliyiṉ (eḻuttu) vēṟōr; oliyaṉākat tiriyak kāṇkiṉṟōmē eṉṟu viṉavalām. itu teḷiyavēṇṭiya viṉāvē. aṅṅaṉam tiriyuṅkāl nilimoḻiyāka mutalilirunta oliyaṉuṭiya poruḷukkut tiripuṇṭā? kuḷam āṟu nī pala paṉi eṉṟa coṟkaṭkup poruḷmāṟu uṇṭā? ōr oliyaṉ ēṉi yoliyaṉākap puṇarccikkaṇ tiripu peṟṟālum taṉ mutaṉilip poruḷiṉiṉṟu tiripu peṟavilli. itaṉit tāṉ `terintu vēṟicippiṉum eḻuttiyal tiriyā' eṉpar tolkāppiyar.
muppatu eḻuttukkaḷum collukku uṟuppāki oru moḻiyākum pōtum, coṟkaḷ puṇarmoḻiyākumpōtum olittiripukaḷ ēṟpaṭum; māttiri kuṟiyavum ceyyum; col vaṭivaṅkaḷ māṟum. ivi poruṇmiyit tākkā. iṅṅaṉam tiripupeṟṟālum ōr eḻuttu muṉṉirunta poruṇmic cuṭṭut taṉmiyiliruntu tiripu peṟā. aḵtāvatu eḻuttukkup poruṇmit tiripilli. ituvē `eḻuttiyaltiriyā' eṉpataṉ viḷakkam.
mēṟkūṟiya karuttukkaḷi vēṟṟunili vaḻakku. tuṇi nili vaḻakku uṟaḻcci eṉṟa kuṟiyīṭukaḷāl vaḷarntuvarum iṟṟi moḻiyiyalār naṉku viḷakka muṟpaṭuvar. tolkāp piyarō eṉittiripukkum tiripillā mutaṟporuṇmi eṉṟa oru mūlakkūṟu eḻuttukku uṇṭu eṉavum atu eḻuttiyal eṉṟa kuṟipeṟum eṉavum nilipēṟu cuṭṭiyiruppatu mēlum nam cintaṉiyi vaḷarppatākum.
vaṭivumayakkam - ai
(49)	akara yikara; aikāra mākum.
iḵtu aikāra oliyaṉukku uruvumayakkam kūṟum.
akalavuri
mēli nūṟpāvil `eḻuttiyal tiriyā' eṉmaṉār pulavar' eṉappaṭṭatu. aḵtāvatu oliyaṉ taṉporuṇmit tiṟattiṉiṉṟum tiriyātu eṉṟu teḷintōm. innūṟpāmutal “ikarayakaram iṟuti viravum” eṉṟu nūṟpāvari varivaṭivu mayaṅkuvatu paṟṟi viḷakkuvar. immayakkaṅkaḷ ai ōḷa eṉṟa iraṇṭu neṭṭuyirkaḷ paṟṟiyaṉa.
uri: akaravaṭivum ikara vaṭivum cērntu aikāra oliyaṉik kuṟippatākum.
vaḻakku:	mayintaṉ     - mintaṉ	kayili   - kili
			tayilam       - tilam	mayilam   - milam
			ayintiram - aintiram	vayiri      - viri
			vayiram       - viram
tiṟaṉuri
aikāram poruṇmiyuṭiya taṉiyoliyaṉ, ataṟku uyirilum uyirmeyyilum vaṭivuṇṭu. eṉiṉum vēṟu iraṇṭu oliyaṉkaḷiṉ vaṭivaṅkaḷ aikāra oliyaṉukkup poruntiyaṉavāka vaṭiva varalāṟṟil vaḻaṅkiṉa. inta uruvu vaḻakki innūṟpā kūṟum. ivvāṟu vaṭiviyal māṟupaṭiṉum muṉþnūṟpāvil kūṟiyapaṭi eḻuttiyal eṉṉum poruṇmic cuṭṭu māṟupaṭā eṉatteḷika. piṉ varuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkum ivviḷakkam poruntum.
akaramum ikaramum cērntapōtu yakaravuṭampaṭumey peṟuvatu iyalpātaliṉ, a + i = ayi eṉavāyiṟṟu. uṭampaṭumey eṉṟu collāmal.
`akara yikaram aikāra mākum'
eṉṟē nūṟpāvip paṭippatilum tavaṟilli. ivvāṟu koṇṭāl ayi eṉpatu ai ākum eṉṟa nērkaruttut tōṉṟum.

vaṭivu mayakkam - oḷa
(50)	akara vukaram oḷakāra mākum.
iḵtu oḷakāra oliyaṉukku uruvu mayakkam kūṟum.
akalavuri
oḷakāram ōr oliyaṉ; ataṟku ōr taṉivaṭivuṇṭu eṉṟum; akaravaṭivum ukaravaṭivum cērntu oḷakāramāka olikkum.
vaḻakku: 
kavutāri	-	kutāri
	kavuriyar	-	kuriyar
	kavuri		-	kuri
	kavutamaṉār	-	kutamaṉār
	pavuttam	-	puttam
	kavunti	-	kunti 
“tavirā vīkik kavuriyar maruka”	(puṟam. 3)
“amari kumari kavuri camari” 	(cilap. 12.67)
tiṟaṉuri
kutāri eṉpatu kavutāri eṉṟu īreḻuttāka varivaṭivil eḻutappaṭṭālum oḷa eṉṟa oliyaṉ taṉ poruṇmiyiṉiṉṟu māṟupaṭavilli aḵtāvatu vaṭiviyal tirintālum eḻuttiyal tiriyavilli.
akaramum ukaramum cērntapōtu vakaravuṭam paṭumey tāṉē varum ātaliṉa+ u = avu eṉavāyiṟṟu. ēṉit tirāviṭa moḻikaḷāṉa teluṅku kaṉṉaṭa ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum ayi, avu eṉṟu kūṟal oppukkuriyatu. uṭampaṭutal eṉṟu collāmal
akara vukaram oḷakāra mākum
eṉṟē nūṟpāvik koḷvatilum tavaṟilli. iṅṅaṉam koṇṭāl avu eṉpatu oḷa ākum eṉṟa poruḷ nērē tōṉṟiviṭum.
ivviṭaṅkaḷ a i (viram) a i (kili) eṉṟu ikarattiyum ukarattiyum taṉivuyir vaṭivāka eḻutal ēlātu iciniṟivukku aṟikuṟiyāka varum kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷē neṭṭeḻuttiṉpiṉ uyirvaṭivāka iṭiyil niṟpaṉa.
“ai oḷa veṉṟum āyīreḻuttiṟku
  ikara ukaram iciniṟi vākum”
eṉṟapaṭi ici niṟitāṉ iṭiyil uyirvaṭivamāka eḻutappaṭum.
`ceyaṟkip poruḷi ākkamoṭu kūṟal' eṉṟa kiḷaviyākak kaviyaliṉpaṭi aikāram ākum. oḷakāram ākum eṉa viṉip paṭuttiyamiyāl ivi iyaṟkiyila eṉavum. aikāra oḷakāra oliyaṉkaṭku iyaṟkiyāṉa taṉi varavu uṇṭu eṉavum koḷka.
vaṭivumayakkam - ai
(51) akarat timpar yakarap puḷḷiyum
aiyeṉ neṭuñciṉi meypeṟat tōṉṟum.
itu mīṇṭum aikārattiṟku uruvu mayakkam kūṟum.
akalavuri
