mēlinūṟpāvil akarattiṟkuppiṉ ikaravaṭivu vantu aikāram ākum eṉak kaṟṟōm. innūṟpāvil akarattiṟkup piṉ yakaravoṟṟu vantum aiyoli kāṭṭum eṉpar.
uri: akarattiṟkuppiṉpu ikaravaṭivu pōlavē `y’ eṉṟa oṟṟuc cērntum ai eṉṟa neṭṭoli poruḷ peṟa niṟkum.
mey - poruṇmi, neṭuñciṉi - collukku neṭṭuṟuppu ciṉi -uṟuppu.
vaḻakku: 	ayyar - aiyar
	ayntu - aintu
	mayyam - miyam
	vayyam - viyam
mayyoṭum pakittu niṉṟa niṟuttiṉāṉ vayira mārpil
poyyoṭum pakintu niṉṟa kuṇattiṉāṉ pukuntu mōta
veyyava ṉataṉit taṇṭāl vilakkiṉāṉ vilakkalōṭum
kayyoṭu miṟṟu maṟṟak katikaḷam kaṇṭa taṉṟē
eṉpatu kampartarum ceyyuḷ vaḻakku.
tiṟaṉuri
mey eṉpatu meyyoliyaṉ, poruḷ, varivaṭivu eṉṟa pala poruḷoru col. atu īṇṭu poruḷik kuṟikkum. `moḻippaṭutticippiṉum' eṉṟapaṭi, eḻuttu collukku uṟuppātaliṉ, `ai eṉ neṭuñciṉi' eṉṟār. ciṉi eṉpa eḻuttilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭu, yāveṉ, ciṉimici, kuṟiyataṉ, iṟuticciṉi, cuṭṭuc ciṉi nīṭiya, īṟu ciṉiyoḻiya eṉak kuṟiyāṭci peṟum.
uṟuppāka varumpōtu `ay' eṉṟa iraṭṭivaṭivu peṟum eṉṟamiyāl ōreḻuttorumoḻiyākiya taṉiniliyil ituvārātu eṉpatum ai eṉṟa taṉvaṭivē peṟum eṉpatu; teḷivām. “ai viyappākum” eṉpatu uriyiyalnūṟpā āṅkilattilum oṉpatāvatu eḻuttākiya ai oli taṉippaṭuṅ kāli taṉittu oru vaṭivaṉ peṟutali oppu nōkkuka.
māttirik kuṟukkam
(52)	 ōraḷa pākum iṭaṉumā ruṇṭē
	nēruṅ kāli moḻivayi ṉāṉa.
iḵtu aikārattiṟku aḷapukuṟivu kuṟikkum'
akalavuri
mēlinūṟpāvil ai eṉpatu ay eṉṟa vaṭivu mayakkam peṟum eṉṟār. appōtu ārāyumiṭattuc cila colliṭi ay eṉpatu iraṇṭu māttiriyiṉṟi oru māttiriyāka olippatum uṇṭu. itu vaḻakkiṟkum ceyyuṭkum potu.
vaḻakku:
aympatu, ayntu, mayyal, vayyam,
ayvaru ḷoruva ṉaṉṉa aṭicilnūl maṭiyaṉ ēnta
mayvari māli mārpaṉ vāṉcuvi yamirta muṇṭāṉ	 (cīvaka.2744)
tiṟuṉuri
ōraḷapākum iṭaṉāvatu mēlinūṟpāviṟkūṟiya akarattiṟkup piṉṉē yakarap puḷḷivarum nili. itu moḻimutal varum. akara ikaram aikāram ākumpōtu māttirik kuṟivilli. iṭaṉumāruṇṭē eṉpataṉāl arukittōṉṟum eṉpatu karuttilli. ōroru cūḻniliyil varum eṉpatē poruḷ. accūḻniliyaḷavil nirampat tōṉṟum.
aikāram mūviṭattum kuṟukumāyiṉ iraṇṭu māttiri eṉṟa mutal viti poruḷaṟṟatākiviṭum. iraṇṭukkum oṉṟukkum iṭippaṭṭu oṉṟari māttiriyāka varum eṉpatum cempāka marapukku māṟām. `ōraḷapākum' eṉṟu tolkāppiyam colvatu takum.
tēruṅkāl ai moḻivayiṉ eṉṟu pirittu innūṟpā aikāram paṟṟiyatu eṉṟu kūṟalām. ippirivu ciṟappilli. atikārattāṟ peṟalāmātaliṉ vēṇṭiyatumilli.
inta aḷapuk kuṟukkam ceyyuṭkut toṭi yoḻuṅkukku mikavum āḷappaṭum. avviṭaṅkaḷil ay eṉṟa vaṭivu peṟavum eḻutappaṭum.
viravu vaṭivam
(53)	ikara yakaram iṟuti viravum.
iḵtu aikāra vaṭivupaṟṟi oru teḷivu kāṭṭum.
akalavuri
aikāram a i eṉavum vaṭiva mayakkaṅkaḷ peṟum eṉṟu mēlē kūṟiṉāraṉṟē. ivviruvaṭivukaḷ paṟṟi oru teḷivu ceyvar āciriyar. akarattiṟkup piṉṉē ikaram; akarattiṟkup piṉṉē yakaram;
ivviṭattil akaram potuvāka muṉṉiṟkiṉṟatu; ikara yakaramē iṟutiyil vēṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivi eppaṭi varum eṉpatu aiyam. ataṉit teḷivikka eḻuttatu innūṟpā.
uri : aikāra vaṭiviṉ pakutiyākac collappaṭṭa ikaramum yakara voṟṟum iṟutiyiṭattil māṟi varum.
yakaram		 - 	yakarappuḷḷi. iṟuti - akarattiṟkuppiṉṉē
viravum	 -	māṟi niṟkum.
vaḻakku
vaytāṉ, ayntu, aympatu, vayyam, kayyāṟu, paytal, payya, mayyam intac coṟkaḷil irakam vārā; yakarap puḷḷi varum. itupōl mayintaṉ, kayili, tayilam vayiram eṉṟa coṟkaḷil yakarap puḷḷi vārātu; ikaram varum.
itaṉāl nām aṟiyakkiṭappatu eṉṉa? aikārattiṟku ai ay eṉa iruvaki vaṭivaṅkaḷ uḷa eṉiṉum oṉṟu varumiṭattu maṟṟoṉṟu vārātu eṉavum coṟkaṭkut takkapaṭi viravi varum eṉavum koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
tiṟaṉuri
vaṭivu mayakkam kuṟittu ai oḷa iraṇṭē pēcappaṭupavi. ivi coṉṉiliyil eḻuttuvaṭivil kāṇappaṭuvaṉa vātaliṉ, moḻimarapiyalukku vitiyāyiṉa. ivi kūṭṭeli yaṉkaḷ alla. ēṉiyuyirolikaḷ pōlat tūya taṉiyoli yaṉkaḷ ataṉāṟṟāṉ nūṉmarapil ai oḷa eṉpaṉa `īraḷa picikkum neṭṭeḻuttu' eṉa iṭampeṟṟaṉa; piṟappiyalilum miṭaṟṟuppiṟanta vaḷivoliyāka eṇṇap peṟṟaṉa. akara ikaram toṭaṅki aintu nūṟpākkaḷiṟ collappaṭuva tellām ivviraṇṭiṟkuk kūṭṭu vaṭivaṅkaḷum uṇṭu eṉpatuvē. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka pattu nūṟu āyiram eṉṟa eṇkaṭku ya,   , cū, eṉṟa taṉivaṭivaṅkaḷ uḷa; iṉṟu eḻutuvatu pōl ka0, ka00, ka000 eṉṟa kūṭṭu vaṭivaṅkaḷum uḷavaṉṟē. ōraḷavu ivaṟṟi oppunōkkuka. urōma moḻiyilum aintu (ē) pattu (ō) aimpatu (ṭu) ainnūṟu (ṉu) āyiram (ā) ivaṟṟukku oṟṟik kuṟikaḷ uṇṭu. nāṇayat tāḷkaḷum ivvāṟu uḷa.
piṉþṉūlōr ittaku eḻuttu māṟṟaṅkaṭku `eḻuttup pōli' eṉṟa kuṟiyiṭuvar. atu koḷḷattakka kuṟiyīṭē eṉiṉum pōli eṉṟu colvataṟku ilakkaṇa varampu vēṇṭum. oliyaṉiyum poruḷiyum tākkāniliyil oḻuṅkukoḷḷa vēṇṭum. pōliyāvataṟkum orumarapu uṇṭu eṉṟa kōṭpāṭṭi maṟavaṟka. varum māṟṟu vaṭivaṅkaḷum tamiḻoliyaṉkaṭku uṭpaṭṭa vaṭivaṅkaḷē. ellām tamiḻeḻuttukkuḷ māṟutalkaḷē. pōliyāyiṉum aya leḻuttup pukutaṟku vitiyilli. itu eḻuttup pōliyiṉ varampu. ataṉāṟṟāṉ vīracōḻiyamum naṉṉūlum vaṭamoḻi yākkattip pōliyākak koḷḷavilli.
“moḻippaṭut ticippiṉum terintuvē ṟicippiṉum
  eḻuttiyal tiriyā veṉmaṉār pulavar”
eṉṟa nūṟpāviṟku aṭuttuvarum ivvintum tol kāppiyamē nūlakattu vaḻaṅkum eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷ itu kāṭṭu neṟiyākum. moḻippaṭum pōtu ai iru vaṭivum oḷa iruvaṭivum peṟṟālum, aikāram ōraḷapāṉālum poruṇmi kāṭṭum oliyaṉākiya eḻuttiyalil kuṟivupaṭā eṉṟa moḻiyuṇmiyit teḷikiṉṟōm.
oḷa eṉpatu piṟkālattu `av' eṉṟa kūṭṭuvaṭivum peṟṟatu. itaṉip piṉṉilakkaṇikaḷ vitiyākkiṉar. ituvum eḻuttiyal māṟṟāta pōliyātaliṉ marapāyiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam varuvaṉa koḷḷat takuvaṉa.
moḻi mutaleḻuttukkaḷ
uyirmoḻimutalkaḷ
(54)	paṉṉī ruyirum moḻimuta lākum.
iṉivarum pattu nūṟpākkaḷil moḻikku mutalāka varum eḻuttukkaḷik kūṟuvar. itaṉāl moḻi varampu peṟappaṭum; moḻikkāppup pulappaṭum. innūṟpā uyirmoḻimutal kūṟum.
akalavuri
moḻi marapu eṉpatu īṇṭu oru colliyalpu. collukku mutalil varum oliyaṉkaḷ evi? iṟutiyil varum oliyaṉkaḷ evi? ivviraṇṭu iyalpukaḷum tamiḻ moḻi paṟṟit teriya vēṇṭiyavi. uyir paṉṉiraṇṭum collukku mutalāki varum.
uyir - meyyoṭu cērāta taṉiyuyir eṉakkoḷka.
vaḻakku
aḻaku, ācāṉ, ilakkaṇam, īḻam, uṇmi, ūkkam, eḻil,. ēkammi, aintamiḻ, oḻukkam, ōviyam, oḷavi eṉavarum. amerikkā, āciyā, intiyā, erāṉ, uruciyā, ūṭṭi, emarcaṉ, ēcu, airāvati, olimpikku, ōmāṉ, ōḷaraṅkacīppu iviyuṅkoḷka.
tiṟaṉuri
mutaleḻuttu vēṟu; moḻi mutaleḻuttu vēṟu. mutaleḻuttukkaḷ muppatu. avi tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ aṭippaṭi oliyaṉkaḷ. itu eḻuttu nili ivi collukku mutalil varumpōtu moḻimutaleḻuttu eṉappaṭum. itu coṉṉili paṉṉīruyirum eṉpatu muṟṟummi. ellāvuyirkaḷum moḻimutalāki niṟkum eṉpatu poruḷ. a ā i u e o eṉṟivi mutalāka varum coṟkaḷ palavuḷa. ai oḷa eṉpaṉa cila coṟkaḷ aḷavē varum.
paṉṉīruyirum eṉpataṉic ciṟappummiyākak koṇṭāl, ivvāṟu patiṉeṭṭu meyyum moḻi mutalākā eṉṟa kuṟippup peṟappaṭum.
uyirmey moḻimutal
(55)	uyirmey yallaṉa moḻimuta lākā.
itu mey uyirōṭu kūṭi moḻimutalākum eṉṟu kūṟum.
akalavuri
taṉiyuyir moḻimutalāṉatupōlat taṉimey moḻi mutalāki varumā? mey mutalākum āṉāl uyirōṭu cērntu aṅṅaṉam varum.
uri: uyirōṭu kūṭiya meykaḷallātu k c t p eṉṟa taṉippuḷḷi meykaḷ moḻi mutalāka māṭṭā.
uyirmey - uyirōṭu kūṭiya mey.
vaḻakku: kaṇ, kāṇ, kiḷi, kīri ivvāṟu uyirmeyyāka varum. itu tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ iyalpu. cruti, tyākam, krupi, mrukam, nyāyam eṉa vaṭamoḻiyilum, prēk, pḷāk kleym, kḷārk,ṭreyṉ, ṭrām eṉa āṅkilattilum iṉṉa piṟamoḻikaḷilum oṟṟu meykaḷē moḻi mutalām eṉa aṟika. itu ammoḻikaḷiṉ iyalpu.
tiṟaṉuri
mey uyiroṭukūṭi moḻi mutalākum eṉa uṭaṉpāṭṭu naṭippaṭuttāmal “uyirmey yallaṉa moḻi mutalākā” eṉṟu etirmaṟiyāṟ kūṟiyatu ēṉ? taṉimey eṉṟu enniliyilum moḻi mutalākā eṉṟu tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ colliyalpiṉit teḷivu ceyvatām. ayaṟcoṟkaḷ kalantālum tamiḻmi peṟa vēṇṭum eṉpatu moḻiyāṇi. ayaṟcoṟkaḷ koḷḷa vēṇṭiya arumi ēṟpaṭṭāl, tiyāṉam, curuti, ciramam eṉa uyirmeyppaṭutta vēṇṭum. itaṉ viriyi vīracōḻiyattilum naṉþþþṉūlilum kaṟka.
mey moḻi mutalāki vārātu eṉpatum uyir meytāṉ mutalākum eṉpatum innūṟpāviṉ karutti lilli. uyirmey eṉpataṉi oṟṟumi nayamāka ōreḻut tākak koḷḷaṟka. uyirmey - uyiroṭu kūṭiyamey eṉpatu poruḷ. eṉavē moḻi mutalil irumeykaḷ iṇintu vārā eṉpatum uyirkaḷ piṉvara meykaḷ mutalākum eṉpatum teḷiyattakum ilakkaṇam.
uyiriṟu coṉmuṉ uyirvaru vaḻiyum
uyiriṟu coṉmuṉ meyvaru vaḻiyum
meyyiṟu coṉmuṉ uyirvaru vaḻiyum
meyyiṟu coṉmuṉ meyvaru vaḻiyum
ivveṉa aṟiyak kiḷakkuṅ kāli
niṟutta collē kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyeṉ
ṟāyī riyala puṇarnilic cuṭṭē
eṉṟa puṇariyal nūṟpāvil varumoḻimutal meyyāka niṟkum eṉṟu puṇarnili kūṟuvatālum, kacatapa mutaliya moḻi,  ñanamayava eṉum mutalākumoḻi, aḷaviṟkum niṟiyiṟkum moḻi mutalāki valleḻuttiyiyiṉ avveḻuttumikumē, valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi valleḻuttu varuvaḻi eṉṟāṅkup piṉṉiyalkaḷil ellām varumoḻi meymmutaṟpuṇarcci kūṟappaṭuvatālum, uyirmey eṉpataṟku uyiroṭu kūṭiya mey mutalākum eṉṟu meykku aḻuttam koṭuttup poruḷ kāṇka. uyirmey eṉpatu mūṉṟām vēṟṟumiyurupum payaṉum uṭaṉtokka tokiyām. tayirvaṭi, pūrikiḻaṅku pōlak koḷka. uyirum meyyum eṉṟa ummit tokiyō, uyirum meyyum kūṭiya ōreḻuttu eṉṟa aṉmoḻit tokiyō aṉṟu.
meyuyirōṭu cērntu moḻimutalākum eṉpataṉāl peṟappaṭum piṟitoru kuṟippuṇṭu. aṇṇal, naṉṉākaṉ eṉpaṉapōlac colliṉ iṭiyilum iṟutiyilum mey taṉiyēyum niṟkum eṉa aṟika. uyir vaṭivaṅkaḷō eṉiṉ iciniṟi aḷapeṭik kaṇṇaṉṟi colliṉ iṭiyilum iṟutiyilum nillā eṉpatum niṉiyattakum.
puṟā, palakkāy, pirāṇṭutal, tarācu, viyāḻaṉ, vaṟaṭṭi, viḷāmpaḻam eṉṟa cila tamiḻccoṟkaḷic cilar iṉṟu meym mutalāka olippatik kēṭkiṉṟōm. itu piḻi vaḻakku, kaṭiyattakum.
mutaleḻuttām meykaḷē uyiroṭu kūṭiya moḻi mutalākum eṉpatu vitiyātaliṉ, kaṭaṉvāṅkum ayaṟcoṟ kaḷukku nam āytatti moḻi mutalākkum pōkkiṉi eṉ ṉeṉpatu? tēviyiṉṟi appaṭiyē coṟkaṭaṉ koḷvatu mutaṟpiḻi; ataṉmēlum āytatti muṉṉiṟuttuvatu aṭimutaṟ piḻi. aṉpuṭiyōr ceyyum aṟivilmoḻik kēṭu itu. moḻiyiṭip pakuttaṟivu vēṇṭum.
moḻimutal meykaḷ
(56)	ka ta na pamaveṉum āvin bḻuttum
	ellā vuyiroṭuñ cellumār mutalē.
itu paṉṉiraṇṭu uyiroṭum kūṭivarum aintumeykaḷ kūṟum.
akalavuri
patiṉeṭṭaṉuḷ ka ta na pa ma eṉṟa aintu meyyeḻuttuk kaḷum paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirōṭum kūṭi moḻimutalāka varum.
mutal - collukku mutaṉili.


vaḻakku
kaliñar	tamiḻ	nāṉṉākaṉ	pacciyappaṉ 	maṟimali

kiḷḷi	tiruppati	nikaḻcciniral	pirāṉmali	miṉṉiyal
kīḻānelli	tīyiṇappu	nīlakkaṇṭam	pīrkkampū	mīṉavar

tolkāppiyam niṉṟunilavum vaḻakku nūlātaliṉ ikkāla vaḻakkukaḷum kāṭṭappeṟṟaṉa.
tiṟaṉuri
uyirmey eṉpataṟku uyiroṭu kūṭiya mey eṉa mēlē poruḷurikkappaṭṭatu. `ellā vuyiroṭum' eṉṟa naṭi ataṉ viḷakkamākum.
`āvinteḻuttum' eṉṟa ummi ēṉimeykaḷ ciṟcila vuyirkaḷōṭutāṉ varum eṉpataṉik kuṟikkum.

cakaramutal
(57)	carak kiḷaviyum avaṟṟō raṟṟē
	a ai oḷaveṉum mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē
iḵtu eṉpatu uyiroṭum kūṭivarum cakaramutal kūṟum.
akalavuri
cakaramey a ai oḷa (ca, ci, cu) eṉṉum mūṉṟuyir nīṅkalāka ellāvuyiroṭum aḵtāvatu ēṉi oṉpati ṉoṭum kūṭi moḻimutalāka varum.
alaṅkaṭi - tavira, nīṅkalāka.
vaḻakku
	cārappaḷḷam
	civapuri
	cīrkāḻi
	cuḻaṟkaḻakam
	cūḷāmaṇi
	centil
	cēyiḻi
	cokkaliṅkam
	cōḻavēntaṉ
tiṟaṉuri
innūṟpā viḷakkiyaṟ potumi eṉṟa naṭiyākum. oppum curukkamum innaṭiyiṉ payaṉ.

“meyn nili cuṭṭiṉ ellā veḻuttum
  tammuṟ ṟāmvarūum raḻavalaṅ kaṭiyē”
eṉṟa nūṟpāvum innaṭippaṭṭatu.
ca ci cu mutalākiya coṟkaḷ tolkāppiyar kālattil illi. cakarakkiḷavi eṉa innūṟpāvil vantuḷḷatēyeṉiṉ, “mutalāvēṉa tampeyar mutalum” eṉṟa vitippaṭi taṉṉic cuṭṭumaḷavil evveḻuttum mutalāka varumaṉṟō.
“ṭakāra ṇakāram nuṉinā aṇṇam”
“ṟaḵkāṉ ṉaḵkāṉ āyiraṇṭum piṟakkum”
“rakāra ḻakāram āyiraṇṭum piṟakkum”
“lakāra ḷakāram āyiraṇṭum piṟakkum”
eṉṟāṅku ōreḻuttut taṉṉik kuṟikkumpōtu mutalāka varutal uṇarattakum. ḻakarattip piḻiyāka olikkātē; ṟakarattip piḻiyāka eḻutāta eṉṟu nām iṉṟum vaḻakkiṟ collukiṉṟōm allavā? piṟa poruḷic cuṭṭiṉ ivi mutalāka vārā eṉṟu teḷika.
tolkāppiyattukkuppiṉṉē aviya nūlkaḷil cakaramutaṟ coṟkaḷ mikaccila vantaṉa. vaṭamoḻik kāppu ēṟiya kālaṅkaḷil ca ci cu mutalākiya coṟkaḷ palvaḻiyākac kāl koṇṭaṉa.
vaṭamoḻik kalappiṉ varalāṟu peritu. ataṉāl ilakkiya vaḷarcciyilum vakimiyilum pala nalaṉkaḷ piṟantaṉa. moḻiyaḷavil tamiḻc coṟkaḷañciyam iyakkamum ākkamumiṉṟi vaḻakkum vāḻvum iḻantaṉa. tamiḻ vaṭamoḻiyiṟ piṟantatu eṉṉum aḷavukku mayakkam nuṉik kompu ēṟiṟṟu. ilakkaṇak kottilum pirayōka vivēkattilum ippuṉ mayakkam kūṭiṟṟu.
“nāḷaṭiviṟ pulavar taṉittamiḻccoṟkaḷit terintu vaḻaṅkum matukiyiṉmiyāṉum, orōvaḻit teriyiṉum avaṟṟiṉum vaṭacoṟṟoṭi cevikku iṉpam payappaṉavām eṉa māṟukoḷa vuṇarntami yāṉum, taṉittamiḻccuvi kaṇṭu iṉpuṟum tavir arukiṉamiyāṉum, vaṭamoḻi yāḷar uvappat tamiḻiṟ pāṭṭu uri mutaliyaṉa iyaṟṟa vēṇṭumeṉṟum viḻivu mēṟkoṇṭamiyāṉum, potuveḻuttāṉum ciṟappeḻuttāṉum āya vaṭacoṟkaḷ palavaṟṟit tamiḻil varampiṉṟi vaḻaṅki varuvārāyiṉar”
eṉa vaṭamoḻik kalappiṉ muṉippukkaḷi eṉ āciriyar irumoḻip pulamip paṇṭitamaṇip pērācāṉ maṉam vetumpi moḻivar. ivvūṟu pōkki aṉṟē uṇarnta uriyāciriyarkaḷāṉa iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉārk kiṉiyarum vaṭacoṟkaḷum āriyac citivuc coṟkaḷum tamiḻilak kaṇattukkuk koḷḷat takkaviyalla eṉa nallāṇi kāṭṭiṉar.
piṟkālattil tamiḻc coṟkaḷē citintu cakara mutaleḻuttāka urumāṟiṉa.
“caricamaḻppuc caṭṭi caruku cavaṭi
  caḷicakaṭu caṭṭi cavaḷi - cavicaraṭu
  cantu cataṅki caḻakkāti yīriṭattum
  vantataṉāṟ cammutalum vi”
eṉa mayilinātar paṭṭiyaliṭum caraka mutaṟcoṟkaḷ perumpālum citinta tamiḻccoṟkaḷ eṉakkoḷḷa vēṇṭum.  amar, camar; ami - cami; araṇ - caraṇ; avi - capi; amaṇ - camaṇ; atir -catir; aṇṇu - caṇṇu: taci - cati: cākāṭu - cakaṭam eṉṟa olimāṟṟaṅkaḷum olikkuṟippaṭi yākap piṟantaṉavum moḻivērkaḷ tirintaṉavum piṉṉar caraka mutaṉmoḻiyākap perukiṉa. ceyki cikiyāṟiṟṟu. `ciyeṉat tiriyēl' eṉpatu olikkuṟippu. iṉṉa uḷmāṟṟam emmoḻikkum iyaṟki.
nūṉmarapiṟ kūṟiya meymmayakkaṅkaḷ olipaṟṟiyaṉa vātaliṉ piṟaḻat takāvarampuṭiyavi. moḻi mutaleḻuttuk kaḷum varampuṭiyaṉavē eṉṟālum poruḷ paṟṟiyaṉavātaliṉ varampukkuṭpaṭṭa vaḷartaru māṟṟaṅkaḷ ceytukoḷḷalām. palvēṟu nākarikat toṭarpum paṉmoḻiyuṟavum oṉṟi neruṅki varuṅkālaṅkaḷil emmoḻiyiṉ varampum tāṉē akaṉṟu virintu nekiḻntu koṭukkum. muṟiyātu oṭiyātu taḻuvi vaḷintu uṭkoḷḷum akavāṟṟal tamiḻukkut toṉṟutoṭṭu iruntalālaṉṟō tamiḻ eṉṟumuḷa teṉ tamiḻākavum cīriḷamik kaṉṉiyākavum niṉṟu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. ippakkuva nekiḻviṉit tolkāppiyam iṭiyilum muṭivilum cuṭṭikkāṭṭat tavaṟavilli.
“viḷampiya iyaṟkiyiṉ vēṟupaṭat tōṉṟiṉ
  vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅkiṉ uṇarntaṉar oḻukkal
  naṉmati nāṭṭat teṉmaṉār pulavar”
“colliya valla piṟavavaṉ variṉum
  colliya vakiyāṟ curaṅka nāṭi
  maṉattiṉ eṇṇi mācaṟat terintukoṇṭu
  iṉattiṟ cērtti yuṇarttal vēṇṭum
  nuṉittaku pulavar kūṟiya nūlē”
eṉṟa nūṟpākkaḷ etirkālat tamiḻkkāppum vaḷarcciyum nōkkiyavi. kālantōṟum vaḷaravēṇṭiya tamiḻmoḻikku māṟṟamum putumiyum cērttukkoḷḷa urimi vaḻaṅkupavi; eṉiṉum ivvurimiyic cīrtūkkip pakuttaṟintu aṟivōṭu aritākap payaṉpaṭutta vēṇṭiyatu nam kaṭaṉ. ettuṇiyaḷavu iyalpāṉa varampu pōṟṟa muṭiyumō attuṇiyaḷavum marapiṉi uṟutiyākap piṉpaṟṟa vēṇṭum. varampu māṟṟatti eḷiya vaḻiyākak karuti viṭalākātu. karutiṉ kiṭkinti marāmaram pōla moḻi melintatu tuḷippuriyōṭiviṭum. tamiḻppalkalikkaḻakam 28-3-1985 ām nāḷ naṭattiya tamiḻilakkaṇak kōṭpāṭu vaḷarccik karuttaraṅkil nāṉ āṟṟiya `tamiḻil moḻi mutaleḻuttukkaḷiṉ varampu' eṉṟa poḻivuk kaṭṭuri varalāṟṟu muṟiyilum vaḷarcci nōkkilum pala moḻimutaṟ kūṟukaḷip pulappaṭuttukiṉṟatu. ikkaṭṭuriyiṇippiṉi ivviyaliṉ iṟutiyiṟ kāṇka.
collākkamō moḻipeyarppō piṟavakiyō ceyyaviyalāta ayaṟpeyarkkuṟikaḷi mikka viḻippōṭu pōṟṟik koḷḷalām. tamiḻp peruṅkāppiyak kaviñarkaḷ ayaṟpeyarkaḷi āṇṭa tamiḻ vaṭivaṅkaḷ nallamoḻic cuvaṭākum. caṅkaraṉ, cappāṉ, camaṇam, ciṉam, civam, curāṭṭiram pōṉṟavi tavirkkamuṭiyātavi. iviyum meymmayakka vitikku māṟāmal varavēṇṭum. tamiḻiṉi `orupāṭi' yeṉṟu iṉṉāṅku pāṭiya ilakkaṇak kottut tēcikarkūṭa olivarampiyum mayakka varampiyum kaṭakkavilli.
caṅkītam, cattam, caṅkari,¨cañcari, caṅkaṭam, campiratāyam, caṉṉiti, caṉmāṉam, caṉṉam, camīpam, cuntaram, cukkiyam, cukariyam, cupākkiyavati eṉṟa coṟkaḷ muṟṟum tavirkka vēṇṭiyavi. pāratiyāriṉ ātticcūṭiyil 87 mutal 102 variyuḷḷa pāṭalkaḷ allilakkaṇamāṉavi; ivaṟṟukku eḷiya  pala tamiḻc coṟkaḷ uḷa. tamiḻccoṟkaḷ taṉ moḻiyil naṭamāṭum urimi vāyppip paṟikkak kūṭātallavā?
iṅku cakarameykku yāṉkūṟiya moḻimutal viḷakkaṅkaḷ ēṉiya moḻimutal tamiḻ meykaṭkum poruntiyavi.
vakara mutal
(58)	u ū o ō veṉṉum nāṉkuyir
	va veṉ eḻuttoṭu varuta lilli.
iḵtu eṭṭuyiroṭum kūṭivarum vakara mutalkūṟum.
akalavuri
vakaramey u ū o ō eṉṟa nāṉkuyirōṭum vārā. eṉavē a ā i ī e ē ai oḷa eṉṟa eṭṭuyiroṭum kūṭi moḻimutalākum.

tiṟaṉuri
iṉṟuvari māṟutal illi.
vōṭṭu eṉa varumē eṉiṉ āṅkilac colleṉa maṟukka. kuṭavōli, vākkuccīṭṭu, vākkurimi, vākkuc cāvaṭi eṉṟa tamiḻccoṟkaḷ uḷa. ōṭṭāṇṭi eṉpatu tamiḻccol ōṭu eṉpatu kiyil ēntivarum piccip pāttirattik kuṟikkum.

akalavuri

ñoḷkutal
tiṟaṉuri
ñaññi, ñamali, ñamaṉ, ñamara, ñayam eṉa akaramum, ñamiṟu ñamira eṉa ikaramum ñēyam eṉa ēkāramum, piṉvanta vaḻakkāṟukaḷ. ivaṟṟuṭ cila nakarattiṉ tiripukaḷ. ivi koḷḷattakkaṉa. ñakaramum nakaramum tammuḷ uṟaḻntu vanta olippōli eṉṟu moḻi varalāṟu kāṭṭum.
yakara mutal
(60)	āvō ṭallatu yakaram mutalātu.
iḵtu ōruyirōṭu varum yakara moḻimutal kūṟum
akalavuri
paṉṉiraṇṭu, oṉpatu, eṭṭu, mūṉṟu eṉṟa iṟaṅku variciyil moḻimutal meykaḷ kūṟivanta naṉmarapuṭiya tolkāppiyar yakaramey ōruyiroṭumaṭṭum kūṭivarum eṉṟu iṟutiyāka innūṟpāviṟ kūṟuvar.
mutalātu - moḻimutalāka vārātu. kuṟippuviṉi muṟṟu.
vaḻakku: yāṭu, yāppu, yākki, yāmi, yāmam, yāḻ, yāṉi, yāṇṭu, yāvaṉ, yāvaḷ, yāvar, yāvi, yātu.
tiṟaṉuri
ā eṉṟa ōruyirōṭu kūṭittāṉ yakaramey moḻi mutalākum eṉṟu uṭaṉpāṭṭu naṭiyiṟ kūṟāmal etirmaṟi naṭiyiṟ kūṟiyataṉ karuttu eṉṉa? tolkāppiyar kālattum cila piḻiyāṉa vaḻakkukaḷ pukuntu paravivarat toṭaṅki yirukkalām; ataṉic cemmippaṭutta ivvāṟu aḻutta mākac colliyirukkalāmaṉṟō?
yakarattiṉ varalāṟu orupāl iṟakkamum orupāl ēṟṟamum uṭiyatu. yāmutalākiya pala coṟkaḷ, āṭu, ākki, āmi, āṉi, āṇṭu, āṟu, āppu, āy, āḷi, ār eṉṟāṅku yakaram vīḻa ā mutalāyiṉa. itaṉāl ivi akarātiyiṟ palaporuḷoru  collāy  viṭṭaṉa. muṉṉirunta poruṭcemmi maṟintuviṭṭatu. yāḻ, yāṉ, yātu, yāmam eṉṟa oru cila coṟkaḷē tiripuvāyppaṭāmal uytipeṟṟaṉa. paḻaṅkaṉṉaṭam putukkaṉṉaṭam pōl ivvolittiripāl caṅka moḻikkum piṟcaṅka moḻikkum olivaḻakkil veṭṭaṉiya vēṟupāṭu ēṟpaṭṭatu.
vīracōḻiyamum naṉṉūlum a ā u ū ō oḷa eṉṟa āṟuyirōṭum yakaram moḻi mutalākum eṉṟu kūṟuvaṉa. ivvaḷarcci orumaruṅku ēṟṟukkoḷḷa vēṇṭiyatē. yavaṉar, yamuṉi, yacōti, yuṉāṉi, yūtar, yūki, yūpam, yūkam eṉṟa coṟkaḷ tavirkka muṭiyātavi. cila coṟkaḷi ecur, acōti, entiram, iyantiram, ukam, ōcaṉi eṉa vēṟu mutaleḻuttākkalum uṇṭu. yuttam, yōkkiyam, yuvaṉam pōṉṟa coṟkaḷiṉ poruṭṭu muṉṉarē pala tamiḻc coṟkaḷ iruttaliṉ tēviyaṟṟavi. iṅṅaṉam pakuttaṟintu koḷvaṉa koṇṭum. tirippaṉa tirittum. taḷḷuvaṉa taḷḷiyum moḻināṭṭam kāṇavēṇṭum. ittakiya nāṭṭam moḻikkaruvik kalikkātu ūṭṭam tarum iṭikkāla vaḷarcciyip pāṅkupeṟac cīrtūkkātu ilakkaṇa viḷakkattiṉ āciriyar `āvōṭu yakāramum mutalum' eṉṟu tolkāppiyattiṉpaṭiyē tiruppi moḻital ilakkaṇa marapākātu.
ellām moḻimutal
(61) 	mutalā vēṉa tampeyar mutalum.
itu muṉkūṟappaṭāta meykaḷum moḻi mutalākum eṉakkūṟum.
akalavuri
meykaḷ 18: ivi 12 uyirōṭum kūṭa 216 uyirmeyyākum itukāṟum kūṟappaṭṭavi taṉivuyirkaḷ. 12 uyir
		ka ta na pa ma 	- 12	uyiroṭum	60	uyirmey
		ca		- 9	        ”	 9	       ”
		va		- 8 	       ”	8	       ”
		ña		- 3	       ”	3	       ”
		ya		- 1       	       ”	1	       ”
1. 	eñciyavi	ca		- 3	uyiroṭum	3	uyirmey			kūṟappaṭṭa	va		- 3	uyiroṭum	3	uyirmey			vaṟṟuḷ :	ña		- 9	       ”	9	       ”
		ya		- 11	       ” 	11	       ”
2.	muṟṟum kūṟappaṭātavi (9)	- 12	       ” 	108					ṅa ṭa ṇa ra la ḻaḷa ṟa ṉa
3.	taṉimeykaḷ 					18
uri: mēlē moḻi mutalākum eṉṟu kūṟiya ka ta na pa ma ca va ña ya eṉṟa oṉpatu meykaḷuḷ irupattēḻu uyirmeykaḷum aṟavē kūṟāta ṅa ṭa ṇa ra la ḻa ḷa ṟa ṉa eṉṟa oṉpatu meykaḷiṉ nūṟṟeṭṭu uyirmeykaḷum patiṉeṭṭut taṉimeykaḷum tammiyē peyarākac cuṭṭic collum pōtu avvaḷavil ammoḻikku mutalāka varum. itaṉ karuttu ellā eḻuttum tammiccuṭṭuṅkāl mutalākum eṉpatām.
vaḻakku: ṅā eṉṟa oliyi naṉku colka. rakarattiyum ṟakarattiyum vēṟupaṭa olikka nakarattiyum ṉakarattiyum oḻuṅkākac colluvāy. lakara ḷakara ḻakara vēṟupāṭukaḷi uṇar. ṇā, ḻu, ṅū, ṭu eṉṟa vaṭivaṅkaḷic cevvaṉam eḻutuvāy.
ṉakāra iṟuvāy, laḷaḵkāṉ muṉṉaṉ, ṇa ṉaḵkāṉ muṉṉar, ṭakāra ṉakāram eṉṟu iṅṅaṉam ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷil eḻuttukkaḷ tam peyar kūṟuṅkāl mutalākavarum.
tiṟaṉuri
poruṇmi, taṟporuṇmi eṉap poruṭcuṭṭu iruvakippaṭum. koli, taṟkoli eṉṉumpōtu piṟar  kolikku aṭi vēṇṭātatu pōla, col kāṭṭum piṟa poruṇmikku aṭiyilli.
“coṉmi teritalum poruṇmi teritalum
  colli ṉākum eṉmaṉār pulavar”
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam poruṇmi eṉṟē āḷutaliyum taṟporuṇmiyic coṉmi eṉaṟu cuṭṭutaliyum kāṇka. “ellāc collum poruḷ kuṟittaṉavē” eṉṟa nūṟpā kuṟippatu poruṇmiyēyākum.
taṉṉittāṉē pārāṭṭikkoḷḷum taṟpukaḻcci pukaḻākātu piṟar pārāṭṭum pōtutāṉ pukaḻ eṉappaṭum. atupōl orucol taṉṉic cuṭṭum coṉṉili viṭṭup piṟaporuḷkaḷik kuṟikkumpōtutāṉ col eṉṟa peyarkku uriyavākum.
	`paṉṉiruyirum’ eṉṟa nūṟpā mutal `āvōṭallatu' eṉpatu vari poruṇmi paṟṟik kūṟappaṭṭaṉa: aḵtāvatu ulakap poruḷ kuṟittaṉa.
eḻuttum collum tammaḷaviṟ pēcappaṭa vēṇṭiya iṭaṅkaḷum uṇṭu. ivi perumpālum ilakkaṇavulakiṟkum payiṟcik kaḷattiṟkum uriyavi. avvaḷavil iṉṟiyami yātavi. ellā meyyeḻuttukkaḷum tammic cuṭṭuṅkāl meymmayakka varampilli eṉṟu nūṉmarapiṟ kūṟiṉāraṉṟē. atupōl īṅkum ellā veḻuttukkaḷum tam peyaric colla vēṇṭiya nilivarumpōtu moḻi mutalākum eṉṟār. iviyellām taṉṉurimineṟi eṉappaṭum. itaṉālum tolkāppiyam naṭimuṟip pārvi taḻuviyatu eṉpatu teḷivām.
moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram
(62)	kuṟṟiya lukaram muṟippeyar maruṅkiṉ
	oṟṟiya nakaramici nakaramoṭu mutalum.
nakarameyyiṉkaṇ kuṟṟiyalukara moḻimutal kūṟum.
akalavuri
nunti, nuntāy eṉṟa coṟkaḷ itaṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷ. ivi muṟip peyarkaḷ. nunti eṉṟa collil n + u +n + ti eṉa iraṇṭu nakaraṅkaḷ uḷa. mutalāvatu nakarattiṉmēl varum ukaram kuṟṟiyalukaramāki moḻi mutalākum.
oṟṟiya nakaram - puḷḷiyuṭiya iraṇṭāvatu nakaram.
mutalum - moḻi mutalākum; kuṟippuviṉi muṟṟu.
tiṟaṉuri
itu cārpeḻuttu ātaliṉ uyir mutalum meym mutalum kūṟimuṭittapiṉ colluvar āciriyar.
“ u ū oōveṉṉum nāṉkuyir
  vaveṉ eḻuttoṭuvaruta lilli”
eṉṟa nūṟpā nāṉkuyirum vakarattoṭu vārā eṉṟu naṭippaṭuttiṉālum, vakaramey nāṉkuyiroṭu vārā eṉpatuvē naṭiyiṉporuḷ. vakaramē iṅku moḻimutal mey. atupōl innūṟpāviyum karutuka. kuṟṟiyalukaram nakaramoṭu mutalākum eṉpataṟku nakarameymēl kuṟṟiyalukaram ūrntu varum eṉavum nakarameyyē moḻimutal eṉavum koḷka.
ivvāṟu koḷḷa vēṇṭiyataṉ kaṭappāṭu eṉṉa? moḻi mutaleḻuttu, moḻiyīṟṟeḻuttu eṉṟu varampu ceyvaṉa vellām puṇarcci nōkkiyaṉṟē; nili moḻiyīṟum varumoḻi mutalum puṇarum viti kūṟutaṟkaṉṟē. nunti, nuntāy varumoḻiyākuṅkāl nakaramey mutalā? ukaram mutalā? kuṟṟiyalukaram mutalāyiṉ, īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇarcci kūṟiyatupōla mutaṟkuṟṟiyalukarap puṇarvu taṉiyē kūṟavēṇṭāmā?
kalittoki marutakkaliyil nunti eṉṟa collāṭci palkivarak kāṇalām.
“uḷameṉṉā nuntimāṭ ṭevva muḻappār”
“uyviṉṟi nunti nalaṉuṇa”
“nalkātu nunti puṟamāṟap paṭṭavar”
“varuntiyām nōykūra nuntiyi yeṉṟum”
“vaṉappellā nuntiyi yoppiṉum”
eṉṟu varum palaviṭaṅkaḷilum, `vañci mūtūr maṇimaṇṭapat tiṭi nunti tāṇiḻa liruntōy' eṉṟa cilappatikārak kātiyilum iyalpāṉa nakarattukkuriya puṇariyal vitikaḷē peṟukiṉṟaṉa.
ukaram varumoḻiyāyiṉ ippuṇarvitikaḷ peṟā. ātaliṉ ēṉi meykaḷoppa nakaramē moḻimutal eṉavum kuṟṟiyalukarac cārpuṭiyatu eṉavum muṟip peyaraḷavil iḵtu amiyum eṉavum teḷivōmāka.
kukaṉoṭum aiva rāṉēm muṉpupiṉ kuṉṟu cūḻvāṉ
makaṉoṭum aṟuva rāṉēm emmuḻi aṉpiṉ vanta
akaṉamar kāta liya niṉṉoṭum eḻuva rāṉēm
pukalaruṅ kāṉan tantu putalvarāṟ polintā ṉunti
eṉṟa kampar kāppiyattil `polintā ṉunti' eṉṟa toṭar polintāṉ + nunti eṉavum polintāṉ + unti eṉavum pirikka iṭaṅkoṭukkum. mutaṟpirivukku nakarameyyē varumoḻiyākum. `uṉṉoṭum' eṉṟillāmal `niṉṉoṭum' eṉṟa naṭiyi nōkkiṉ nunti eṉṟa pirippē perumitam tarum.
nuntāṭu. nunnakar, nunnutal eṉṟa coṟkaḷilum oṟṟiya nakaramici nakaram iruppiṉum, muṟippeyaraṉmiyiṉ kuṟṟiyalukaram ākā. muṟippeyarkkē ivvukaratti varampu ceytār. appeyar vaḻakkaṭippaṭṭuk kuṟintamiyiṉ.
puṇarcci nili
(63) 	muṟṟiya lukaramoṭu poruḷvēṟu paṭāatu
	appeyar maruṅkiṉ niliyiya lāṉa
itu moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukarattukku oru teḷivu kūṟum.


akalavuri
immoḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram nunti eṉṉum muṟippeyariṭattu niliyāka varuvatāl muṟṟiyalukarat tōṭu poruḷ māṟupaṭuvatilli. aḵtāvatu puṇarcci yilakkaṇam vēṟupaṭuvatilli.
tiṟaṉuri
eḻuttatikāra iṟutiyil kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal eṉa oru taṉiyiyal uṇṭu. itu kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟkē uriya iyal. īṟṟukkuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ puṇarccic ceyki kaḷumē ivviyaliṟ collappaṭuvaṉa. moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiya lukaramum iruttaliṉ ataṉ vitikaḷum ivviyaṟkaṇ collappaṭa vēṇṭumaṉṟō? aḵtāvatu, uyirkkum meykkum muṉ nunti eṉpataṉ nakaram varumoḻiyāka, varumpōtu eṉṉa vitipeṟum eṉṟu colvatumuṟi. itu ivviyalil iṭam peṟavilli. aṅṅaṉam collāmiyiyum colla vēṇṭāmiyiyum teḷivupaṭuttuvatutāṉ `muṟṟiyalukaramoṭu' eṉṟa inta nūṟpāvākum. eṅṅaṉam teḷivikkiṉṟār?
īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukarac coṟkaḷ palporuḷuṭiyavāyp palavātaliṉ īṟupaṟṟiya potu niliyil puṇarvitikaḷik kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal parantupaṭa iyampukiṉṟatu. uyirmayaṅkiyal, puḷḷi mayaṅkiyalkaḷ pōla ivviyalum īṟṟaṭippaṭi koṇṭatu.
moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukaramōveṉiṉ muṟip peyariṭattu ōriru collil varuvatu. nunti eṉpataṉi muṟṟiyalukaramāka olittup pārttālum poruḷ vēṟupaṭuva tilli. muṟippeyar eṉac colliṭam variyaṟukkap paṭutaliṉ itaṉ karuttu eṉṉa? eṉac colliṭam  variyaṟukkappaṭutaliṉ itaṉ karuttu eṉṉa? muṟṟiyalukaram vanta nukam, nuṉi, nutal, nuṇmi eṉṟa nakarac coṟkaḷ puṇarum vitikaḷē kuṟṟiyalukaram vanta nuntikkum poruntum. ēṉi nakaraṅkēḷāṭu innakarattiṟku puṇarcci vēṟupāṭiṉmi itaṉāṟ peṟappaṭum. “ña namaya vaveṉum mutalākumoḻiyum” eṉṟa toki marapu nūṟpāvil nuntiyatu eṉa uriyāciriyarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum eṭuttuk kāṭṭut tantiruppatu eṉ viḷakkattukkuc cāṉṟākum. mutaleḻuttāṉa uyirmutaṉilikkum meymmuta ṉilikkum aṭuttu iccārpu mutaṉiliyic cuṭṭiyami yāṉum ikkaruttu valiyuṟum.
nunti kuṟṟiyalukaramātaliṉ, ukarattiṉ mēṟpuḷḷi peṟum. “avitām kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukaram āytam” eṉac cārpeḻutti eṇṇiyaviṭattum, “avviyal niliyum ēṉi mūṉṟē” eṉa ari māttiri matippiṭṭa viṭattum īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉṟa ciṟu kuṟippumilli. ātaliṉ puḷḷippēṟum arimāttiri matippum moḻi mutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟkum uṇṭām. (nunti.)
moḻiyiṟuti yeḻuttukkaḷ
(64)	uyir oḷa eñciya iṟuti yārum
iḵtu uyir iṟutikaḷ kūṟum.
akalavuri
moḻimutaleḻuttukkaḷi niralpaṭa vititta āciriyar moḻiyiṟuti yeḻuttukkaḷip patiṉmūṉṟu nūṟpākkaḷil moḻiyat toṭaṅkuvar.
uri: uyir oḷa nīṅkalāṉa a i u e o ā ī ū ē ai ō eṉṟa uyirkaḷ iṟutiyāka varum.
eñciya - eñciyavi, eñciyavuyirkaḷ.
vaḻakku: uyir: a i u cuṭṭuniliyil īṟāyiṉa. ēe eṉat tēṟṟattilum ōo eṉac ciṟappilum īṟāka vantaṉa. ā ī ū ē ai ō - poruḷ niliyil īṟāyiṉa.
tiṟaṉuri
iḵtu ōraṭic curukka nūṟpāvāka iruntālum muṉpiṉ varum vitikaḷik koṇṭukoḷḷuvaṉavum vilaṅku vaṉavum teḷivākak kāṇalām.
“kuṟṟeḻut tintum moḻi niṟipilavē”eṉṟu muṉṉark kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, poruṇmi niliyil avi īṟākā. “ a i u ammūṉṟuñ¨ cuṭṭu” eṉṟa kuṟippu niliyil avi mūṉṟumē īṟāvaṉa. ataṉālaṉṟō uyir mayaṅkiyalil ōreḻuttāyiṉum ivaṟṟic cuṭṭiṉ iṟuti eṉa moḻintu puṇarvitikaḷ kūṟiṉar.
“tēṟṟa ekaramum ciṟappiṉ ovvum
mēṟkū ṟiyaṟki valleḻuttu mikā”
eṉa īṟupaṟṟi uyirmayaṅkiyalil puṇarcciviti kūṟutalāṉum,
“teḷiviṉ ēyum ciṟappiṉ ōvum
  aḷapiṉ eṭutta iciya veṉpa”
eṉa iṭiyiyalil aḷapeṭiyik kuṉṟici niṟikka vanta aṟikuṟikaḷ eṉṟa koḷḷāmal poruṭ kuṟippiṉa eṉṟu ēkāra ōkārattōṭu oliyaṉpaṭa iṇittuk kūṟutalāṉum ē e koṇṭāṉ,  ō ō koṇṭāṉ eṉpavaṟṟil ekarokaram nili moḻiyīṟukaḷākak koḷḷappaṭum. eṉiṉum ivi a i uccuṭṭuppōlat taṉi niliyīṟalla eṉpatum niṉiyat takum.
āa īi  ūu ēe ōo eṉak kuṟil aintum aḷapeṭiyil uyir īṟāyiṉ eṉṟu iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum koḷvar. “kuṟṟuyir aḷapiṉ īṟām” eṉṟu pavaṇantiyārum iyampuvar. moḻiyīṟukaḷ colvatum moḻimutaṉilikaḷ colvatum.
“niṟutta colliṉ īṟāka keḻuttoṭu
  kuṟittuvaru kiḷavi mutaleḻut tiyiya”
eṉṟapaṭi puṇarcci varampukkaṉṟō? iciniṟikka vanta kuṟṟuyirkaḷ aḷapeṭiyil īṟākumeṉṟāl, avi puṇarccikku uriyaṉavākumā? paṭāa eṉpatu puṇarcciyil ākāravīṟā, akaravīṟā?
“kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarum ōreḻuttu moḻikkum
  aṟiyat tōṉṟum akarak kiḷavi”
eṉavarum iṭaṅkaḷil ellām neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷē nilimoḻiyīṟāka vitikkappaṭṭuḷa.
`e eṉa varumuyir' eṉavum. `ovvumaṟṟē' eṉavum īṟāka vantaṉavāleṉiṉ avi tammiyē cuṭṭik koḷvaṉa vātalāl, potuviyaṟkiyākum.
“kuṉṟici moḻivayiṉ niṉṟici niṟikkum
  neṭṭeḻut timpar otta kuṟṟeḻuttē”
eṉat tolkāppiyamum,
icikeṭiṉ moḻimutal iṭikaṭi nilineṭil
aḷapeḻum avaṟṟavaṟ ṟiṉakkuṟil kuṟiyē
eṉa naṉṉūlum ikkuṟilkaḷi olivaṭivākak kūṟāmal aṟikuṟi vaṭivākak kūṟutal cintaṉikkuriyatu. itaṉāl  avimoḻikku īṟāka eṉpatum puṇarccikkuk karuviyākā eṉpatum teḷivu.
innūṟpā uyirkkum uyirmeykkum potu eṉakkoṇṭu viḷa, palā, kiḷi, kurī, paṉi eṉavāṅku muṉṉiyurikaḷ kāṭṭukkaḷ tarum. “uyir oḷa eñciya” eṉṟu uyirmēl vittu eñciyavi eṉṟamiyāl, uyirmey koḷḷa iṭaṉilli. aṅṅaṉam koṇṭāl ku, veḷa eṉṟu oḷakāram uyirmeyyāy varutaliṉ, eñciyavi eṉpataṉuḷ aṭaṅkat taṉinūṟpā nuvala vēṇṭiya nili vārātaṉṟō? ātaliṉ eñciyavi eṉpatu oḷakāram oḻinta taṉiyuyirkaḷiyē kuṟikkum. “paṉṉīruyirum moḻi mutalākum”eṉat taṉiyuyirmutaṉilikku orunūṟpā yāttatu pōl, taṉiyuyir iṟuti nilikkum taṉippā yāttār eṉak koḷka. aṭuttuvarum nūṟpākkaḷ uyirmeyyīṟu paṟṟiyaṉa.
epporuḷ, eccol, evvayiṉ, evvaḻi eṉṟu ekaravuyirum iṟutiyil varum vaḻakki ēṟṟukkoḷka. “cuṭṭumutal vayiṉum ekara mutal vayiṉum” eṉap puḷḷimayaṅkiyalil āciriyar uṭampoṭu puṇarttik kūṟiyiruppatu karutattakum.
oḷa īṟu
(65)	kaḷavō ṭiyiyiṉ oḷavu mākum.
itu oḷakāra vuyirmey varavu kūṟum.
akalavuri
mēlē taṉiniliyāka vārāteṉappaṭṭa oḷakāram kakarattōṭum vakarattōṭum cēriṉ ku, veḷa eṉa iṟutiyāka varum.
vaḻakku
ku peyarākavum viṉiyākavum varum. ku - koḷḷu eṉṟa tāṉiyam. veḷa - viṉiccol.


tiṟaṉuri
tamiḻil oḷakāra iṟutiyuṭiya coṟkaḷ iraṇṭē eṉpatu varampu. iṅṅaṉam tamiḻ moḻik kaḷañciyattil cila coṟkaḷiṉ eṇṇikkiyi eṭuttu viḷampuvatu tolkāppiya neṟi. itaṉāl tamiḻ muḻuttaṟinta āciriyaṉ pulamiyum perumiyum nūṉmatippum māṇakkar uḷḷaṅkaḷiṟ patiyum.
ekaravuyir
(66)	e eṉa varumuyir meyyī ṟākā
iḵtu ekarattiṉ iyalpu kūṟum.
akalavuri
“uyiroḷa eñciya iṟutiyākum” eṉṟa nūṟpāvil ekaram taṉiniliyāka iṟuti peṟātu eṉṟu kaṇṭōm. oḷakāram aṅṅaṉam iṟuti peṟāviṭiṉum, ku veḷa eṉa mēli nūṟpāviṟ meykku iṟutiyāka varuvatiyum kaṇṭōm. atupōl ekaram enta meykkāyiṉum īṟākavāvatu varumō eṉṟa viṉā eḻukiṉṟatu. ekaram emmeykkum īṟākātu eṉṟu viṭiyiṟuppatu innūṟpā.
tiṟaṉuri
akara ikara ukaraccuṭṭuppōla ekaram taṉiniliyilum īṟākātu; ku, veḷa, no pōla uyirmeyyiṉum īṟupeṟātu. eṉavē tamiḻc coṟkaḷil īṟākum iyalpu ekarattiṟku illi. ekaravuyir eṉṉātu e eṉa varumuyir eṉṟa iḻuppunaṭiyāl ataṉ taṉmikkuṟivu kāṇka. moḻi mutalilō eṇ eṉat taṉi uyirākavum peṇ, cey eṉa meyyoṭu kūṭiya uyirākavum varum.
cettu, centaṉaṉ, centaṉaḷ eṉa viṉiyecca vaṭivilum muṟṟu vaṭivilum, caṅkavilakkiyattil cila coṟkaḷ payiṉṟu varakkāṇkiṉṟōm. oppākak karuti, oppākak karuti mayaṅki eṉa itaṟkup poruḷuṇṭu. ce eṉṟa uyirmey itaṉ mutaṉiliyāka irukkavēṇṭum. iṟattaṟ poruḷil varum cettu, cettāṉ eṉpataṟkuc cā eṉpatu pakutiyākum. oppākak karuti eṉṟa poruḷuṭiya ce eṉ pakuti tolkāppiyattiṟkuppiṉ tōṉṟi cila nūṟṟāṇṭu vaḻakkiliruntu naṉṉūl kālattiṟku muṉpē vaḻakkiṟantatu. tīmi naṉmiyi aḻikkum eṉṟa ōrukoḷkippaṭi, iṟattaṟ poruḷuṭiya vaṭivaṅkaḷ, oppupporuḷ tanta vaṭivaṅkaḷi vaḻakkoḻiyac ceytaṉa. māṇṭa, māṇṭāṉ eṉpaṉavaṟṟi oppunōkkuka.
venta, ventu, ventāṉ eṉṟa vaṭivaṅkaḷil ve eṉṟa vakara ekaram mutaṉiliyākap paṭṭālum. vē eṉpataṉ kuṟukkamākavē karutuvatuṇṭu. muṉṉili ēval vaṭivu atuvātaliṉ.
okaravuyir
(67)	ovvum aṟṟē navvalaṅ kaṭiyē.
iḵtu okarattiṉ iyalpu kūṟum.
akalavuri
okarattiṉ iṟutiniliyum ekaram pōla meyyīṟākā. nakarameyyoṭu cērntu no eṉa ōriṭattē varum eṉakkoḷka. piṟameykaḷōṭu vārā.
vaḻakku:	 no viṉiccol, ōreḻuttorumoḻi.
“a u aṟiyā aṟivil viṭimakaṉē
  noaliyal niṉṉāṭṭi nī”
tiṟaṉuri
ekaravuyirpaṟṟi muṉkūṟiyapaṭi, okaram ōo perumi eṉacciṟappup poruḷil aḷapeṭiyāka iṟutiyil varum. a i uc cuṭṭupōlat taṉiyuyirāka vārātu. ekarattiviṭa okarattiṟku oru naṉṉiliyuṇṭu. atutāṉ na eṉṟa oru meyyilāvatu īṟāka varuvatu.
otta, ottu, ottāṉ, oppu eṉṟa coṟkaḷil, o eṉṟa taṉiyuyir mutaṉili eṉṟu koḷḷalām pōla niṟkiṉṟatu. eṉiṉum o eṉpatu apporuḷil taṉittuvarum viṉi vaḻakkiṉmiyāṉ koḷḷavilli.
ē ō
(68)	ē ō veṉumuyir ñakārat tilli.
itu ē ō eṉṟa īṟukaḷiṉ nili kūṟum.
akalavuri
ē ō eṉṟa iru neṭiluyirkaḷ ñakarameyyoṭu iṟutiyāka varuvatilli. ñē ñō eṉṟu vārā. eṉavē varattakka a ā i ī u ū ai eṉṟa ēḻuyiroṭum iṟutiyāka varum.
vaḻakku
uriña, uriñā, uriñi, uriñī, uriñu, uriñū, maññi
tiṟaṉuri
ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ meykaḷiṉ mēl īṟāka varuvaṉa perumpālum iṭic coṟkaḷāka irukkum.
ñakaram uriñ¨eṉa oru kiḷaviyil maṭṭum īṟāka varutaliṉ pala uyirkaḷiyum īṟākap peṟum tiṟamuṭiyataṉṟu.
u ū
(69) u ū kāram navvoṭu navilā.
itu u ū eṉṟa īṟukaḷiṉ nili kūṟum.
akalavuri
mutaluri u ū eṉṟa īruyirkaḷ nakarameyyoṭum vakara meyyōṭum iṟutiyāka vārā. nu nū,  vu vū eṉa iṟuti vārā. eṉavē a ā i ī ē ai o ō eṉa nakaram eṭṭuyiroṭum a ā i ī ē ai ō oḷa eṉa vakaram eṭṭuyiroṭum varum.
vaḻakku: poruna, nā, paḻani, nī, nē,poruni, no, nō, uva; uvā, kuvi, vī. vē, oḷavi, vāḻvō, veḷa.
tiṟaṉuri
ivvurippaṭi, nu, nū, vu, vū eṉavārā. āṉāl porunu. nū (eḷ) porunū (ceyyū eṉṉum viṉiyeccam) eṉavarak kāṇkiṉṟōm. ēṉi mūṉṟum varutal aritāka iruntālum vukaravīṟu kaḷavu. uṟavu, kaṉavu, iravu eṉap pala coṟkaḷil uḷa. ātaliṉ ivvuri kuṟipāṭuṭiyatu. ātaliṉaṉṟō `navilā' eṉṟataṉālē vakaravukaram katavu, turavu, kuvavu, puṇarvu, nukarvu, novvu, kavvu eṉap payiṉṟu varutaluṅkoḷka eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉayar nirappuri eḻutavēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu.
iraṇṭām uri
ukaram nakara meyyyoṭu iṟuti vārātu. ūkāram varaka meyyōṭu iṟuti vārātu. ivvāṟu niraṉiṟiyākap poruḷ koḷḷalām. eṉavē nu iṟutiyum vū iṟutiyum vārā.
tiṟaṉuri
“nakara viṟutiyum ataṉō raṟṟē” eṉpataṉāl porunukkaṭitu eṉa nakara vukaram varak kāṇkiṉṟōm. ku, veḷa eṉpaṉa viṉiccollātaliṉ kuvū, veḷavū eṉac ceyyū eṉṟa vāyppāṭṭu viṉiyeccamāka vaṭivupeṟa iṭaṉuṇṭu. ātaliṉ ivvuriyum kuṟipāṭuṭiyatu.
mūṉṟāmuri
“uūkāram navvoṭu navilā” eṉap pāṭaṅkoṇṭu nu, nū eṉpaṉa iṟuti vāra eṉap poruḷ ceytalumuṇṭu. īṇṭu vakaram vilakkappaṭukiṉṟatu.
tiṟaṉuri
muṉṉurikaḷil ittakiya pāṭamilli. itu ūkamē. itu pāṭamāyiṉ `ovvum aṟṟē navvalaṅkaṭiyē' eṉpataṉi aṭuttatāka irukkavēṇṭum. mēlum porutu eṉavum porunū eṉavum eḷ eṉṟa poruḷil nū eṉavum varutaliṉ, vārā eṉṟa uri mikak kuṟipāṭuṭiyatu.
tiṟaṉmuṭipu
kaḷavu, varavu, puravu, tuṟavu, uṟavu, virivu eṉavāṅku eṇṇiṟanta coṟkaḷ tolkāppiyattilē irukkumpōtu, vakaravukarayiṟuti vārātu eṉṟal eṅṅaṉam? itu perun taṭiyātaliṉ mutaluri koḷḷumāṟilli. niraṉiṟiyākap poruḷ ceyyum iraṇṭāvatu uriyē perumpālum poruntum.
kālappōkkil nūṟpāvil ētum tiripu nikaḻntirukkumō? innūṟpā mēlum nōkkattakum.
cu iṟuti
(70)	ucca kāram irumoḻik kurittē.
itu cukara iṟutikku variyaṟi kūṟum.
akalavuri
ukarattōṭu kūṭiya cakaram aḵtāvatu eṉṟīṟu iraṇṭē coṟkaḷil varum.
vaḻakku: 		pacu, mucu,
tiṟuṉuri
innūṟpā īreḻuttorumoḻi paṟṟiyatu. iraṇṭiṟat ticikkum toṭarmoḻi ukarac coṟkaḷ varampila. iṭakkark kāṟṟāṉa col olik kuṟippātaliṉ iṭam peṟavilli.
pu iṟuti
(71)	uppa kāram oṉṟeṉa moḻipa
	iruvayiṉ niliyum poruṭṭā kummē.
itu pukaraviṟutikku variyaṟiyum poruḷ niliyum kūṟum.
akalavuri
ukarattoṭu kūṭiya pakaram aḵtāvatu pukaraviṟuti orē oru colliṟṟāṉ varum eṉṟu colluvar. avvāṟu varumpōtu taṉṉic cārntum piṟitic cārntum kuṟikkum poruḷatākum.
vaḻakku
tapu eṉṟa viṉiccol. itu aḻivu, nīkkam eṉṟa  poruḷil varum. “tāytapa varūum talippeyal nili” (tol.puṟam) - tāy taṉṉi aḻittuk koḷḷa varum cērkki nili. iṅku taṉpāl vantatu. `kuṟunaṭippuṟaviṉ taputiyañci” (puṟam.43) eṉpatum atu.
“aril tapat terintu”	 (pāyiram)
“kātali iḻanta taputāranili” 	(tol.puṟam.)
“mātaputta vēlōy”	 (paripā.18)
“maṟantapak kaṭantē”	 (patiṟ.11)
“matan tapu ñamali”	 (malipaṭu. 609)
iviyellām piṟitiṉpāl vantaṉa. aḻivu piṟaporuḷāyiṉa.
tiṟaṉuri
`iruvayiṉ niliyum poruṭṭākum’ eṉpatil iruvayiṉ yātu?
“ituceyal vēṇṭum eṉṉuṅ kiḷavi
  iruvayiṉ niliyum poruṭṭā kummē
  taṉpā lāṉum piṟaṉpā lāṉum”
eṉṟa viṉiyiyal nūṟpā innūṟṟapāvukku viḷakkamākum. eṉavē uppakāramākiya tapu eṉṟa orucol tāṉaḻitaliyō piṟitaḻi taliyō oruṅkē kuṟikkum.
veḷu eṉṟa viṉiccollum iṅṅaṉam varutal kāṇka.
taṉviṉi piṟaviṉi eṉpataṟkuc ceyappaṭu poruḷuṇṭu. anta ilakkaṇa nōkkil tapu eṉpatu cuṭṭappaṭavilli. taṟpukaḻcci, taṟkoli, taṟperumi, taṟkuṟi eṉṟāṟpōl varuvatu taṉpāl eṉavum ayalāka varuvatu piṟaṉpāl eṉavum paṭum. piṟaṉ eṉpatu īṇṭu orupāṟ kiḷavi ēṉippāliyum iṉamākak kuṟikkum eṉakkoḷka. cāttaṉ veḷuttāṉ allatu veḷuttuviṭṭāṉ; cāttāṉ āṭiyi veḷuttāṉ eṉat taṉpālum āṭiyiṉ pālum veḷumi ēṟal kāṇalām.
moḻiyiṟutiyeḻuttuk kūṟupavar ataṉporuḷ niliyiyum uṭaṉ kūṟiyatu ēṉ? ivviṟutic colliṉ ariya amippiṉi eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉār. kūṭutaṟ ceyti kuṟṟamilli. iṉiyavi kūṟal eṉṟa kuṟal atikārattil `paṇivuṭiyaṉ iṉcolaṉātal' eṉṟa otta ceytiyi uṭaṉ moḻintati oppunōkkuka.


viṭṭavi koḷḷal
(72)	eñciya vellām eṅcutal ilavē.
itu mēl viḷakkamāka eṭuttukkāṭṭātu viṭṭaṉavaṟṟik koḷka eṉṟu kūṟum.
akalavuri
uyir oḷa eṉṟa nūṟpā mutal ivvuyirkaḷ immeymēl iṟutiyil vārā eṉṟu vilakkappaṭṭaṉa. iviyivi moḻi yiṟutiyākum eṉṟu uṭaṉpāṭṭu muṟiyiṟ collappaṭavilli. avvāṟu collāta uyireḻuttukkaḷum koḷḷappaṭa vēṇṭum. eṉpatu innūṟpāviṉ karuttu.
uri: iṉṉa uyir iṉṉa meymēl varumeṉa uṭaṉpāṭṭu neṟiyiṟ collāta ēṉi uyireḻuttukkaḷum moḻiyiṟutiyeḻuttukkaḷāka varuvatiṟ kuṟivilli.

cērkka kuveḷa no 3
taṉi uyirīṟu (e o oḷa nīṅkalāka) 9
kūṭutal 159 īṟāka varum.
tiṟaṉuri
ṅakaram muppatu eḻuttukkaḷuḷ ōr oliyaṉ. col naṭuvē uṟuppāka varum. collukku mutalilum varuvatilli. iṟutiyilum varuvatilli. itu veḷippaṭi yātaliṉ āciriyar ṅakaram paṟṟi īṇṭu yātum kūṟiṟṟilar. muḻutum vārāmi kuṟippiṭattakkataṉṟu.
eñciyavellām eṉpataṉ poruḷ yātu? mēlinūṟpākkaḷiṉ iṉṉavuyirkaḷ iṉṉameykaḷōṭu varum eṉṟu uyir variciyākavō meyvariciyākavō vitikkavilli. curukkaṅkaruti iṉṉavaṟṟōṭu cila varum, cila vārā eṉṟaḷaviṟ cuṭṭiṉār. ōreḻuttorumoḻi, īreḻut torumoḻiyaḷavilum mikac cilavaṟṟik kāṭṭiṉār. ēṉiyavaṟṟiṉ nili eṉṉa eṉpataṟku viṭiyākum. “eñciyavellām eñcutal ilavē” eṉṟa nūṟpā. ivi eḻuttu nilipaṟṟiyaṉavalla; coṉṉili paṟṟiyaṉa. eṉavē colliṟku iṟuti eṉṟē `eñciyavellām eṉpataṟkup poruḷ koḷḷavēṇṭum.
itaṉ karuttu eṉṉa?
“mutalā vēṉa tampeyar mutalum” eṉṟa nūṟpāvil collukku mutalil vārā eṉṟu vilakkappaṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷum tammiccuṭṭip peyarāka varumpōtu mutalākak koḷḷavēṇṭum eṉṟu ilakkaṇaneṟi kāṭṭiṉār. nūliṉ nuṉivarumeṉṟāl aṭippakuti varuvati uṭaṉ kuṟippatu pōla, eḻuttu taṉ peyar cuṭṭuṅkāl mutalāka varumeṉṟāl, atē kāraṇattāl iṟutiyākavum varum eṉpatu veḷippaṭi. ataṟkōr taṉiviti vēṇṭiyatilli. ivvāṟu aṭaṅkutaliṉ īṟil ēṉi tam peyar īṟām eṉṟu tolkāppiyar nūṟpā ceyyavilli eṉṟu teḷika. itaṉit teḷinta pavaṇantiyār
“tampeyar moḻiyiṉ mutalum mayakkamum
  immuṟi māṟiyum iyalum eṉpa”
eṉṟu nūṟpittār. “īṟum” eṉac cērkkāmi niṉiyattakum.
`eñciyavellām' eṉpataṉuḷ kuṟṟiyalukaravīṟum aṭaṅkum aṭakkik koḷḷavum vēṇṭum. `neṭṭeḻut timparum toṭarmoḻiyīṟṟum' eṉa muṉpē ivvīṟu cuṭṭappaṭṭatu. `kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟutiyum uḷappaṭa' eṉavum `kuṟṟiyalukarak kiṉṉē cāriyi' eṉavum `yāteṉṉiṟuti' eṉavum piṉṉiyalkaḷil ivvīṟu puṇarkkappaṭutal kāṇka. “aṟunāṉ kīṟṟoṭu neṟi niṉṟiyalum” eṉṟu puṇariyaliṉ mutal nūṟpāvil kuṟṟiyalukarattiyum cērttut toki kūṟuvatālum iḵtu uṟutippaṭum.
meyyiṟutikaḷ
(73)	ñaṇanama ṉayarala vaḻaḷa veṉṉum
	appati ṉoṉṟē puḷḷi yiṟuti.
itu patiṉoru meykaḷ moḻiyīṟākum eṉakkūṟum.
akalavuri
mēlē uyirkaḷ taṉiyākavum meyyōṭu cērntum īṟākum nili nuvalappaṭṭatu. innūṟpāvil colli ṉiṟutiyil puḷḷiyuṭiya meykaḷ īṟātal nuvalappaṭum.
ñ ṇ n m ṉ eṉṉum melleḻuttu aintu: y r l v ḻ ḷ eṉṉum iṭiyeḻuttu āṟu ākap patiṉoṉṟē puḷḷi yākac colliṉ iṟutiyil varum. eṉavē k c ṭ t p ṟ eṉṟa valleḻuttāṟum ṅ eṉṟa melleḻuttoṉṟum vārā.
vaḻakku: 	uriñ¨	eṇṇey
	muraṇ	vaḷḷalār
	porut	aṇṇal
	kaḻakam	tev
	veḷḷiyappaṉ	tamiḻ
		tirukkuṟaḷ
tiṟaṉuri
“uyirmeyyallaṉa moḻi mutalākā” eṉṟapaṭi collukku mutalil puḷḷiyuṭiya meyvaruvatilli; āṉāl iṟutikkaṇ puḷḷiyoṭu patiṉoru meykaḷ varum. itaṉit teḷivikkavē meyyiṟuti eṉṉāmal `puḷḷiyiṟuti' eṉṟu vaṭivoṭu kūṟiṉār āciriyar.
moḻi mutaṟkaṇ valleḻuttu kūṭutalāṭci peṟutaliyum iṟutikkaṇ aṟavē peṟāmiyum kāṇka. ōriṭattu uyarntōr piṟitōriṭattut tāḻvārpōlum. iṭiyeḻuttu āṟum iṟutic celvākkuṭiyaṉa.
meyynilikaḷ
a. moḻi mutalilum iṟutiyilum varupavi	ña na ma ya va
ā. moḻi mutalil maṭṭum varupavi	ka ca ta pa
i. moḻi yiṟutiyil maṭṭum varupavi	ṇa ṉa ra la ḻaḷa.
ī. moḻi mutalilum iṟutiyilum vārātavi	ṭa ṟa ṅa
iviyellām puḷḷiyāka naṭuvil varum. 	ivi tamiḻc 			collamippukkaḷ.
ayaṟcoṟkaḷit taḻuva vēṇṭiṉ ivvamippiṉip pōṟṟavēṇṭum. pārat, alakāpāt, aitarāpāt, iculāmāpāt, pāratcēvakcaṅk, pant, āṉant, eṉpaṉavellām tamiḻ vaṭiviḻantavi. tamiḻeḻuttil eḻuti viṭuvatālē tamiḻeṉavō tamiḻukku vaḷarcciyeṉavō mayaṅka vēṇṭā. tuṇintu otukkuka. teḷivukku iraṇṭoru viḷakkam taruvaṉ: pōtum. pārat eṉpatu takarap puḷḷiyīṟu urupukaḷ cēruṅkāl pārati. pāratāl, pārakku. pāratiṉ, pāratatu, pāratkaṇ eṉa varumā? iyiyumā? pāratatti, pāratattāl, pāratattukku, pāratattiṉ, pāratattiṉatu, pāratattiṉkaṇ eṉṟē varum. eṉavē pāratam eṉa makaravīṟē nam tamiḻ marapu. pāratanāṭu, pārata tēcam, pārata tēvi, pārata nāyaki, pāratappōr, pārata makkaḷ eṉṟu mākkavi pārati tamiḻ marapuppaṭi pāṭutal kāṇka. alakāpāttu, aitarāpāttu, iculāmāpāttu, pārata cēvaka caṅkam, piracātu, pāṉipaṭṭu, tēcumukam eṉṟu iṟuvatē tamiḻ vaṭivam. ai, āl, ku, iṉ, atu, kaṇ eṉṟa urupukaḷic cērttup pārttāl, moḻiyamippup puriyum. valloṟṟu iṟutiyākat tamiḻil irukkamuṭiyā eṉpatu paḷicciṭum. ivvakiyil tamiḻ piṟa moḻiyamippukaḷōṭu vēṟupaṭṭatu. valloṟṟukkaḷ īṟākum ayaṉmoḻikaḷiluṅkūṭa iṟuti uyiriḻuppāka ōṭi niṟpatic ceviyāl uṇaralām.
tamiḻmoḻiyil colliṟuti paṟṟiya kaṇippu:

						170 īṟukaḷ
170 īṟukaḷ eṉṟu eṇṇikki peritākat tōṉṟiṉālum puṇarccikku nilimoḻiyil niṟkum uṇmiyāṉa īṟukaḷ uyir paṉṉiraṇṭum mey patiṉoṉṟum āka irupattumūṉṟēyām. kuṟṟiyalukaramum cērumpōtu irupattu nāṉkām. “āvi ñaṇa namaṉa yaralava ḻaḷamey cāyu mukaram nālāṟu mīṟē” eṉṟu naṉṉūl eḷiya iṉiya varampukāṭṭum.
eḻuttu allatu oliyaṉ eṉpatu poruṇmi yuṭiyatum poruḷ vēṟṟumi ceyvatum ām. ituvē itaṉ taliyilakkaṇam; moḻi mutalilum varalām. iṟutiyilum varalām. vārāmalirukkalām; aṅṅaṉam vārātavi iṭiyilāvatu varum; eṅkō ōriṭattu vantu poruṇmi tōṟṟum. ṅakaram mutalilum iṟutiyilum vārā toḻiyiṉum iṭiyil vantu paṅkam ō pakkam kaṅku ō cukku; pāṅku ō pākku eṉap poruḷ vēṟṟumit tiṟaṉ ceykiṉṟatu. moḻip parappil aritāka ōriṭattut tōṉṟit taṉakkeṉap poruṇmi kāṭṭiviṭiṉ, atupōtum ataṉi oliyaṉ eṉṟu matippataṟku; ataṉāṟṟāṉ ṅakaram muppatu eḻuttiṉuḷ oṉṟāyiṟṟu.
nakarameyyiṟuti
(74)	ucca kāramoṭu nakārañ civaṇum.
itu nakarameyyiṟutikku variyaṟi kūṟum.
akalavuri
cu eṉṟa iṟuti irucollaḷavil vantatupōla nakaramey yiṟutiyum iru collaḷavil varum. aḵtāvatu nakaraviṟuti cukarattiyokkum.

vaḻakku
porun - ottal. otta vēṭaṅkoḷḷal; viṉiccol
verin - mutuku; peyarccol.
tiṟaṉuri
porunaṉ eṉṟa col ottavaṉ, oppuṭiyavaṉ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuṅkāl, porun eṉpatu pakutiyākum; vīraṉ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuṅkāl poru eṉpatu pakutiyākum eṉṟa vēṟṟumiyuṇarka. māṇṭāṉ eṉpatu māṇpuṭiyāṉ eṉṟakkāl māṇpakuti eṉṉavum iṟantāṉ eṉṟakkāl māḷ pakuti eṉavum varutali oppunōkkuka.
verin eṉṟa col paṇṭu mikka payiṟciyuṭiyatu eṉpataṉip puḷḷi mayaṅkiyal kāṭṭum. cilappatikāram vari iccolvaravik kāṇkiṉṟōm. itu uyartiṇi mutukik kuṟiyātu. ceṭikoṭi vilaṅkukaḷiṉ piṉpuṟatti allatu mēṟpuṟattik kuṟikkum eṉpataṉi aviyanūl āṭcikaḷāl aṟiyalām. vaḻakkiṟkuk koṇṭu varattakum aṟiviyaṟcol itu.
ñakara meyyiṟuti
(75)	uppa kāramoṭu ñakariyum aṟṟē
	apporu ḷiraṭṭā tivaṇi yāṉa.
itu ñakarameyyiṟutikku variyaṟi kūṟum.
akalavuri
pu eṉṟa iṟuti oru collaḷavil vantatu pōla ñakarameyyīṟum oru collil varum. intavaḷavil ñakaram pukaratti yokkum. pukaram pōlat taṉpāl piṟaṉpāl eṉap poruḷiraṭṭutal ñakarattiṟku illi.
vaḻakku:	uriñ - uriñcutal, viṉiccol
tiṟaṉuri
porun poruntu eṉavum uriya uriñcu eṉavum uriya valliṉam peṟṟup piṟkālattu nīṇṭaṉa. puṟam puṟampu eṉa varutali nōkkuka.
māṇākkarkku muṉṉum piṉṉum niṉivūṭṭi oppumi kāṭṭi nūl muḻutum karuttōṭac ceytal kalvi muṟi yātaliṉ, tolkāppiyam māṭṭēṟṟiṉi nirampap pōṟṟiyatu. ottataṟital, ovvāmiyaṟital, āyvukāṇal ivi māṭṭēṟṟiṉ payaṉkaḷ.
vakara meyyīṟu
(76) vakarak kiḷavi nāṉmoḻi yīṟṟatu.
itu vakarameyyīṟṟukkuc colvariyaṟi kūṟum.
akalavuri
vakarameyyeḻuttu nāṉku collil īṟāka varum.
vaḻakku: 	av iv uv tev
mutal mūṉṟum aḵṟiṇip paṉmic cuṭṭup peyarkaḷ; avi, ivi, uvi eṉpatu poruḷ. tev - paki; peyarccol.
tiṟaṉuri
maliyāḷam tōṉṟiya kālattu uv eṉpatu vaḻakkiṉmiyiṉ ilīlā tilakam av, iv, tev eṉṟa mūṉṟiṉiyē uṭaṉpaṭum. av, iv eṉpaṉa uṭampaṭu meyyalla eṉpatum innūl teḷivikkum karutturi.
tolkāppiyattiṉip piṉpaṟṟi naṉþṉūl nāṉmoḻiyīṟu collum. kav, vav eṉṟa pōli vakaravīṟukaḷi naṉṉūl kuṟittālum, uṇmiyākak koṇṭu meyyīṟṟuppuṇariyalil puṇarccikku mēṟkoḷḷavilli.
kavviyāṟ kavvitu kāmam atuviṉṟēl
tavveṉṉum taṉmi yiḻantu
eṉṟa tirukkuṟaḷil, kav, tav eṉa vakaravīṟukaḷ vantiruntum piṟkāla ilakkaṇattār ivaṟṟic cērkkavivli. pōli eṉṟu kavviyum olikkuṟippeṉat tavviyum karutiviṭṭaṉar pōlum; allatu kavvu, tavvu eṉa ukaravīṟākak koṇṭu mirukkalām.

ṉakara makara mayakkam
(77)	makarat toṭarmoḻi mayaṅkutal varinta
	ṉakarat toṭarmoḻi oṉpaḵ teṉpa
	pukaraṟak kiḷanta aḵṟiṇi mēṉa.
itu ṉakaravīṟṟu meykku mayakkamum variyaṟiyum kūṟum.
akalavuri
tamiḻccoṟkaḷiṉ uyiriṟutikaḷiyum meyyiṟuti kaḷiyum varaṉmuṟi ceytuvanta āciriyar meyyiṟutikkaṇ ōr olippōliyi innūṟpāvil iyampuvar.
aḵṟiṇip peyarc collākat toṭarmoḻiyil varum ṉakarameyyīṟu makara vīṟāka mayaṅkutaluṇṭu. itu potuviti eṉiṉum oṉpatu ṉakarap peyarkaḷ maṭṭum avvāṟu mayakkam peṟuvatilli eṉṟu pulappaṭuppar pulavar.
pukar aṟakkiḷanta - kuṟṟamiṉṟit teḷivākac collappaṭṭa
mēṉa - mēlē, iṭattu.
vaḻakku:	potuviti: nilaṉ -nilam; aṟaṉ - aṟam; kalaṉ -kalam; valaṉ -valam; akaṉ - akam; puṟaṉ- puṟam; tiṟaṉ - tiṟam. ivvāṟu pala.
mayaṅkātaṉa oṉpatu
	1. ekiṉ 	-	aṉṉam, puḷi
	2. payiṉ 	-	piciṉ
	3. aḻaṉ 	-	piṇam
	4. kuyiṉ 	-	mēkam
	5. vayāṉ 	- 	kārippuḷa
	6. cekiṉ 	- 
	7. viḻaṉ		poruḷ teriyavilli
	8. puḻaṉ 
	9. vayāṉ 	-	.

aḻaṉ, puḻaṉ, ekiṉ, kuyiṉ eṉpavi tolkāppiya nūṟpāvil āḷappeṟṟuḷa.
tiṟaṉuri
immayakkam aḵṟiṇip peyaril toṭarmoḻiyil varum eṉpatu varampu.
toṭarmoḻi iraṇṭiṟanticikkum collātalitaṉ, valiyaṉ, kaluḻaṉ, alavaṉ eṉpaṉavaṟṟiyum kuṟikkumāyiṉum iruvaki vaḻakku nōkki, nilaṉ, akaṉ, puṟaṉ, uraṉ eṉa vāṅku mūveḻuttuc coṟkaḷē ēṟpuḻik koḷḷappaṭum. muṉpē viḷakkiyapaṭi poruḷ vēṟṟumi ceyyāmi olippōliyiṉ ilakkaṇamaṉṟō.
makarattōṭu mayaṅkiṉālum ṉakaravīrākavē vittup puṇarkkappaṭum eṉpatu innūṟpāviṉ aṟivippu.
mayaṅkāta coṟkaḷ oṉpatu: mayaṅkum coṟkaḷ tolkāppiyakkālattē mikappala vaḻakkiliruntaṉa. ātaliṉ makara vīṟākap puṇarkkappaṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷum tolkāppiyattu uḷa. maṟaṅkaṭi, curameṉamoḻipa, nilampeyarntu, maṭampaṭa eṉpaṉa cāṉṟukaḷ.
iṭikkālattu makaravīṟṟu vaḻakku marapāyiṉamiyiṉ piṉṉilakkaṇikaḷ makaram ṉakaramāka mayaṅkum eṉac cuṭṭiṉar. ituvum keṭutalilli, poruḷit tākkāmiyiṉ.
oru karuttiṉip pulappaṭutta moḻikkaṇ col vaṭivaṅkaḷum ilakkaṇavaṭivaṅkaḷum kūṭutalāka iruppatu makkaṭku eḷimiyākum; moḻikku vaḷamākum; naṭikku āṟṟalākum.
moḻiyiṟutiyeḻuttukkaḷik kūṟum tolkāppiyam ōreḻuttorumoḻi, īreḻuttorumoḻi, toṭar moḻi eṉṟa mūṉṟu moḻi nilikaḷil vittuc cila variyaṟi ceytiruppatik kaṟkiṉṟōm. innūṉmuṟi piṉṉūlkaḷil illi.


moḻi marapu - iyal muṭivuri
nūṟpā : 34
kuṟṟiyalukaravilakkaṇam paṟṟip palvēṟu koḷki kaḷum ataṉāl cila kuḻappaṅkaḷum vaḻivaḻi vantuḷḷaṉa. itaṉit taṉipporuḷāka iṅku muḻutuṟa āytal iṭaṉaṉṟu. ātaliṉ cintaṉikku uramākum cila kuṟippukkaḷit taruvēṉ mūṉṟu nūṟpākkaḷ nam viḷakkattukku uriyavi.
“iṭippaṭiṟ kuṟuku miṭaṉumā ruṇṭē
kaṭappā ṭaṟinta puṇariya lāṉa”
itu moḻimarapu nūṟpā.
“allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟuimik kaṇṇum;
   ellā viṟutiyum ukaram niṟiyum”
“valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi valleḻuttu varuvaḻi
   tolli yiyaṟki niliyalu muṟittē”
ivviraṇṭum kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal nūṟpā.
`ukaram niṟiyum' eṉa iḷampūraṇarum pērāciriyarum, `ukaram niliyum' eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum pāṭaṅ koḷvar.
piṟarkūṟum karutturikaḷ
1. `iṭaṉumāruṇṭu' eṉpataṉāl kuṟṟiyalukaram puṇarmoḻiyil ciṟupāṉmiyāka aruki varum.
2.	 vaṉṟoṭark kuṟṟiyalukarattiṉ muṉṉē varumāyiṉ arimāttiri yākum. kālmāttiriyākum. oru māttiri yākum eṉṟa mūṉṟu karuttukkaḷ uḷa. cekkukkaṇi cukkukkōṭu eṉpaṉavē mūvarum kāṭṭum cāṉṟukaḷ.
3. 	kuṟṟiyalukaram irumoḻippuṇarccikkaṇ alvaḻiyilum vēṟṟumiyilum orumāttiriyāka niṟiyum eṉṟa karuttum arimāttiriyākavē niliyum eṉṟa karuttum uḷa.
ivvēṟupāṭukaḷuḷ nām mikavum karutattakkatu puṇarmoḻikkaṇ kuṟṟiyalukaram oru māttiriyāki muṟṟukaram ām eṉpatu. urakam niṟiyum eṉṟu pāṭaṅ koḷḷum iḷampūraṇarum pērāciriyarum avvaḻiyiṉarum ikkaruttuṭiyar. itu poruntumā?
coṟkaḷ ellām puṇarmoḻippaṭupavi. puṇar moḻikku vārāta coṟkaḷ illi. kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷ alvaḻi vēṟṟumip puṇarmoḻiyākuṅkāl oru māttiri yāka niṟintuviṭum eṉiṉ, taṉimoḻik kuṟṟiyalukarac coṟkaṭkup payaṉilli. uyirīṟṟup puṇariyalil ukara vīṟṟilē aṭakki viṭalāmē. kuṟṟiyalukarappuṇariyal eṉat taṉip pēriyal vēṇṭumā? nilimoḻiyiṉ iṟuti kuṟṟiyalukaramāka niṉṟāṟṟāṉē, taṉip puṇariyalukkup poruḷuṇṭu. kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟkut taṉippuṇarcci vitikaḷum vēṟṟumikaḷum uṇṭu eṉpavi ellārum uṭaṉpaṭa vēṇṭiya kōṭpāṭukaḷām.
“iruvaki yukaramō ṭiyintavi variṉē
  nērpum niripum ākum eṉpa”
eṉṟa ceyyuḷiyalil iruvakiyum eṉṟa vēṟupāṭu paṭumikiyākiviṭumaṉṟō? ceyyuḷiṟ coṟkaḷ aciyākic cīrākiyaṭiyākit toṭarcci peṟṟākumpōtu, nērpu niripu eṉṟa kuṟṟilukara vāypāṭukaṭku iṭam yāṇṭuṇṭu? muṟṟukaramākumeṉiṉ, `pōntu pōntucārntu cārntu' eṉṟa kaṭṭaḷiyaṭiyil eṭṭeḻuttāka eṇṇappaṭa vēṇṭumaṉṟē? pērāciriyar itaṟkuk kūṟum amiti nam uḷam toṭavilli. cārpeḻuttu puṇarcciyil mutaleḻuttākiviṭum eṉṟa kōṭpāṭu aṭimuraṇ eṉat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu.
puṇarmoḻikkaṇ kuṟṟiyalukaram kāl māttiri yākumeṉṟāl oru māttiri yākumeṉṟālum nūṉmarapil `avviyal niliyum ēṉi mūṉṟē'eṉṟataṉiyaṭuttu ivi collappaṭṭirukkum; vaṭiva nili paṟṟiyum colliyirukka vēṇṭum. makarattukkuc colliyiruppati niṉivukoḷka. `iṭippaṭiṟkuṟukum iṭaṉumāruṇṭē' eṉṟumoḻi marapiṟ colliyatupōla, `iṭippaṭiṉ mikum iṭaṉumuṇṭu' eṉṟoru nūṟpā yāttirukka vēṇṭumaṉṟō? `ukaram niṟiyum' eṉṟa oru colli vittup pala ilakkaṇak kūṟum paḻutākumpaṭi karutturippatu cevvitaṉṟu. `niliyum' eṉṟa oru pāṭam uṇṭu. `tolliyiyaṟki niliyalum urittē' eṉṟu aṭuttuvaruvati nōkkiṉ, ippāṭamum poruntum pōlum. eṉiṉum nacciṉākkiṉiyarukku muntiya uriyāciriyar iruvarum `niṟiyum' eṉṟē oru taliyākap pāṭaṅkoṇṭuḷḷaṉar. ātaliṉ atuvē valiyuṭittu.
`ukaram niṟiyum' eṉṟa pāṭattiṉiyē koḷvōm. ataṟkāka muṟṟukaramāka, oru māttiri niṟiyum eṉṟa poruḷ koḷḷavēṇṭum eṉpatilli. pērāciriyar ipporuḷiṉar eṉpatu teḷivu.
ukaram niṟiyum eṉpataṟku māttiri niṟiyum eṉṟa poruḷ koḷḷa vēṇṭā. `kuṉṟici moḻivayiṉ niṉṟici niṟikkum' eṉpatu pōla iciniṟivākak karutalām. atāvatu kuṟṟiyalukaram puṇarmoḻiyilum taṉakkuriya arimāttiriyōci niṟiyākavē peṟum eṉpatu karuttu. īreḻuttorumoḻi eṉṟu toṭaṅkum muntiya nūṟpāvil `āyiru mūṉṟē ukaram kuṟukiṭaṉ' eṉa varuvatālum ikkaruttu valiyuṟum. `āṟu īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaram niṟintē niṟkum' eṉṟa iḷampūraṇar uriyum ikkuṟippē koṇṭatu. ivvuriyil māttirik kuṟippilli.
mēlē mutaṟkaṇ kāṭṭiya mūṉṟu nūṟpākkaṭkum nāṉ koḷḷum poruḷ varumāṟu:
1. 	orumoḻiyilēyaṉṟik kuṟṟiyalukaram puṇar moḻiyilum kuṟukutal uṇṭu. kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyalil itaṉ viḷakkattik kāṇalām.
2. 	aṟuvakik kuṟṟiyalukara vīṟukaḷum puṇarmoḻiyil alvaḻiyilum vēṟṟumiyilum arimāttiri yōci  niṟintu niṟkum. arimāttiriyiṉiṉṟu kuṟitalō, arimāttiriyiṉum mikutalō irā.
3. 	cekkukaṇi, cukkukkōṭu pōṉṟa valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻikku muṉ valleḻuttu varumpōtu, kuṟṟiyalukaram mutaleḻuttu ukarampōla oru māttiri peṟutalum uṇṭu.
ivi maṉa niṟivu tarum uriyalla eṉiṉum uḷḷavaṟṟuḷ ottatu kāṇal neṟiyākum.
ilakkaṇap perumpulavar vēṅkaṭarāculu eḻutiya `kuṟṟiya lukaram' eṉṟa āyvupperuṅ kaṭṭuri māṇavar pārvikkuriyatu.
nūṟpā 38
iciniṟi kūṟum innūṟpāviṟkum,
“nīṭṭam vēṇṭiṉ avvaḷa puṭiya
  kūṭṭi eḻūutal eṉmaṉār pulavar”
eṉṟa aḷapeṭi nūṟpāviṟkum iyipuṇṭu. vēṇṭu meṉṟāl vēṇṭum aḷapuṭiya olikaḷik kūṭṭi olittuk koḷḷalām eṉpatu itaṉ karuttu. īṅkum ceyyuḷukkē eṉṟa kuṟippilli eṉṟālum ceyyuṭkē peruvaravākum eṉpatil aiyamilli.
iciniṟi allatu aḷapeṭi eppaṭi uṇṭākiṉṟatu. eppaṭip payaṉpaṭukiṉṟatu? mūvaḷapu icittaltāṉ ēṟakkuṟiyap peruviḻukkāṭu viḷinilikaḷil kūṭutal varum; `ceṟāaay vāḻiyeṉ neñcu' eṉṟaviṭattu nāṉku māttiri eṉpataṟkāka `ceṟāāy' eṉa mēlum oru neṭṭeḻuttu varuvatilli. `neṭṭeḻuttimpar otta kuṉṟeḻuttu’ eṉṟa innūṟpāviṉ teḷivāl, evvaḷavu aḷapeḻuntālum kuṟṟeḻuttuttāṉ piṉvarum eṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
aḷapeṭi kuṟittu iru koḷkikaḷ uḷa. avaṟṟi īṇṭut teḷivatu poruntum:
1. neṭṭeḻuttē mūṉṟu nāṉku eṉa aḷapeṭukkum. aṟikuṟikaḷākavē otta kuṟṟeḻuttu varum. `nīṭṭam vēṇṭiṉ kūṭṭi eḻūutal' eṉpatu tolkāppiyam. nīṭṭam eṉpataṉālēyē neṭṭeḻuttiṉ nīṭṭam eṉpatu teḷivām; naṉṉūl `neṭil aḷapeḻum avaṟṟavaṟṟiṉak kuṟil kuṟiyē' eṉṟu veḷippaṭi ceyyum. aḷapeṭi vanta coṟkaḷic collumpōtu kūṭṭi eḻūutal eṉṟapaṭi neṭili nīṭciyākavē mūṉṟu māttiriyaḷavu olikkiṉṟōm. iraṇṭum oṉṟumāka viṭṭolippatilli. āṉāl oru cikkal ceyyuṭkaṇ alakiṭumpōtu karāam varūum eṉpataṉi niri nērākap pirittatu vāypāṭu kūṟuvatē yāppilakkaṇac cemmiyākum. mūṉṟu māttiriyi ōr eḻuttāka vikkum nili yāṇṭum illi.
2. iṉṉoru koḷki: neṭṭeḻuttu aḷapu niṟikka villi. otta kuṟṟeḻuttuttāṉ niṟikkiṉṟatu eṉpatu iṉṉoru koḷki. `kuṉṟici moḻivayiṉ' eṉṟa inta nūṟpāvil otta kuṟṟeḻuttu niṉṟu ici niṟikkum eṉṟu eḻuvāy payaṉili amintuḷḷaṉa. ici niṟippatu kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ ceyalākac collappaṭukiṉṟatu. `neṭṭeḻut timpar' eṉpataṉālum itu peṟappaṭum. iciniṟivil kuṟṟeḻuttukkut taṉimatippaḷittāl `karāam' eṉpataṉi nirinēr, puḷimā eṉa alakiṭum yāppilakkaṇattukkum ottuvarukiṉṟatu eṉpatu ikkoḷkiyar viḷakkam.
ivviru koḷkikaḷum tammuḷ muraṇāṉavi; oṉṟiyē nām uṟutippaṭutta vēṇṭum. eḻuttu, kuṟil, neṭil, mey, valmelliṭi, cārpu ellāmē oli niliyil, kēḷviniliyil viḷakkappaṭuvaṉa. varivaṭivi vittu oliyilakkaṇam vakukkappaṭuvatilli eṉṟa pēruṇmiyip pōṟṟavēṇṭum. nākarika moḻivaḷarcciyil varivaṭivat tiṟkup pērātikkam uṇṭeṉiṉum, oli nilikku nikarāṉa matippu varinili peṟuvatilli.
ōraḷapicikkum kuṟil, īraḷapicikkum neṭil eṉṟu aḷapu kūṟivanta tolkāppiyar mūvaḷapu paṟṟiyum avvaḷapuṭiyaṉa kūṭṭi olittal paṟṟiyum toṭarntu kūṟuvar. itaṉāl varivaṭivu kuṟitta eṇṇattukku iṭaṉilli eṉpatu teḷivākum eḻuttu, kuṟṟeḻuttu, neṭṭeḻuttu eṉpaṉavellām oliyaṉ, kuṟṟoliyaṉ, neṭṭoliyaṉ eṉṟē poruḷpaṭum. eṉavē toḻāar, paṭāa, varūum eṉpaṉavaṟṟi mūṉṟoliyaḷavākavē nīṇṭolikka vēṇṭum eṉpatu teḷivātalōṭu ivvāṟē iṉṟum olikkumuṟi poruntum eṉpatum pōtarum. mēlum mūvoliyākumpōtu poruṇmi yuṭiya oliyaṉilli eṉpatum muṉviḷakkiyapaṭi uṇarappaṭum. `kuṉṟici moḻivayiṉ' eṉpataṉāl ici kuṉṟiṟṟēyaṉṟip poruṇmi kuṉṟavilli eṉpatum ici niṟikkum eṉpataṉāl poruṇmi niṟivilli eṉpatum viḷaṅkum.
“kuṉṟici moḻivayiṉ niṉṟici niṟikkum
   neṭṭeḻut timpar ottakuṟ ṟeḻuttē”
eṉṟa nūṟpāvi maṟukālum cintippōm. tolkāppiyar muṉpē tamiḻukku varivaṭivum cemmiyāyiṟṟu eṉavum ceyyuḷ vaḻakkum oru nikarāka moḻiyappaṭutaliṉ aṉṟē eḻuttuvaḻakkum mutaṉmi peṟṟatu eṉavum ataṉāl tolkāppiyar vēṟṟumi teḷikkumuṟiyil vaṭivaṉ paṟṟiya ceytikaḷiyum iṭimaṭuttuḷḷār eṉavum nām aṟivōm. varivaṭivi aṭippaṭiyāka vittu oliyilakkaṇam moḻiyappaṭutal illi eṉiṉum varivaṭivac ceyti varuvatu muraṇākātaṉṟē. “meyyiṉiyaṟki puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal” eṉṟāl atu meyyoliyaṉ ilakkaṇam ākātu. puḷḷivaṭivam oliyaṉilakkaṇattukku evvāṟṟāṉum toṭarpilli. ōr oliyaṉukku eḻuttulakil vanta varikkuṟiyik kāṭṭum aḷavē ataṉ payaṉ atu pōṉṟu varivaṭivam paṟṟiyatē innūṟpā eṉa aṟika.
otta kuṟṟeḻuttu niṉṟu ici ciṟikkum eṉṟa aṭiyil `niṉṟu' eṉṟa viṉiyeccam kāṭṭuvatu eṉṉa? aṟikuṟiyāṉa varivaṭivik kuṟippatākum. ici kuṉṟiya collil iciniṟivukku aṭiyāḷamāka neṭṭeḻuttu vaṭivukkup piṉ ottakuṟṟeḻuttu vaṭivam niṟkum. ataṉāl olinīṭci peṟappaṭum. iṉṉoru kuṟippum ivviṭattu uṇṭu. collukku iṭiyilum iṟutiyilum taṉiyuyir vaṭivaṅkaḷ iruppatilli. itaṉip `puḷḷiyillā ellāmeyyum' eṉṟa nūṉmarapu nūṟpāvil kaṟṟōm. `pōoy, toḻāar, keṭuppatūum, paṭāa eṉa iciniṟikkum coṟkaḷil taṉikkuṟṟuyir vaṭivu niṟpatāl ivi niṟikka vanta aṟikuṟikaḷ eṉpatu teḷivākum. itaṉāl `otta kuṟṟeḻuttu' eṉpataṟku otta taṉikkuṟilvaṭivu eṉapporuḷ koḷḷavēṇṭum. `ceṟāaay' eṉṟa nālaḷapu ici niṟippiṉum anta aḷapukku camamāṉa kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷē aḵtāvatu iraṇṭu kuṟil vaṭivaṅkaḷē varum eṉṟa kuṟippup peṟappaṭum. ātaliṉ kuṟṟeḻuttu niṟpataṉāl ici niṟiyavilli eṉavum, iciniṟiviṉikkāṭṭum eḻuttuk kuṟiyē kuṟṟeḻuttu eṉavum koḷḷum kōṭpāṭē poruntum. mēṟkūṟiya kuṟikkōṭpāṭu okkum eṉiṉum ceyyuṭkaṇ alakiṭuvatupaṟṟi oru cikkal uṇṭu. ēṉik kōṭpāṭṭiṉar viṉavum oru karuttukkut teḷivāṉa viḷakkam ituvari kiṭikkavilli eṉpataṉi uṭaṉpaṭuttu kiṉṟēṉ.
aḷapeṭiyil oliyilakkaṇamē yaṭippaṭi eṉiṉ, kuṟṟeḻuttu aṟikuṟiyeṉiṉ - pōōy - nērnēr, tēmā; tōḻāar - nirinēr, puḷimā; ceṟāaay - niri nērnēr, puḷimāṅkāy eṉa alakiṭṭu vāypāṭu colluvatu poruntumā? mūṉṟu nāṉku māttiri nīṭṭal aḷavukku oru vāyppāṭukaḷ vēṇṭumaṉṟō? mūṉṟiṉi iraṇṭum oṉṟumākavum nāṉkiṉi iraṇṭum oṉṟum oṉṟumākavum olinīṭciyi pirittu vāypāṭu kūṟuvataṟku ilakkaṇam ceyyuḷiyalilum illiyaṉṟē. ivi karutavēṇṭiya viṉākkaḷē.
iccikkalāṉa viṉākkaṭkup piṉvarum viḷakkam oruvāṟu poruntiṉālum poruntum.
iraṇṭu māttiri neṭṭeḻuttē poruṇmi yuṭiya oliyaṉ. ataṟku mēlē varum olinīṭṭam oliyaṉaṉṟu. neṭṭeḻuttup pōla aḷapeṭiyeḻutteṉa oṉṟilli. ataṉāl oliyaṉukku mēṟpaṭṭa aḷapiṉit taṉiyāka alakuk kaṇippu ceytirukka vēṇṭum. pattukku mēṟpaṭa eṇṇum pōtu oru collāṟ collāmal patiṉoṉṟu paṉṉiraṇṭeṉac collum muṟiyi oppu nōkkuka.
tamiḻukku eḻuttu vaṭivu tolpaḻaṅkālattē amivupeṟṟu nalvaḻakkum eytiṟṟu eṉa ivvuri paṉmuṟi valiyuṟuttiyuḷḷatu. tolkāppiyar kālattum ceyyuḷ vaḻakku ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṟṟu. vāyvaḻakkiṉik kāṭṭilum ceyyuḷ vaḻakku eḻuttu vaṭiviṉip perumpālum taḻuvi niṉṟatātaliṉ, aci cīr mutaliyaṉa kāṇappaṭum eḻutturuvi vittum eḷimiyākak kaṟpikka muṭintatu eṉak koḷḷavēṇṭum. ittaku kalvip payiṟci aḷapeṭikkaṇ aṟikuṟiyāka eḻutappaṭṭa uyirvaṭivāṉa kuṟṟeḻuttuk kaḷit taṉiyaciyāka aṟutticikkum marapiṉi uṇṭākkiṟṟu. pērāciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar urikaḷ “aḷapeṭi” yacinili yākalum urittē”eṉṟa ceyyuḷiyal nūṟpāviṉ kaṇ ikkaruttukku ūṉṟākiṉṟaṉa.
eḻuttuvaḻakkiṟ cila coṟkaḷ taṉivaṭivu peṟuvati `nāṉ' eṉṟa āṅkilac collilum kaṭavuḷik kuṟikkumpōtu avaṉ eṉṟa āṅkilac collum kāṇka. itukāṟum nām ārāytaviyellām uyiraḷapeṭi kuṟittavi. ivvici niṟivu vaḻakkiṟkum perumpāṅku varumātaliṉ eḻuttatikārattiṟ kūṟiṉār. oṟṟaḷapeṭi ceyyuṭkē yurimiyiṉ, ataṉ nili ceyyuḷiyaliṟ kūṟappaṭum. cārpeḻuttukkaḷukku aḷapeṭi yākum taṉmiyilli.
aḷapeṭi vaḻakkiṟkum ceyyuṭkum iṉiyatōr moḻikkaruvi. iṉṟuvaḻakkil iṉṉōcipaṭak kūṟiṉālum urinaṭikkaṇ kuṟikaḷ eḻutappaṭuvatilli. innūṟṟāṇṭuc ceyyuṭkaḷil aḷapeṭiyāṭci illiyeṉṟē collalām. ici kuṉṟumpōtu ataṉi nirapputaṟkeṉṟu paṭikkappaṭṭa aḷapeṭi marapiṉic piṉpaṟṟāmal, ikkālak kaviñarkaḷ coṟkaḷic cērttu nirampukiṉṟaṉar. itaṉāl poruḷ niṟikuṟikiṉṟatu. ātaliṉ ōraḷavēṉum aḷapeṭi vaḻakkiṉip pāṭṭukkaḷil uyirppittal pulamiyākum.
nūṟpā 40
innūṟpā uyirkkum uyirmeykkum potu eṉpatu uriyāciriyarkaḷ karuttu. ataṉāl kā tī pū ku eṉa uyirmey neṭilākavum eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷ eḻutiṉar. uyirmeykkum uriyatu eṉṟa karuttiṉāl `kuṟṟeḻuttu aintum' eṉpataṉi eccavummiyākak koṇṭu cila kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ moḻikaḷām eṉṟu poruḷeḻuti, tu, no eṉak kāṭṭum tantaṉar.
uyirmey eṉpatu tolkāppiya nōkkamāyiṉ `ōreḻuttorumoḻi īreḻuttorumoḻi” eṉṟa nūṟpā viṟkuppiṉ innūṟpā kiṭakka vēṇṭum. avvāṟiruppiṉ, mūṉṟumoḻi nilikaḷuḷ ōreḻuttoru moḻikku maṭṭum eṭuttukkāṭṭuc colvāṉēṉ eṉṟa viṉāt tōṉṟum. uyir meyyāyiṉ neṭṭeḻuttu ēḻ eṇṇikkiyil nillātu. naṉþṉūlār kūṟiyatupōla nāṟpatu neṭilkaḷ eṉṟu muḻutuṟac colliyirukka vēṇṭum.
uyirmey eṉpatu tolkāppiyar nōkkamāyiṉ, neṭṭeḻuttu ēḻē eṉṟu varuvatu kūṟiyatupōl, kuṟṟeḻuttil nakaravokaram oṉṟē ōreḻuttorumoḻi eṉap paṭṭāṅkuc colliyiruppar.
ivvāṟu uyirmey paṟṟi maṟuppuk kāraṇaṅkaḷ uḷa.
ōreḻuttorumoḻi īreḻuttorumoḻi eṉṟa nūṟpāvukku muṉpē kūṟiyiruttalālum `neṭṭeḻuttēḻē' “kuṟṟeḻuttintum” eṉa vāḷā moḻintiruttalālum taṉiyuyirkaḷē innūṟpāviṉ poruḷ eṉakkoḷḷa vēṇṭum.
avvāṟu koḷḷuṅkāl `kuṟṟeḻuttintum' eṉpatu muṟṟummiyāki enta uyirkkuṟilum muḻumoḻiyākā eṉpatu ēṟkappaṭum. neṭṭeḻuttu ēḻē eṉpatil `oḷa' oṉṟumaṭṭum uyirmeyyākavē varutal kāṇkiṉṟōm. ēṉiyāṟum uyiraḷavilē muḻupporuṇmiyuṭiyaṉa. oḷa eṉpatum oru muḻuccollāka aritākat tolkāppiyak kālattu vaḻaṅkiyirukkakkūṭum. `avvittu' eṉpataṉip pōliyākak koṇṭāl `oḷa' viṉic collākum. itu oru kuṟippē. eṉiṉum nūṟpāc curukkam karuti `neṭṭeḻut tēḻē' eṉṟu kūṟiṉārāka vittuk koḷḷalām. iṅṅaṉam mutaṟkaṇ potunaṭiyiṟ kūṟiviṭum pōkkut tol kāppiyattup palliṭaṅkaḷil uṇṭu.
cakarak kiḷaviyum avaṟṟō raṟṟē
a ai oḷa veṉum mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē
eṉṟa nūṟpā naṭi ōr eṭuttukkāṭṭu.
neṭṭeḻuttimparum toṭarmoḻiyīṟṟum kuṟṟiyalukaram vallāṟu ūrntuvarum eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppiṉum cila vīṟukaḷ vārāmi veḷippaṭi.
ituvari ceyta toṭiviṭiyāl nām aṟiya muṭivatu eṉṉa? uyirneṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ, atāvatu uyir neṭiloliyaṉkaḷ niṉṟāṅkē collaṉkaḷākavum viḷaṅku kiṉṟaṉa. aṅṅaṉam poruṇmi cuṭṭumpōtu oliyil etuvum māṟṟamilli. ivvirupālamippu uyirkaḷuḷ neṭilukku uṇṭu. kuṟilukkum uṇṭō eṉiṉ illi.
nūṟpā 46
piṟaruri: rakara ḻakaram kuṟṟoṟṟāka varuvatilli eṉpatu muṉ nūṟpāviṉ karuttu. akar, akaḻ, eṉṟāṅku kuṟṟeḻuttukku aṭuttu varukiṉṟaṉavē eṉṟu viṉavuvārkku. iṅṅaṉam vantaṉa valla; aka eṉṟa kuṟiliṇikkīḻ vantaṉavākavē koḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉpar. ēṉeṉṟāl kuṟiliṇiyākumpōtu iraṇṭu māttiriyaḷavu peṟṟu neṭilāki viṭukiṉṟatallavā? neṭiṟ kīḻ vantaṉavākak koḷḷalām eṉṟu innūṟpāvukkup poruḷ ceytuvarupa.
rakara ḻakaram ivvāṟu varum eṉṟu amitikāṇa ippaṭi oru nūṟpā vēṇṭumā? kuṟiṟkīḻ vārā eṉa ōriṭam vilakkappaṭṭatu. neṭiṟkīḻākavō kuṟiliṇikkīḻākavō eḻuttut toṭarntu piṟavāṟu varumeṉpatu tāṉē karuttu. aṅṅaṉam kūṟal vēṇṭiṉ neṭiṟ kīḻum toṭarmoḻiyilum varumeṉac cuṭṭiṉāṟ pōtumē.  attoṭarmoḻi yellām neṭṭeḻuttām eṉṟu camaṉkūṟum nōkkeṉṉō? rakara ḻakaram neṭiṟ kīḻallatu vārā eṉavum  ellāvaṟṟiyum neṭiṟ kīḻākavē aṭakkik koḷka eṉavucam  viti vaḻakku uṇṭā? akal āl eṉṟum akaḻ āḻ eṉṟum marup paṭṭāl toṭarmoḻi īreḻuttoru moḻiyāki viṭumaṉṟō? aṅṅaṉam toṭarmoḻi yellām ākumā? ākumāyiṉ, toṭarmoḻi eṉṟa oru pākupāṭu eṟṟukku!
“meynnili cuṭṭiṉ ellā veḻuttum
  tammuṟ ṟāmvarūum raḻavalaṅ kaṭiyē” 	(tol.27)
eṉṟa meymmayakka nūṟpāvil raḻanīṅkalāka ēṉiyavi tamakku muṉṉē tām varum eṉṟār. ataṉāṟ peṟappaṭuvatu eṉṉa? vilakkappaṭṭa rakara ḻakaram piṟavē peṟum eṉpatu veḷippaṭi. atupōlavē rakara ḻakaram kuṟṟoṟṟākā eṉṟāl vēṟuvakiyil oṟṟāki varum eṉpatu uri. “avi neṭiṟkīḻ oṟṟām; kuṟiṟkīḻ uyirmeyyām eṉṟa iḷampūraṇariṉ karutturi poruntum.
kuṟil neṭil eṉpaṉa muḻutum māttiri kāraṇamāka vantaviyalla. iru kuṟil oru neṭilukkuc camam eṉavō oru neṭil irukuṟiliṉ iṇippu eṉavō koḷvatu tavaṟu. muppatu eḻuttukkaḷum poruṇmiyuṭiya taṉiyoli yaṉkaḷ eṉṟu muṉṉarē kaṇṭōm. ātaliṉ “kuṟumiyum neṭumiyum aḷaviṟ kōṭaliṉ” eṉṟa aṭikaṭku muṉṉi yurikaḷi ēṟpiṉ' oliyaṉaṭippaṭi kulintuviṭum. aḷavu eṉṟa coṟku māttiri yaḷapu eṉṟu muḻutum karutik koṇṭamiyāl ivvurippiḻi nērntatu.
eṉṉuri
muvvakic coṟpākupāṭu vakuttuk koṇṭatu puṇariyal vitikaḷi vaki toki ceytaṟkē. puṇarneṟi paṟṟiya oru kōṭpāṭṭiṉi nām teḷintu koḷvōmāka. īṟu paṟṟip puṇarviti kūṟiṉālum atē īṟu ōreḻuttoru moḻiyākavō īreḻuttorumoḻiyākavō toṭar moḻiyākavō irukkum pōtu palaviṭaṅkaḷil tiripukaḷ vēṟupaṭum. aḵtāvatu īṟuṭiya colvakikkum puṇar vitikkum toṭarpum vēṟupāṭum uṇṭu. īkārayiṟuti, ukaraviṟuti, ṇakāraviṟuti, yakaraviṟuti eṉṟellām iṟutipaṭa naṭiyiruntālum avviṟutikaḷ ōreḻuttoru moḻiyiṉ, īreḻuttorumoḻiyiṉ, toṭarmoḻiyiṉ uṟuppu eṉpataṉiyum  eṇṇik koḷḷa vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam niṉiya vēṇṭum eṉpataṟkākavē ā mā kā pū ū nī mī eṉṟāṅku ōreḻuttoru moḻikaḷiyum; pala cila irā nilā paṉi paḷi eṉṟāṅku īreḻuttoru moḻikaḷiyum: aḻaṉ puḻaṉ veyil iruḷ ātaṉ pūtaṉ patakku makkaḷ eṉṟāṅku toṭar moḻikaḷiyum vitantu puṇarviti kāṭṭuvar tolkāppiyar.
“kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarum ēreḻuttu moḻikkum
 aṟiyaṟtōṉṟum akarak kiḷavi”
“palavaṟ ṟiṟuti nīṭumoḻi yuḷavē
  ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya toṭarmoḻi yāṉa”
eṉṟavāṟu muvvakik kuṟikaḷiyum āṅkāṅku payaṉpaṭuttuvar. eṉavē puṇariyal kaṟkumpōtu moḻi marapiṟ colliya muvvakic coṉṉiliyiṉ iṉṟiyami yāmi peṟappaṭum.
“kuṟumiyum neṭumiyum aḷaviṟ kōṭaliṉ
  toṭarmoḻi yellām neṭṭeḻut tiyala”
eṉṟa nūṟpāvi maṟupaṭiyum cintippōm. aḷavu eṉpataṟku eṇṇikki eṉpatu poruḷ, ōreḻuttu, īreḻuttu toṭarmoḻi eṉṟa vaki eḻuttuvaṭiviṉāl amintatu eṉṟu kaṇṭōm. “eḻuttaḷaveñciṉum” eṉṟa ceyyuḷiyalil varum kaṭṭaḷic cīr ilakkaṇam itaṉit teḷivākkum. itteḷivupōtātu, iṉṉum teḷivu vēṇṭum.
“toṭarmoḻi yellām neṭṭeḻuttiyala”
eṉṟu colliyataṉ karuttum payaṉum eṉṉa? kuṟaḷaṭiyi nōkkac cintaṭiyum nēraṭiyum neṭiyavāyiṉum avaṟṟiṉ nīṇṭavaṟṟiyē neṭilaṭi, kaḻineṭilaṭu eṉap peyariṭu kiṉṟōm. ivi eḻutteṇṇikkiyip poṟuttaṉa. atupōl toṭarmoḻi iraṇṭukaṭanta pala eḻuttukkaḷi yuṭiyatātaliṉ, nīṇṭa vari eṉpatu pōla neṭṭeḻuttu eṉappaṭṭatu. tolkāppiyaṉ eṉpatu palaveḻuttuṭiya neṭiya oru col eṉpatu karuttu. neṭṭeḻuttu - nīṇṭa col. “palavaṟṟiṟuti nīṭumoḻi yuḷavē” eṉṟa uyir mayaṅkiyal nūṟpāvil `nīṭumoḻi' eṉa āṭci peṟutaliyum kāṇka.
ellāt toṭarmoḻiyum neṭṭeḻuttāka iyalum eṉpataṉāl puṇariyalil īṟu paṟṟi viti kūṟiṉālum toṭarmoḻi muḻutiṉiyum orumiyākak karutip pārkka vēṇṭum eṉpatu itaṉ payaṉ. itaṉi “īraḷapicikkum neṭṭeḻuttu” eṉṟa kuṟiccolloṭu mayaṅkak kūṭātu.
iḷampūraṇar uri kuṟṟamilli; nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uri poruttamilli; eṉṉuri iṉṉum cintaṉikkuriyatu. `neṭṭeḻuttiyal' eṉpataṟku maṉaniṟiyum poruḷ kāṇavēṇṭum.
nūṟpā - 49 - 53 vari
ivvintu nūṟpākkaḷum ai oḷa eṉṟa oliyaṉkaḷukkut taṉ vaṭivukaḷēyaṉṟik kūṭṭuvarivaṭiṅkaḷum uṇṭu eṉṟa oru poruḷnutaliyaṉa. mēlum `moḻippaṭutticippiṉum' eṉṟu varum muṉnūṟpāvukku viḷakkamāka amintaṉa ivi. iṅṅaṉam atikārap pōkkik kaṇṭu poruḷ ceyvatu nallatu.
aintiṉuḷ nāṉku aikāram paṟṟiyaṉa eṉpatu eṉka ruttu. ātaliṉ, `ikara yakaram aikāram iṟuti viravum’ eṉpataṟku aikāram cārntu uri ceyya vēṇṭum ikkaruttu civañāṉa muṉivarkku uṭaṉpāṭākum. `akara ikaram aikāram ākum' eṉavum `akarattimpar yakarap puḷḷiyum aiyeṉ neṭuñciṉi' eṉavum colliyapiṉ, innūṟpā kiṭattalāṉum, nūṟpāvil vēṟoru cūḻnili collamiyāṉum, aikāravaṭivac cārpāṉatu eṉpatu teḷivu. vayiram tayilam eṉavum ayntu mayyam eṉavum koḷḷum uriyē atikāra vōṭṭattukkup poruntum. nāy, nāyi, nāi, eṉṟāṅku karuttuk koḷvataṟku nūṟpāvilum iṭamilli, atikāra varaṉmuṟiyumilli.
olippōli, eḻuttup pōli eṉa iruvakiyuṇṭu. maṟaṉ - maṟam; aṟaṉ- aṟam; pētimi - pētami; uṭimi; uṭami; miyal - mayal eṉṟa coṟkaḷil ōr oliyaṉukkup piṟitōr oliyaṉ vantu niṟkiṉṟatu. iḵtu oliyaṉmayakkam. poruḷ vēṟṟumi ceyyāmiyiṉ pōliyāyiṟṟu.
viram - vayiram; mintaṉ - mayintaṉ; aintu - ayntu; aivaṉam - ayvaṉam eṉṟa coṟkaḷil ai eṉṟa ōr oliyaṉukku uriya oru varivaṭivum irukkavum vēṟu iru vaṭivaṅkaḷ vantuḷa. iṅku oliyaṉ varivaṭivattāl vēṟupaṭavilli. uriya vari vaṭivattukku māṟāka atuvum iruvaṭivukaḷ varakkāṇkiṉṟōm. eṉavē iṅku `ai' eṉṟa vaṭivukkum `ay' eṉṟa vaṭivukkum viraval ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. ai eṉṟa oliyaṉip paṟṟiya nōkkilli. ivvāṟē oḷakārattukkum koḷka.
“ammuṉ irakam yakara meṉṟivi
  eytiṉ aiyot ticikkum avvō
  ṭuvvum vavvum oḷavō raṉṉa”
eṉṟa naṉṉūṟpā eḻuttuppōli kūṟuvateṉa mayilinātarum caṅkara namaccivāyarum urippatu poruntum. `aiyot ticikkum' `oḷavōraṉṉa' eṉpataṉāl mutal oliyaṉākiya aikāramum oḷakāramum tūyaṉa kalappaṟṟaṉa eṉpatu teḷivām. ammuṉ ikaram aiyotticikkum; ammuṉ yakaram aiyotticikkum eṉa niralē uri ceyyavēṇṭum. ici vaṭivu paṟṟiya eḻuttup pōlikaḷ. pōli eṉpatu iṉattuḷ varuvatu. varivaṭivukku iṉṉoru varivaṭivu pōliyāka muṭiyumēyaṉṟi, olivaṭivukku varivaṭivu vara muṭiyātaṉṟō. ātaliṉ eḻuttukku eḻuttu māṟṟāka varuvatu eḻuttuppōli eṉatteḷika.
cila oliyaṉkaḷiṉ cērkkiyāl cila voliyaṉkaḷ piṟakkum eṉavum uyiroliyaṉkaḷil meyyoliyaṉkaḷ kalakkum eṉavum viritturikkum civañāṉamuṉivariṉ karuttu mutaleḻuttukkaḷ muppatu eṉṟa moḻiyiṉ aṭittaḷat tukkup peritum muraṇākum. oliyaṉukku muṉpu yāṉ eḻutiya viḷakkam koṇṭu teḷika. vaṭamoḻikkum piṟa emmoḻik kuṅkūṭa muṉivar karuttup poruntātu. piṟitoṉṟaṟ killāt taṉakkeṉṟa poruḷāṟṟal uṭiyatē eḻuttu allatu oliyaṉ eṉappaṭum. itaṉkaṇ kalappukku iṭaṉilli. muṉivar karuttiṉi uṭaṉpaṭṭāl, tamiḻukku uyiroliyaṉ mūṉṟē; akara mutalaveḻuttellām; eṉpataṉi nōkkiṉ akaram oṉṟē eñciniṟkum. tamiḻ nūṟpayiṟci yoṉṟumē yuṭiyār poruḷuṇarār eṉṟu muṉivar perumakaṉ uriyāciriyar uḷḷiṭṭōrip paḻitturittālum, ettuṇi moḻip payiṟciyāḷarum tamiḻiṉ uṇmi kāṇpatē meynneṟiyākum.
nūṟpā: 62 mayilinātar karuttu
“kuṟṟiya lukaram muṟippeyar maruṅkiṉ
   oṟṟiya nakaramici nakaramoṭu mutalum”
eṉa āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār ivvāṟu kuṟṟiyalukaram moḻikku mutalām eṉṟārōveṉiṉ.
nunti yukaraṅ kuṟuki voḻimutaṟkaṇ
vanta teṉiṉuyirmey yāmaṉittum - cantik
kuyirmutalā vantaṉiyum meyyuṇarcci yiṉṟi
mayalaṇiyu meṉṟataṉi māṟṟu.
ivaṟṟi viritturittu vitiyum vilakkum aṟintu koḷka” eṉa mēṟkaṇṭavāṟu naṉþṉūl uriyāciriyar mayilinātar tolkāppiyatti vēkamākac cāṭuvar.
nakarameyyē moḻimutal eṉpatu tolkāppiya nūṟpāviṉ poruḷ eṉṟu naṭinōkkilum puṇarcci nōkkilum innūṟpāviṉ tiṟaṉuriyil muṉpu nāṉ viḷakkiyuḷḷēṉ. nūṟpāviṉ poruḷ ituvātaliṉ mayilinātariṉ tolkāppiyak kuṟṟappāṭṭukku iṭaṉilli.
iṉi mayilinātar kūṟṟiyum vēṟuvakiyāl viḷakkuvōm. kuṟṟiyalukarattip poṟuttavari ukarattiyum ukaram ēṟiya valleḻutti yum oṟṟumi nayamākak koḷkiṉṟōm. aḵtāvatu ku cu ṭu tu pu ṟu eṉṟa iṟuti uyir meykaḷi ōreḻuttākak karutukiṉṟōm. ataṉālaṉṟō eḵku, paricu, pāṭu, eytu paṇpu, paṟṟu eṉpavi īṟṟayal pārttu aṟuvakik kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷāka eṇṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ku cu ṭu tu pu ṟukkaḷi īreṉa ōreḻuttākak karutāviṭṭāl ukaramē īrākak koṇṭāl. vaṉṟoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram oṉṟutāṉē irukkamuṭiyum.
itaṉāl nām aṟiyakkiṭappatu eṉṉa? kuṟṟiya ṉukarattiyum atu ēṟiyameyyiyum oṉṟākak karutuvatē ilakkaṇak kōṭpāṭām. atupōl nunti eṉṟa moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟkum `nu' eṉpataṉip pirittu nakiyāṭāmal, oṉṟākak koḷḷavēṇṭum. `kuṟṟiyalukaram nakaramoṭu mutalum' eṉṟu oru naṭiceyta tolkāppiyak karuttum ituvē.
cuṭṭu viṉā vaḻi akaratti oṭṭi ṅakaram mutalāka varum eṉṟu naṉṉūl kūṟum ariya varavi etirtiṟaṉ ceyyāta mayilinātar kuṟṟiyalukaratti yoṭṭi nakaram mutalākum eṉṟa ariya ilakkaṇattiyum ēṟpatē poruntum.
nūṟpā 63
moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram nunti muṟip peyaraḷavil amintu niṟṟalāṉum vēṟupāṭiṉmiyāṉum taṉippuṇarcci viti vēṇṭiyatilli. kuṟṟiyalukara nakarap puṇarcci vitikaḷē cālum eṉpatu eṉ urimuṭivu.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uri: `kātu kaṭṭu kattu murukku teruṭṭu eṉpaṉa muṟṟukaramum kuṟṟukaramumāyp poruḷ vēṟupaṭṭu niṉṟāṟpōla nunti eṉṟu itaḻkavittu muṟṟakkūṟiya viṭattum itaḻ kuviyāmal kuṟiyat kūṟiyaviṭattum oru kūṟiyaviṭattum oru poruḷē takkavāṟu kāṇka” eṉpatu ivar uri. nunti paṟṟi eḻutiya karuttu okkum; āṉāl kātu kaṭṭu eṉpaṉa muṟṟukaramāyiṉ viṉiyākavum kuṟṟukaramāyiṉ peyarākavum poruḷpaṭum eṉṟu oru putiya koḷkiyik kāṇkiṉṟōm. ilakkaṇap perumpulavar vēṅkaṭa rācaṉārum ikkoḷkiyi uṭaṉpaṭuvar. “moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram peyarccollilē niṟṟalāṉ muṟṟukaramāka olikkappaṭiṉum poruḷ vēṟupaṭātu” eṉṟataṉāṉē moḻiyiṟutik kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟṟiyalukaramākavum olikkum eṉpatum avvāṟu olittal viṉic colliṉ kaṇṇē eṉpatum āṇṭup poruḷ vēṟupaṭum eṉpatum pōtarum” eṉpatum ivar tarum viḷakkam. “appeyar maruṅkiṉ” eṉpatu nūṟpā. akarac cuṭṭi otukki viṭṭu peyar maruṅkiṉ eṉa vittukkoṇṭu viḷakkañ ceykiṟār. itu virittāyntu maṟukkavēṇṭiya koḷkiyātaliṉ, ivvuri takumiṭaṉaṉṟu; cila cuṭṭiyamivēṉ.
“neṭṭeḻut timparum toṭarmoḻi yīṟṟum
  kuṟṟiya lukaram vallā ṟūrntē”
eṉpatu kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟku iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum kūṟum moḻimarapu nūṟpā. ivvukaram peyarccoṟkaḷil varum eṉṟa kuṟippumilli; viṉiccollil neṭṭeḻuttimparum toṭarmoḻiyīṟṟum varum ukaram muṟṟukaramāka olikkum eṉṟa kuṟippum illi. peyarviṉi paṟṟiya pēccē illi. kuṟṟiyalukaram varum cūḻnili itu eṉpatu innūṟpāviṉ poruḷ. iccūḻniliyil nāṭu eṉpatu peyarāka varuka, viṉiyāka varuka. evvāṟu variṉum kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉpatuvē muṭivu. nāṭaṭintāṉ eṉap peyarāka iruppiṉum ataṉi nāṭeṉṟāṉ eṉa viṉivāka iruppiṉum puṇarcci muṭipu oṉṟātal kāṇka.
“muṟṟiya lukaramoṭu poruḷvēṟu paṭāatu
  appeyar maruṅkiṉ niliyiya lāṉa”
eṉṟa nūṟpā nunti muṟṟiyalukaramākavum olikkum eṉṟu kūṟavilli. avvāṟu koḷvōmēl moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉpatu valuviḻantu vēṇṭāta oṉṟeṉa ākiviṭātā? `iraṇṭutaliyiṭṭa mutalākirupaḵtu” eṉṟu puṇariyalil moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟ ēṉi mutaṉili yoppa naṉmatippuk koṭuttaliṉ muṟṟiyalukaramākavum olikkum eṉpatu poruntātu. muṟṟiyalukarattiṟkuriya nakarap puṇarcci viti peṟum eṉavum appuṇarcci yoṟṟumiyāl immuṟip peyar poruḷ vēṟṟumip paṭātu eṉavum teḷivippatē innūṟpāviṉ karuttu.
innūṟpāvi moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukaramākiya nuntiyaḷaviṟ koḷḷātu. īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟkuk koḷvatum īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaramellām muṟṟiyalukaramum ākum eṉak koḷvatum, aṅṅaṉam ākuṅkāl iruvaki yukaramum peyarum viṉiyumākap poruḷ vēṟupaṭum eṉak koḷvatum ellām karuttuc cumattalākum. inta nūṟpā ivvaḷavu perum moḻikkoḷkikku iṭaṉ tarumā?
`appeyar maruṅkiṉ niliyiyalāṉa' eṉṟu muṟip peyarccuṭṭu teḷivāka iruppavum akaraccuṭṭi viṭuttu, `peyar maruṅkiṉ' eṉa pirittukkoṇṭu, kuṟṟiyalukara mellām peyarām; muṟṟiyalukaramellām muṉṉili viṉiyām eṉṟu palapaṭak kūṟuvatu uriyikal eṉka. iṉṉa vīṟellām peyarākum. iṉṉavīṟellām viṉiyākum eṉṟa pākupāṭum tamiḻiyalpaṉṟu.


nūṟpā 71
iḷampūraṇar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar urikaḷ:
tapu eṉpatu ōcivēṟṟumiyāl iruporuḷ tarum eṉavum paṭuttuccolla nī cā eṉat taṉviṉiyām; eṭuttuc colla nī oṉṟaṉic cāvi eṉap piṟaviṉiyām eṉavum uriceykuvar. ōci vēṟṟumiyāl ilakkaṇa vēṟṟumiyō poruḷ vēṟṟumiyō yuṟutal tamiḻiyalpaṉṟu. tattam kuṟippum poruḷ tarum um ē ō eṉṟa iṭiccoṟkaṭkuk kūṭa āciriyar ōci vēṟṟumippāṭu collavilli. peyariyum viṉiyiyum teriniliviṉiyiyum kuṟippu viṉiyiyum viṉiyālaṇiyum peyariyum toṭar niliyālallatu ōciyāl vēṟupāṭuṇaralām eṉṟu tamiḻilakkaṇam kūṟiṟṟilatu. innūṟpāvilum; `itu ceyal vēṇṭum' eṉṟa viṉiyiyalnūṟpāvilum iruvayiṉ niliyi ōci kāṭṭum eṉṟu cuṭṭak kāṇōm. eṉavē mīyoliyaṉ eṉṟa olikkūṟu tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ aṭippaṭiyilli eṉpatu teḷivu.


nūṟpā: 57. peruviḷakkak kaṭṭuri - iṇippu
tamiḻil moḻi mutaleḻuttukkaḷiṉ varampu
(itu tamiḻppalkalikkaḻakam 28-3-1985 il kūṭṭiya tamiḻ ilakkaṇak kōṭpāṭṭu vaḷarccik karuttaraṅkil va.cupa.māṇikkam paṭitta āyvukkaṭṭuri)
tamiḻmoḻikku uriya eḻuttukkaḷ ittuṇiya eṉavum avi uyir, mey, cārpu eṉṟa perumpirivum, kuṟil neṭil valliṉam melliṉam iṭiyiṉam eṉṟa uṭpirivum paṭum eṉavum ellā ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷum toṭakkattē kaṟpip patik kāṇkiṉṟōm. ataṉoṭu amiyātu coṟku mutalāka varum eḻuttukkaḷ, iṟutiyāka varum eḻuttukkaḷ, iṭinili mayakkākavarum eḻuttukkaḷ eṉavāṅku mutalīṟiṭi nilikaḷ niralākac cuṭṭappaṭuvatiyum kāṇkiṉṟōm. ciṟuvakuppukkut tamiḻ payiṟṟum ciṟṟilakkaṇaṅ kaḷilum mutal īṟu iṭinili paṟṟiya varampukaḷ iṭampeṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa. ayalnāṭṭavarkkuk kaṟpikkum payiṟci kāṭṭikaḷilum im muttaṉmikaḷ olikaḷiṉ āyvoṭum viḷakkam peṟukiṉṟaṉa.
tolkāppiyam mutalāvatu nūṉmarapil iṭinili mayakkattik kūṟi iraṇṭāvatu moḻimarapil mutaleḻuttu īṟṟeḻuttukkaḷi virivāka iyampum. naṉṉūl eḻuttiyalil mutaṉili iṭinili iṟutinili mūṉṟiyum ivvariciyiṟ kūṟum. vīracōḻiya mutalāṉanūlkaḷum immarapip pōṟṟi moḻikiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl mutalīṟu iṭi eṉṟa mukkūṟu eḻuttilakkaṇa vitikaṭkellām aṭicamayam eṉpatu oruvāṟu uṇarappaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷmoḻi mutaleḻuttiṉ varalāṟum vaḷarcciyum varampum etirnōkkum paṟṟi ik kaṭṭuriyil cila neṟikaḷi ārāyvōm.

ā. vēṟupāṭu
