ilakkaṇaviḷakkamum tamiḻnūlum iṭippaṭṭa nūlkaḷic cārātu tolkāppiya vaḻic cellukiṉṟaṉa tamiḻppērakarāti, vīracōḻiya naṉṉūl pōla yakarattukku āṟuyirkoḷḷum. yippōtu, yeppōtu eṉac cila eḻuttu vaḻakku iruppiṉum avi ēṟkappaṭavilli. 
tolkāppiyam kūṟiya nunti eṉṉum moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram piṉṉar vīḻcciyāyiṟṟu. ivvilakkaṇatti uṭaṉpaṭṭāl meymmutaṟpuṇarcci eṉṟa nili illātu pōkum eṉṟu mayilinātar orupāṅku maṟutturippar. naṉṉūlār koḻukompupaṭa moḻinta ñakara mutaṉ moḻiyum piṉṉar kaṭipōkavilli. `avvi oṭṭi ṅavvum mutalākum' eṉṟa tiṟaṉil naṭiyē ṅakaram ēṉi meykaḷukku nikarāṉataṉṟu eṉpatik kāṭṭum eṉac caṅkara namaccivāyar teḷivupaṭuttiyuḷḷār.
uriyāciriyarkaḷiṉ karuttukkaḷ
mēṟkāṭṭiyavaṟṟuḷ cakara ñakara yakara mutaṉmoḻikaḷiṭi ilakkaṇikaḷ vēṟupaṭak kāraṇam yātu? ilakkiyam kaṇṭataṟku ilakkaṇam iyampal' eṉpatu vitiyātaliṉ, ilakkiyam iruppavum vēṟupaṭal takumā? uḷḷattip paṭṭāṅku uṭaṉpaṭuvatu ilakkaṇamā?  uḷḷatit tiṟaṉāyntu cīrtūkkuvatu ilakkaṇamā? ivi vampāṉa viṉākkaḷ alla. karuttu vēṟṟumi matikkum viṉākkaḷ.
iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum caṅkappaṉuvalkaḷ, cilappatikāram, maṇimēkali, cintāmaṇi, peruṅkati teyvap pācuraṅkaḷ mutalāṉavaṟṟil putiya moḻi mutalkaḷ palavāṟu pukuntukiṭappati aṟiyātārallar.
yūkamoṭu māmuka mucukkali (muruku.300)
yūpam naṭṭa viyaṉkaḷam palakol (puṟam.15)
yūpa neṭuntūṇ vēta vēḷvi (puṟam.224)
piṇiyūpam eḻuntāṭa (maturik. 27).
talinumin teñciya poṉmaliyūpamoṭu (patiṟ. 67)
uñciyiṟ ṟōṉṟiya yūkiyantaṇaṉ (maṇi.15)
nāka naṉṉāṭṭu nāṉūṟi yōcaṉi (maṇi.9)
pakuvāy ñamaliyoṭu pimputal (porun.112)
tāḻaṭumpu malinta puṇarivaḷi ñarala (patiṟ.30)
terikōl ñamaṉṉ pōla (puṟam.6)
vari ñimiṟu imiṟum mārpupiṇi makaḷir (patiṟ.50)
illat tuṇikkup pāleyta iṟiyamaṉ (pari.11)
yavaṉar ōtima viḷakkiṉ (perumpāṇ. 315)
valipuṇar yākki vaṉkaṇ yavaṉar (mullip. 61)
yavaṉar iyaṟṟiya viṉimāṇpāvi (neṭunal. 101)
iṉṉaṇam pērilakkiyaṅkaḷil vantukiṭakkum palvēṟu (yū, yō, ñī, ya) moḻi mutalikaḷiyum cakaṭam, camam, cantu, cantam, cavaṭṭum, civam, ciṉam mutalāṉa varavukaḷiyum nam tolluriyāciriyarkaḷ kāṇātār eṉṟuaṟiyāmi ēṟṟa muṭiyumā? cevvaṉ kaṇṭuvittu iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum eḻuttatikāravuriyil koḷḷāmikkuc cila viḷakkaṅkaḷ varintuḷḷaṉar. avi varumāṟu:
1. 	cakaṭam eṉavum ciyam eṉavu; vilakkiṉavum varumāveṉiṉ avaṟṟuḷ āriyac citivallātaṉa kaṭi collilli eṉpataṉāṟ koḷka. (iḷam)
2. 	ñaḻiyiṟṟu eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa vilakkiṉavum varumāveṉiṉ avi aḻivaḻakkeṉṟu maṟukka. (iḷam)
3. 	yavaṉar eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa vilakkiṉavum varumāveṉiṉ avi āriyac citiveṉṟu maṟukka. (iḷam)
4. 	caṭṭi cakaṭam camaḻppu eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa kaṭi collilli eṉpataṉāṟ koḷka. ciyam curiyam eṉpavaṟṟi vaṭacolleṉa maṟukka (nac)
5.	‘ñamalitanta maṉavuc cūluṭumpu’ eṉpatu ticiccol, ñaḻiyiṟṟu eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa iḻivaḻakku (nac)
6.	yavaṉar yutti yukam yōkam yuvaṉam eṉpaṉa vaṭacolleṉa maṟukka (nac)
7. 	ñeṇṭu eṉpatu ñaṇṭu āyp piṉṉar naṇṭu eṉa marīiyiṟṟu (nac.col)
8. 	campu, caḷḷi, caṭṭi, camaḻppu eṉa varum. ivi toṉṟutoṭṭu vantaṉavāyiṉ mutalākāvaṟṟiṉ kaṇ 

cakarak kiḷaviyum avaṟṟō raṟṟē
a ai oḷa veṉum mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē
eṉa vilakkār āciriyar. ataṉāṉ avi piṟkālattut tōṉṟiya collēyām eṉpatu (cēṉā)
urineṟiyaṅkaḷ
tolkāppiyavuriyāciriyarkaḷiṉ kuṟippiliruntu, moḻi mutaleḻuttukkōḷ paṟṟic cila neṟiyaṅkaḷi avar tam karuttākak koḷḷalām.
a. vaṭacoṟkaḷ
ā. āriyaccitivukaḷ
i. ticiccoṟkaḷ
ī. aḻi vaḻakkukkaḷ
u. maruviya vaṭivaṅkaḷ
tollilakkiyaṅkaḷil yūkam, yūpam, yūki, yōcaṉi eṉpaṉa payiṉṟiruntālum mutaṟkāraṇattāl maṟukkap paṭṭaṉa. ñāla, ñamaṉ, ñimīṟu pōṉṟaṉa tirivaṭivaṅkaḷ eṉṟa karuttāl oppup peṟavilli pōlum. maruviya urumāṟikaṭku ilakkaṇa vīṟu koṭuttāl, nāḷ vaḻakkil virivu paṭṭum uṇarccivayappaṭṭum uṟuppuk kuṟipaṭṭum pēṇātu pēcum olittiri pellām mutaleḻuttāki vantuviṭum. reṇṭu, rempa, rompa, rēḻi, rāppicci, rāvutal, rampam, rāttiri, rāvāṇṭi, rācaṉ, rattam, rakki, rāppi, lakku, lāttu lāvāṇṭi eṉṟāṅku tamiḻccoṟkaḷē innili eytumeṉiṉ, ayaṟcoṟkaḷiṉ tākkam tamiḻmuḻakkamāki viṭātā? eṉavē nam paṇṭi uriyāciriyarmārkaḷ kocci yuruttamiḻum vaṭamoḻiyum tamiḻiṉ moḻimutal ilakkaṇavarampukku aṭippaṭiyākā eṉṟu  koṇṭa moḻic cemmi teḷivākum. iṭik kālattup putiya moḻi mutaleḻuttukkaḷ taḻuvi vīracōḻiya mutalāṉa nūlkaḷip pārttum vaṭamoḻi mikuntu pukunta ilakkiyap paṉuvalkaḷip pārttum ataṉpiṉṉarē ivvāṟu oru naṉmuṭivu kaṇṭaṉar eṉṟu koḷḷavēṇṭum. vaṭamoḻi matippu mikka urip perumakaṉ nacciṉārkkiṉiyar patiṉāṉkāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭil vaṭamoḻi pērātikkam ceyta kālattil:- “kālam, ulakam eṉpaṉa vaṭacol alla; āciriyar (tolkāppiyar) vaṭacoṟkaḷi eṭuttōti ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟār ākaliṉ” (kiḷavi.58) eṉṟu aṟuti yiṭṭurittiruppatu eṉṟum naccutaṟkuriya iṉiya eḻuttākum.
iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum piṟkāla vaḷarcciyil caṭṭi cakaṭam pōlum cakara mutaṉmoḻi yoṉṟi ottuk koṇṭārkaḷēyaṉṟip piṟavaṟṟi uṭaṉpaṭavilli. vaṭamoḻic cārpuṭiya cēṉāvariyarum `kaṭicollilli kālat tuppaṭivē' eṉṟa nūṟpāvil cakaramutalic cuṭṭiṉārēyaṉṟi ci, cu, ya, yu, yū, yu mutalāyavaṟṟik kāṭṭavilli. ilakkiyaṅkaḷil putiya mutaleḻuttukkaḷ peruki vantiruntum ittolluri yāḷarkaḷ avaṟṟit taḻuvāmikku mēṟcuṭṭiya neṟiyaṅkaḷē kāraṇamākum. cakaratti maṭṭum taḻuvik koṇṭataṟku iccakaramoḻital tamiḻiliruntē piṟantaṉa eṉpatu kāraṇamām. iṉṉōr neṟimiyip piṉpaṟṟi ilakkaṇa viḷakka nūlārum tamiḻnūlārum moḻi mutaleḻuttu vakuppārāyiṉar.
vīracōḻiyam nēminātam naṉṉūl, cāminātam muttuvīriyam mutaliya nūlkaḷ, pukuntavi vaṭacolleṉap pārātu. ilakkiyam kaṇṭataṟku ilakkaṇam kūṟavēṇṭum eṉṟa potu neṟiyil ci, cu, ya,yu, yū, yō, yo eṉṟa mutalikaḷiyum ēṟpārāyiṉar. tolkāppiyattukkuppiṉ ivvaṭamoḻikaḷum ticiccoṟkaḷum tamiḻiṉkaṇ payiṟṟu vantamiyāl koḷḷa vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu eṉpar civañāṉa muṉivar. ivarkaḷ muttiriyē piṟkālap peruvaḻikkāyiṟṟu. īṇṭuṅkūṭa 11 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiya vīracōḻiya kāla mutal 19 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil eḻunta muttuvīriyamvari vēṟu putumoḻi mutalkaḷukku iṭamilli eṉpatu niṉiyattakum.
maṉṉiya meyvarampu
itukāṟum kūṟiya karuttut tokutiyiliruntu moḻimutal varalāṟṟil maṉṉiya oru pēruṇmiyi eṭuttukkāṭṭa virumpukiṉṟēṉ. tolkāppiyak kālatti liruntu ituvari vanta moḻimutal eṇṇikkiyeḻutti nōkkiṉ 94 - 105 eṉṟa vaṭṭattuḷ vēṟupāṭu aṭaṅkum. cakarattil mūṉṟum yakarattil aintum piṟavaṟṟil mūṉṟumē kūṭiyavi eṉa aṟiyappaṭum. mūvāyiram āṇṭumoḻi varalāṟṟil māṟṟam 10 viḻukkāṭu cālap peritaṉṟu. inniliyilum māṟiya iṭam etu? māṟāta iṭam etu eṉṟu cīrtūkka vēṇṭum.
tolkāppiyar kūṟiya uyir paṉṉiraṇṭum moḻi mutalākum eṉṟa ilakkaṇam māṟavē illi. atu maṭṭumaṉṟu enta meykaḷ moḻi mutalākumeṉṟu aṉṟu vitittārō aviyē kūṭutal kuṟiviṉṟip piṉ ilakkaṇaṅ kaḷilum koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. eṉpatu viyappāṉa ceytiyākum. ka, ca, ta, pa, ña, ma, ya, va eṉṟu valliṉattil nāṉkum melliṉattil mūṉṟum iṭi yiṉattil iraṇṭum āka oṉpatu meykaḷ tolkāppiyattiyal varampuppaṭṭaṉa. piṉ vanta enta ilakkaṇa nūlāvatu ṟa, ṭa, ṇa, ṉa, ra, la, ḻa, ḷa eṉṟa mey mutalāka varum eṉṟu mēlum cērntuḷḷatā? illiyē. innūlkaḷ cērttaṉavellām tolkāppiyam vēliyiṭṭa meyttaḷattil, cakarattiṉmēl mūṉṟuyirum yakarattiṟku aintuyirum kūṭutalāka ēṟivarum eṉṟa uyirp perukkamēyām. eṉavē moḻi mutalākum paṉṉīruyirilum oṉpatu meyyilum māṟāviyaṟkiyi aṟutiyākak kāṇkiṉṟōm. ikkaṭṭuri moḻimutalāyvil putitākak kaṇṭa muṭipu itu. tamiḻmoḻi eṉṟum viḷaṅkun taṉmiyum cemmiyum peṟṟiyiruppataṟku moḻi mutalvarampu peruṅkariyākum.
moḻimutal immeyvarampu, vaṭamoḻi evvaḷavu kalappiṉum, uṟutiyāka mēṟkoḷḷappaṭṭatu eṉpataṉi ilakkiyattālum ilakkaṇattālum teḷikiṉṟām. vaṭamoḻiyil ellāveḻuttum collukku mutalāki varuvatum taṉimeyyum mutalāki varuvatum uṇṭu. ammoḻiyiṉ iyalpu atu. avi tamiḻukku varuṅkāl enta vaṭaveḻuttu entat tamiḻeḻuttāka māṟal vēṇṭum eṉṟu cuṭṭi olivēli kōliya perumi puttamittirarkkum pavaṇantiyārkkum uṇṭu. irumoḻikkum uyirilum meyyilum potuveḻuttāka uḷḷa nili cikkalaṟṟatu ātaliṉ ciṟappu nili eḻuttukkaḷukku māṟṟuvaḻi kāṭṭiṉar.
vaṭanūl marapum taḻuvit tamiḻilakkaṇam kūṟuval eṉṟu veḷippaṭac colliya puttamittiraṉār ulōpam, vekuviriki, taṟpuruṭaṉ, cāmacam, tuvantuvam, kaṉmatārayaṉ viyañcaṉam, pirakiruti, tattitam, kāritam eṉavāṟu vaṭamoḻik kuṟiyīṭukaḷi aḷḷik koṇṭa pōtum, tamiḻoli varampik kaṭṭik kāttār.
“kūṭṭeḻut tippiṉ yaralakkaḷ tōṉṟiṭiṟ kūṭṭiṭiyē
  ōṭṭeḻut tākap peṟumo rikāram vavvukko ruvvām
  mīṭṭeḻut tuttami ḻallaṉa pōmvēṟu tēyaccolliṉ
  māṭṭeḻut tummita ṉālaṟi maṟṟi vikārattiṉē”	 (59)
eṉṟa kārikiyilum vēṟu iraṇṭu kārikikaḷilum altamiḻ oliccoṟkaḷ mutal īṟu iṭiniliyil tamiḻāntaṉmiyi vitikkiṉṟār. `tamiḻallaṉa pōm' eṉṟu ātti cūṭi pōla oliyilum kūṟukiṉṟār.
vīracōḻiyatti ivvakiyil cevvaṉ piṉpaṟṟum pavaṇantiyār vaṭamoḻic colveḷḷam kariyuṭittu ōṭuvatik kaṇṭu pataviyalil nāṉku nūṟpākkaḷ yāttaṉar. iṉṉa vaṭacol varalām. varakkūṭātu eṉṟu poruḷpaṟṟik kūṟavilli eṉpatum, olinili māṟṟattukkē varampiṭṭār eṉpatum, tamiḻiṉa olik kāppukku ivvarampu cempiṭṭuc ceyta,¨iñciyāyiṟṟu eṉpatum niṉiyattakum.
“ravviṟ kammuta lāmuk kuṟilum
  lavviṟ kimmuta liraṇṭum yavviṟ
  kiyyu momuḻita lāki muṉ varumē” 	(148)
ra, la, ya eṉpavi tamiḻil illāta meykaḷalla, uḷḷaviyē. eṉiṉum maṉṉiya meyvarampu kākkum nōkkōṭu pavaṇantiyār arakkaṉ, arataṉam ilāpam, irāku, ulōpam, iyakkaṉ eṉa uyireḻuttukkaḷi oṭṭiṉār. ittakiya māṟṟam pārvikkuc ciṟiyatākat tōṉṟiṉum, tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ kaṭṭukkōppukkup pēraraṇāyiṟṟu. moḻiyiṉ amippu ituvātaliṉ, maṉṉiya meykaḷ māṟāmal ilaṇṭaṉ, ilēcu, urōmāpuri, uruciyā, urūpā, ireyil, iracītu, iliṅkam, irāci, irēki, irāṇuvam, irāmaliṅkam, irāmacāmi, irāmāṉucam, irāypakatūr, irāmēccuram, iraṅkūṉ eṉa makkaḷ naṭimuṟiyil iyalpāka eḻutuvatiyum kāṇkiṉṟōm.
tamiḻ olikkūṭṭamippu
iṭikkālak kāppiyaṅkaḷilum piṟa ilakkiyaṅkaḷilum ūr vakiyālum peyarvakiyālum tuṟivakiyālum vaṭamoḻit tākkam mikuti eṉpatu veḷippaṭi. irāmaṉ, ilakkuvaṉ, irāvaṇaṉ, iraṇiyaṉ, uruttirattaṉ, patumāvati, cītattaṉ, cītaraṉ. irācamāpuram, iratanūpuram, irattiṉa pallavam, titi, puṉaruṟpavam, cakacamalam, attuvitam, piraṇavam mutalāṉa coṟkūṭṭamellām vaṭacollā yiṉum, vīramāmuṉivariṉ tamiḻttōṟṟampōla, mutalīṟu iṭinilikkaṇ tamiḻurup peṟṟu amikiṉṟaṉa.
cīvaka cintāmaṇi, peruṅkati, cūḷāmaṇi, irāmāyaṇam. pāratam ellām taḻuvu kāppiyaṅkaḷāyiṉum poruḷiyum colliyum taḻuviṉavēyaṉṟi vaṭamoḻi yolik kaṭṭamippit taḻuvavilli eṉpatum, puṟavoliyamippit tamiḻoliyamippukku uṭpaṭuttik koṇṭaṉa eṉpatum nām karutavēṇṭiya teṟiyākum. vaṭamoḻikkum tamiḻmoḻikkum ilakkaṇam oṉṟē; aḵtāvatu vaṭamoḻiyilakkaṇamē tamiḻilakkaṇamām eṉṟa poyyāṉa orumikkōṭpāṭu kāṇattuṭitta pirayōka vivēka nūlāciriyarum ivvoli māṟṟatti aṟavē otukkiviṭa muṭiyavilli. “potuveḻuttāyp paṟpala vākit tirivatum cāṟṟiṉar paṇṭuṇarntōr” eṉṟu uṭaṉpaṭuvar.
“ilayitta taṉṉilila yittatāma lattāl
   ilayitta vāṟuḷatā vēṇṭum - ilayitta
   tattitiyi veṉṉi ṉaḻiyā taviyaḻiva
   tattitiyu mātiyumā maṅku.
lakaram tamiḻil moḻi mutalātu eṉpataṉāl, iccivañāṉa pōta veṇpāvil tavamutalvar meykaṇṭār ikarattic cērttu ilayittatu eṉṟu varampu naṉikaṭip piṭittiruppatu moḻimutal ilakkaṇattiṉ oḻuṅkikkāṭṭum. iv varampiṉi aṭi toṭi koṇṭa ceyyuḷiṭattēyum nam muṉṉōr māṟātu pōṟṟiṉar eṉṟāl, nekiḻccimikka urinaṭiyil kikkoḷvatu maṉamiruntāl naṉi eḷitu eṉpatu veḷippaṭi.
“vaṭacoṟ kiḷavi vaṭaveḻut  torīi
   eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta colla kummē” 	(tol.884)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam vaṭamoḻit taḻuvilakkaṇattic curukkamākak kūṟukiṉṟatu. vīracōḻiyam naṉṉūl pōla iṉṉa vaṭaveḻuttu iṉṉatamiḻeḻuttākum eṉṟu taṉi vitappuc ceyyavilli. eṉiṉum“eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta col”eṉṟa nuṭpattil cila kuṟippu aṭaṅkiyuḷa. īṇṭu eḻuttu eṉpatu eḻuttilakkaṇattik kuṟittu niṟkiṉṟatu. aṅṅaṉam varum vaṭacol moḻimutal, moḻiyīṟu, moḻiyiṭi eṉpavaṟṟilum tamiḻccol vaṭivupeṟal vēṇṭum eṉavum olimāṟṟam tamiḻc collamippukku ottirukka vēṇṭum eṉavum koḷḷa vēṇṭum. ikkuṟippiṉi uṇarntē vīracōḻiyarum naṉþṉūlārum ivvilakkaṇatti mēlum virivupaṭuttiṉar.
vēṟu ayaṉmoḻikaḷ
vīracōḻiyam `vēṟu tēyac colliṉ māṭṭeḻuttum itaṉālaṟi' eṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyallāta vēṟu ayalmoḻi kaḷiṉ kalappiṟkum etiratu nōkkik kaṭṭamippuc cuṭṭi yuḷḷatu. ulakap pēruṟavil palvēṟu moḻikaḷiṉ tākkam ceytik karuvikaḷāl taṭukkavoṇṇātu vaḷarntu varum, iññāṉṟu, moḻi mutaṉilikaḷ paṟṟi muṉṉōrtam neṟiyaṅkaḷi nām cīrtūkkak kaṭamip paṭṭirukkiṉṟōm. piṟa tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ, āṅkilam, piṟa ayaṉmoḻikaḷ ivaṟṟilellām vaṭamoḻi yotta olikaḷē perumpālum uḷḷaṉa. vaṭamoḻiyil illāta olikaḷ piṟa moḻikaḷil cilaviṉum cilavē. ātaliṉ nammuṉṉōr vaṭamoḻi māṟṟukkuk kūṟiya olippaṭu varampukaḷi vēṟu ayalmoḻikaṭkum ēṟṟuk koḷḷalām. ataṉpiṉṉum cila moḻikaṭkuc cila ciṟappeḻuttukkaḷ irukkumeṉiṉ, tēvippaṭiṉ, iṉṉatamiḻettākat tiriya vēṇṭum eṉṟu tēvikkēṟpa viti ceytu koḷḷalām. evvāṟāyiṉum, tolkāppiyamum piṟkāla ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum māṟātu teḷinta moḻi mutal eḻuttukkaḷiyum, maṉṉiya meym mutalikaḷiyum pōṟṟik kāttu avvaṭṭattiṟkuḷ ayaṉmoḻi varavi aṭakkikkoḷḷavēṇṭum.
ulakam peritāyiṉum ovvoru viḷiyāṭṭiṟkum matikkattakka iṭavarampum veḷi eṉṟu collattakka matippilāp puṟam pōkkum iruppatupōla moḻikaṭkum uṇṭu. atilum ivvarampu tamiḻukkuc ceṟivāka uṇṭu. piṟamoḻikaḷil iṉṉa ivveḻuttut tāṉ moḻi mutalākalām. ātaṟkilli eṉṟa vitivilakku iruppatāka yāṉaṟintavari kāṇōm. ammoḻikkuriya akarātikaḷip pārttāl itu pulappaṭum. ammoḻi ilakkaṇaṅkaḷil, uyireḻuttu, meyyeḻuttu, neṭil, kuṟil, iṉṉavakip pākupāṭu cuṭṭappaṭuvataṉṟi, moḻi taṉilikaḷ kūṟappaṭa villi.
puṇarccikkōḷ
tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ nilivēṟu. mutaṟkaṇ kuṟittapaṭi moḻi mutal, moḻiyīṟu, moḻiyiṭi eṉṟa mukkaḷaṉ tamiḻil eḻuttilakkaṇattukku iṉṟiyamiyātatu. tolkāppiya eḻuttatikāra oṉpatu iyalkaḷil piṉṉuḷḷa ēḻiyalkaḷ puṇarccic ceyki paṟṟiyaṉa, nūṉmarapu, moḻi marapu eṉṟa mutaliraṇṭum puṇarnilikku vēṇṭiya karuvi kaḷiyum aṭittaḷaṅkaḷiyum varampaṟukkum iyalkaḷākum.
puṇarcci eṉpatu nilimoḻiyīṟu varumoḻi mutaloṭu iyivatu, iyiyuṅkāl peṟum iyalpukaḷiyum tiripukaḷiyum virivupaṭak kūṟuvatu.
“uyiriṟu colmuṉ uyirvaru vaḻiyum
  uyiriṟu colmuṉ meyvaru vaḻiyum” 	(tol.107)
“niṟutta colliṉ īṟā keḻuttoṭu
  kuṟittuvaru kiḷavi mutaleḻut tiyiya” 	(tol.108)
“niṉṟacol līṟum varuñcol mutalum niravi”	(vīra. 9)
“meyyuyir mutalīṟām iru pataṅkaḷum” 	(naṉ. 151)
eṉa varum palvēṟu puṇarvu nūṟpākkaḷil col paṟṟip pēcātu īṟum mutalum paṟṟiya aṭippaṭiyil puṇarcci cuṭṭappaṭuvatāl, evveḻuttu īṟākum, evveḻuttu mutalākum eṉṟa moḻikkaṇippu tamiḻukku oruvantam vēṇṭaṟpāṟṟu. moḻi marapu eṉṟa iraṇṭāvatu iyal pattu nūṟpākkaḷāl mutaṉili paṟṟiyum, patiṉāṉku nūṟpāk kaḷāl iṟutinili paṟṟiyum kaṟpikkiṉṟatu. itupārvikkuc ciṟṟilakkaṇamākat tōṉṟiṉālum oṉṟu mutal pattuvari eṇṇa aṟiyātāṉ katiyē inta ilakkaṇa vaṟivilakku ēṟpaṭum.
`meṉmi yum iṭimiyum varūuṅkāli'	(tol.130)
`moḻimuta lākum ellā veḻuttum
varuvaḻi niṉṟa āyiru puḷḷiyum 	( 147)
`aḷaviṟkum niṟiyiṟkum moḻimuta lāki
uḷaveṉap paṭṭa oṉpatiṟ ṟeḻuttē' 	(170)
`valleḻut tiyiyiṉ avveḻuttu mikumē' 	(  357)
`melleḻut tiyiyiṉavveḻut tākum' 	( 380)
iṉṉaṇam varum iṭaṅkaḷil ellām melleḻuttu, iṭiyeḻuttu, valleḻuttu, moḻi mutaleḻuttu eṉa varumoḻi curukkamākac cuṭṭapaṭṭatēṉṟi iṉṉa taṉiyeḻuttu eṉṟu cuṭṭavilli. ataṟkuk kāraṇam moḻimarapil ivi paṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷ uṇṭu. eṉavē tamiḻppuṇarccikku īṟṟu nili, mutalnili eṉṟa irumuṉiyil vaḷarntuḷḷa māṟā, māṟṟaviyalā varalāṟṟamippiṉi nām uṇarkiṉṟōm.
eḻuttu, col, poruḷ eṉṟa mūṉṟatikāraṅkaḷuḷ eḻuttatikāram īṟu mutal iṇinta puṇarcciyatikāramākum. tamiḻil moḻiyiyalpum oliyiyalpum mayaṅkātu terikkum kaṭṭumāṉa atikāramumākum eḻuttum collum moḻi paṟṟiya irukūṟukaḷē. moḻiyatikāram eṉṟa oru peyarāl ivviraṇṭiṉiyum aṭakki ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiyirukkalāmē. ēṉ aṅṅaṉam muṉṉōr kūṟavilli? eḻuttatikāram mēṟkāṭṭiyapaṭi tamiḻukku virintu paranta iṉṟiyamiyāp puṇarccik kaṭṭamippiṉik kūṟuvatātaliṉ, taṉiyatikāram eṉṟa ciṟappu aḷikka vēṇṭiya tāyiṟṟu.
ippēruṇmiyi niṉiviṟ koṇṭāl moḻimutal eḻuttu varalāṟṟil ēṟpaṭṭa maṉṉiya oḻuṅkiyalip purintu koḷḷaviyalum. paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirum ka ca ta pa ña na ma ya va eṉṟa oṉpatu meyyum tolkāppiyar kāla mutal maṉṉiya mutalkaḷāka varuvatik kaṇṭōm. tamiḻil taṉimey mutalāka varātu. `uyirmey yallaṉa moḻi mutalākā' eṉṟapaṭi vyāti, tyāṉam, krupi, praṇavam eṉṟu vārāmal, vantāl, viyāti, tiyāṉam, kirupi. piraṇavam eṉṟu uyirmeyyākavē varum. eṉiṉum varumoḻi mutal kūṟumpōtu kaṇ + pārvi; tamiḻ +nūl; vaḷḷi + koṭi; koṭi + yātu eṉpavaṟṟil p, n, k, y eṉat taṉimeykaḷiyē puṇarccikkuriya eḻuttukkaḷākak karutukiṉṟōm. tamiḻamip piṉpaṭi, uyirmeyyē collukku mutalāka varumeṉṟālum, puṇarnilikku vēṟṟumi nayattāl meyyimaṭṭum koḷkiṉṟōm. ātaliṉmoḻi mutaleḻuttu eṉṟu kaṇakkiṭuṅkāl taṉimeykaḷē kaṇakkiṭappaṭum. muṉṉarē maṉṉiyameykaḷ eṉṟu ivaṟṟik kuṟippiṭṭikkiṟēṉ.
tolkāppiyarukkuppiṉ kūṭutalāṉa cilavuyirkaḷ cakarameykkum yakarameykkum vantuḷa. meymmēl ēṟiya evvuyirkkum puṇarcciyilakkaṇattu matippilli. muṉṉiṉṟum meyyaḷavukku iyalpum tiripum kūṟappaṭum. ātaliṉ, tolkāppiyattukkum piṉþṉūlkaḷukkum moḻi mutaleḻuttukkaḷil cila vēṟṟumikaḷ iruntālum avvēṟṟumikaḷ ēṟiya uyirpaṟṟiyaṉavātalāl, putiya puṇarccittākkattukku iṭamilli eṉpatu uṇaravēṇṭiya oru karuttu. curuṅkakkūṟiṉ tolkāppiyattup puṇarccikku varumoḻiyākak kūṟappaṭṭa ka ca ta pa ña na  ma ya va eṉṟa maṉṉiya oṉpatu meykaḷē piṉṉūlkaḷilum mēṟkoḷḷap paṭṭaṉa. eṉavē varumoḻip puṇarkōṭukaḷ māṟāmi teḷivākum.

putiya moḻi mutal?
toṉṟutoṭṭu itukāṟum palvēṟu māṟutal koṇṭa tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlkaḷil moḻimutalmey maṭṭum orupeṟṟittāka niṟpataṟkē kāraṇam puṇarccikkōḷ eṉpatu eṉ tuṇivu. itaṉi vēṟoru vakiyākak kāṇalām. ravi, reṭi, roṭṭi, rēṭiyō, ruṭi mutalāṉa rakarac coṟkaḷiyum; laṭṭu, lāṭam, luṅki, lēvātēvi eṉṟiṉṉa lakarac coṟkaḷiyum; ṭamāram, ṭāṇā, ṭilli, ṭelipōṉ mutalāṉa ṭakarac coṟkaḷiyum ivvāṟē piṟa mutaṟ coṟkaḷiyum moḻimutalākum eṉṟu ēṟṟukkoṇṭāl eṉṉa? vēṟṟumoḻikaḷil ellām neṭuṅkaṇakkil uḷḷa ellā eḻuttukkaḷum moḻi mutalākumpōtu, tamiḻum avvaḻicceṉṟu vaḷarntāl eṉṉa? eṉṟu nallaṉparkaḷē viṉavalām. viṉavukiṉṟaṉar. viṉavik koṇṭutāṉiruppar. innalviṉāviṟku muḻumiyāka etirmaṟumoḻikūṟuvataṟku ikkaṭṭuri poṟpaṉṟu eṉiṉum, mūvāyiram āṇṭukku mēlāka ellā ilakkaṇanūlum oru paṭittākap pōṟṟi vanta marapiyē nāṉ mīṇṭum cuṭṭik kāṭṭa virumpukiṉṟēṉ. rakara lakara ṭakaram pōṉṟa mutaṉmoḻikaḷik koṇṭāl eḻuttilakkaṇattil ipputu mutalkaṭkup puttilakkaṇam vakukka vēṇṭumaṉṟō?
avar + ravi vāṅkiṉār
cāppāṭu + reṭi
vīṭṭil + rēṭiyō
palar + ruṭiyāyiṉar
pattu + laṭṭu vāṅki vā
kutirikku + lāṭam kaṭṭiṉar
nālu + luṅki vēṇṭum
kār + ṭayar
avaṉ + ṭilli ceṉṟāḷ
iṅṅaṉam rakara lakara ṭakara mutalāya coṟkaḷ varumpōtu enta nilimoḻikku muṉ evvāṟellām māṟum eṉa ovvoru meykkum puṇariyalkaḷil viti collavēṇṭum. varumoḻi mutal eṉa oṉṟik koṇṭāl tamiḻilakkaṇam ataṟkeṉa ōriṭam aḷittu vaḻi cuṭṭa vēṇṭum eṉpatu marapu. ittakiya marapiṉit tamiḻccol vaṭṭattukkuḷḷē vittuk koṇṭāl ilakkaṇam taṉittaṉmiyuṭiyatākavum curukkamākavum kaṟpārkku vēṇṭumaḷavu viḷakkamākavum irukkum. piṟamoḻik kalappāl varum eḻuttu mutalkaṭṭum eḻuttīṟu kaṭkum ellām puṇarcciyilakkaṇam vakukkappaṭṭāl orumoḻikku ilakkaṇam vakuppatu, varampu kāṭṭuvatu eṉa ākāmal ūrmoḻikkellām vantavāṟu ētō vitippaṟi aṭippatākavē pōymuṭiyum. ōṭivarum nīriyellām kuṭikkalāmā? oru moḻiyiṉ ilakkaṇa nalamākāl ilakkaṇat toḻunōy ākiviṭum eṉṟu teḷinta nam ilakkaṇa maruttuvarkaḷ tolkāppiyar kālamutal vanta maṉṉiya meykaḷi varumoḻiyākap pōṟṟik koṇṭaṉar. ēṟum uyirkaḷ aḷavil puṇarcci iṭiyūṟillā māṟṟaṅkaḷit taḻuvikkoṇṭaṉar. eṉavē tamiḻ moḻimutal varampu puṇarcci varampākum eṉṟu ikkaṭṭuriyāl aṟiyalām.
āytakkēṭu
itteḷiniliyil cila āṇṭukaḷākap puṟṟīcal pōlap perukivarum varum oru moḻikkēṭṭiṉi īṇṭuccuṭṭuvatu eṉ kaṭamiyum poṟuppum ām. āytam cārpeḻuttu eṉavum aḵtu eḵtu eṉṟāṟpōlak kuṟṟeḻuttiṉpiṉṉum valleḻuttiṉ muṉṉum iṭiyē nalintuvarum eṉavum nām aṟivōm. mutaleḻuttāka eṇṇappaṭṭa ṭakara ṟakara lakara ḻakaraṅkaḷē moḻi mutaṉiliyāka vārātapōtu, nalipu vaṇṇam eṉṟu taḷḷappaṭṭa kuṟṟāytam ippōtu pala ayaṟcoṟkaḷil mutalāytamāka vantukoṇṭirukkiṟatu.
ḵpuḷu	ḵpiṉāṉciyar
ḵpil	ḵpiṭṭar
ḵpērm	ḵpelōcip
ḵpipilim	ḵpicceṭ
ḵpirī	ḵparṉiccar
ḵpērlēṭi	ḵpēṉkaḷ
ḵpōrṭu	ḵpiyarmeṉ
ḵpipcu	ḵpuṭvēr
ḵpēṉci	ḵpiṉleṇṭ
ḵpārmaci	ḵpirāṉcu
āṅkilattiṉ āṟāvatu eḻuttukku nikarāka iv vāytattik koṇṭuvarum pōkkiṉi viḷamparaṅ kaḷilum mikutiyākak kāṇkiṉṟōm. itaṉālvarum. varappōkum keṭutalkaḷi uṇarvār cilariṉum cilarē. tamiḻ tērntu teḷinta cemmoḻi eṉṟu aṟiyātārum, pukuntaṉa vantaṉa vellām vaḷarcci eṉṟu karutuvārum ivvāykkēṭṭiṉi uṇaramāṭṭārkaḷ. itaṉāl putiya coṟkaḷil ākkam taṭippaṭuvatōṭu, vēṟṟolikaḷ ellām tamiḻukku mutaleḻuttukkaḷāki viṭum. ātikkattiyum uṇara vēṇṭum. āytamum āṅkilattiṉ āṟāvatu eḻuttum oṉṟu eṉṟa mayakkam ēṟpaṭṭuviṭṭāl, aḵṟiṇi, eḵku, aḵkutal, veḵkāmi, paḵṟoṭi, aḵtu, iḵtu, paḵṟuḻi, kaḵcu mutalāṉa tamiḻccoṟkaḷ varuṅkālattil eṉṉa olippāṭu paṭumō?
ilakkiyam kaṇṭataṟku ilakkaṇam iyampal eṉpatu naṉmarapāyiṉum, aṅṅaṉam ilakkaṇam iyampuvataṟkumuṉ etu  ilakkiyam eṉṟu pakuttaṟivup pārvi vēṇṭum. ippakuttaṟivip peṟṟiruntavarkaḷ nam ilakkaṇac cāṉṟōrkaḷ. ataṉālaṉṟō nam toṉmoḻi iṉṟum ilaṅkumoḻiyāka niṟkiṉṟatu purikiṉṟatu.
kaṇṇutaṟ peruṅkaṭavuḷum kaḻakamō ṭamarntu
paṇṇuṟat terintāynta ippacuntamiḻ ēṉi
maṇṇiṭic cilavilakkaṇa varampilā moḻipōl
eṇṇiṭip paṭakkiṭantatā eṇṇavum paṭumō.

tolkāppiyam moḻimarapum
māṇikkavuriyum muṟṟum.
